Abstract: Attention is essential for modeling spatio-temporal dependencies in video diffusion transformers, but its quadratic cost makes it a major inference bottleneck. Its high sparsity offers an opportunity to accelerate generation by focusing computation on important interactions. However, accuracy and execution favor different granularities: each query attends to its own key tokens, whereas GPU kernels work on tiles. We present Token2Tile, a training-free sparse attention operator that combines per-query scoring and a per-query residual with tile execution. An online low-rank space, computed directly from queries and keys to preserve attention logits, makes clustering, scoring, and residual approximation efficient. Token2Tile unites query-specific selections to allocate exact work across query clusters, then packs the selected keys into GPU tiles. On MiniMax-H3 and Wan2.1-14B, Token2Tile delivers 1.48-1.54x end-to-end speedup over dense FlashAttention-3 with VBench scores within 0.5 points of dense. At comparable speeds on H3, it improves PSNR over SOL and SubBlock by 5.5-6.8 dB; on Wan, it exceeds SOL by 4.6-4.8 dB while running faster.

</details>


### [37] [Domain Adaptation Based Pipeline for Character Classification and Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17952)
*Florent Imbert,Simon Corbillé,Hui Han,Elisa H. Barney Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于无监督域适应的三阶段流水线，利用未标注目标数据缩小源域与目标域分布差距，在字符分类和历史手写文本识别任务上取得新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符分类和手写文本识别（尤其是专门任务）常面临标注数据稀缺问题，而大量未标注数据未被利用。目标是通过无监督域适应减少源域与目标域之间的分布差距。

Method: 三阶段流水线：1）在源数据上预训练专家模型学习通用表征；2）执行无监督域适应，利用未标注目标数据将模型适配到目标域特性；3）在目标风格化的源数据上训练专家模型，通过对齐源域与目标域特征分布实现跨数据集泛化。无监督训练仅增加训练阶段计算时间，不影响推理。

Result: 在字符级和历史手写行识别任务上验证了灵活性；在DAGECC竞赛Track 2的Safran-MNIST-DLS数据集上以76.17% Macro F1取得新SOTA；在READ 2018各具体数据集上不使用目标标签即取得有竞争力结果。

Conclusion: 该无监督域适应流水线能有效利用未标注数据、缓解标注稀缺问题，并在字符分类与手写文本识别任务上实现灵活且领先的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Character classification and handwritten text recognition, particularly in specialized tasks, often suffer from limited labeled data, while a large amount of unlabeled data remains unused. To address this challenge, we propose a novel unsupervised domain adaptation-based pipeline to reduce the distribution gap between source and target domains. Our pipeline follows three main steps. In the first step, we pretrained an expert model on source data, to learn a strong general representation. In the second step, we perform unsupervised domain adaptation to leverage unlabeled target data, adapting the model to the specific characteristics of the target domain. In the third step, we trained the expert model on source data in the target style. By aligning the feature distributions between the source and target, our method enables the model to generalize effectively across diverse datasets. The unsupervised training increases the computation time only during training not in inference. We evaluate the pipeline on both character-level and historical handwritten line recognition tasks, demonstrating its flexibility. We establish a new state-of-the-art on the Safran-MNIST-DLS dataset defined in Track 2 of the DAGECC competition with 76.17% Macro F1-score and achieve competitive results on each of the specific datasets of READ 2018 without using the labels of the target data. Code is available at https://github.com/simon-corbi/DRANet-for-classification-and-htr

</details>


### [38] [nnAEU: a Simple and Efficient CNN-Autoencoder for Hyperspectral Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17956)
*Edgard Dabier,Christophe Kervazo,Pietro Gori,Florence Tupin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 nnAEU，一个轻量级 CNN 自编码器用于高光谱解混，通过裁剪 UNMamba 的 Mamba 模块得到，参数量比 UNMamba 少 10 倍以上，但精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱解混（HSU）的深度学习方法沿"架构越来越复杂"的路线演进（自编码器→Transformer→Mamba），各方法都宣称达到 SOTA。作者质疑这一趋势，希望验证：复杂架构是否真有必要，能否用极简 CNN 取得更好效果。

Method: 采用消融/剪枝研究：以 UNMamba（2025）的网络主干为起点，系统移除 Mamba 组件，得到一个轻量级 CNN 自编码器 nnAEU；保持极简结构并公开可复现代码。

Result: nnAEU 参数量比 UNMamba 少 10 倍以上，且远少于其他 Transformer 和 GAN 类方法，但在解混精度上优于它们。

Conclusion: 复杂架构并非 HSU 性能提升的必要条件；简单、轻量、可复现的 CNN 自编码器即可超越更复杂的 SOTA 方法，为社区提供了反思架构复杂化趋势的基线。

Abstract: Deep learning-based hyperspectral unmixing (HSU) has followed a trajectory of increasing architectural complexity, from simple autoencoders to transformers and, most recently, Mamba-based models, each claiming to advance the state of the art. Contrary to this trend, we propose a lightweight CNN-based autoencoder, coined "Not New Autoencoder Unmixing" (nnAEU). It is derived from the backbone of UNMamba (2025) through a pruning study that removes the Mamba component entirely. nnAEU uses less than 10 times fewer parameters than UNMamba, and far fewer than other transformer- and GAN-based methods, while outperforming them in accuracy. We provide a complete, minimal, reproducible code, available at https://gitlab.telecom-paris.fr/ring/nnAEU.git.

</details>


### [39] [MacroGS: Macro-Pixel Shared Gaussian Primitives for Arbitrary-Scale Light Field Super-Resolutio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17957)
*Zeqiang Wei,Angulia Yang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MacroGS，一种前馈解码器，按"宏像素"联合预测单一共享高斯集合（而非逐视角生成），实现任意尺度光场空间超分，使高斯基元数量与视角数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意尺度光场超分中，高斯基元可做连续重建，但逐视角生成独立基元集会冗余编码跨视角共享的场景结构，且基元数随视角数线性增长。

Method: 每个宏像素只预测一个共享高斯集；用视差相关的替换机制与角度条件的外观，使该集合可复用于所有输出视角；解码器迭代预测每基元视差、检索对应多视图特征并更新基元状态；再用高斯泼溅与残差细化重建输出光场。训练无需几何监督。

Result: 预测视差与真实场景几何高度相关；在五个基准上与多种光场编码器有效结合，固定尺度质量提升，多数任意尺度设置达领先性能；相比SOTA隐式光场解码器加速约2–10×，相比逐视角高斯基线加速10–18×。

Conclusion: 以宏像素共享高斯表示可消除视角冗余、解耦基元数与视角数，兼顾质量、任意尺度泛化与效率。

Abstract: Arbitrary-scale light field spatial super-resolution reconstructs high-resolution views at any requested scale from low-resolution multi-view observations. Gaussian primitives enable continuous reconstruction, but generating separate primitive sets for each view redundantly encodes shared scene structure across views and causes the primitive count grow linearly with the number of views. To avoid such redundancy, we propose MacroGS, a feed-forward decoder that jointly predicts a single shared Gaussian set per macro pixel rather than generating separate sets for each view. Disparity-dependent replacement and angle-conditioned appearance allow each set to be reused across all output views, keeping the predicted primitive count independent of the number of views at each requested scale. The decoder iteratively predicts per-primitive disparities, retrieves the corresponding multi-view features, and updates the primitive states. Gaussian splatting and residual refinement then reconstruct the output light field. Despite being learned without geometric supervision, the predicted disparities strongly correlate with underlying scene geometry. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that MacroGS integrates effectively with diverse light field encoders, improving reconstruction quality at fixed scales while achieving leading performance across most evaluated arbitrary-scale settings. The decoder also achieves speedups of approximately $2$-$10\times$ over state-of-the-art implicit light field decoders and $10$-$18\times$ over the view-wise Gaussian baseline.

</details>


### [40] [From Images to Grounded Edits: A Real-World Benchmark for Chinese Grammatical Error Correction in Handwritten Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17966)
*Weiru Zhang,Yuzhong Hong,Junwei Bao,Lemao Liu,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出G-CGEC（图像到定位编辑）新任务与G-CGEC-Eval基准，要求模型在手写中文作文图像上同时预测语法纠错内容及其图像位置，并评测开源/闭源VLM在单次直推、流水线与智能体编排下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CGEC被建模为纯文本到文本问题，适用于数字文本；但K-12教育场景中AI批改需直接作用于以手写为主、极不规则的作文图像，现有范式无法满足这一真实需求。

Method: 构建含细粒度标注的真实K-12中文作文图像基准G-CGEC-Eval；评测指标上迁移目标检测的IoU指标，并提出基于中心距匹配的更宽松替代指标；在单次直推、OCR/字符定位辅助的流水线、智能体编排（含按错误类型的多智能体工作流）等多种设置下评测开源与闭源VLM，并进行消融与细粒度分析。

Result: 即便顶级闭源模型在单次直推下表现也出奇地差；流水线与智能体编排带来显著提升；基于错误类型的多智能体工作流效果最佳，成为最强基线；消融分析显示语言错误发现与字符级定位是主要瓶颈，任务远未解决。

Conclusion: G-CGEC揭示了视觉感知与语言推理、视觉定位需深度结合的需求，指明未来研究方向：提升错误发现与字符级grounding能力，并推动多组件协同的智能体式纠错框架。

Abstract: Chinese Grammatical Error Correction (CGEC) is typically formulated as a text-to-text problem. While suitable for digital texts, this formulation falls short in real-world K-12 education, where AI-assisted corrections are expected directly on essay images, which are primarily handwritten and exhibit many irregularities. To bridge this gap, we introduce Grounded Chinese Grammatical Error Correction (G-CGEC), an image-to-grounded-edit task requiring systems to predict both corrections and their positions on essay images. We propose G-CGEC-Eval, a benchmark comprising real-world images of K-12 Chinese essays with fine-grained annotations. For evaluation, we adapt IoU-based metrics from object detection and propose a more tolerant alternative based on center-distance matching. Using this benchmark, we evaluate open-source and proprietary vision-language models under a direct single-pass setting, as well as settings with auxiliary components (OCR and character grounding) coordinated via pipelining or agentic harnessing. Results show that even top-tier proprietary models perform surprisingly poorly in the single-pass setting, while pipelining and agentic harnessing yield meaningful improvements. Notably, an error-type-based multi-agent workflow achieves the best performance, establishing it as the strongest baseline for future G-CGEC research. Through ablations and fine-grained analysis, we find the task remains far from solved, with linguistic error discovery and character-level grounding as the primary bottlenecks. This underscores the need for models that better integrate visual perception with linguistic reasoning and visual grounding.

</details>


### [41] [RecurIR: Learning When and How to Revise Restoration Policies via Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18006)
*Hu Gao,Haoran Zhang,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RecurIR，一个递归自我改进框架，让复原智能体显式学习"规则何时失效、如何修正"，通过假设发现、规则证伪驱动的任务演化与条件感知的策略修正循环提升复原策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的图像复原依赖成功/失败执行的经验累积来改进决策，但未显式识别已学复原策略的适用条件。当退化严重程度、空间覆盖范围或退化间交互发生变化时，旧决策可能失效，且缺乏如何修订这些决策的指导。

Method: 1) 策略假设发现机制：通过匹配的工具执行，识别可检验、依赖条件的规则；2) 规则证伪驱动的任务演化机制：生成有针对性的退化变体，检验规则有效性并确定其适用边界；3) 条件感知的策略修正机制：学习退化条件变化应如何调整工具选择、执行顺序、操作强度与处理区域；4) 引入独立验证保留有效修正，并将反馈用于后续的假设发现与任务演化，形成递归闭环。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提框架的有效性；递归过程使RecurIR能随退化条件变化逐步精炼复原策略。

Conclusion: RecurIR通过显式刻画策略的适用边界与失效条件，并据此进行有依据的规则修订，实现了复原智能体的递归自我改进，优于仅靠经验累积的智能体。

Abstract: Agent-based image restoration has achieved promising performance by coordinating restoration tools to handle heterogeneous and coupled degradations. However, existing agents often improve their decisions through accumulated successful and failed executions, without explicitly identifying the conditions under which learned restoration strategies remain effective. Consequently, changes in degradation severity, spatial coverage, or interactions may invalidate previous decisions, while providing limited guidance on how these decisions should be revised. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RecurIR}, a recursive self-improvement framework that enables restoration agents to learn when and how to revise their decision rules. Specifically, we introduce a policy-hypothesis discovery mechanism to identify testable, condition-dependent rules through matched tool executions. We then design a rule-falsification-driven task evolution mechanism to generate targeted degradation variations, examine the validity of learned rules, and identify their applicability boundaries. Based on the resulting evidence, we develop a change-conditioned policy revision mechanism that learns how variations in degradation conditions should modify tool selection, execution order, operation strength, and processing regions. We further incorporate independent verification to retain effective revisions and use the resulting feedback to guide subsequent hypothesis discovery and task evolution. Through this recursive process, RecurIR progressively refines its restoration policies as degradation conditions change. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [42] [RSSA: Robust Semantic and Spatial Aligner for Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18023)
*Zhihao Yang,Zhiyu Xiang,Peng Xu,Tianyu Pu,Kai Wang,Eryun Liu,Dongping Zhang,Yong Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用模块RSSA，通过语义变换与概率空间变换实现V2X协同感知中鲁棒的特征对齐，在干净与噪声位置下均大幅超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X协同感知方法仅依赖简单空间变换对齐协同特征，导致融合时出现语义错配；且这类确定性变换对位置噪声高度敏感。

Method: RSSA包含三部分：(1) Semantic Transformation (SemT) 块，依据观测到的相对位姿调制特征通道以纠正语义不一致；(2) Probabilistic Spatial Transformation (P-SpaT) 块，从高斯建模的相对位姿空间采样实现概率化warping；(3) Relative Transformation Augmentation (RT-Aug) 策略，增强相对位姿以辅助训练。

Result: 在仿真与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，RSSA在干净和噪声位置配置下均显著提升SOTA方法性能。

Conclusion: 语义与概率空间双重对齐能有效缓解语义错配与位置噪声敏感性，RSSA可作为通用插件提升现有协同感知框架的鲁棒性。

Abstract: V2X collaborative perception improves single-vehicle perception by aggregating multi-view features from collaborative agents. However, existing methods typically rely on simple spatial warping to align the collaborative features, which can cause semantic misalignment during fusion. Meanwhile, these deterministic warping-based methods are highly sensitive to the positional noises. In this paper, we propose a plug-in module called Robust Semantic and Spatial Aligner (RSSA), which performs semantic and probabilistic spatial transformations for robust feature alignment. Specifically, a Semantic Transformation (SemT) block modulates feature channels based on the observed relative pose to correct semantic inconsistencies. Subsequently, a Probabilistic Spatial Transformation (P-SpaT) block performs probabilistic warping by sampling from the Gaussian-modeled relative pose space. A Relative Transformation Augmentation (RT-Aug) strategy is further introduced to augment the relative poses and facilitate the training of the RSSA. Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate that RSSA improves the state-of-the-art methods by a large margin under the configuration of both clean and noisy positions.

</details>


### [43] [JaRS: Contrast-Free Localization of Memorized Regions in Diffusion Models via Jacobian Row Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18024)
*Saisuchith Mahajan,Aditey Nandan,Rohit Singh,Nishanth Shetty,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Jacobian 行和(JaRS)及检测分数 JaRS+，无需参考图即可定位扩散模型对训练图像的记忆区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型会记忆并复现训练图像的区域，带来隐私与版权风险，需要定位这些区域。已有基于对比的方法使用去噪器 Jacobian 对角线反映像素后验方差，但对角线小值存在歧义（可能是记忆，也可能是在平滑区域做邻域平均），因此需减去参考图（空提示或早期检查点），而无条件模型无空提示、早期检查点通常不可得。

Method: 指出 Jacobian 行和(JaRS)与对角线不同，能区分记忆与局部平均，无需参考图；并将 JaRS 与早期去噪步使用同一统计量的图像级检测分数配对，构成 JaRS+。

Result: 在 Stable Diffusion v1.4、v2.1、Realistic Vision v5.1 及无条件扩散 Transformer(DiT)上，JaRS+ 在定位性能上优于基于对比的方法。

Conclusion: Jacobian 行和是解决对角线歧义的有效替代统计量，可在无参考图、无条件模型等更一般设定下实现记忆区域定位与图像级检测。

Abstract: Diffusion models sometimes memorize and reproduce regions of their training images, raising privacy and copyright concerns. We aim to localize these regions. Existing contrast-based methods use the diagonal of the denoiser Jacobian, which reflects the posterior variance of each pixel. However, a small diagonal entry is ambiguous: it signals either memorization or averaging over many neighboring pixels in smooth or predictable regions. To resolve this ambiguity, these methods subtract a reference map, which requires a second evaluation with a null prompt or an earlier checkpoint. Unconditional models have no null prompt, and earlier checkpoints are unavailable in general. We show that Jacobian row sums (JaRS), unlike the diagonal, separate memorization from local averaging without a reference map. Pairing JaRS with an image-level detection score that uses the same statistic at an early denoising step yields JaRS+. Across Stable Diffusion v1.4 and v2.1, Realistic Vision v5.1, and an unconditional diffusion Transformer (DiT), JaRS+ outperforms contrast-based localization methods.

</details>


### [44] [Multi-level latent-diffusion adaptation for cross-region point cloud segmentation of ancient terraces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18033)
*Sina Mohammadi,Roderik Lindenbergh,Javier Ruiz-Pérez,Marco Madella,Sebastian Fajardo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MELD：用多层级嵌入空间潜扩散做无监督域适应，实现跨区域古梯田LiDAR检测；加入局部几何描述子后F1达0.542/0.606。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR点云的自动化特征检测可辅助考古调查，但模型跨景观迁移性能差，原因包括地形、植被、采集条件和特征形态差异。本文针对无目标标签的跨区域古梯田检测问题。

Method: 提出MELD（多层级嵌入空间潜扩散）：在四个PointNet++层级特征上分别训练目标域扩散去噪器，得到目标分布先验，用于在监督训练中修正源域表示、在推理时修正目标域表示，使适应直接作用于分割解码器所用的多尺度特征。另探究基于KNN邻域的局部几何描述子（法向、坡度、坡向）。在哥伦比亚Teyuna与Miraflores两个LiDAR测区做双向评估，对比源域模型和9种主流域适应配置。

Result: Teyuna→Miraflores的F1从0.397升至0.459，反向从0.557升至0.594，两个方向均为对比方法中最高。加入局部几何后分别升至0.542和0.606。多层级消融显示修正全部四个PointNet++层级在两个方向均最优。

Conclusion: 在分层点云特征空间显式建模目标特征分布，并辅以局部几何，是在无目标标签条件下实现跨区域考古制图的有效途径。

Abstract: Automated lidar feature detection from lidar point clouds can support archaeological surveys, but models trained in one landscape may transfer poorly to another because of differences in terrain, vegetation, acquisition characteristics, and feature morphology. We address this problem for cross-region detection of ancient terraces using transductive unsupervised domain adaptation that exploits an unlabelled target survey. We propose MELD (Multi-level Embedding-space Latent Diffusion), which trains separate diffusion denoisers on target-domain representations at four hierarchical PointNet++ feature levels. These target distribution priors are then used to refine source representations during supervised training and target representations during inference, allowing adaptation to act directly on the multi-scale features consumed by the segmentation decoder. We additionally investigate local geometric descriptors derived from K-nearest-neighbour neighbourhoods, including surface normals, slope, and aspect. MELD is evaluated bidirectionally between the Teyuna and Miraflores lidar surveys in the Sierra Nevada de Santa Marta, Colombia, against a source-only model and nine configurations of established domain adaptation approaches. MELD raises F1 over the source-only model from 0.397 to 0.459 for Teyuna-to-Miraflores transfer and from 0.557 to 0.594 in the reverse direction, achieving the highest F1 among the evaluated adaptation methods in both directions. Adding local geometry further raises F1 to 0.542 and 0.606, respectively. Multi-level ablation shows that refining all four PointNet++ levels provides the strongest performance in both directions. These results show that explicit modelling of target feature distributions in hierarchical point cloud feature space, complemented by local geometry, provides an effective route to cross-region archaeological mapping without target labels.

</details>


### [45] [InfiHall-Bench: A Benchmark for Hallucination Evaluation in Autoregressive Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18053)
*Yuxia Qiao,Xinhao Zhong,Junhao Li,Yi Sun,Jiaxin Hong,Shuoyang Sun,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向视觉自回归(VAR)模型的幻觉基准 InfiHall-Bench(411条提示,基于五种可验证视觉技能组合构建),发现VAR模型幻觉具有"跨尺度持续性",并据此提出置信度掩码微调方法CMHM以缓解幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图幻觉基准多围绕扩散模型设计,对VAR模型诊断价值有限;且提示构建存在两大缺陷:许多提示对现代生成器已不具挑战性,基于类别的构建依赖重复的"物体-属性-关系"模板,难以测试多视觉需求在同一图像中的交互。

Method: 构建InfiHall-Bench:系统组合属性绑定、空间关系、动作关系、部件一致性、场景一致性五类基础且可视觉验证的技能,每条提示平均覆盖4.05个标注技能。基于"幻觉跨尺度持续"的观察,提出CMHM:在教师强制微调中对每个自回归尺度的目标视觉token估计预测置信度,降低高置信token权重,使优化聚焦于跨尺度的不确定预测。

Result: VAR模型的幻觉呈现尺度级持续模式:语义错误可在不同自回归尺度产生,且很少被后续细尺度步骤纠正。InfiHall-Bench能区分被现有组合基准评为相当的模型;CMHM在VAR文生图中持续优于统一LoRA微调。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了VAR模型幻觉评测与缓解的空白,揭示了跨尺度持续性幻觉模式,并证明基于置信度的非均匀微调是有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: Hallucination benchmarks for text-to-image generation are predominantly designed around diffusion models, limiting their diagnostic value for visual autoregressive (VAR) models and the development of mitigation methods. We identify two limitations in existing prompt construction paradigms: many prompts are no longer challenging for modern generators, while category-based construction relies on recurring object-attribute-relation templates and rarely tests interactions among multiple visual requirements within a single image.
  We introduce InfiHall-Bench, a controlled benchmark of 411 prompts constructed by systematically composing five foundational and visually verifiable skills: attribute binding, spatial relation, action relation, part consistency, and scene consistency. Each prompt covers 4.05 annotated skills on average, enabling the evaluation of hallucinations under diverse combinations of visual requirements. Using this benchmark, we find that hallucinations in VAR models exhibit a scale-wise persistence pattern: semantic errors can emerge at different autoregressive scales and are rarely corrected by subsequent fine-scale steps.
  Motivated by this observation, we propose Confidence-Masked Hallucination Mitigation (CMHM), which estimates the prediction confidence of target visual tokens at each autoregressive scale during teacher-forced fine-tuning. CMHM downweights reliably predicted tokens, allowing optimization to focus on uncertain predictions across scales. Experiments show that InfiHall-Bench distinguishes models that existing compositional benchmarks rate as comparable, while CMHM consistently outperforms uniform LoRA fine-tuning for VAR-based text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [46] [SWAM: Saliency-Weighted Asymmetric Merging for Efficient Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18057)
*Utkarsh Kumar Singh,Manish Das,Shiv Ram Dubey,Satish Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWAM：基于显著性加权的非对称token合并方法，在DeiT-Small上以27.5%~33.8% FLOPs削减实现匹配或更优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT自注意力对token呈二次复杂度，部署成本高；现有token reduction方法（如BSM）未显式考虑token显著性差异，合并时可能损害重要token信息。

Method: 在Bipartite Soft Matching基础上引入两个显著性机制：(1)两步匹配——余弦相似度决定候选对，乘性最大显著性惩罚调节合并选择；(2)非对称指数显著性加权聚合，使高显著token在合并表示中权重更大。另设轻量MLP显著性预测模块（每阶段75k参数，约占主干1%），以attention rollout监督，与DeiT-Small端到端联合训练，损失为交叉熵+知识蒸馏+显著性对齐的复合目标。

Result: 保守调度（196→100 token，保留CLS，FLOPs降27.5%，加速1.35×）下，在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet、ImageNet-100上与基线持平或更优；激进调度（196→70，FLOPs降33.8%，加速1.46×）下ImageNet-100提升0.23%，其余两数据集损失极小。

Conclusion: 显著性感知的两步匹配与非对称加权聚合可在显著降低计算量的同时保持甚至提升精度，且额外参数开销可忽略，具备实用部署价值。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance on image recognition tasks, yet their quadratic self-attention complexity over input tokens creates prohibitive computational costs for practical deployment. Token reduction methods offer a practical solution by progressively reducing the token sequence during the forward pass. We propose Saliency-Weighted Asymmetric Merging (SWAM), a training-based token merging framework that extends the Bipartite Soft Matching (BSM) with two saliency-aware mechanisms. We introduce a two-step matching formulation in which cosine similarity decides candidate pairs, while a multiplicative maximum saliency penalty regulates their selection for merging. We then further use asymmetric exponential saliency-weighted aggregation, which allows more salient tokens to contribute more strongly to the merged representation. A lightweight MLP-based saliency prediction module (75k parameters per stage, 1% of backbone parameters) supervised via attention rollout is trained end-to-end jointly with a DeiT-Small backbone using a composite cross-entropy, knowledge-distillation, and saliency-alignment objective. On CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and ImageNet-100, under a conservative schedule that reduces tokens from 196 to 100 patch tokens, with the CLS token retained throughout, 27.5% FLOPs reduction and 1.35 times speedup, SWAM matches or exceeds baseline across the reported results. Under an aggressive schedule (196 to 70 tokens with the CLS token kept throughout, 33.8% FLOPs reduction, and 1.46 times speedup), accuracy gains are achieved on ImageNet-100 (+0.23%) with minimal losses on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet.

</details>


### [47] [TelePhysica: Agentic Reconstruction of Physically Interactive Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18078)
*Ruiqi Shu,Xufeng Zhan,Yuanzhi Liang,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TelePhysica：从单张照片闭环重建可执行的多物理体系世界，并以此驱动有状态3D交互与轨迹约束的视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接的图生视频模型把像素合成与隐式潜在运动纠缠在一起，无法维持持续的三维力学平衡，也不支持中途干预和反事实力控制；而单次程序化三维重建受单目深度与支撑间隙约束不足影响，重建场景会互相穿模或在重力下坍塌。

Method: 用视觉语言编码智能体在两阶段验证循环中合成可分离视觉网格与声明式物理模型（像素对齐轮廓评判 + 零重力平衡预检）；验证后的世界编译进统一求解器，耦合刚体、铰接链、同维布料壳与三维四面体弹性动力学；模拟轨迹以像素对齐结构流形式条件化预训练视频扩散模型。

Result: 100场景单图基准上，平衡验证的世界恢复率从单次合成的0%提升到81%；本构消融显示有限元布料局部角点位移与面外褶皱比匹配刚板代理大一个数量级以上；以模拟轨迹条件化使动态运动掩码重叠提升2–5倍，并在反事实力反转下保持运动方向，而直接图生视频模型失败。

Conclusion: 将物理可执行性与视频生成闭环耦合，可同时获得可干预、可验证的物理世界重建与更符合动力学的视频合成。

Abstract: Direct image-to-video diffusion models entangle pixel synthesis with implicit latent motion, precluding persistent 3D mechanical equilibrium, mid-rollout intervention, and counterfactual force control. Conversely, single-pass programmatic 3D reconstruction from an uncalibrated photograph leaves monocular depth and support clearance underconstrained, causing reconstructed scenes to interpenetrate or collapse under gravity. We present TelePhysica, a closed-loop framework that reconstructs an executable, multi-regime physical world from a single photograph to drive stateful 3D interaction and trajectory-grounded video synthesis. A vision-language coding agent synthesizes separable visual meshes and declarative physical models within a two-stage verification loop that pairs pixel-aligned silhouette critique with a zero-force gravitational equilibrium preflight. The verified world compiles into a unified solver coupling rigid bodies, articulated chains, co-dimensional cloth shells, and 3D tetrahedral elastodynamics, whose simulated rollouts condition pretrained video diffusion models through pixel-aligned structural streams. On a 100-scene single-image benchmark, closed-loop execution raises equilibrium-verified world recovery from $0\%$ in single-pass synthesis to $81\%$. Controlled constitutive ablations show over an order-of-magnitude larger localized corner displacement and out-of-plane pleating in finite-element cloth than in matched rigid-plate proxies. Conditioning video diffusion on simulated trajectories increases dynamic motion-mask overlap by $2{\times}\text{--}5{\times}$ and preserves motion direction under counterfactual force reversals where direct image-to-video models fail.

</details>


### [48] [AG-JEPA: Anatomically Guided Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Neurodegenerative Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18097)
*Azad Singh,Ayantika Das,Amog Rao,Aiman Farooq,Anirban Porya,Ananya Shukla,Sowbhagya Suresh,Srinivasan Iyengar,Mohit Jain,Mercy Prasanna Ranjit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AG-JEPA与Prog-AG-JEPA，引入解剖token学习脑区特异表征，提升阿尔茨海默病早期诊断与进展预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI可在认知下降可测前揭示AD早期迹象，但CN向MCI过渡期信号微弱且局限于少数脑区；现有JEPA学习通用结构表征，未显式捕捉AD相关解剖区域。

Method: AG-JEPA在预训练中引入解剖token与patch token交互，学习神经退行相关脑区的区域特异表征；在此基础上Prog-AG-JEPA将疾病进展轨迹建模为该表征空间中的条件化潜位移。

Result: 在5个下游任务（AD诊断与进展预测）上评估：CN/MCI分类AUROC较最强基线提升8.4个百分点；MR-RATE数据集OOD CN/AD分类提升7.1个百分点；在未来诊断与脑体积变化预测上均优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 解剖学引导的神经退行表征对早期检测、跨队列泛化及预后建模均有价值。

Abstract: Brain MRI can reveal early signs of Alzheimers disease (AD) before measurable cognitive decline becomes apparent. However, during the transition from cognitively normal (CN) to mild cognitive impairment (MCI), the disease-related signals are often subtle and confined to only a few brain regions. Existing Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) models learn general structural representations but do not explicitly capture the anatomical regions affected in AD. We propose Anatomically Guided JEPA (AG-JEPA), which introduces anatomical tokens that interact with patch tokens during pretraining to learn region-specific representations from brain regions relevant to neurodegeneration. Building on this representation space, we further propose Prog-AG-JEPA, which models disease progression trajectory as a conditioned latent displacement within this space. We evaluate AG-JEPA across five downstream tasks spanning Alzheimer's disease diagnosis and progression prediction. AG-JEPA improves diagnosis and MCI prognosis, achieving an 8.4 percentage point AUROC gain over the strongest baseline on CN/MCI classification and a 7.1 percentage point gain on out-of-distribution CN/AD classification on the MR-RATE dataset, highlighting its effectiveness across both early-stage detection and cross-cohort generalization. For disease progression modeling, Prog-AG-JEPA consistently outperforms all baselines on future diagnosis and brain volume change prediction, demonstrating the value of anatomically grounded neurodegeneration representations for diagnosis and prognosis.

</details>


### [49] [EcoVGGT: Pinned-Key Inference for Efficient and Streamable Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction from Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18099)
*Hongsen Cao,Shanxin Yuan,Mona Jaber,Ahmed Sayed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EcoVGGT 是一个免训练推理框架，用固定选出的 31 帧作为“Pinned Key Set”供所有帧共享注意力键，从而把长视频多视角重建的跨帧注意力成本变为每帧常数，并配合中间全局块的复用、失败检测与重聚焦微调提升长视频精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT、π³ 等前馈多视角 Transformer 能一次前向恢复相机与几何，但跨帧注意力随帧数二次增长，长视频精度下降；现有方法用预算近似或按内容选键，作者假设长视频的键选择可以不依赖内容，仅用均匀分布的少数帧即可代表全体。

Method: 在 VGGT 与 π³ 上验证均匀采样几十帧作为共享键即可；提出 EcoVGGT：Pin pass 将 31 帧均匀帧的 token 存入 Scene Cache，其余帧分批读取该缓存，独立批推理精确复现联合推理结果且每帧成本恒定；Online Stream 在录制过程中更新固定帧；六个中间全局块构成可复用 VGGT 模块，Alignment Score 读取其注意力以标记失败，Alignment Refocusing 仅微调这些块（3.6 GPU 小时）来降低失败。

Result: EcoVGGT(π³) 在 1000 帧 ScanNet 视频上成功率与 π³ 持平，速度提升 13.8×；加入 refocusing 后 EcoVGGT(VGGT) 在 500 帧上成功率从 70% 提升到 82%，速度提升 8.5×；单 GPU 可重建 4000 帧；EcoVGGT(π³) 以 18.6 FPS 流式处理 1000 帧并获得最低在线绝对轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 长视频的注意力键选择可以不依赖内容，均匀少量固定帧足以支撑精确且恒定成本的长视频重建；结合轻量微调的复用模块可同时提升速度与成功率，使方法具备长序列和在线流式能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward multi-view transformers such as VGGT recover the cameras and geometry of a video in one pass, but the cost of their cross-frame attention grows quadratically with the frame count, and their accuracy drops on long videos. Most remedies approximate attention over all frames within a budget or select a few keys by their content. We hypothesize that the keys of a long video can be chosen without looking at its content, and our experiments on VGGT and $π^3$ support it: a few dozen evenly spread frames can serve as the keys of every frame. We present EcoVGGT, a training-free inference framework whose keys form a Pinned Key Set of 31 such frames. A pin pass stores their tokens in a Scene Cache, and the other frames read it in independent batches that reproduce joint inference with these keys exactly, at a constant cost per frame. An Online Stream updates the pinned frames during recording. Six middle global blocks align the frames and form a reusable VGGT module: the Alignment Score reads their attention to flag failures, and Alignment Refocusing reduces them by fine-tuning only these blocks (3.6 GPU hours). EcoVGGT ($π^3$) matches the success rate of $π^3$ on 1,000-frame ScanNet videos at 13.8 times its speed. With refocusing, EcoVGGT (VGGT) raises the success rate of VGGT from 70% to 82% at 8.5 times its speed on 500 frames. EcoVGGT reconstructs 4,000 frames on one GPU, and EcoVGGT ($π^3$) streams 1,000 frames at 18.6 frames per second with the lowest online absolute trajectory error.

</details>


### [50] [KnowVis: Dual-View Evaluation and Diagnosis of World-Knowledge Grounding in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18101)
*Yonghang Yu,Ruoyu Wang,Haoyu Zhang,Lin Qi,Yawei Zhou,Changqian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KnowVis：面向T2I模型世界知识落地的双视角评测与诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I评测多聚焦显式提示-图像语义对齐或特定知识子域，缺少对世界知识落地的统一评测与诊断。

Method: 基于分层知识分类体系与案例级标注，构建Skill-Tree（原子知识细粒度评测）与Generalized（开放式知识组合与表达）两个互补基准视角，并配合结构化MLLM-VQA评判协议：前者用Required VQA判定原子知识正确性，后者融合Required、Optional与动态发现的知识表达，辅以人机协同评估。

Result: 在Skill-Tree验证集上验证协议可靠性，实验显示现有T2I模型在世界知识落地上存在显著差距。

Conclusion: KnowVis支持可扩展评测与诊断式错误归因，为知识密集型图像生成提供结构化诊断信号。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have made substantial progress in visual realism, aesthetic quality, and instruction following. However, real-world prompts often go beyond explicit visual descriptions and require implicit facts, structured knowledge, and domain-specific commonsense. Existing evaluations mainly focus on explicit prompt-to-image semantic alignment or specific knowledge subdomains, lacking unified evaluation and diagnosis of world-knowledge grounding in T2I models. We introduce KnowVis for dual-view evaluation and diagnosis of world-knowledge grounding in T2I generation. Built on a hierarchical knowledge taxonomy with case-level annotations, KnowVis combines two complementary benchmark views with a structured MLLM-based VQA judge protocol. The Skill-Tree Benchmark supports fine-grained atomic knowledge evaluation, while the Generalized Benchmark examines open-ended knowledge composition and expression. The protocol uses Required VQAs to assess atomic knowledge correctness in the Skill-Tree Benchmark, and combines Required, Optional, and dynamically discovered knowledge expressions with human-judge collaborative evaluation in the Generalized Benchmark. We validate the protocol's reliability on the Skill-Tree validation split, enabling scalable evaluation and diagnostic error attribution. Experiments show that KnowVis reveals notable gaps in world-knowledge grounding across existing T2I models and provides structured diagnostic signals for knowledge-intensive image generation.

</details>


### [51] [DRA-HTR: Disentangling Representation and Adaptation for Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18143)
*Gayan H. Pathirage,Florent Imbert,Denis Coquenet,Elisa H. Barney Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRA-HTR，一种面向手写文本识别(HTR)的无监督生成式域适应流程，并结合大模型纠错形成DRAL-HTR，在7个数据集上取得SOTA域适应性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史文档HTR面临标注数据稀缺及书写风格、文档退化、采集条件、语言差异导致的显著域偏移；虽有大量无标注目标域数据，但缺乏适配HTR需求的有效利用策略。

Method: 基于DRANet框架构建无监督生成式域适应流程：源域预训练 + 生成目标风格源样本进行任务特定训练以实现视觉适应；并引入基于大语言模型的后置HTR纠错(DRAL-HTR)实现语言适应；所有源-目标组合使用同一套超参数。

Result: 在4种语言、7个HTR数据集上评估：识别模型在4个数据集达SOTA，其余3个具竞争力；域适应在所有7个数据集均刷新SOTA，相较原DRANet平均CER降低13.52个百分点，标准差从7.23降至1.11个百分点；后置纠错进一步降低CER 2.16个百分点。

Conclusion: DRA-HTR有效提升HTR域适应的性能、鲁棒性与训练稳定性，且视觉适应与基于LLM的语言适应相结合可进一步增益。

Abstract: Handwritten text recognition (HTR), particularly when applied to historical documents, remains challenging due to the scarcity of annotated data and the substantial domain shifts caused by variations in handwriting styles, document degradation, acquisition conditions, and linguistic characteristics. While large amounts of unlabeled target-domain data are often available, effectively exploiting them requires adaptation strategies tailored to the specific requirements of HTR. We propose DRA-HTR, an unsupervised generative domain adaptation pipeline specifically designed for HTR and built upon the DRANet framework. The proposed approach combines source-domain pretraining, unsupervised adaptation to the visual characteristics of the target domain, relying on the generation of target-style source samples for task-specific training. We further investigate the integration of post-HTR correction to complement visual adaptation with language adaptation, relying on large language models, which we introduce as DRAL-HTR. The proposed framework is evaluated across seven HTR datasets spanning four languages, using a single set of hyperparameters for all source-target combinations. The recognition model achieves state-of-the-art performance on four datasets and competitive results on the remaining three. In addition, the proposed DRA-HTR establishes a new state-of-the-art for domain adaptation across all seven datasets and substantially improves overall robustness and training stability compared to the original DRANet framework: it reduces the Character Error Rate (CER) in average by 13.52 percentage points, and the average standard deviation decreases from 7.23 to 1.11 percentage points. Finally, post-HTR correction further reduces the CER by 2.16 percentage points.

</details>


### [52] [ALoMa: Affine Correspondences Beyond Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18170)
*David Nordström,Georg Bökman,Viktor Larsson,Fredrik Kahl,Jonathan Ventura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视仿射对应(Affine Correspondence, AC)估计问题，将估计范围从局部图像块扩展到图像级与图像对级，并联合学习完整的4自由度仿射矩阵，提出基于LoMa关键点描述的AC估计器ALoMa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AC通过约束点对应的局部几何，为多视图几何提供鲁棒高效的求解器，在最小求解器中作用显著。然而，AC估计本身研究不足，现有方法多局限于局部图像块且分组件独立估计仿射变换。

Method: 将AC估计从局部图像块拓展至图像级和图像对级；不再分别估计仿射变换各分量，而是联合学习完整4-DoF矩阵；基于该形式，利用LoMa关键点描述构建AC估计器ALoMa。

Result: ALoMa在广泛的下游任务中展现出持续一致的性能提升。代码已开源（github.com/davnords/loma）。

Conclusion: 联合学习完整4-DoF仿射矩阵并基于LoMa描述的AC估计器是有效且有前景的方向，可在多种下游任务中带来稳定收益。

Abstract: Affine correspondences (ACs) enable robust and efficient solvers for multiple-view geometry by constraining the local geometry of point correspondences (PCs). Despite their utility in minimal solvers, AC estimation remains understudied. We revisit AC estimation beyond local image patches, extending it to the image and image-pair levels. Rather than estimating the components of the affine transformation separately, we learn the full 4-DoF matrix jointly. Building on this formulation, we introduce ALoMa, an AC estimator using LoMa keypoint descriptions, demonstrating consistent improvements across a wide range of downstream tasks. Code at https://github.com/davnords/loma.

</details>


### [53] [STaG: Human-Annotation-Free Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Text-Based Person Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18172)
*Ji Yucheng,Bai Yang,Li Yang,Zhou Xinyu,Cao Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出更实用的"无人工标注"无监督域适应文本行人检索设定,并用双路框架STaG解决目标域跨模态对齐缺失与视觉域偏移问题,在三基准上达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UDA-TBPR方法仍假设目标域存在人工撰写的文本标注(即使与图像不配对),在新域缺乏文本标注时难以落地,实用性受限。

Method: 提出STaG双路框架:1) Style-Agnostic Text Alignment(SATA),利用源域数据将身份判别语义与机器生成的风格伪影解耦,为目标域建立鲁棒的跨模态对齐;2) Heterogeneous Graph Memory Alignment(HGMA),通过基于记忆的图挖掘跨域语义邻域,学习域不变视觉特征。

Result: 在该现实设定下,于三个基准数据集上取得最优性能(SOTA)。

Conclusion: 无需目标域人工文本标注的UDA-TBPR可行,通过语义解耦与跨域图记忆对齐可同时缓解跨模态对齐缺失与视觉域偏移。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of Text-Based Person Retrieval (TBPR) is bottlenecked by the absence of textual annotations in new domains. Existing Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA-TBPR) methods still assume access to target-domain human-written annotations (albeit unpaired with images), limiting their practicality. We consider a more practical setting: human-annotation-free UDA-TBPR, with labeled image-text data in the source domain and annotation-free, unlabeled images in the target domain. This setting introduces two core challenges: the absence of cross-modal alignment in the target domain, and visual domain shifts from source to target. To tackle these, we propose STaG, a dual-path framework comprising Style-Agnostic Text Alignment (SATA) and Heterogeneous Graph Memory Alignment (HGMA). Specifically, SATA leverages source-domain data to decouple identity-discriminative semantics from machine-generated stylistic artifacts, establishing robust cross-modal alignment for the target domain. Meanwhile, HGMA exploits cross-domain semantic neighborhoods via a memory-based graph to learn domain-invariant visual features. Ultimately, STaG achieves state-of-the-art performance across three benchmarks under this realistic setting.

</details>


### [54] [SemRAG: Semantic-Guided Sentence-Level Retrieval-Augmented Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18189)
*Xiao Liu,Shiwei Gan,Yafeng Yin,Peishuo Liu,Bowen Guo,Kuizhuang Liu,Cong Wang,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SemRAG，一种语义引导的句子级检索增强手语生成框架，直接检索完整句子手语模板作为先验，避免依赖词汇/手语词典，并通过SLT引导的模板语义定位与分层可学习门控解决检索模板的语义错配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLP方法从数据学习文本到姿态映射，长句/复杂句语义不准；检索增强可引入真实手语先验，但已有检索式方法仅停留在gloss或词级，依赖昂贵标注的词典或孤立手语片段，且无法保留连续句子级动态。

Method: 无需构建gloss/词级词典，检索完整句子级手语模板作为先验；引入SLT（手语翻译）引导的模板语义定位模块，定位检索模板中与目标语义相关的时间区域并抑制无关手语内容；再用分层可学习门控自适应地调节模板特征在各生成器层的贡献。

Result: 在回译（back-translation）与运动精度两类指标上，SemRAG相较基线均提升了语义一致性和运动质量。

Conclusion: 句子级检索增强结合语义定位与分层门控能有效利用真实手语先验，缓解语义错配，提升手语生成质量。

Abstract: Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate sign pose sequences that faithfully express spoken-language text while preserving the rhythm and coordination patterns of signing. Common SLP methods learn text-to-pose mappings from training data but often suffer from semantic inaccuracies in long or complex sentences. Retrieval augmentation offers a way to introduce realistic signing priors to provide more explicit guidance for text-to-pose generation. However, existing retrieval-based SLP methods typically operate at the gloss or word level, relying on gloss-pose dictionaries or isolated sign segments that require costly annotations and fail to preserve continuous sentence-level dynamics. To bridge this gap, we propose SemRAG, a semantic-guided sentence-level retrieval-augmented framework that retrieves full-sentence signing templates as priors without constructing gloss/word-level sign dictionaries. While such templates preserve local motion patterns and global rhythm, they also introduce a semantic mismatch problem: irrelevant motions in the retrieved sentence may interfere with the target semantics. To address this issue, we introduce an SLT-guided template semantic localization module that identifies target-relevant temporal regions within the retrieved template while suppressing irrelevant signing content. To adaptively incorporate the template features into the generator, we further introduce layer-specific learnable gates that regulate their contribution across generator layers. Extensive experiments show that SemRAG improves semantic consistency and motion quality over baselines under both back-translation and motion accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [55] [SCAR: Bridging Coarse Retrieval and Fine Localization through Candidate Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18240)
*Shuhan Dong,Tianyi Shang,Zhenyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCAR（空间一致性感知重排序），将候选格子与其Fine坐标视为定位假设，用Fine条件证据读出与23维表示训练打分为最终坐标选择，实验在24个配置中23个Top-1@5m优于原始方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言点云定位通常先检索候选地图格子再在格子内预测坐标，最终输出仍由检索顺序决定。但检索分数衡量的是区域描述相关性，而非该区域给出的坐标是否准确：高相关区域可能给出偏移预测，低排名区域也可能包含成功定位。

Method: SCAR把每个"格子–Fine坐标"对当作一个定位假设，将方向关系解释为位置的函数，并在主干网络实际Fine点处读取空间支持（Fine条件证据读出）；再以中心支持、语义存在性、检索分数和几何偏移组织成23维表示，用定位成功标签训练共享打分器，推理时对已有假设重排序并返回其原始坐标。

Result: 四个公开主干加修正版Des4Pos复现，覆盖五个域。排除一个协议参考配置后，其余24个配置的探索性TEST点估计中，23个配置Top-1@5m高于Original，24个配置Cell R@1均高于Original。VAL上，修正版Des4Pos*KITTI中Fine读出比中心替代物提升5.77pp；MambaPlace×nuScenesFull上完整评分方案比FSR提升13.98pp（后者衡量完整方案而非仅学习排序）。

Conclusion: 在候选池与Fine预测固定后，基于空间一致的假设级重排序可稳定提升最终坐标选择；但其增益为探索性点估计，且部分提升来自完整评分方案而非单独的学习排序。

Abstract: Natural-language point-cloud localization typically retrieves candidate map cells and then predicts a position inside each candidate, yet the final output is still determined by retrieval order. A retrieval score measures region-description relevance, not whether the coordinates a region yields are accurate: a relevant region may produce a displaced prediction, and a lower-ranked region may contain a successful one. We study the final selection problem after the candidate pool and Fine predictions are fixed---how to choose the coordinate most likely to localize successfully, and under which conditions the gain holds. We propose SCAR (Spatial Consistency-Aware Reranking). SCAR treats each cell--Fine-coordinate pair as a localization hypothesis, interprets directional relations as functions of position, and reads spatial support at the backbone's actual Fine point (Fine-conditioned Evidence Readout). It organizes this readout with center support, semantic presence, retrieval score, and geometric offsets into a 23-D representation, trains a shared scorer with localization-success labels, and at inference reranks existing hypotheses and returns their original coordinates. Experiments cover four public backbones and a corrected Des4Pos reimplementation across five domains. Excluding one protocol-reference configuration, exploratory TEST point estimates on the remaining 24 configurations show Top-1@5\,m above Original on 23 and Cell R@1 above Original on all 24. On development-set VAL, the Fine readout improves Top-1@5\,m by 5.77 percentage points (pp) over a center surrogate on corrected Des4Pos*KITTI, and the complete scoring scheme improves it by 13.98\,pp over FSR on MambaPlace$\times$nuScenesFull; the latter measures the complete scheme, not learned ranking alone.

</details>


### [56] [Lens: Training-Free Semantic Search for Cross-Tile Object and Scene Retrieval in Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18251)
*Ata Akbari Asanjan,Anit Upadhyaya,Jeremy Kravitz,Subash Yeggina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lens 是一个免训练的卫星影像语义目标/场景搜索系统，用冻结的 DINOv3 特征，去除位置偏置并中心化后建立量化余弦相似度索引，实现毫秒级点击式检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超大卫星镶嵌影像上按任意类别搜索目标或场景，通常需要为该类训练检测器；Lens 希望无需针对目标类训练检测器，且能跨传感器、实时交互式检索。

Method: 用冻结自监督 ViT（DINOv3）表示每个瓦片；用常数图像估计位置基，将稠密特征投影到其正交子空间去除位置偏置，再以全区域平均特征中心化；查询由点击点邻域 patch（或整瓦片）的中心化特征平均构成，按余弦相似度匹配；patch 特征做字节量化存入索引支持实时范围搜索，另有粗粒度整瓦片索引用于场景检索；每瓦片运行一次 AnyUp 引导上采样恢复像素级边界并缓存。

Result: 在 DFC2020 的 5128 瓦片测试集上，单次点击八类土地覆盖的像素 F1 为 0.09–0.80（各类最优阈值），平均 F1 0.39；在八类中的六类优于 pairwise INSID3 基线，查询成本约为其 1/400。索引 131 万 patch 单 CPU 线程耗时 169 s，单次点击 6–10 ms 返回渲染叠加图；并在五个 Sentinel-2 区域（农业、工业、沙漠）作为交互工具使用。

Conclusion: Lens 证明免训练、依赖通用自监督特征的点击式检索可在卫星镶嵌图上实现高效语义搜索，兼顾精度、实时性与跨传感器通用性。

Abstract: We developed Lens, a training-free system for semantic object and scene search across large satellite mosaics that requires no detector trained for the class of interest. Lens represents each tile with a frozen, self-supervised vision transformer (DINOv3), removes the positional bias in its dense features by projecting them onto the subspace orthogonal to a positional basis estimated once from a constant image, and centers the debiased features on the mean feature computed across the whole area before comparison. Lens forms a query by averaging the centered features of a small neighborhood of patches around a chosen point, or of an entire tile for scene-level search, and matches it against the index by cosine similarity. It stores the centered patch features in a byte-quantized similarity index that supports real-time range search over a mosaic of thousands of patches, and a coarser whole-tile embedding index for scene-level retrieval. A guided upsampling network (AnyUp) runs once per tile to recover pixel-accurate boundaries beyond the native feature grid, and the result is cached so later queries reuse it. We evaluated Lens on the 5,128-tile DFC2020 test set and ran it as an interactive tool on five Sentinel-2 areas spanning agricultural, industrial, and desert terrain; the pipeline is not tied to a specific sensor. On DFC2020 a single click reaches 0.09 to 0.80 pixel F1 across eight land-cover classes at the per-class best threshold, with a mean F1 of 0.39, and it beats a pairwise INSID3 baseline on six of the eight classes at roughly four hundred times lower query cost. Indexing 1.31 million patches takes 169 s on one CPU thread, and a click returns a rendered overlay in 6 to 10 ms.

</details>


### [57] [Deep Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18256)
*Pak Hong Chui,Hector Andrade-Loarca,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeepBA 是一个图神经网络，将捆集调整（Bundle Adjustment）摊销为可学习网络，从多视图 2D 匹配中恢复相机位姿、内参与场景结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 SfM 逐场景求解非线性最小二乘、更新需解析推导且依赖精细初始化，成本高；本文希望用学习方式替代这一过程以实现冷启动收敛。

Method: 构建 GNN，从几何残差迭代精化估计；以尺度不变监督（真值旋转、平移方向、内参 + 重投影自一致性）训练，可混合任意度量单位数据；与匹配器解耦，仅需 2.04M 参数。

Result: 在场景重建与多数位姿指标上优于以往基于匹配的学习 SfM；在中精度阈值下媲美经典 SfM，并随视图数增加在运行时间上反超；接近 VGGT（约1.2B参数）等单体图像到几何 Transformer，但模块化更强。

Conclusion: 学习式摊销可替代逐场景优化的 BA，兼顾精度、效率与模块化，是轻量且可扩展的 SfM 新范式。

Abstract: We present \textbf{DeepBA} (\textbf{Deep} \textbf{B}undle \textbf{A}djustment), a graph neural network (GNN) that recovers camera poses, intrinsics, and scene structure from 2D matches across an arbitrary number of views. Rather than solving a non-linear least-squares problem per scene, DeepBA amortizes bundle adjustment into a learned graph network that iteratively refines its estimate from geometric residuals. Where classical SfM computes its updates analytically and needs careful initialization, DeepBA learns them and converges from a cold start. It is trained with scale-invariant supervision (ground-truth rotation, translation direction, and intrinsics, plus reprojection self-consistency), so datasets with arbitrary metric units can be mixed without per-scene normalization. DeepBA is decoupled from the matcher. At 2.04M parameters, it outperforms prior learned SfM from matches on scene reconstruction and on most pose metrics, is competitive with classical SfM at moderate accuracy thresholds and overtakes it in wall time as the view count grows, and approaches monolithic image-to-geometry transformers such as VGGT ($\sim$1.2B parameters) with greater modularity. Project~page: https://cphjsn.github.io/deep-ba/

</details>


### [58] [Structural Self-Teaching for Compositional Generation in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18261)
*Jaeyoo Park,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Structural Self-Teaching (SST)框架，利用UMM理解路径的内部注意力图作为结构监督信号，提升生成路径的组合式图像生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型(UMM)的生成路径在密集组合提示下常出现物体缺失、属性绑定失败和空间错误，而其理解路径能正确地将提示短语定位到图像区域，二者能力不对称，因此希望利用这种内部理解能力为生成路径提供结构监督。

Method: 从理解路径的内部注意力图中提取短语条件的支撑掩码(support masks)，通过两种机制利用：(1) 短语局部对比对齐——以掩码作为池化区域，将生成短语区域与目标图像同一区域对齐；(2) 结构脚手架——由掩码构建短语级布局，在训练时调制生成路径的中间空间状态，训练中逐步淡出并在推理时完全移除。无需外部结构标注，测试时无额外输入。

Result: 在多个UMM上的广泛实验表明，SST在同一数据上优于LoRA微调，提升了组合保真度，且推理时无额外开销。

Conclusion: SST以自监督方式将理解路径的短语定位能力迁移为生成路径的结构监督，是一种无外部标注、无推理成本、可跨模型适用的组合式图像生成增强方法。

Abstract: We propose Structural Self-Teaching (SST), a novel framework that enhances compositional image generation in diffusion-based unified multimodal models (UMMs) by leveraging their internal understanding path as a source of structural supervision. The generation path of UMMs often struggles with dense compositional prompts, leading to missing objects, attribute binding failures, and spatial errors. In contrast, their understanding path localizes the same phrases in images that satisfy these prompts. SST converts this phrase-level grounding into training-time structural signals for the generation path. Specifically, we extract phrase-conditioned support masks from internal attention maps derived from the understanding path and employ them through two mechanisms. Phrase-local contrastive alignment uses these masks as pooling regions and aligns each generated phrase region with the same region of the target image. A structural scaffold builds a phrase-level layout from the same masks and modulates an intermediate spatial state of the generation path during training. The scaffold is phased out during training and removed at inference. SST requires no external structural annotations for training and no additional input at test time. Extensive experiments across multiple UMMs demonstrate that SST improves compositional fidelity over LoRA fine-tuning on the same data, with no inference-time cost. Project page is available at https://bellos1203.github.io/sst

</details>


### [59] [Unlocking Cross-Attention in Label-Scarce Regimes: A Weakly Supervised Approach for Video Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18324)
*Andrea Marelli,Alessandro Ardenghi,Giacomo Boracchi,Luca Magri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemCAM提出一种弱监督视频语义分割框架，通过特征空间自监督直接学习时序交叉注意力，无需光流等外部先验即可获得稳定的时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督视频语义分割仅用视频级标签，需借时序一致性提升定位。但全监督模型靠交叉注意力学到的时序权重在弱监督下训练不稳定，甚至不如单帧基线；现有方法转而依赖光流等外部先验，而这些线索非语义、计算昂贵且有噪声，反而损害监督信号。

Method: 提出MemCAM框架：设计CAM传播算法，依据隐空间中patch级特征相似度将历史CAM传播，合成监督信号；并以预测CAM与传播CAM之间的一致性目标来约束，从而在不直接监督注意力权重、不用外部先验的情况下稳定训练时序交叉注意力。

Result: 在YouTube-Objects v2.2和WS²数据集上，MemCAM的时序定位与分割性能均稳定优于现有WS-VSS方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在标签稀缺的视频场景中，交叉注意力可通过内部自监督被有效训练，时序一致性无需外部先验即可习得。

Abstract: Weakly Supervised Video Semantic Segmentation (WS-VSS) aims to learn temporally consistent semantic object masks in videos using only video-level labels. While fully supervised models successfully learn temporal relationships by means of cross-attention, training these temporal weights under weak supervision is unstable and even pushes the model below its single-frame baseline. Therefore, existing WS-VSS methods exploit temporal consistency by relying on external priors, such as optical flow. Unfortunately these cues are non-semantic, computationally expensive and noisy, degrading the supervisory signal. Instead of supervising attention weights directly or using external priors, we propose MemCAM, a WS-VSS transformer framework that learns temporal cross-attention directly through feature-space self-supervision. MemCAM introduces a CAM Propagation Algorithm that synthesizes a supervisory signal by propagating past CAMs according to patch-level feature similarities in the latent space. Specifically, we constrain their induced CAMs through a consistency objective between predicted and propagated maps, stabilizing temporal attention learning. Experiments on the YouTube-Objects v2.2 and WS$^2$ datasets demonstrate that MemCAM consistently outperforms the temporal localization and segmentation performance of WS-VSS alternatives, showing that cross-attention can be effectively trained in label-scarce video settings through internal self-supervision.

</details>


### [60] [DIRT: Depth-completed Image-based Rendering using Two-band Blending for Live Novel-view Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18340)
*Mathias Harrer,Laura Fink,Noah Lewis,Paul Himmler,Tim Weyrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练和优化的实时新视角合成方法，基于深度传感器稀疏点云在目标视域屏幕空间补全深度，再投影多视角图像并用频域双频段融合，适配RGB-D、LiDAR、热成像等多种传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA新视角合成需逐场景优化，无法用于实时场景；而实时/即席NVS要求低延迟且强泛化，已有方法通过改造训练/优化方法牺牲质量换低延迟，本文希望摆脱训练数据与场景优化依赖。

Method: 遵循传统基于图像的渲染思路：利用主动传感器获得显式稀疏点云几何，构建稀疏填充的深度缓冲，并在目标（新）视角的屏幕空间补全；以此代理将一组带位姿图像投影到目标帧，再通过频率感知的双频段融合策略合成结果。整条流水线完全非神经、无需优化。

Result: 在手持RGB-D相机、车载LiDAR、热成像等异构传感器配置及低光照等挑战场景下验证有效；实验显示在实时重建场景中优于既有方法，在常规NVS基准上保持有竞争力表现。

Conclusion: 非神经、免优化的显式几何+屏幕空间补全+双频段融合方案可显著降低采集到渲染的延迟，并摆脱对网络泛化的依赖，适合实时与多传感器NVS应用。

Abstract: State-of-the-art novel-view synthesis (NVS) methods commonly require per-scene optimization before rendering, limiting their applicability in live settings. Live or ad-hoc NVS, however, demands low-latency rendering during capture and strong generalization to new scenes. Some previous work tried to address this task by adapting training- and optimization-based methods, trading quality for low latency. We introduce a live NVS approach following traditional image-based rendering that operates on a posed set of depth sensor observations and images. Our method leverages explicit, sparse point-based geometry obtained from active sensors, resulting in a sparsely filled depth buffer that is completed in the (novel) target view's screen-space. Using this proxy, a posed set of images is projected into the target frame before fusing the resulting observations through a frequency-aware two-band blending strategy. The pipeline is entirely non-neural and optimization-free, requiring no training data or scene-specific optimization. As a result, our approach reduces the delay between capture and novel-view rendering and does not rely on favorable network generalization. We validate the method's applicability to heterogeneous sensor setups - including handheld RGB-D cameras, automotive LiDAR, and thermal cameras - as well as challenging scenarios, such as low light captures. Our experiments demonstrate that the approach outperforms prior methods in live reconstruction scenarios, while maintaining competitive performance in conventional NVS benchmarks.

</details>


### [61] [MaskSCD: Bitemporal Mask Classification for Semantic Change Detection in Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18350)
*Weikang Yu,Pedram Ghamisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskSCD将语义变化检测(SCD)重新表述为双时相标签对图像的掩膜分类，每个"双时相分割"输出一个两期共享掩膜及每期一个类别，变化图由类别对导出，保证变化图与语义图一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度SCD网络用独立的逐像素解码器分别预测变化图和两期地物图，输出可能互相矛盾——某像素被标为变化却在两期同类，且逐像素决策导致地物破碎。

Method: 将SCD建模为双时相标签对图像的掩膜分类：以双时相掩膜注意力Transformer解码器分别在两幅影像中读取同一分割并关联两次读取，配合提供各期与变化感知特征的双时相融合金字塔；标签空间随类别数线性增长。

Result: 在SECOND、Landsat-SCD和Hi-UCD三个数据集（0.1–30 m分辨率、4–9类）上，与统一协议重训的14个近期SCD模型对比，MaskSCD在分离kappa、语义变化F1和转移F1上均最优，平均分离kappa领先最强对手约5个点，且不产生矛盾变化像素（部分多任务网络中该类像素占预测变化的近30%）。

Conclusion: 掩膜分类范式能从根本上保证变化与语义图的一致性并减少碎片化，在跨分辨率多数据集上取得一致领先，代码已开源。

Abstract: Semantic change detection (SCD) identifies where the land surface has changed and which land-cover transition has taken place. Most deep SCD networks predict the change map and the land-cover map of each date with separate per-pixel decoders. These outputs need not agree, so a pixel can be labeled as changed although it has the same class at both dates, and per-pixel decisions fragment objects. We present MaskSCD, which formulates SCD as the mask classification of a bitemporal label-pair image. Each bitemporal segment, a region of constant land-cover class pair, is predicted as one mask shared by both dates together with one class per date, and the change map is derived from the predicted class pair. This formulation keeps the change and semantic maps consistent and the label space linear in the number of classes. A bitemporal masked-attention transformer decoder implements it by reading each segment in both images and relating the two readings, and a bitemporal fusion pyramid provides per-date and change-aware features. MaskSCD is evaluated on SECOND, Landsat-SCD and Hi-UCD, which span 0.1 to 30 m resolution and four to nine land-cover classes, against fourteen recent SCD models re-trained under one protocol. MaskSCD achieves the best separated kappa, semantic change $F_1$ and transition $F_1$ on all three datasets and exceeds the strongest competitor by about five points in mean separated kappa. It produces no contradictory change pixels, which account for nearly 30% of the predicted change of some multi-task networks. The code is available at https://github.com/EricYu97/MaskSCD.

</details>


### [62] [Adaptable Watermarking: Transforming Image Watermarking Models via Lightweight Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18363)
*Mehrdad Saberi,Vishal Asnani,Shruti Agarwal,Soheil Feizi,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种轻量适配框架，可将现有神经图像水印模型转变为模块化、可升级架构，用1-2M参数实现跨模型水印风格转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经水印模型彼此不兼容，欲获得新水印风格通常需重新训练整个编解码器，成本高且阻碍互操作与升级。

Method: 为编码器和解码器各附加紧凑的参数高效适配器，在多域残差对齐损失（空间、梯度、频率）下微调，并加入秘密损失项保证比特准确率；适配器仅约1-2M可训练参数，无需重训全模型。

Result: 在TrustMark、InvisMark、RoSteALS、HiDDeN、WAM上评测，适配后的编码器能紧密复现目标残差，解码器在标准增强与失真下以近最优准确率恢复消息；多解码器推理中仅用2M参数的解码器适配器即可实现跨模型适配。

Conclusion: 跨模型适配以极少参数即可实现，支持可互操作、可升级的水印系统，为水印模块化与复用提供可行路径。

Abstract: We explore the adaptability of neural image watermarking models; the extent to which one watermarking method can be transformed into another through lightweight adaptation. We introduce a framework that enables encoder-decoder watermarking architectures to become modular and upgradable, supporting multiple watermarking styles without re-training full models. Adaptation is performed by attaching a compact parameter-efficient adapter and fine-tuning under a multi-domain residual alignment loss (spatial, gradient, and frequency) while preserving bit accuracy via a secret-loss term. We evaluate this approach across a suite of recent watermarking models, including TrustMark, InvisMark, RoSteALS, HiDDeN, and WAM, demonstrating that adapted encoders closely reproduce target residuals and that adapted decoders recover messages with near-optimal accuracy under standard augmentations and distortions. We show that cross-model adaptation, with applications in interoperable and upgradable watermarking, can be achieved with as few as 1-2M trainable parameters per component (e.g., the 2M decoder adapter used in our multi-decoder inference pipeline).

</details>


### [63] [AI-Based Multi-View Echocardiographic Image Synthesis using Dynamic Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18375)
*João Freitas,Anca Balinisteanu,Ahmed Youssef,Vitor Hugo Pereira,Jan D'hooge,Sandro Queirós*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于形状控制的多视角超声心动图序列合成框架，由动态全心网格截面提取 + 两阶段AI管线（GAN-扩散图像翻译 + 掩码视频重建）组成，可生成多视角一致的带标注超声数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动超声分析方法受限于大规模高质量标注数据难以获取（伦理与后勤限制）。现有合成数据方法多依赖左心室形状监督或仅针对特定切面，无法生成其他标准及非常规成像平面，也难以保证多心腔、多视角间的一致性。

Method: 1）从动态全心网格中提取多视角截面；2）两阶段AI管线赋予超声样外观：Stage 1 用非配对GAN-扩散模型将截面逐帧翻译为真实感超声帧；Stage 2 用掩码视频重建提升帧间时序连贯性与心脏运动一致性。

Result: 两阶段均实现较高的帧级真实感；引入Stage 2后，视频级运动质量与时序一致性显著提升；可从多样心脏模型生成真实感多视角超声序列。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据增强和多视角心脏评估方法开发提供了可扩展的带标注数据来源，突破了形状监督与固定视角的局限。

Abstract: Although automated methods have improved echocardiographic workflows, progress remains constrained by the need for large, well-annotated datasets. As the development of clinical databases is often limited by ethical and logistical challenges, synthetic data generation has emerged as a promising alternative. However, existing approaches commonly rely on left ventricle shape-based supervision or are tailored to specific cardiac views, limiting the generation of additional standard and unconventional imaging planes and the preservation of multi-view consistency across cardiac chambers. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework for shape-controlled synthesis of multi-view echocardiographic sequences. First, multi-view cross-sections are extracted from dynamic whole-heart meshes. Ultrasound-like appearance is then introduced through a two-stage AI-based pipeline. In Stage 1, an unpaired GAN-diffusion model translates these cross-sections into realistic echocardiographic frames. As this translation is performed independently at the frame level, Stage 2 employs masked video reconstruction to improve temporal coherence and cardiac motion. Evaluation demonstrated high frame-level realism for both stages, while the integration of Stage 2 substantially improved video-level motion quality and temporal consistency. Overall, the proposed framework enables the generation of realistic multi-view echocardiographic sequences from diverse cardiac models, providing a scalable source of annotated data for dataset augmentation and the development of multi-view cardiac assessment methods.

</details>


### [64] [CoPre-MoE: Consolidating and Preserving MoE for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18417)
*Tao Hu,Ling-Xiang Zhang,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoPre-MoE 通过共享-私有专家架构与检索键路由器，在多模态持续指令微调中抑制灾难性遗忘，同时显著减少参数冗余与线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 MoE 持续指令微调方法为每个任务分配独立专家以避免覆盖共享参数，但导致严重的跨任务冗余和线性参数增长（不同任务的专家常学到重叠功能，如 ScienceQA 与 TextVQA 都需定位图像区域）。需在不为引发遗忘的前提下抑制参数冗余。

Method: 先为每个新任务学习任务专属 MoE；构建共享-私有专家架构：先评估功能重叠，将重叠专家选择性整合进固定容量的通用专家池，再把剩余任务专属专家分解为共享投影基与紧凑任务核心，放入私有专家池；路由器为每个专家配可学习检索键，历史键冻结、新键施加正交性惩罚；推理时基于输入动态路由组合两池，无需测试时任务标签。

Result: 大量实验表明 CoPre-MoE 取得更优性能，同时大幅降低参数开销。

Conclusion: 通过功能重叠整合与低秩分解实现专家共享，配合冻结历史键与正交正则的路由设计，可在无任务标签的推理下兼顾抗遗忘与参数高效。

Abstract: Multimodal continual instruction tuning requires multimodal large language models to sequentially acquire new capabilities without catastrophic forgetting. Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures offer a promising way to mitigate task interference through expert specialization and combination. However, while existing MoE-based methods avoid overwriting shared parameters by allocating separate experts for each task, this progressive expansion incurs severe cross-task redundancy and linear parameter growth. Specifically, experts across tasks often learn overlapping functions alongside task-specific capabilities. For instance, ScienceQA and TextVQA both locate relevant image regions. To curb parameter redundancy without inducing forgetting, we propose Consolidating and Preserving MoE (CoPre-MoE). For each incoming task, CoPre-MoE first learns task-specific MoE to capture specialized capabilities. To eliminate redundancy and reduce parameter growth across tasks, we design a shared-and-private expert architecture. Specifically, we first evaluate functional overlap to selectively consolidate overlapping experts across tasks into a fixed-capacity generic expert pool. We then factorize the remaining task-specific experts into shared projection bases and compact task-specific cores within a private expert pool. To mitigate forgetting, we construct the router by pairing each expert with learnable retrieval keys, where historical keys remain frozen during continual updates while newly added keys are regularized with an orthogonality penalty. At inference, input-dependent routing dynamically combines both pools without test-time task labels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoPre-MoE achieves superior performance while substantially reducing parameter overhead.

</details>


### [65] [Do Underwater Image Quality Metrics Agree with Human Perception? A Benchmark with Human Choices, Simple Edits and Generative Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18429)
*Rishii Kumar Singh,Abhinav Rajput,Dinesh Kumar Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估73种无参考图像质量评价（NR-IQA）指标在水下图像增强任务中是否与人类偏好一致，发现没有指标能稳定对齐人类判断，并发布TrustUIQA基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像增强缺乏干净参考图，研究者依赖NR-IQA指标，但这些指标是否与人类判断一致尚不清楚。

Method: 以五个参考（四个人类主观评分集+一项新的成对比较研究）为基准，评估73种指标（手工设计水下、学习型水下、视觉语言、通用型）。测量指标在比较同一场景不同版本时与人类选择一致的比例；并测试简单编辑和对抗扰动能否把指标分数抬到人类偏好增强之上，以及重复生成编辑加噪时分数是否下降。

Result: 没有任何指标能稳定匹配人类偏好；五参考上最佳最差情况Cohen's kappa仅0.23（Q-Align），而人类评分者间中位kappa为0.41；常用UCIQE常偏向人类拒绝的编辑；在应保持不变的重建链上，73个指标中仅22个在每个检查点分数都下降，而噪声增长2.5倍。

Conclusion: 现有NR-IQA指标与人类判断存在显著差距，不能可靠用于水下图像增强评估；论文提出TrustUIQA基准，整合人类参考、研究选择、编辑/攻击/生成图像及全部指标分数，并开源代码与数据。

Abstract: Underwater image enhancement is challenging to evaluate due to the lack of clean reference images, so researchers rely on no-reference image quality assessment (NR-IQA) metrics. However, it remains unclear whether these metrics agree with human judgments. We evaluate 73 handcrafted underwater, learned underwater, vision-language and general-purpose metrics against human preferences on five references: four published opinion-score sets and a new paired-comparison study. For each metric, we measure how often it selects the same image as people when comparing versions of the same scene. We further test whether simple image edits and small adversarial perturbations raise metric scores above the enhancement people preferred, and whether scores decrease as repeated generative edits add noise. Our results show that no metric consistently matches human preferences. The best worst-case Cohen's kappa over the five references is only 0.23 (Q-Align), while two raters in our study agree with a median kappa of 0.41. The widely used UCIQE often favors edits that people reject. On a Nano Banana Pro replica chain that should leave the image unchanged, only 22 of 73 scores decrease at every checkpoint, while noise grows by 2.5 times. We also introduce TrustUIQA, a benchmark that combines the human references, our study choices, the edited, attacked and generated images, and the scores of every metric on every image. The code is available at https://github.com/TrustUIQA/TrustUIQA and TrustUIQA at https://huggingface.co/datasets/TrustUIQA/TrustUIQA.

</details>


### [66] [Toward On-Device Markerless 6DoF Tracking of a Physical Manipulative on a Mixed-Reality Headset: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18438)
*Guangxi Feng,Philip Yasskin,Wei Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AR-Classroom 在 Meta Quest 3 上用无标记 6DoF 追踪 LEGO 飞机，通过 YOLO 关键点检测加 PnP 解算实现虚拟叠加对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR-Classroom 使用实体模型与虚拟叠加帮助学习者探索空间变换，但独立头显上需要将叠加层准确对齐到实物，因此需要为 LEGO 飞机构建无标记六自由度追踪。

Method: 用 Unity 合成渲染训练轻量 YOLO 模型，从动态 RGB 裁剪图（128×128）预测 20 个关键点；再由 PnP 求解器结合对应 3D 地标估计飞机位姿。

Result: 在 1000 张合成验证图像上配对评估：真值框引导裁剪使标注图像检出率从 32.5% 提升到 46.2%，关键点平均 F1 从 42.9% 提升到 44.6%（但裁剪视图下关键点精度下降）。头显初步测试中，2 Hz 是应用维持约 72 FPS 的最高测试追踪目标；演示显示手动旋转前后叠加层均能对齐。

Conclusion: 方案在合成数据和初步头显测试中可行，但真实头显视频上的 6DoF 位姿精度尚待未来量化评估。

Abstract: AR-Classroom uses physical models and virtual overlays to help learners explore spatial transformations. To align these overlays on a standalone headset, we developed a markerless six-degree-of-freedom(6DoF) tracker for a LEGO airplane on the Meta Quest 3. A compact YOLO model trained on synthetic Unity renders predicts 20 keypoints from dynamic RGB crops resized to 128 by 128 pixels. A perspective-n-point solver estimates the airplane's pose from these predictions and the corresponding 3D landmarks. In a paired evaluation on 1,000 synthetic validation images, cropping guided by ground-truth boxes increased the detection rate on annotated images from 32.5 to 46.2 percent. Mean keypoint F1 increased from 42.9 to 44.6 percent, with lower keypoint precision in the cropped view. In preliminary headset tests, 2 Hz was the highest tested tracking target at which the application maintained approximately 72 frames per second. A headset demonstration illustrates virtual overlay alignment with the physical airplane before and after a manual rotation. Quantitative 6DoF pose accuracy on real headset footage will be measured in future work.

</details>


### [67] [MMFlash: Faster Speculative Decoding for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18482)
*Sicheng Feng,Zigeng Chen,Song Wang,Gongfan Fang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMFlash 提出面向多模态推测解码的扩散草稿器框架，通过分析视觉依赖度导致的接受率失衡问题，自适应加权难预测的视觉相关 token 并引入稀疏注意力，实现最高 3.58× 无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式草稿器（如 DFlash）主要面向纯文本任务，多模态草稿研究不足；作者发现 token 的视觉依赖度越高，草稿接受率越低（低四分位 46% vs 高四分位 22%），且多模态训练后差距仍存在。

Method: 1) 提出基于有无视觉输入时下一 token 分布 KL 散度的 token 级视觉依赖度指标用于分析；2) 提出 MMFlash 框架，自适应上调草稿器预测较差的高视觉依赖 token 权重；3) 针对长视频输入采用稀疏注意力，减少冗余注意力计算并提升草稿质量。

Result: 在多模态基准上实现最高 3.58× 的无损解码加速，相比 EAGLE-3 和 DFlash 分别最高提升 195.4% 和 17.1%。

Conclusion: 视觉依赖是限制多模态推测解码接受率的关键因素，针对性地加权难预测视觉 token 并优化长视频注意力可显著提升草稿质量与整体解码效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates generation by letting a lightweight drafter propose tokens for parallel verification by a target model. Diffusion drafters such as DFlash propose an entire token block in a single forward pass, but mainly focus on text-only tasks, leaving multimodal drafting underexplored. To study this setting, we first introduce a metric of token-level visual dependence based on the KL divergence between next-token distributions with and without visual input. We find that acceptance rates decrease from 46% in the lowest visual-dependence quartile to 22% in the highest, with this disparity persisting after multimodal training. Motivated by this observation, we propose MMFlash, a simple and effective framework that adaptively upweights visually dependent tokens that the drafter predicts poorly. For long video inputs, MMFlash further employs sparse attention to reduce redundant attention computation and improve drafting quality. Across various multimodal benchmarks, MMFlash achieves lossless decoding speedups of up to $3.58\times$, outperforming EAGLE-3 and DFlash by up to 195.4% and 17.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [68] [Box-conditional Hypothesis Testing for Out-of-Distribution Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18519)
*Donghoon Lee,Jusung Park,Shinjin Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练OOD检测评分PLUM：将检测框视为假设检验，依据框尺寸与边界接触条件在同条件下ID框中排序特征距离，在PASCAL-VOC和BDD100K上取得对比方法最高AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测器在真实部署中需可靠识别分布外(OOD)目标。现有做法常把所有ID框混在一个null分布下比较，而null分布实际随框尺寸及是否接触图像边界而变，导致误报率难以控制、真实差异被掩盖；同时仅面向ID类训练的后续层缺乏保留未知目标信息的理由。

Method: 把每个检测框的判定建模为假设检验，null假设为该框覆盖ID类目标；按框尺寸与边界接触情况分组，在同组ID框内对分数排序，从而对每个框给出无分布假设的误报率界。评分读取最后一个backbone阶段（先于仅服务ID类的层）的特征，在框内池化后计算其到ID类的距离，作为免训练分数PLUM。

Result: 在标准基准上，无外部模型条件下PLUM取得对比方法中最高AUROC：PASCAL-VOC为91.5和94.5，BDD100K为95.1和97.5。

Conclusion: 条件化(尺寸/边界)排序配合早期backbone特征是一种简单有效的免训练OOD检测方案，可提供无分布误报率界并在多个基准上领先。

Abstract: Detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) objects is essential for ensuring the reliability and safety of object detectors in real-world deployment. We treat the decision on a detected box as a hypothesis test with the null hypothesis that the box covers an object of an in-distribution (ID) class. The null distribution changes with the size of the box and its contact with the image border, and the detector observes both. The test can therefore rank a box only against ID boxes of the same size and border contact, which gives a distribution-free bound on the false-alarm rate for each of them and uncovers the separation that one null distribution hides. The power of the test also rests on the layer it reads. Layers built only for the ID classes have no reason to keep what sets an unknown object apart, so the test reads the features before them. We propose \textbf{PLUM}, a training-free score that pools the last backbone stage inside the box and ranks its distance to the ID classes among ID boxes of the same size and border contact. On the standard benchmark, PLUM reaches the highest AUROC among the compared methods without external models, with 91.5 and 94.5 on PASCAL-VOC and 95.1 and 97.5 on BDD100K. Code: https://github.com/DDOZMI/PLUM

</details>


### [69] [Understanding Contextual Bias in Agricultural CNNs Through Hidden-Neuron Concept Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18530)
*Abhilekha Dalal,Avishek Das,Samatha Ereshi Akkamahadevi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文用Concept Induction将隐藏神经元激活与可解释的图像描述符（边缘、Gabor、Tamura纹理）关联，在五个农业数据集、四种CNN架构上验证，发现27.4%的神经元-特征对通过验证，且中间层验证率最高，上下文偏置强度与神经元可解释性并不正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN会利用背景主导的图像块进行分类，说明其利用了上下文信息，但以往研究多以分类准确率检测该行为，对隐藏层究竟表征什么缺乏解释。作者希望探究Concept Induction能否揭示与上下文图像块上隐藏神经元激活相关的可解释视觉属性。

Method: 选取五个具有高、中、低上下文偏置的农业数据集，分析四种CNN架构在三个网络深度和五个图像块位置上的表现。将每个图像块的定量图像描述符编码进知识图谱，作为Concept Induction的背景知识，从而推导刻画神经元激活的逻辑表达式；再用Mann-Whitney U检验和Cliff's delta在留出图像上验证这些表达式。

Result: 在48,013个被检验的神经元-特征对中，有13,164个（27.4%）得到验证。中间层验证最频繁（中层平均40.2%，深层12.0%），且没有某个图像块位置在所有数据集上占主导。边缘、Gabor和Tamura纹理特征的验证率最高。上下文偏置更强并不意味着验证率更高：中等偏置的Sugarcane最高（40.5%），高偏置的Cassava最低（19.2%）。

Conclusion: 行为层面的上下文依赖性与隐藏神经元的语义可解释性反映CNN行为的不同方面；Concept Induction可补充基于图像块的偏置检测方法。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) can classify images using background-dominated patches that contain little or none of the target object, showing that they exploit contextual information. Previous studies detected this behavior through classification accuracy but say little about what CNN hidden layers represent. We investigate whether Concept Induction can reveal interpretable visual properties associated with hidden-neuron activations on contextual image patches. Using five agricultural datasets with high, moderate, and low contextual bias, we analyze four CNN architectures at three network depths and five patch locations. Quantitative image descriptors of each patch are encoded in a knowledge graph and used as background knowledge for Concept Induction, which derives logical expressions characterizing neuron activation. These expressions are tested on held-out images using the Mann-Whitney U test and Cliff's delta. Of 48,013 tested neuron-feature pairs, 13,164 (27.4%) were validated. Validation was most frequent at intermediate depths (mean 40.2% at mid layers versus 12.0% at deep layers), and no patch location dominated across datasets. Among the tested descriptors, edge, Gabor, and Tamura texture features had the highest validation rates. Stronger contextual bias did not correspond to higher neuron-feature validation rates: moderately biased Sugarcane had the highest rate (40.5%) and highly biased Cassava the lowest (19.2%). These results suggest that behavioral context dependence and the semantic interpretability of hidden neurons capture different aspects of CNN behavior, and that Concept Induction can complement patch-based bias detection.

</details>


### [70] [JVT: Just Video Transformer makes pixel-space video diffusion simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18549)
*Jianjun Zhou,Hua Geng,Haoyu Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JVT：一种简洁的大patch DiT式Transformer，直接在像素空间做视频扩散，无需预训练自编码器、辅助细化器、级联结构或U-Net式跳跃连接，生成质量可与潜空间扩散基线竞争，并天然支持像素对齐多模态联合生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维原始视频数据直接扩散建模困难，现有像素空间方法往往依赖专门架构（自编码器、级联、细化器、U-Net 跳跃连接等），结构复杂。作者希望验证一个简洁的 DiT 式大patch Transformer 是否足以胜任像素空间视频扩散。

Method: 提出 JVT（Just Video Transformer）：纯 DiT 风格、采用大patch的视频扩散模型，直接在像素空间训练，不含预训练自编码器、辅助细化器、级联阶段和 U-Net 跳跃连接。系统研究视频 patch 嵌入、register token 与自适应层归一化（AdaLN）等设计，权衡生成质量与计算效率。利用像素空间公式天然支持像素对齐模态的联合生成，展示了相机条件 RGB-视差联合生成。

Result: 在类别条件与无条件设置下，生成质量与潜空间扩散基线相当；相机条件 RGB-视差生成相比仅 RGB 基线提升了相机跟随效果，并在推理时无需额外重建模型即可获得显式场景几何。

Conclusion: 结果表明简单 DiT 式 Transformer 在像素空间视频扩散上具有潜力，所总结的设计经验可为未来研究提供实用指导。

Abstract: Direct diffusion modeling of high-dimensional raw video data is challenging, and existing pixel-space methods often rely on specialized architectures. We introduce JVT (Just Video Transformer), a simple large-patch DiT-style Transformer for pixel-space video diffusion, without a pretrained autoencoder, auxiliary detailers, cascaded stages, or U-Net-style skip connections. We systematically examine video patch embeddings, register tokens, and adaptive layer normalization to identify design choices that balance generation quality and computational efficiency. The resulting JVT design achieves generation quality competitive with latent diffusion baselines in both class-conditioned and unconditional settings. The pixel-space formulation of JVT also naturally supports joint generation of pixel-aligned modalities. We demonstrate this capability through camera-conditioned RGB-disparity generation, which improves camera following relative to RGB-only baselines and yields explicit scene geometry without an additional reconstruction model at inference. These results highlight the potential of simple DiT-style Transformers for pixel-space video diffusion, and we hope our findings offer useful insights and practical guidance for future research.

</details>


### [71] [Restoring Diversity in Distilled Image and Video Generators by Probing Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18550)
*Furkan Horoz,Ozgur Kara,Kaan Oktay,Adil Kaan Akan,Atakan Kara,Anand Bhattad,James Matthew Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 蒸馏扩散模型产生少步学生模型会严重损失多样性，根源在于学生模型将激活方差集中到少数方向上（方向性坍缩），作者提出ReDiverse从训练和推理两方面恢复缺失的方差方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型蒸馏成少步学生模型虽大幅降低采样延迟，但导致严重的多样性坍缩：不同随机种子生成的样本几乎无差异。论文希望从内部表征层面找出该失败的根因。

Method: 探测学生模型相对教师的内部表征：发现学生总激活方差与教师相当，但集中在远少的方向上；该方向性坍缩定位于初始去噪步的可识别中间块。据此提出ReDiverse：ReDiverse-FT在训练时微调轻量适配器，将学生激活回归到教师激活；ReDiverse-TF在推理时以无训练方式，沿预计算的教师主方向引导激活。

Result: 在图像与视频蒸馏基准上，ReDiverse在保持生成质量的同时一致地恢复样本多样性，并得到用户研究支持。

Conclusion: 多样性坍缩源于激活方差的错误分配而非生成能力的内在丧失；通过恢复教师特征方差方向可有效缓解该问题，训练时与推理时两种方案均可行。

Abstract: Distilling diffusion models into few-step student models substantially reduces sampling latency, but induces severe diversity collapse: samples generated from different random seeds show minimal variation. In this work, we investigate the root cause of this failure by probing the internal representations of student models relative to their teachers. We find that distilled students maintain total activation variance comparable to their teachers, but concentrate it into substantially fewer directions. This directional collapse is localized to an identifiable intermediate block at the initial denoising step.
