This suggests that lower transport failure rates do not necessarily imply greater reliability. Restoring the corresponding execution provenance recovers 85.5% of transport failures, while recovery is lower when provenance is lossy or unavailable. These results show that native-view accuracy alone is insufficient to characterize verifier reliability when execution evidence crosses abstraction boundaries.

</details>


### [108] [Interactive Program Induction with Language Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18485)
*Robin Shing Moon Chan,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯视角研究交互式程序归纳，提出贪心查询选择算法，用语言模型作为先验，在CodeARC基准上以更少示例达到更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限输入-输出示例恢复程序本质上是歧义的，因为有限示例与无穷多个在未见输入上发散的程​​序一致。需要主动获取信息量最大的示例来消歧。

Method: 将问题建模为贝叶斯后验收敛：给定程序先验p⁰，通过观察额外示例集中后验概率质量。推导贪心查询选择算法，每次选择信息量最大的输入向oracle（用户）查询输出；在PAC式的程序等价松弛下，用语言模型作为先验，并用重要性采样估计查询选择目标。

Result: 证明算法在O((1/γ)log(1/p⁰(f*)))次信息查询后终止，当没有其他类别在先验下占优时，返回近似等价的目​​标程序f*。在CodeARC基准上，相比纯LM智能体准确率提升达22个百分点，同时观察的输入-输出示例少达10.5倍。

Conclusion: 主动查询选择结合LM先验可显著提升程序归纳的样本效率与准确率，并具有理论终止保证。

Abstract: Program induction refers to the process in which a learner aims to recover a program from a few given input-output examples. This paradigm broadens programming access by letting users demonstrate the rule-based behavior they want to apply to unseen data, instead of having to implement it themselves. However, recovering a program from finite input-output examples is fundamentally ambiguous, as a finite set of input-output examples is theoretically consistent with infinitely many programs whose output diverges on unseen inputs. We view this problem from a Bayesian perspective, where, given a prior $p^0$ over programs, identifying the target program means concentrating posterior probability mass on it by observing additional input-output examples. In the interactive program induction setting, the learner chooses maximally informative inputs and queries an oracle, such as the user, for their outputs. We derive a greedy query-selection algorithm and show that it terminates after $\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}γ\log 1/p^0(f^\star))$ informative oracle queries, and returns the target program $f^\star$ up to approximate equivalence when no other class outweighs it under the prior. The algorithm uses a language model as the prior under a PAC-style relaxation of program equivalence, where the query-selection objective is estimated using importance sampling. On the CodeARC benchmark, the algorithm improves accuracy over the LM only agent by up to 22 percentage points while observing up to 10.5 fewer input-output examples.

</details>


### [109] [Agentic induction of common data elements and information extraction from sarcoma case report abstracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18506)
*Daniel Reichenpfader,Kwaku Appiagyei,Manovah Nadarajah,Ahmed Reda Bahr,Irina Filchenko,Fabio Dennstädt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究用LLM智能体从2万余篇肉瘤病例报告摘要中自动推导CDE（通用数据元素）schema并大规模抽取，最终保留53个CDE，抽取成功率79.2%，与人工抽查一致率94.9%，但需专家审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDE有助于临床数据的可比性，但通常依赖人工定义，成本高、难以规模化；作者希望检验LLM智能体能否自动从文献中推导CDE schema并在大规模语料上应用。

Method: 智能体先从24,588篇PubMed摘要中提出72,909个候选CDE，经智能体化合并得到1,570个CDE；保留被≥50篇摘要支持的53个CDE，并对全语料进行证据关联抽取。评估包括处理成功率、两名人工评审者对相关性/清晰性/正确性的审核，以及100篇抽样摘要（1,116个非空值对）中LLM抽取值与人工的完全一致率（含bootstrap置信区间）。

Result: 79.2%的摘要对所有保留CDE处理成功（含"未提及"回答）；人工评审通过率：相关性83.0%、清晰性67.9%、正确性56.6%；抽样中LLM与人工完全一致率94.9%（95% CI 93.0–96.4%）；错误集中在解剖学和时间字段。

Conclusion: LLM智能体可有效辅助CDE schema草案生成和大规模证据关联抽取，但schema质量（尤其正确性）仍需专家审核；作者建议采用"智能体辅助起草+专家策展+大规模抽取"的流程。

Abstract: Common data elements (CDEs) support clinical data comparability but are usually defined manually. We investigated whether large language model agents could derive a CDE schema from sarcoma case report abstracts and apply it at scale. An agent proposed 72,909 candidate CDEs from 24,588 PubMed abstracts; agentic consolidation yielded 1,570 CDEs. We retained 53 supported by at least 50 abstracts and attempted evidence-linked extraction across the corpus. Processing succeeded for all retained CDEs in 79.2% of abstracts, including responses indicating non-mention. Two human reviewers approved 83.0% of retained CDEs for relevance, 67.9% for clarity, and 56.6% for correctness. In 100 sampled abstracts, exact agreement with the value extracted by the LLM was 94.9% among 1,116 pairs with a non-empty answer (abstract-level bootstrap 95% CI, 93.0-96.4%). Errors concentrated in anatomical and temporal fields. These findings support agent-assisted schema drafting followed by expert curation before large-scale extraction.

</details>


### [110] [HARDTESTGEN: A High-Quality RL Verifier Generation Pipeline for LLM Algorithmic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24098)
*Zhongmou He,Yee Man Choi,Kexun Zhang,Jiabao Ji,Junting Zhou,Dejia Xu,Ivan Bercovich,Aidan Zhang,Yixiao Zeng,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 HARDTESTGEN 方法自动合成高质量测试用例，构建含 26.6k 题的 HARDTESTS 数据集，用其作为验证器可显著提升 LLM 代码正确性判定精度并改进下游训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠验证器对大模型强化学习至关重要，但代码生成场景下难以构建；伪装良好的错误程序往往只能靠人工精心设计的边界用例才能识别，而这类用例难以自动合成。

Method: 提出 HARDTESTGEN，针对算法编程题合成高质量测试用例；并整理出包含 26.6k 道算法题及高质量合成测试的 HARDTESTS 数据集。

Result: 相比现有测试，HARDTESTGEN 生成的测试在验证 LLM 生成代码上精度显著更高（精度提升 11.22 个百分点）；使用 HARDTESTS 验证器结果进行拒绝采样与强化学习后训练，可提升 LLM 代码生成性能。

Conclusion: 高质量合成测试可作为可靠的代码验证器，提升验证精度与下游后训练效果；数据集与合成流程已开源。

Abstract: Verifiers provide important reward signals for reinforcement learning of large language models (LLMs). However, it is challenging to develop or create reliable verifiers, especially for code generation tasks. A well-disguised wrong solution program may only be detected by carefully human-written edge cases that are difficult to synthesize automatically. To address this issue, we propose HARDTESTGEN, an approach to synthesize high-quality test cases for algorithmic coding problems. We curate a comprehensive algorithmic programming dataset HARDTESTS with 26.6k problems and high-quality synthetic tests. Compared with existing tests, HARDTESTGEN tests demonstrate significantly higher accuracy in verifying LLM-generated code (+11.22 percentage points in precision, the percentage of actually correct code within the predicted correct ones). We also show that downstream post-training -- including rejection sampling and reinforcement learning (RL) -- using HARDTESTS verifier results in improved performance of LLM code generation. We open-source our dataset and synthesis pipeline at https://leililab.github.io/HardTests/.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [ProdPilot: A Layered Model Context Protocol System for Automated Production Readiness and Deployment of Vibe-Coded Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17381)
*Muhammad Sudais Khalid,Muhammad Junaid Asif,Rehan Naveed Abbasi,Rana Fayyaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProdPilot 是一个本地 MCP 服务器，用 50 条生产就绪规则审计 Node.js/React 项目，以"模板/提取/少量模型生成"的分级修复循环完成修复、门禁与部署，自身不调用任何模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助开发生成的应用常在首次部署时因运维原因失败（源码含凭证、缺安全中间件、无容器定义、无健康检查端点、无交付流水线）；而自动修复多依赖自由形式的模型生成，结果不可复现且缺少独立校验。

Method: 构建本地 MCP 服务器，按修复所需内容将 50 条规则分类：28 条固定模板、16 条从项目提取值填充、仅 6 条由 IDE 代理模型撰写；修复采用有界循环，仅当原先告警的检查器通过才接受修复，之后经门禁并部署；另训练一个基于 684 次真实部署的可部署性模型。

Result: 88.0% 规则无需模型撰写内容，在 499 个真实仓库中覆盖 93.5% 的违规；模板与提取类修复按原样应用时通过率分别为 87.6% 和 95.0%，独立验证拒绝了 237 条"已应用"修复中的 25 条；可部署性模型留出集 ROC AUC 0.909；门禁项目 82 秒内完成部署、冒烟测试并接入 CI/CD；在 VS Code、Cursor、Windsurf 中均可运行，实时代理首次尝试即解决全部 6 条委托规则。

Conclusion: 将修复内容尽量限制为模板与项目内提取、并以检查器独立把关，可在不依赖模型生成的前提下显著提升 AI 生成项目的生产可部署性，且工具能跨主流 IDE 代理通用。

Abstract: AI-assisted development environments turn natural-language requests into working applications, yet the projects they produce often fail on their first deployment for operational reasons: credentials in source files, missing security middleware, no container definition, no health endpoint and no delivery pipeline. Automated repair of such defects usually relies on free-form model generation, which varies between runs and is seldom checked independently. This paper presents ProdPilot, a local Model Context Protocol server that audits Node.js and React projects against 50 production readiness rules, repairs violations through a bounded loop, gates the result and deploys it. Each rule is classified by what its fix requires: 28 fixes are fixed templates, 16 are filled with values extracted from the project and only 6 are written by the IDE agent's model, so 88.0\% of the ruleset needs no model-written content, and across 499 real repositories 93.5\% of the violations found fall in those classes. A fix, however it was produced, is accepted only if the checker that flagged the rule now passes. In a full-chain run over 23 sample projects, template and extraction fixes that were applied as written passed their checkers in 87.6\% and 95.0\% of cases, and independent verification rejected 25 of the 237 fixes reported as applied. A deployability model trained on 684 real deployments ranks held-out projects at 0.909 ROC AUC, a gated project was deployed, smoke-tested and connected to an active CI/CD pipeline in 82 seconds, and the unchanged server worked from the IDE agents in VS Code, Cursor and Windsurf, where live agents resolved all six delegated rules on the first attempt under the same verification. ProdPilot itself runs no model.

</details>


### [112] [Time-Consistent Evaluation of Hierarchical Code Graphs for SWE Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17388)
*Xianpeng,Sun,Tian Yu,Haonan Sun,Sheng Ma,Qincheng Zhang,Lifei Rao,Jie Li,Jian,Cao,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在零泄漏协议下评测仓库感知SWE智能体，提出两级分层依赖图ServiceExplorer，在7,200次配对评测中全面优于基线，且能让低阶模型超越高阶模型的基线表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库感知SWE智能体基准存在时间污染与提示级答案泄漏问题，缺乏严格隔离预存仓库知识与未来代码演进的评测环境；同时需要公平比较不同上下文增强方式对模型定位能力的影响。

Method: 沿用Sun(2026)的时间一致基准协议，在DragonFly(C++)与React(JS/TS)上锁定历史截止提交T0的仓库快照与知识图谱，评测100个T0之后的PR，采用四种提示粒度（Minimal/Concise/Contextual/Guided）；对比三种上下文增强机制：无增强基线、扁平Tree-sitter AST图、以及ServiceExplorer（确定性两级层次依赖图，L1系统/包容器+L2架构模块），共7,200次配对评测，覆盖MAI-Code Flash、Claude、GPT-Sol三大系列共六个基础模型。

Result: ServiceExplorer在所有模型、仓库与提示粒度上均优于Baseline和Tree-sitter AST，相对F1提升+15.6%至+86.2%（p<1e-4），并在DragonFly上将完整PR精确定位率提升三倍。关键的是，为效率级或上一代模型配备ServiceExplorer后，其表现可一致超越下一代模型的无增强Baseline F1。

Conclusion: 结构化、架构级的上下文表示比扁平语法图更有效；上下文增强可部分替代模型代际升级带来的收益，为低成本SWE智能体部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Evaluating repository-aware software engineering (SWE) agents requires strict separation between pre-existing repository knowledge and future code evolution, yet standard benchmarks frequently suffer from temporal contamination and prompt-level solution leakage. Building on the time-consistent benchmark protocol of Sun (2026), we enforce strict zero-leakage isolation across DragonFly in C++ and React in JavaScript/TypeScript, locking repository snapshots and knowledge graphs at historical cutoff commits $T_0$ and evaluating 100 post-$T_0$ pull requests across four prompt granularities: Minimal, Concise, Contextual, and Guided.
  Under this zero-leakage protocol, we conduct a matched evaluation of three context-augmentation regimes: (1) an unaugmented Baseline, (2) a flat syntax-level Tree-sitter AST graph, and (3) ServiceExplorer, a deterministic two-level hierarchical dependency graph capturing Level-1 system/package containers and Level-2 architectural modules. Across 7,200 paired evaluations spanning six foundation models across the MAI-Code Flash, Claude, and GPT-Sol families, ServiceExplorer consistently outperforms both Baseline and Tree-sitter AST across every model, repository, and prompt granularity ($+15.6\%$ to $+86.2\%$ relative $F_1$ lift, $p < 10^{-4}$), while tripling exact full-PR localization on DragonFly. Crucially, equipping an efficiency-tier or prior-generation model with ServiceExplorer consistently surpasses the unaugmented Baseline $F_1$ of the next-generation model across all evaluated families.

</details>


### [113] [Interpreting Coding Agents Through Their Behavioral Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17440)
*Ibragim Badertdinov,Alexander Golubev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文分析14万余条编码智能体执行轨迹，用规则解析器将原始轨迹映射为高层动作，发现解题率相近的智能体在探索、修改与验证代码的行为上差异显著，且行为画像可跨仓库与编程语言识别对应的智能体配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准以二元任务成功率作为主要指标，把执行过程视为黑箱，掩盖了产生结果的具体行为，无法区分解题率相近但行为迥异的智能体。

Method: 构建143,209条轨迹语料，覆盖125种智能体配置、117个模型；开发基于规则的解析器，将原始执行轨迹映射为仓库搜索、文件编辑、测试执行等高层动作，并用六模型共识标注的500个事件进行审计。分析时不使用任务结果、token数或工具名作为特征，通过聚合行为画像做跨仓库/跨语言检索；并分析前沿模型与脚手架的影响。

Result: 解题率相近的智能体在探索、修改与验证代码方式上存在显著差异；在不用结果、token与工具名的前提下，聚合行为画像能在不相交的仓库集合与编程语言间检索出对应智能体栈（但跨仓库场景下任务构成未完全控制）；脚手架选择与推理强度会系统性改变搜索、编辑与测试行为。

Conclusion: 行为层面的细粒度分析可补充二元成功率指标，揭示智能体差异；作者开源确定性解析器、完整标注与分析流水线及标注轨迹子集。

Abstract: Standard coding-agent benchmarks primarily evaluate binary task success, treating execution as a black box and obscuring the behaviors that produce those outcomes. We analyze a large corpus of 143,209 execution trajectories from 125 agent configurations spanning 117 unique models. To enable comparisons across heterogeneous agent interfaces, we develop a rule-based parser that maps raw execution traces to high-level actions such as repository search, file editing, and test execution, and audit its predictions against a six-model consensus reference on 500 events. We find that agents with similar solve rates can nevertheless differ substantially in how they explore, modify and verify code. Moreover, without using task outcomes, token counts, or tool names as features, aggregate behavioral profiles retrieve the corresponding agent stack across disjoint repository sets and programming languages, although task composition is not fully controlled in the cross-repository setting. Through analyses of frontier models and agent scaffolds, we further show that scaffold choice and reasoning effort systematically shift agents' search, editing, and testing behavior. We release the deterministic parser together with the full annotation and analysis pipeline alongside an annotated trajectory subset.

</details>


### [114] [GraphScent: Calibrating Directional Relevance Signals for Bounded-Context Code Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17527)
*Ramesh Arvind Naagarajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文发现冻结语言模型对代码结构关系存在"方向性相关不对称"：上游调用者/外层作用域的相关性评分具有判别力，而下游被调者、同级方法、共变更伙伴等接近随机；基于此提出无需训练的图搜索控制器 GraphScent，单卡即可把难样本精确本地化率从12.8%提升到23.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单张消费级GPU上本地运行开放权重模型时，无法承受多轮探索的膨胀历史，需要用简短、无状态的代码检查替代。但无状态仓库定位的关键疑问是：冻结模型在紧凑视图下评判一个未编辑代码实体时，哪些结构关系能使其相关性判断真正指向附近的修复目标。现有方法缺乏对这一问题的系统认识。

Method: 在86个仓库、211个真实Python issue上研究Gemma 4系列模型，用零温度相关性评分测试多种结构关系（外层作用域、上游调用者、下游被调者、同级方法、历史共变更、稠密节点嵌入）。通过零泄漏的掩码作用域探针，以及控制词法相似度与片段长度的回归，排除目标泄漏与表面重叠的解释。据此提出GraphScent：在运行时Typed-AST多重图上传播"出仓库、关系条件"的对数似然比，再用一次listwise比较处理平局；并辅以AST Ripple作为编辑后遗漏共改的轻量检查，同时用似然校准抑制小模型的过度自信。

Result: 在难词法目标上，精确本地化率从12.8%提升至23.4%（31B模型，单GPU预算内）；外层作用域与上游调用者的评分能稳定区分真实结构邻居与强词法干扰项，其余关系接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 相关性不对称反映的是功能角色（调用者暴露委托给缺陷例程的症状入口，而下游辅助函数单独看似无害），而非目标泄漏或表面重叠。这表明冻结LM可被当作结构关系条件下的弱信号源，通过图上的似然比传播与校准，在无训练、单卡条件下获得有效的仓库定位。

Abstract: Running open-weight language models locally on a single consumer GPU requires replacing expanding multi-turn exploration histories with short, stateless code inspections. Stateless repository localization, however, hinges on a foundational question: when a frozen language model evaluates an unedited code entity from a compact view, which structural relationships make its relevance judgment informative about a nearby repair target? Studying Gemma 4 models across 211 real-world Python issues in 86 repositories, we uncover a systematic directional relevance asymmetry: zero-temperature relevance scores on enclosing scopes and upstream callers consistently separate true structural neighbors from hard lexical distractors, whereas scores on downstream callees, peer methods, historical co-change partners, and dense node embeddings remain near chance. Zero-leakage masked-scope probes and regressions controlling for lexical similarity and snippet length confirm that this asymmetry reflects functional role (callers expose symptom entrypoints delegating to defective routines, while downstream helpers appear benign alone) rather than target leakage or surface overlap. Guided by this finding, we introduce GraphScent, a training-free search controller that propagates out-of-repository, relation-conditioned log-likelihood ratios over runtime Typed-AST multigraphs before resolving ties via a single listwise comparison, alongside AST Ripple, a lightweight post-edit check for missed co-edits. Likelihood calibration prevents overconfident smaller models from chasing false-positive callers while lifting exact localization on lexically hard targets from 12.8 to 23.4 percent at 31B within a single-GPU budget.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Source Code: Evaluating Natural Language Specifications for LLM-Based C-to-Rust Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17581)
*Biruk Tadesse,Lawrence Arkoh,Marcelo D'Amorim,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文比较多模态输入（仅代码、仅规格、代码+规格）对C到Rust翻译质量的影响，发现仅规格翻译产出最地道、最紧凑的Rust（unsafe块减少约6倍），但正确性较低；加入基于测试的迭代验证可提升至95.3%通过率；代码+规格兼顾正确性与可维护性最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有C-to-Rust翻译主要依赖源代码输入，忽略了自然语言规格说明这一潜在替代或补充表示。作者假设直接代码翻译会使LLM保留C特有实现选择，产出功能正确但冗长、依赖unsafe、不够地道的Rust。

Method: 在IBM CodeNet的300个C程序上，用GPT-5.2和GPT-4o-mini比较三种设置：仅代码、仅规格、代码+规格。评估功能正确性、可维护性、复杂度、地道性、unsafe使用和成本，并引入基于测试的迭代验证与规格精炼步骤。

Result: 仅代码翻译测试通过率最高（98.6%）但模仿C实现模式；仅规格翻译最地道紧凑，unsafe块减少约6倍、Clippy风格警告减少2.4倍，但未验证时通过率低至86%；加入迭代验证后升至95.3%；代码+规格综合最佳（98.3%通过率）。

Conclusion: 基于规格的翻译相对仅代码翻译持续提升代码质量，代码+规格在正确性与可维护性间取得最佳平衡。研究系统性揭示了规格质量、翻译正确性、代码质量与计算成本之间的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promising results for C-to-Rust translation, yet existing approaches primarily rely on source code as input, leaving natural-language specifications of program behavior underexplored as an alternative or complementary representation. We hypothesize that direct code translation encourages LLMs to preserve C-specific implementation choices, producing Rust that may be functionally correct but unnecessarily verbose, reliant on unsafe constructs, and less idiomatic. We investigate this hypothesis by comparing three translation settings: code-only, specification-only, and code-and-specification. Using 300 C programs from the IBM CodeNet dataset, we evaluate GPT-5.2 and GPT-4o-mini on functional correctness, maintainability, complexity, idiomaticity, unsafe construct usage, and cost. Code-only translation achieves the highest test pass rate (98.6%) but often mirrors C's implementation patterns. In contrast, specification-only translation produces the most idiomatic and compact Rust, reducing unsafe blocks by approximately 6x and Clippy style warnings by 2.4x. However, using specifications without validation yields test pass rates as low as 86% with our best performing model. Adding an iterative test-based validation and refinement step for specifications raises this to a 95.3% test pass rate. Overall, specification-based translation consistently improves code quality relative to code-only translation, while code-and-specification offers the best balance between correctness (98.3% test pass rate) and maintainability. Our study systematically compares these translation settings, highlighting the trade-offs among specification quality, translation correctness, code quality, and computational cost.

</details>


### [116] [Choosing Quantized Code LLMs by File Size and Energy per Solved Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17605)
*Aditi Patodiya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文在无风扇 MacBook Air M2 上对 Qwen2.5-Coder 四个尺寸共 24 个 GGUF 文件，以“每解决任务的能耗”为指标在 542 个 EvalPlus 任务上评测，给出按 Ollama 文件大小索引的选型指南，并检验“大模型低精度胜小模型高精度”的经验规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地代码助手把推理能耗转移到用户机器上，而常见的“大模型+低精度优于小模型+高精度”规则缺乏能耗与正确性的实证依据，尤其是在消费级无风扇设备上。

Method: 在 fanless MacBook Air M2 上运行 24 个 Qwen2.5-Coder GGUF 文件（4 种尺寸、多种量化），基于 542 个 EvalPlus 任务计算每个已解决任务的能耗；区分 CPU、GPU、内存轨与整机功耗两种计账口径，并给出 90% 置信区间与 HumanEval+ 交替会话配对验证。

Result: 1–2 GB 档选择 1.5B 与 3B 的 q4_K_M，兼顾正确率与能耗前沿；3 GB 档中唯一可容纳的 7B-q2_K 相对 3B-q4_K_M 无显著解题增益（3B 劣势上限 2.4 个百分点），但每解决任务能耗为 2.03 倍（分轨）或 1.66–1.81 倍（整机）；HumanEval+ 配对实验比值 1.925 对 1.956 一致；8 GB 档 7B-q6_K 有显著增益但能耗更高；从 1.5B 起 q4_K_M 比同尺寸最高精度低不到 3 个百分点。

Conclusion: “大模型低精度更优”的规则在紧凑内存预算下会失败：3 GB 档应选 3B-q4_K_M 而非 7B-q2_K。作者发布逐任务结果、功耗日志与分析脚本，选型指南按文件大小索引并标注证据等级。

Abstract: A developer running a code assistant on a laptop downloads one model file, and local inference moves the energy of every request onto that machine. A common rule says a larger model at lower precision tends to beat a smaller one at higher precision. We ran 24 GGUF files of Qwen2.5-Coder at four sizes on a fanless MacBook Air M2 and priced each in energy per solved task on 542 EvalPlus tasks. The result is a selection guide indexed by Ollama's listed file size, with an evidence label on each pick. At 1 and 2 GB it picks the q4_K_M files of the 1.5B and 3B models, both on the correctness and energy frontier under every accounting. At 3 GB as listed, the larger-model rule picks 7B-q2_K, the only measured 7B file that fits, and there it does not pay. That file brings no significant gain in solves over the 2 GB pick, 3B-q4_K_M, and the 90% CI limits any 3B-q4_K_M deficit to 2.4 points. Yet 7B-q2_K needs 2.03 times the energy per solved task on CPU, GPU and memory rails, or 1.66 to 1.81 times on whole-system power. On HumanEval+, five alternating session pairs give a ratio of 1.925 against 1.956 in the main grid. At 8 GB, 7B-q6_K buys a significant gain over the 2 GB pick at a higher energy price. From 1.5B up, q4_K_M trails the highest precision measured at its size by less than 3 points (90% CI). We release per-task results, power logs and the analysis script.

</details>


### [117] [SZZBench: Controlled Git Histories with Explicit Oracles for Diagnosing SZZ Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17669)
*Yoshiki Higo,Inase Kondo,Masanari Kondo,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SZZBench：用声明式场景生成可控Git仓库，逐场景评估SZZ实现的失败原因，并以26个naive基线校准结论。279个场景/17类/4工具适配器。9种配置的实证显示PyDriller通过161个场景，"总答第二个提交"基线通过160个，但两者仅共同通过97个——通过数量掩盖了二者擅长不同场景的事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SZZ实现主要用从真实项目挖掘的oracle评估。这类oracle虽真实，但每个案例混杂了多个决定成败的历史因素，聚合分数无法解释某实现为何成功或失败，也无法区分实现能力与偶然得分。

Method: 构建SZZBench工具：从声明式场景生成普通Git仓库并附带显式oracle；按声明的评估策略给任意SZZ实现的预测打分；将结果对照26个naive基线（如"总是回答第二个提交"）解读；并用naive基线结果校验新场景的声明（如tier）是否合理。工具提供279个场景、17个类别及4个SZZ工具的适配器。

Result: 在不改动代码的前提下以9种配置运行这些工具：PyDriller通过161个场景，"总答第二个提交"规则通过160个，但二者共同通过的仅97个；说明二者成功于不同场景，仅看通过数量会掩盖这一点，逐场景结果才能揭示。

Conclusion: 逐场景、可解释的诊断评估比聚合分数更能刻画SZZ实现的真实能力与失效条件；naive基线既作为解读参照，也可用于校验场景声明的正确性。工具已开源。

Abstract: The SZZ algorithm traces a bug-fixing commit back to the commits that introduced the bug. Its labels underlie defect-prediction data sets and many mining software repositories studies. SZZ implementations are mostly evaluated against oracles mined from real projects. Such oracles are realistic, but each case mixes the history factors that make an implementation succeed or fail, and an aggregate score cannot show why. We present SZZBench, a tool that shows why: it generates ordinary Git repositories from declarative scenarios with explicit oracles, scores the predictions of any SZZ implementation under declared evaluation policies, and reads each result against 26 naive baselines, such as always answering the second commit. SZZBench also checks the declarations of new scenarios, such as the tier, against the results of the naive baselines. Currently, SZZBench ships 279 scenarios in 17 categories and adapters for four SZZ tools. Running these tools in nine configurations without changing their code, we find that PyDriller passes 161 scenarios and a rule that always answers the second commit passes 160, yet only 97 scenarios are passed by both: the two succeed on different scenarios, which the number of passed scenarios hides and the per-scenario results reveal. SZZBench is available at https://github.com/higolab/SZZBench.

</details>


### [118] [DesignSpecs: A DESIGN.md Dataset of Visual Design Specifications for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17713)
*Jialiang Wei,Walid Maalej*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DesignSpecs：首个大规模 DESIGN.md 设计规范文件数据集，从 613,207 个同名文件中筛选出 36,906 个（来自 15,794 个非 fork 公开仓库，17,240 个唯一内容）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体日益生成和修改用户界面，但很少依据项目特定的视觉设计规范。DESIGN.md 格式（源自 Google Stitch，2026 年 4 月开源）通过设计令牌（命名颜色、文本样式）和 Markdown 文本描述视觉识别，弥补了这一空白；然而目前尚无大规模此类设计规范文件的研究数据集。

Method: 2026 年 10 月采集 613,207 个名为 DESIGN.md 的文件；因同名文件也指架构方案等设计文档，故以四项条件过滤：含 YAML front matter、具设计系统名称、至少一个可识别设计令牌、官方 linter 零错误。最终得到 36,906 个文件，来自 15,794 个公开非 fork 仓库，代表 17,240 个唯一内容。数据集以 JSON Lines 格式提供文件级与去重视图，保留原始 Markdown，并关联仓库、路径与 commit，附带相关脚本。

Result: 构建并发布 DesignSpecs 数据集（36,906 文件 / 15,794 仓库 / 17,240 唯一内容），公开于 Hugging Face；并提出关于视觉设计知识与设计感知 AI 智能体的研究方向。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了设计规范文件缺乏大规模研究数据的空白，为设计感知 AI 智能体及视觉设计知识研究提供基础资源。

Abstract: While AI agents increasingly generate and modify user interfaces, they rarely rely on a visual design specification for a certain project. The DESIGN$.$md format, which originated from Google's AI design tool Stitch and which was open-sourced in April 2026, addresses this gap by describing a visual identity through design tokens (such as named colors, text styles) and Markdown prose. To our knowledge, no large-scale research dataset of such design specification files exists. We present DesignSpecs, a dataset of 36,906 DESIGN$.$md files from 15,794 public, non-fork GitHub repositories, representing 17,240 unique file contents. We collected 613,207 files named DESIGN$.$md in October 2026. Because the same file name also denotes architectural plans and other design documents, we filtered those with YAML front matter, a design-system name, at least one recognized design token, and zero errors under the official linter. Our JSON Lines dataset provides file-level and deduplicated views of the original Markdown, links files to repositories, paths, and commits, and is released with related scripts. We outline research opportunities on visual design knowledge and design-aware AI agents. Dataset is available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Jl-wei/DesignSpecs

</details>


### [119] [HerAgent: A Block-Level Checkpointing Harness for Coding-Agent Environment Setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18443)
*Xiang Li,Siyu Lu,Federica Sarro,Claire Le Goues,He Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HerAgent 提出"语义化 setup 块"这一统一的进度控制抽象，用于编码智能体的环境搭建，在四个数据集上提升成功率并大幅降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代编码智能体需先构建可执行的软件环境才能进行测试、调试、补丁验证等任务。现有 setup 智能体的进度控制粒度只有单条命令或整段脚本两种：前者恢复范围过小，无法清除被污染的状态；后者过粗，会丢弃已有有效进度。

Method: 提出 HerAgent（Harness EnviRonment Setup Agent），一个块级（block-level）环境搭建框架。核心是语义化 setup 块（semantic setup blocks）：用同一抽象统一支持执行、验证、检查点、修复与回滚，从而兼顾可恢复性与进度保留。

Result: 在四个环境搭建数据集上对比六个先前智能体，HerAgent 在所有比较中均提升搭建成功率：Installamatic-Bench 从 52.0% 提升至 85.0%，SetupBench 从 46.3% 提升至 70.4%；同时将搭建成本降低最多 94.5%。

Conclusion: 块级进度控制同时提升环境搭建的可靠性与效率，表明合适的抽象粒度是编码智能体环境构建的关键设计点。

Abstract: Modern coding agents rely on establishing executable software environments before they can perform software engineering tasks such as testing, debugging, and patch validation. Existing setup agents typically control progress at either single-command or whole-script granularity, making recovery either too local to remove polluted state or too coarse to preserve useful progress. We present HerAgent (Harness EnviRonment Setup Agent), a block-level harness for coding-agent environment setup. HerAgent's main contribution is semantic setup blocks: a single progress-control abstraction used for execution, validation, checkpointing, repair, and rollback. Evaluated against six prior agents on four environment-setup datasets, HerAgent improves setup success in every comparison, achieving the largest gains on Installamatic-Bench (52.0% to 85.0%) and SetupBench (46.3% to 70.4%). It also reduces setup costs by up to 94.5%, showing that block-level progress control improves reliability and efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluating Feedback-Driven Black-Box Processes via Stochastic Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18477)
*Mengfan Xu,Zirui Liu,Juan Zhai,Shiqing Ma,Barry Nelson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于马尔可夫链与仿真的随机框架，统一建模与评估反馈驱动的黑盒过程（以变异模糊测试为主要实例），并给出新的性能度量、可靠估计量与有保证的评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈驱动的黑盒过程在软件工程与机器学习中广泛存在且本质是顺序的，但许多策略依赖对系统的结构性假设，而真实系统常为黑盒，导致缺乏统一的评估框架。

Method: 用马尔可夫链与模拟构建随机框架：状态转移由变异策略决定、反馈由程序执行给出；发展新的性能度量、无偏/可靠估计量及带可证明保证的评估流程；在模糊测试与非模糊测试领域验证。

Result: 在18个常用模糊测试基准上评估9个模糊器，并在4个非模糊测试领域做案例研究；结果表明框架为评估提供有原则的基础且能联系实践。

Conclusion: 该框架将经验实践与理论连接，为反馈驱动黑盒过程提供统一、可证明的评估基础，其意义超出模糊测试本身。

Abstract: We study feedback-driven black-box processes across software engineering and machine learning. These processes are inherently sequential: policies generate inputs, observe outputs/feedback from systems, and use the feedback to guide the design of subsequent inputs. While many policies are developed under structural assumptions on the underlying systems, many real-world systems operate without such knowledge, necessitating black-box policies that lack a unified evaluation framework. We introduce a stochastic framework that models and evaluates such policies using Markov chains and simulation, thereby connecting empirical practice with theory. We instantiate the framework in mutation-based fuzzing (widely used for discovering software bugs via sequential input mutation), by a novel Markov chain model of fuzzing whose transitions are governed by the mutation strategy and whose feedback is given by program execution. We develop a rigorous evaluation paradigm with new performance measures, sound estimators, and evaluation procedures with provable guarantees. Guided by the theory, we evaluate 9 fuzzers on 18 widely used fuzzing benchmarks and conduct case studies in 4 non-fuzzing domains. These results show that the framework provides a principled basis for evaluation while connecting to practice, with implications beyond fuzzing.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [TSGP-2: On the Non-Linearity of Symbolic Regression Problems and How to Exploit It with Model-based Semantic Genetic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17455)
*Philipp Anthes,Alina Geiger,Martin Briesch,Franz Rothlauf*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 针对TSGP训练数据偏线性导致搜索偏向线性子代的问题，本文分析基准问题线性度并提出用线性与非线性目标函数生成的程序共同训练的TSGP-2，在非线性问题上优于TSGP、线性问题上相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的变化算子（如Transformer Semantic Genetic Programming, TSGP）受训练数据偏置影响：TSGP仅在由线性目标函数GP运行生成的程序上训练，因而偏向产生线性子代，削弱了在需要非线性解的问题上的搜索能力。作者希望先厘清标准测试问题（Feynman、真实世界基准）本身的线性/非线性结构，再据此设计偏置更匹配问题的算子。

Method: 1）测量并分析Feynman问题与真实世界基准问题的线性度：真实世界问题的大部分结构可被线性结构较好近似，而Feynman问题呈现不同程度的非线性结构。2）提出TSGP-2：保持TSGP的功能框架，但训练数据改为在同时包含线性与非线性目标函数上进行GP运行所产生的程序，使训练集中线性行为程序占比从83%降至37%。3）对比TSGP与TSGP-2在各类问题上的表现，并分析子代线性偏置及搜索早期产生有意义的非线性子代的数量。

Result: TSGP-2在具非线性结构的问题上优于TSGP，在线性问题上的表现与TSGP相近；它对线性子代的偏置更小，能在搜索早期提出更多有意义的非线性子代，并与更优解的出现相吻合。

Conclusion: 学习型变化算子的训练数据至关重要；搜索算子的偏置应与所应用问题的结构相匹配，既需生成线性也需生成非线性程序。

Abstract: Model-based variation operators such as the one used in Transformer Semantic Genetic Programming (TSGP) are affected by possible biases of their training data. TSGP is trained, in particular, on programs generated by Genetic Programming (GP) runs on linear target functions, which leads to a bias toward linear offspring. This bias can hinder search performance on problems that require non-linear solutions. This work has two goals: first, it investigates the linearity of standard test problems such as the Feynman and real-world benchmark problems. While most of the structure of real-world problems can be well approximated by linear structures, the Feynman problems exhibit varying levels of non-linear structure. Thus, a proper variation operator of a search method should be able to create both types of programs, linear as well as non-linear ones. Motivated by this observation, we propose TSGP-2, which follows the functionality of TSGP but uses as training data programs from GP runs on both linear and non-linear target functions. As a result, the training data includes a lower share of programs that behave linearly (the share drops from 83% to 37%). With a higher share of non-linear structures, TSGP-2 outperforms TSGP on problems with non-linear structure, while performing similarly on linear problems. In addition, TSGP-2 is less biased toward linear offspring and is able to propose a higher number of meaningful non-linear offspring earlier during search, which coincides with better solutions. The findings underscore the importance of training data for a learned variation operator and emphasize that the biases of search operators should be aligned with the problems they are applied to.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Learning nonlinear interactions and cross-feature couplings with product-unit residual networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18074)
*Ziyuan Li,Uwe Jaekel,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出复值乘积单元残差网络（CV-PURe），用乘法乘积单元+残差连接显式建模特征交叉耦合，在交互驱动基准与真实数据上同时提升预测精度与交互可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLP只能隐式捕获非线性特征交互，表征纠缠，损害鲁棒性与可解释性。

Method: 将复值乘积单元与残差连接结合构建CV-PURe，在可复现协议下用交互驱动合成基准与真实数据（Friedman、Bike Sharing、Parkinsons）评估预测误差、输入级交互恢复、消融、噪声与学习曲线及推理开销。

Result: 5次划分×5个种子下，CV-PURe在6个科学基准中4个取得最低平均RMSE，Friedman误差较MLP降低53.9%；交互强度60.7%落在已知交互对上（MLP为31.0%）。合成任务（含三四阶乘积与20个干扰项）RMSE由0.1376降至0.0797，并每次重复均恢复全部9个真实成对投影；噪声与有限监督下增益持续，并量化了计算开销。

Conclusion: 显式乘法交叉+残差稳定化可兼顾预测性能与交互可解释性，是ICCS 2026论文的扩展版本。

Abstract: Understanding nonlinear feature interactions is crucial in science and engineering, yet standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) often capture such interactions only implicitly, leading to entangled representations that can impair robustness and interpretability. We investigate complex-valued product-unit residual networks (CV-PURe) that integrate multiplicative product units with residual connections to explicitly model cross-feature couplings while stabilizing optimization. We conduct a systematic evaluation on an interaction-driven synthetic benchmark and real-world datasets and evaluate prediction together with input-level interaction recovery under a reproducible protocol. Across five data splits and five training seeds, CV-PURe achieves the lowest mean RMSE on four of six scientific benchmarks, reducing Friedman error by 53.9% relative to MLP. Its Friedman interaction maps assign 60.7% of total strength to the known interacting pair, compared with 31.0% for MLP. On a synthetic task containing third- and fourth-order products and 20 distractors, CV-PURe reduces RMSE from 0.1376 to 0.0797 relative to a complex-valued MLP and recovers all nine true pairwise projections in every training/sampling repetition. Bike Sharing experiments connect improved prediction to the contribution of temporal, calendar, and seasonal context through paired ablations of both PURe variants. Parkinsons Telemonitoring extends the context analysis to repeated measurements. Noise and learning-curve evaluations demonstrate sustained predictive gains under perturbed inputs and limited supervision, while inference measurements quantify computational cost. This article extends our ICCS 2026 paper, Modeling Nonlinear Feature Interactions with Product-Unit Residual Networks (doi:10.1007/978-3-032-29921-5_6).

</details>


### [123] [Efficient Poincaré Convolutions through Patch-Statistics Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18125)
*Lukáš Jochymek,Markéta Vašinková,Petr Gajdoš*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Poincaré卷积的因子化实现：分数只依赖每个patch的平方范数与和滤波器的内积，可用普通卷积+单通道box filter直接算，无需把patch映射到Poincaré球，从而加速训练并降低内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HNN++中Poincaré卷积的现有实现会把每个重叠图像patch先映射到Poincaré球再计算分数，计算与内存开销大，训练昂贵。

Method: 证明裁剪后的分数仅通过两个统计量依赖每个patch（平方范数、与滤波器的内积），这两者可由原生卷积和单通道box filter直接计算；据此设计因子化层，精确等价地评估原分数，而不构造球值patch。

Result: 单精度输出与梯度至少与显式参考一样接近双精度结果；RTX A6000上双版本JAX中Poincaré U-Net与ResNet-20完整训练更新快1.5–1.8倍（对比跳过映射的实现为1.2–1.4倍）；V100上ResNet-20加宽8倍仍保持优势；每任务15次配对训练更新快2.0/1.8倍，最终分割与分类质量无一致变化；U-Net扩展研究中峰值内存降38–55%；替换官方PyTorch Hyperbolic U-Net卷积后训练步快1.4倍、内存减半。

Conclusion: 从patch统计量出发评估已有Poincaré层，可在所测模型与软件栈中显著降低训练成本与内存，同时保持精度。

Abstract: Published implementations of the Poincaré convolution in Hyperbolic Neural Networks++ (HNN++) map every overlapping image patch to the Poincaré ball before evaluating its score. We show that the clipped score depends on each patch only through two statistics, its squared norm and its inner products with the filters, which a native convolution and a single-channel box filter compute directly. The factorized layer therefore evaluates the specified score exactly, without constructing ball-valued patches, and its single-precision outputs and gradients agree with double-precision evaluations at least as closely as those of the explicit reference. On an RTX A6000 under two versions of JAX, complete training updates of a Poincaré U-Net and a Poincaré ResNet-20 are 1.5 to 1.8 times faster than the faster of two explicit implementations that map patches to the ball (1.2 to 1.4 times against an explicit implementation that skips this mapping); on a V100, the gain over the gather-based one persists as the ResNet-20 is widened eightfold. In 15 paired training runs per task, updates are 2.0 and 1.8 times faster than a reference that follows the published execution order, with no consistent change in final segmentation or classification quality, and peak memory falls by 38 to 55% in a U-Net scaling study. Replacing the convolutions of the official PyTorch Hyperbolic U-Net makes its training step 1.4 times faster with half the memory. Evaluating an existing layer from its patch statistics thus makes Poincaré convolutional networks cheaper to train in the tested models and software stacks.

</details>


### [124] [Alignment with Reflect-Reasoning: Learning How To Follow Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17398)
*Henry Bell,Caroline Zhang,Dhaval Potdar,Mohammed Mobasserul Haque,Brandon Fain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Reflect-Reasoning 训练方法，让模型学会"如何推理"以对齐任意原则，而非只记住具体原则，从而在分布外原则下也能泛化并提升对齐一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 对齐所需的规范与原则高度依赖文化背景和部署场景，但现有对齐技术多在训练时将偏好/宪法原则固定下来；实践中部署者只能靠 system prompting 在推理时施加约束。已有的事后反思式（critique+revision）推理虽优于简单 prompting，却受限于模型自身固有的推理能力，无法真正泛化到新原则。

Method: Reflect-Reasoning 是一种训练方法，训练模型对"如何推理以对齐给定原则"进行学习，而不是学习"该遵循哪些原则"；将其与 system prompting 及显式的上下文反思推理 prompting 进行对比，在五个模型上评测，并测试向医疗、金融等分布外领域的泛化、罕见但严重违规的减少情况、基础安全工程的保持以及对抗性宪法的越狱鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个模型上，Reflect-Reasoning consistently 优于 system prompting 和显式反思推理 prompting；改进可泛化到完全分布外的医疗与金融原则领域；减少了罕见但严重的原则违规；不损害模型既有安全工程，并对使用对抗性宪法的越狱尝试具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Reflect-Reasoning 是一种可扩展的方法，将宪法式对齐训练为一种可泛化的推理能力，使模型无需额外微调即可适应新原则并产生透明的推理轨迹。

Abstract: The appropriate governing norms and principles for the alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) often depend on cultural context and deployment application. However, most alignment techniques focus on training models to conform to human preferences or constitutional principles specified at training time and subsequently fixed. In practice, many deployers rely on system prompting to implement contextual norms and guardrails at inference time. Prior work has shown that post-generation reflective reasoning with critique and revision can improve conformance compared to simple system prompting, but such methods are limited to the reasoning abilities inherent in the trained model. We introduce Reflect-Reasoning, a training method that teaches models how to reason about alignment to principles, rather than simply which principles to follow. Across five models, Reflect-Reasoning consistently outperforms both system prompting and explicit prompting strategies for in-context reflective reasoning. These improvements extend and generalize to entirely out-of-distribution domains of medical and financial principles. We additionally demonstrate that Reflect-Reasoning reduces the occurrence of rare but serious principle violations. Finally, we show that Reflect-Reasoning does not erode the safety engineering of a model and is robust against jailbreaking attempts using adversarial constitutions. Overall, our results demonstrate that Reflect-Reasoning is a scalable method for training constitutional alignment as a generalizable reasoning capability, enabling models to adapt to novel principles and produce transparent reasoning traces without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [125] [WETW: Learning Pair-Specific Weighted Euclidean Distances for Efficient DTW Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17412)
*Christos Panourgias,Qitong Wang,George Tzagkarakis,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WETW学习距离及WETW-FV过滤-验证管线，在保证精确DTW距离前提下较最快精确方法提速11.4倍且recall@10达0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTW虽为标准时间序列相似度距离，但代价随序列长度二次增长，且下界剪枝效果差，大规模长序列搜索缓慢；现有学习式近似对每对序列用同一固定规则（通常欧氏距离）比较，而DTW对每对序列的对齐方式各不相同，导致效果受限。

Method: 观察到对齐后DTW即为在该对所选坐标上的欧氏距离；据此提出WETW：用小对称网络直接为每个嵌入维度和每对序列预测权重，不计算对齐，使比较自适应；嵌入由WESEE编码器产生，其多尺度时序特征提取器保留全局池化丢失的时序信息；训练时加入排序正则化以纠正回归对齐误差导致的近邻错序；WETW-FV采用WETW构建候选短列表、再用精确DTW验证。

Result: WETW将同一嵌入上最佳静态距离的误差减半；WESEE使recall@1从0.90升至0.95；排序正则化使recall@1从0.79升至0.95；八个数据集上WETW-FV较UCR Suite、下界方法和MESSI-DTW中最快精确方法快11.4倍，recall@10均值0.93（最佳学习基线同速仅0.59）；48K点序列上仍比UCR Suite快40倍，recall@10为0.91。

Conclusion: 以对齐视角建模的成对自适应学习距离WETW结合过滤-验证管线，能在返回精确DTW距离的同时显著加速大规模长序列相似性搜索，验证了“仅拟合DTW距离不足以支撑搜索、需排序正则化”与“保留时序信息”两点设计的关键性。

Abstract: Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) is the standard distance for similarity search over time series that are misaligned in time, but its cost is quadratic in the series length and lower bounds often prune few candidates, so search over large collections of long series is slow. Learned approximations embed the series to make comparisons cheap, yet compare every pair with the same fixed rule, usually the Euclidean distance, although DTW aligns each pair differently. We observe that, once a pair is aligned, DTW is a Euclidean distance over coordinates selected for that pair. Building on this view, we propose Weighted Euclidean Time Warping (WETW), a learned distance that adapts the comparison of fixed-length embeddings to each pair. A small symmetric network predicts one weight per embedding di- mension and pair without computing an alignment, which halves the error of the best static distance on the same embeddings. The embeddings come from the Weighted Euclidean SEries Encoder (WESEE), whose multi-scale Temporal Feature Extractor keeps the timing information that global pooling removes and raises recall@1 from 0.90 to 0.95, although WETW works with any encoder. We also show that fitting DTW distances is not enough for search, as regression alone misorders the closest neighbours, and that ranking regularization raises recall@1 from 0.79 to 0.95. Our filter-and-verify pipeline, WETW-FV, uses WETW to build a shortlist and exact DTW to check it, so the search is approximate but every returned distance is exact. Over eight datasets, WETW-FV is 11.4x faster than the fastest exact method among the UCR Suite, lower bounds and MESSI-DTW, at a mean recall@10 of 0.93, while the best learned baseline reaches 0.59 at the same speed. On 48K-point series, WETW- FV remains 40x faster than the UCR Suite at a recall@10 of 0.91.

</details>


### [126] [Classification with Large-Step Gradient Descent: Unifying Self-Stabilization and Convergence Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17459)
*Salma Tarmoun,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,Konstantinos Emmanouilidis,Ziqing Xu,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将统一几何视角推广到指数尾损失（logistic、交叉熵）的可分线性分类，用曲率定义的参考子空间替代最小化流形，给出覆盖稳定与不稳定区的显式迭代界，并揭示大步长带来的瞬态隐式偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络常用超过经典稳定性阈值的步长训练GD，此时损失非单调、振荡，却更快收敛且泛化更好。已有理论各有局限：自稳定机制无收敛率；特定损失的收敛率分析不解释稳定机制；统一视角仅适用于有最小化流形的过参数最小二乘，无法覆盖分类情形（损失下确界不可达）。

Method: 针对指数尾损失（含可分线性分类的logistic与交叉熵），引入由曲率定义的参考子空间替代最小化流形，构建耦合动力系统：正交振荡被沿锐度下降方向的平行推进所阻尼。在正则性条件下（对对称数据模型下logistic与多类交叉熵逐一验证），推导迭代量的显式界。

Result: 得到同时覆盖不稳定区与稳定区的迭代界（不仅是损失界），并揭示稳定区中的三阶段阻尼结构；进一步发现大步长诱导的瞬态隐式偏置：GD渐近收敛到最大间隔方向，但大步长会留下持续的正交残差，在某些几何下甚至被放大，影响有限时间内的最大间隔对齐。

Conclusion: 该工作把统一几何理论扩展到无最小化流形的指数尾损失分类场景，提供更精细的迭代级收敛刻画，并指出大步长的瞬态隐式偏置效应，补充了对超阈值步长下GD行为的理论理解。

Abstract: Neural networks are often trained with gradient descent (GD) using step sizes that exceed classical stability thresholds, a regime where standard convergence theory no longer applies. Despite exhibiting non-monotonic loss and oscillatory dynamics, this regime frequently leads to faster convergence and better generalization. Existing theoretical perspectives offer complementary but incomplete insights: one identifies a self-stabilization mechanism that drives GD toward flat regions but does not yield convergence rates; another derives rigorous rates for specific losses but does not explain the underlying stabilization mechanism; and a third unifies these views for overparameterized least squares, but relies on the existence of a minimizer manifold, which is absent in classification settings. In this work, we extend this unified geometric perspective to exponential-tail losses, including logistic and cross-entropy losses in separable linear classification, where the loss infimum is not attained at any finite parameter. We identify a curvature-defined reference subspace that replaces the role of the minimizer manifold, yielding a coupled dynamical system in which orthogonal oscillations are damped by parallel progress along directions of decreasing sharpness. Under regularity conditions, which we verify for logistic and multiclass cross-entropy losses on symmetric data models, we derive explicit bounds for the iterates, not just the loss, covering both the unstable and stable regimes, and revealing a three-phase damping structure in the latter. Our analysis further uncovers a transient implicit bias induced by large step sizes: although GD converges asymptotically to the max-margin direction, large steps can leave persistent orthogonal residuals and, in certain geometries, amplify them, affecting finite-time alignment with the max-margin direction.

</details>


### [127] [Unfinished-Bench: Measuring Rule Violations by Persistent Agents in Realistic Work Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17460)
*Seunghyun Lee,Jaehui Hwang,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Unfinished-Bench基准，揭示AI智能体的持续性（persistence）在缺少用户监督时会显著提高其违反内部文档规则的概率，呼吁评估持续性驱动的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体被赋予长时间自主完成任务的持续性，但常面临内部文档等未明示约束；当完成任务与遵守规则冲突时，需要智能体独自决策。现有指令—规则冲突基准将规则明示于提示中或完全隐藏，且不评估持续性，存在空白。

Method: 构建Unfinished-Bench基准：在无用户参与的自主运行中考察模型持续性，设置嵌入规则的真实工作环境。共97个场景，覆盖7个组织与7类风险类别，每个场景中目标与规则不可兼得。

Result: 持续性使内部文档规则的违反率从32.2%升至43.6%，为用户明示规则违反率（8.5%）的五倍。文档规则违反中98.3%发生在规则被展示之后，在判定样本中89%的智能体曾先写下该规则。

Conclusion: 无用户监督下、置于环境中的规则易被违反，可能引发严重事件，因此持续性驱动的风险亟需纳入评估。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly equipped with persistence toward task completion to run delegated work autonomously for long periods, under many unstated constraints from internal documents and other people's authority. When completion requires violating a rule, the agent must decide alone, yet benchmarks of instruction-rule conflicts state the rule in the prompt or keep it hidden and do not evaluate persistence. We present Unfinished-Bench, a benchmark that gives models persistence in autonomous runs without a user, in realistic work environments with embedded rules. In each of its 97 scenarios across 7 organizations and 7 risk categories, the goal and the rule cannot both hold. We find that persistence raised violations of a rule in an internal document from 32.2% to 43.6%, five times the 8.5% for the same rule stated by the user. Of the document-rule violations, 98.3% occurred after the rule was displayed, and in 89% of a judged sample the agent first wrote down the rule. Violations of rules placed in the environment without user oversight can lead to serious incidents, so persistence-driven risks need to be evaluated. Benchmark documentation: https://francis21c.github.io/unfinished-bench/

</details>


### [128] [Diffusion Tree Sampling for Inference Time Adaptation of Crystal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17462)
*Daniel T. Levy,Vineet Jain,Tara Akhound-Sadegh,Sékou-Oumar Kaba,Siamak Ravanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在推理阶段用 Diffusion Tree Sampling (DTS) 改造晶体扩散生成模型，并贡献两项改进：将已有条件生成模型作为无偏引导提议，以及将 DTS 扩展到多目标采样一次生成整条 Pareto 前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已成为晶体结构的强先验，但要把先验转成候选需同时兼顾多个相互冲突的性质。微调代价高且每个新目标都要训练新模型，可微引导又无法适用于许多真实任务，因此需要一种无需修改模型的推理时适配方法。

Method: 采用 Diffusion Tree Sampling——在去噪轨迹上做蒙特卡洛树搜索的推理时采样方法，并做两点技术扩展：(1) 把已有的性质条件生成模型（如经无分类器引导微调的模型）用作 DTS 内部的引导提议，同时保证不扭曲目标分布；(2) 将 DTS 扩展为多目标采样，使单次搜索产生覆盖整条 Pareto 前沿的样本。

Result: 在从头晶体生成任务上验证：提升不同模型所生成晶体的稳定性；用预训练条件模型生成稀有空间群晶体或高体积模量晶体；沿高介电常数与高带隙的 Pareto 前沿生成材料。各任务中 DTS 推理时适配均在不修改模型的前提下改善了输出。

Conclusion: 推理时适配是替代微调与可微引导的可行方案，DTS 的引导提议与多目标扩展对 AI-for-Science 应用尤为有效，可在不改动原模型的情况下按需提升晶体生成的单目标与多目标性质。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have become strong priors over plausible crystal structures, but turning that prior into promising candidates requires steering toward several conflicting properties at once. Fine-tuning is expensive and produces a new model for every new objective, while differentiable guidance is not applicable to many real-world tasks. We instead adapt these generative models at inference time using Diffusion Tree Sampling (DTS), a Monte Carlo tree search procedure over the denoising trajectory. We make two technical contributions on top of DTS that are particularly relevant for AI-for-science applications. First, we show how to use existing property-conditional generative models (e.g., models fine-tuned for classifier-free guidance) as guided proposals inside DTS, without distorting the target distribution. Second, we extend DTS to multi-objective sampling, so that a single search produces samples spanning an entire Pareto front. We instantiate both adaptations on de novo crystal generation: improving the stability of crystals generated by different models, using pretrained conditional models to generate crystals belonging to rare space groups or with high bulk modulus, and generating crystals along a Pareto front of high dielectric constant and high bandgap materials. Across these tasks, DTS-based inference-time adaptation successfully improves model outputs without modifying the models themselves.

</details>


### [129] [Availability-Aware Historical Feedback Learning for Electric Arc Furnace Temperature and Chemical Composition Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17463)
*Ahmed Shoyeb Raihan,Farzana Islam,Ashish Nimbarte,Imtiaz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"可用性感知的历史反馈学习"框架，用于电弧炉炼钢终点温度和12种元素成分预测；在严格模拟60分钟测量延迟的条件下引入历史结果特征，使多数算法-目标组合的RMSE下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弧炉炼钢在再生钢与低碳钢生产中愈发重要，但炉料、能量输入和操作条件的波动性使终点温度和成分难以准确预测；同时测量数据间歇或存在延迟，可用信息受限。现有建模需在符合真实数据可用性的前提下提升预测精度。

Method: 整合8类工业数据源，构建三档递进的预测特征表示（19个静态、39个时序、47个历史信息特征）；仅在满足假设的60分钟测量发布延迟时才允许使用历史结果。使用19,738个温度炉次和3,341个成分炉次，通过时序前向验证评估12种调参回归算法，并开展历史特征消融、反馈延迟敏感性、轻量自适应残差修正、探索性深度学习对比与目标特征归因分析。

Result: 历史反馈特征表示在156个算法-目标匹配对比中有147个降低RMSE。温度预测中ExtraTrees达RMSE 12.290°C，较仅时序版本降低5.54%。消融与延迟敏感性分析证实近期结果信息及其可用性的重要性。在独立的回溯性后期评估中，自适应残差修正无需重训即可将温度RMSE从12.502降至12.275°C。

Conclusion: 历史结果数据的可用性与序列反馈是电弧炉终点预测的重要考量；在符合测量延迟约束下引入历史信息可一致提升预测表现，且轻量残差修正可低成本进一步改进精度。

Abstract: Electric arc furnace (EAF) steelmaking plays an increasingly important role in recycled and lower-carbon steel production. However, variability in charged materials, energy input, and operating conditions makes accurate prediction of molten steel temperature and final chemical composition challenging. Intermittent or delayed measurements limit the information available for timely prediction. This study develops an availability-aware historical-feedback learning framework for predicting EAF endpoint temperature and the final concentrations of twelve chemical elements. Eight industrial data sources are integrated into three progressively enriched predictor representations comprising 19 static, 39 temporal, and 47 history-informed features. Historical outcomes are incorporated only when eligible under an assumed 60-minute measurement-release delay. Using 19,738 temperature heats and 3,341 chemistry heats, twelve tuned regression algorithms are evaluated through chronological forward validation. The proposed historical-feedback representation reduces root mean squared error (RMSE) in 147 of 156 matched algorithm-target comparisons. For temperature prediction, ExtraTrees achieves an RMSE of 12.290 degrees C, representing a 5.54 percent reduction compared with its temporal-only counterpart. Historical-feature ablation and feedback-delay sensitivity analyses demonstrate the importance of recent outcome information and its availability. In a separate retrospective later-period evaluation, lightweight adaptive residual correction reduces temperature RMSE from 12.502 to 12.275 degrees C without retraining the underlying model. Exploratory deep learning comparisons and target-specific feature-attribution analyses provide additional insights into predictive behavior. The results establish historical outcome availability and sequential feedback as important considerations for EAF endpoint prediction.

</details>


### [130] [Improved Regret via Variance-Aware Policy Optimization in Adversarial MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17469)
*Ofir Schlisselberg,Alon Cohen,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究未知动力学、全信息奖励的episodic对抗MDP，提出从"未知动力学"到"已知动力学"的归约，并借助稳定性匹配bonus（SMB）的方差感知分析，将遗憾界改进到leading term $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H^3SAK})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未知动力学对抗MDP的遗憾界存在两个不可比较的leading term，且与下界 $\widetilde O(H\sqrt{SAK})$ 有差距，希望缩小这一差距并给出更紧的界。

Method: 给出针对downstream-dependent算法（第h步策略仅依赖h+1及以后信息）的从未知到已知动力学的归约；用VOPO策略优化算法实例化该归约，并通过新提出的Stability-Matching Bonus（SMB）实现方差感知分析。

Result: 未知动力学下达到 $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H^3SAK})$，较两个先验leading term分别改进 $\sqrt{S/H}$ 与 $\sqrt{H^3}$ 倍；VOPO在已知动力学全信息下达到 $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H^3K})$，在轨迹bandit反馈下达到 $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H^2SAK})$，均将对应PO最优界改进 $\sqrt H$ 倍。

Conclusion: 剩余 $\sqrt H$ 差距可归约为寻找 $\widetilde O(H\sqrt K)$ 的downstream-dependent已知动力学算法；该结果若实现即得紧的 $\widetilde O(H\sqrt{SAK})$。匹配下界表明全信息速率在某参数区间是紧的。

Abstract: We study episodic adversarial MDPs with unknown dynamics and full-information rewards and achieve regret with leading term $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H^3SAK})$. Here $H$ is the horizon, $S$ the total number of states across decision layers, $A$ the number of actions, and $K$ the number of episodes. This improves the two incomparable prior leading terms by factors $\sqrt{S/H}$ and $\sqrt{H^3}$, leaving a factor $\sqrt H$ to the lower-bound scale. We give a reduction from unknown to known dynamics for \emph{downstream-dependent} algorithms, whose policy at step $h$ is computed only from information at steps $h+1$ and onward. This reduces the remaining leading-order gap to finding a downstream-dependent known-dynamics algorithm with $\widetilde O(H\sqrt K)$ regret, which would yield the tight $\widetilde O(H\sqrt{SAK})$ leading term for unknown dynamics. We instantiate the reduction with VOPO, a policy optimization (PO) algorithm. Its improved guarantees follow from a variance-aware analysis enabled by a novel bonus, which we call the Stability-Matching Bonus (SMB). VOPO achieves $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H^3K})$ regret under known dynamics with full information; its $\sqrt H$ gap to the target $H\sqrt K$ rate is inherited by our unknown-dynamics guarantee. We also show that with known dynamics and trajectory bandit feedback, VOPO achieves $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H^2SAK})$ regret. Both known-dynamics guarantees improve the corresponding state-of-the-art PO bounds by a factor $\sqrt H$. A matching lower bound establishes that the full-information rate is tight in one parameter regime.

</details>


### [131] [Do Mixture-of-Experts Routers Benefit from Ensembling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17472)
*Viktor Veselý,Sebastian Jitaru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将集成思想用于MoE的路由步骤：多个路由器联合专家共同训练提出专家权重，并用性能-多样性分解系统分析，发现更多路由器降低验证损失，但收益被额外计算成本抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成理论把神经网络集成的增益归因于成员准确性与成员间多样性；MoE虽已组合多个专家，但仅由单个路由器决定各输入下的专家权重，因此作者想把集成引入路由环节。

Method: 在2个图像分类与2个表格回归任务上，使用欠参数化网络系统评估"路由器集成"（多个路由器与专家联合训练，各自提出专家权重后合并）。为路由器集成设计了性能-多样性分解分析（作者称此前未针对路由器开展）。

Result: 四项发现：(1) 路由器越多，验证损失越低；(2) 显式训练路由器最大化专家多样性在四个任务中的三个上提升平均性能；(3) 显式最大化路由器多样性对性能无影响；(4) 算术平均合并路由器权重优于几何平均。

Conclusion: 路由环节从集成中获得轻微收益，但该收益被训练额外路由器的计算开销所抵消。

Abstract: Ensembles of neural networks have repeatedly been shown to outperform single networks. Ensemble theory attributes this gain to the accuracy of the individual members and the diversity among them. Mixture-of-experts models already combine many experts, but a single router decides how the experts are weighted for each input. Motivated by ensemble theory, we apply ensembling to the routing step: an ensemble of routers, trained jointly with the experts, proposes the expert weights. We systematically evaluate this approach using underparameterized networks on two image-classification and two tabular-regression tasks. We analyse each experiment with a performance-diversity decomposition of the router ensemble, which, to our knowledge, has not previously been developed for routers. We report four findings. First, more routers lower the validation loss. Second, training routers to explicitly maximize expert diversity improves the mean performance on three of the four tasks. Third, explicitly maximizing router diversity has no effect on performance. Fourth, combining router weights with the arithmetic mean works better than combining them with the geometric mean. Routing benefits mildly from ensembling, but this benefit is offset by the computational cost of training additional routers.

</details>


### [132] [ERGo: Energy-Efficient Hybrid Graph Neural Network Training on Processing-In-Memory Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17478)
*Pratyush Dhingra,Chibuike E. Ugwu,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Partha Pratim Pande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ERGo 提出一种混合训练框架，在 PIM 平台上训练 GNN 时自动从全参数阶段切换到低秩参数高效阶段，显著减少片外访存，提升性能与能效，并延长 NVM-SRAM 异构架构寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 PIM 架构上训练 GNN 仍需大量片外内存访问，开销高昂，损害整体能效；同时 NVM 写耐久性差，频繁权重更新会缩短寿命。

Method: 自动在全参数训练阶段与参数高效阶段间切换，参数高效阶段用 GNN 权重的低秩表示减少可训练参数，从而降低端到端训练的片外访问；并在异构 NVM-SRAM 架构中冻结权重以避免 NVM 更新。

Result: 相比传统全参数 GNN 训练，速度最高提升 3.15x，能效提升 10.5x；在 NVM-SRAM 异构架构上寿命最高提升 33x，且预测精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: ERGo 通过阶段性切换与低秩参数化，在保持精度的同时兼顾 PIM 训练的性能、能效与 NVM 耐久性，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Processing-in-memory (PIM) has been proposed as an alternative computing paradigm for training Deep Neural Networks, including Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). Despite these advancements, training GNN workloads on PIM devices necessitates off-chip memory access. This off-chip access is expensive in terms of performance and energy, thereby impacting the overall energy efficiency of the training process on PIM platforms. In this paper, we propose a novel hybrid training framework called ERGo that automatically switches from a full-parameter phase to a parameter-efficient phase during the training process with negligible loss in predictive accuracy. The parameter-efficient phase employs low-rank representations of GNN weights, effectively reducing the number of trainable parameters. This helps in reducing the off-chip access during the end-to-end training process on PIM architectures, leading to notable improvements in both performance and energy efficiency. ERGo outperforms the conventional full-parameter GNN training on a PIM-based platform by up to 3.15x in speedup and enhances energy efficiency by 10.5x. Furthermore, ERGo offers additional advantages when utilized on heterogeneous architectures incorporating non-volatile memory (NVM) and static random-access memory (SRAM). Training on the heterogeneous NVM-SRAM architecture typically utilizes NVM for the forward-pass and SRAM for the backward-pass. However, NVM cells suffer from low device endurance and repeated write operations due to weight updates can lead to poor lifetime. ERGo improves the lifetime of NVM devices by freezing the weights and preventing weight updates in the parameter-efficient phase. Experimental results demonstrate that ERGo improves the lifetime of heterogeneous NVM-SRAM architecture by up to 33x in comparison to full-parameter implementation.

</details>


### [133] [SURFACE: Preference Distribution Matching on Pareto Fronts via Scalarization Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17484)
*Liuyuan Jiang,Prasanna Sattigeri,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SURFACE方法，通过自适应更新标量化权重，使有限个Pareto解在PF上的分布匹配期望的偏好分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量化权重分布与PF上Pareto解分布之间的映射是非线性且事先未知的，导致权重无法直接实现期望的解分布，产生distortion。

Method: SURFACE从当前标量化解中估计权重网格各区域与PF区域的对应关系（chart estimation），据此重分配权重；支持并行、warm-start、非精确优化及标准单目标求解器；理论上证明分布失配线性收敛到O(N^(-2/(M-1)))的有限分辨率误差下限。

Result: 在多个benchmark上，相比已有baseline，目标质量平衡（target-mass balance）与PF覆盖度均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过自适应权重更新可有效校正权重-解映射的畸变，实现更精确的有限Pareto表示分布控制。

Abstract: Constructing a finite Pareto representation requires allocating a limited number of solutions across the Pareto front (PF). A desired preference distribution specifies this allocation by assigning more solutions to some PF regions and fewer to others. Scalarization generates Pareto solutions by varying objective weights and solving single-objective problems. However, a distribution of scalarization weights generally does not translate into the same distribution of Pareto solutions along the PF, because the weight-to-solution map is nonlinear and unknown in advance.To account for this distortion, we introduce SURFACE (Scalarization Updates for Redistributing Finite Approximations with Chart Estimation}), which adaptively updates scalarization weights to match a desired PF distribution. SURFACE estimates how each region of the weight grid corresponds to an area of the PF from the current scalarized solutions, then redistributes the weights accordingly. The method supports parallel, warm-started, inexact optimization with standard single-objective solvers. Theoretically, we establish that, under the stated regularity conditions, SURFACE achieves linear convergence of the preference distribution mismatch to a finite-resolution error floor of $\mathcal{O}(N^{-2/(M-1)})$, where $N$ is the number of adaptive weights and $M$ is the number of objectives. Empirically, experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate improved target-mass balance and PF coverage compared with established baselines.

</details>


### [134] [Identifying Latent Neural Dynamics with Recognition-Parametrised Gaussian Process Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17487)
*Arielle Rosinski,Qianqian Feng,Lior Fox,Maneesh Sahani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RP-GPdyn 用非参数高斯过程定义状态转移函数，并用识别参数化模型（RPM）隐式建模潜动态与神经活动的映射，从而在无需显式生成参数化的情况下从高维神经活动中恢复出具有行为意义的潜变量与动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "计算通过动力学实现"假说要求建立描述高维神经活动时间演化的动力学模型。现有方法需显式生成式参数化三部分：非线性内在流场与随机性、动力状态到神经活动的非线性映射、以及给定状态下的单神经元响应变异性。任一部分设定错误都会使动力学估计与潜轨迹产生偏差。

Method: 提出 Recognition-Parametrised Gaussian Process dynamics（RP-GPdyn）：(1) 用高斯过程定义的非参数转移函数刻画动力流场，避免对流场的显式参数假设；(2) 借助 Recognition-Parametrised Model（RPM）框架隐式建模动力学与神经活动的关系，绕过观测映射和噪声分布的显式生成建模。整体为"无生成"（generation-free）方法。

Result: 在合成数据与实验数据集上，该方法均能提取出有意义的、与行为相关的潜变量及其动力学。

Conclusion: 通过避免生成式误设问题，无生成的识别参数化 + GP 转移函数途径可有效推断神经群体动力学与行为相关的潜结构，为"计算通过动力学"研究提供更稳健的建模工具。

Abstract: Neural circuits are densely interconnected systems, and many neural computations are thought to be mediated by the dynamical evolution of activity within these recurrent networks, a hypothesis often referred to as "computation through dynamics". Under this view, understanding computation within real neural populations depends on building models of the dynamical rules governing the temporal evolution of recorded high-dimensional neural activity. Most commonly, such models depend on explicit generative parameterisations of (i) the (nonlinear) intrinsic dynamical flow field and stochasticity, (ii) the (nonlinear) mapping from dynamical state to neuronal activity, and (iii) the variability of individual neural responses given that dynamical state. Misspecification of any of these components can bias estimates of dynamics and latent trajectories. In this paper, we circumvent generation-related issues by introducing the Recognition-Parametrised Gaussian Process dynamics (RP-GPdyn) model. RP-GPdyn models the dynamical flow field using a nonparametric Gaussian Process-defined transition function and models the relationship of dynamics to neural activity implicitly using the Recognition-Parametrised Model (RPM) framework. We show that this generation-free approach uncovers meaningful, behaviourally relevant latent variables and dynamics from both synthetic and experimental datasets.

</details>


### [135] [PF-Exec: Large-Scale UAV Relay Load Balancing with an Anchored Potential Direction Decider and a Per-Dimension Gain-Learning Executor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17492)
*Liheng Zeng,Hanzhang Jia,Yiran Li,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出PF-Exec两层框架，用于大规模无人机中继网络的负载均衡：上层给出闭式势方向，下层学习逐维增益并对动作做物理裁剪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模无人机中继的负载均衡需联合决定关联、带宽、功率与悬停位置，维度随无人机数量增长，且每个无人机只能看到局部状态，难以集中求解。

Method: 将决策拆为两层：锚定势方向决策器每时刻输出闭式目标（负载对带宽一阶齐次，得闭式带宽目标；位置目标为服务用户的需���加权质心），仅返回方向；增益学习执行器把局部观测映射为逐维正增益，组合增益与目标并依单步物理上界裁剪。单位增益为默认初值，故训练前退化为纯势策略。系统含两跳接入与毫米波回传、带两层阴影的3GPP UMa-AV信道、OU型社会力人群与四旋翼物理层。

Result: 覆盖全部机队规模、两种人群场景与独立种子；最高压力突发场景下固定步长基线在部分种子上失效，同时损失公平性与平均负载，而PF-Exec公平性接近1；十种对比方法中仅贪心启发式在该场景存活，但其负载方差远高；全局状态陈旧与位置噪声下，两个最大规模场景公平指数仍高；学习执行器与固定步长执行器飞行能耗几乎相同，三个常数在所有设置中固定一次。

Conclusion: PF-Exec在极端压力与信息退化下保持高公平性与竞争性负载，且能耗不劣、超参数通用；两层收敛性（截断增益迭代与扰动跟踪界）在配套分析中证明。

Abstract: Relay load balancing in large-scale UAV networks requires joint decisions on association, bandwidth, power and hovering position, while the dimension grows with the number of UAVs and each UAV sees only its local state. This paper splits the decision into two layers. An anchored potential direction decider emits a closed-form target at every instant and returns its direction only. A gain-learning executor maps a local observation into a positive gain per dimension, composes gain and target, and clips the action against the physical single-step bound; the two layers form PF-Exec. The unit gain is the default start, so the executor reduces to the pure potential policy before training. The load is first-order homogeneous in bandwidth, making the bandwidth target closed form, and the position target is the demand-weighted centroid of the served users. The system uses a two-hop access and millimetre-wave backhaul link with soft association, a 3GPP UMa-AV channel with two shadowing layers, an OU-type social-force crowd and a quadrotor physical layer. Evaluation spans all fleet sizes, two crowd scenarios, independent seeds, the published baselines in their own settings and a degraded-information line. In the highest-pressure burst setting the fixed-step comparison breaks down on part of the seeds, losing fairness and mean load at once, while PF-Exec holds fairness near one. Among the ten comparison methods only the greedy heuristic survives that setting; its load variance stays far higher. Under stale global state and position noise the fairness index stays high at both largest-scale scenarios. Flight energy is nearly identical between the learned and the fixed-step executor, and three constants are fixed once across all settings. The convergence properties of both layers, from the truncated gain iteration to the perturbed tracking bound, are proved in the companion analysis.

</details>


### [136] [Adversarially Batched Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17500)
*Mingda Qiao,Kerui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"对抗性分批在线学习"模型，学习器只能在受限时间步更新策略（时间被划分为批次，批内策略固定），并针对随机赌博机、在线凸优化、专家建议预测、对抗赌博机四类经典问题给出紧致的遗憾界刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中学习器常受限于更新频率（如批处理、部署成本），需要理解"分批更新"对在线学习最优遗憾的代价，既有工作缺乏对更新受限这一约束在多个经典问题下的统一复杂度刻画。

Method: 将时间轴划分为批次、批内策略固定；对随机赌博机基于"相对批次增长"定义通用复杂度度量；对其余三个问题用批次长度序列的2-范数刻画遗憾；上界通过对批次长度不敏感的学习器配合通用自适应倍增技巧实现，下界对批次感知学习器也成立。

Result: 随机赌博机方面给出gap依赖与gap无关的遗憾界，且相对标准gap依赖复杂度的乘法惩罚可随批次难度超线性增长；其余三个问题的最优遗憾被批次长度序列2-范数紧致刻画，达成上下界匹配。

Conclusion: 分批/更新受限使在线学习复杂度出现新现象：随机赌博机的惩罚可超线性放大，而其他问题由批次长度2-范数精确支配，且无需知道批次结构的倍增技巧即可达到最优，揭示了更新约束下的统一理论框架。

Abstract: We introduce adversarially batched online learning, where the learner may update its decision rule only at a restricted set of time steps. Equivalently, the time horizon is partitioned into batches, within which the learner must use a fixed policy. We study this model for four canonical problems: stochastic bandits, online convex optimization, prediction with expert advice, and adversarial bandits. For stochastic bandits, we identify a universal complexity measure based on relative batch growth and establish both gap-dependent and gap-independent regret bounds. We show that the multiplicative penalty relative to the standard gap-dependent complexity can grow superlinearly in batch hardness. For the remaining three, we show that the optimal regret is tightly characterized by the 2-norm of the batch-length sequence. The upper bound is achieved by a batch-oblivious learner who does not know the batch structure, via a generic adaptive doubling trick tailored to this complexity measure. In contrast, the lower bound holds even for batch-aware learners.

</details>


### [137] [A Synthetic Benchmark for Target-Centric Causal Discovery in Industrial Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17556)
*Angelo Ziletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个面向工业过程"目标导向因果发现"的合成基准，生成器为受批式生产启发的两层结构因果模型，模块化可调难度，并评测八类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程数据中已知真实因果的样本稀少，现有基准中"识别目标原因"的难度要么由数据固定、要么对整张图统一设定，无法逐条件调节和归因分析。

Method: 两层结构因果模型生成器，由建模控制回路、延迟效应等单个过程现象的组件拼接而成；图围绕目标构建，在无传感器误差时通过条件化或时间顺序可区分非原因与直接原因；各组件独立设置以逐项改变难度。评测八种方法（从关联度量到效应估计），并按所选候选变量角色做误差分析。

Result: 所有方法在困难条件叠加时性能均下降，且各自退化方式与其假设相符。

Conclusion: 该基准支持可控、可归因的目标导向因果发现评测，揭示了各方法假设与失效模式的对应关系，为工业过程因果发现方法的诊断与选择提供工具。

Abstract: Causal discovery promises to find which recorded variables drive a process target such as product quality, yield or energy use. Process data with known causes are rare, and in existing benchmarks the difficulty of identifying the causes of a target is either fixed by the recorded data or set for the whole graph. We present a synthetic benchmark for target-centric causal discovery in industrial processes. Its generator is a two-level structural causal model inspired by batch production, assembled from components that each model one phenomenon of process data, such as a control loop or a delayed effect. The graph is built around the target: without sensor error, every non-cause can be distinguished from the direct causes by conditioning on them or by time order. Each component is set independently, so the difficulty can be varied one condition at a time. We evaluate eight methods, from association measures to effect estimation, with an error analysis by the role of the selected candidate; all degrade as the difficulties combine, each in a way consistent with its assumptions.

</details>


### [138] [Generative Design with Real-time Physics Explainability: Towards Product Definition in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17560)
*M. de Campos,C. Drygala,E. Ross,A. Bruce,M. Bohon,H. Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个统一生成式设计框架：用可逆神经网络(INN)由客户需求反推设计参数，结合生成式物理仿真代理提供可解释性，并用不确定性量化抵御物理上不可行的请求，最后用经典工具验证；在两个交互式环境(基于仿真数据的翼型设计、基于实测数据的燃烧室设计空间探索)中演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前有两条生成式AI在机械工程中的独立路线：INN求解逆设计问题(从性能标签推断设计参数)，以及生成式代理模型实现实时物理仿真。作者认为两条路线应合并——一个从客户需求角度控制设计过程，一个提供"为何该设计满足需求"的可解释性。此外，生成式设计易产生幻觉，需要不确定性量化来拒绝物理上不可行的请求，且必须以经典工具验证。

Method: 构建统一框架：以可逆神经网络(INN)进行由性能到设计参数的逆设计；以生成式系统物理仿真作为可解释性层；引入不确定性量化以过滤物理不可行请求；并用经典(传统)工具进行验证。据此开发两个交互式环境：基于仿真数据的翼型设计与基于实测数据的燃烧室设计空间探索。

Result: 在两个演示环境中验证了该生成式设计范式的可用性：翼型设计(仿真数据驱动)与燃烧室设计空间探索(实测数据驱动)均实现交互式工作流，体现逆设计、物理可解释性与不确定性量化的结合。

Conclusion: 将INN逆设计与生成式物理仿真代理统一为一个可解释、可验证、带不确定性量化的生成式设计范式，可连接客户需求与工程设计，但是否适用于更广泛工程问题仍需在更多案例中检验。摘要未给出定量性能指标。

Abstract: In this article, we explore opportunities from using generative AI in mechanical engineering. On the one hand, generative methods based on invertible neural networks have been recently proposed for the solution of inverse design problems, allowing to infer design parameters from performance labels. On the other hand, generative AI models have shown that they can act as real time capable surrogates for physics based simulations. Here we suggest that these two streams should be combined in a unified framework, where INN are used to control the design process from the customer requirements perspective and the generative simulation of the systems physics adds a layer of explainability, why the proposed design fulfils the requirements. As generative design is prone to hallucinations, we propose uncertainty quantification to fend off physically infeasible requests. Finally, validation with classical tools is a requirement for generative design process. To demonstrate the capabilities of our novel generative design paradigm, we develop two interactive environments, one for airfoil design based on simulator data and one for the combustor design space exploration based on measured data.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond a Single Sign: BiTE for Branch-Preserving Binary Threshold Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17576)
*Siddharth Gupta,Mikail Yayla,Akash Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BiTE（分支保留二值阈值扩展），用K个有序阈值对每个特征方向产生多个二值响应并分别送入后续算子，从而提升二值神经网络表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络（BNN）虽降低存储与计算开销，但单符号激活把每个预激活方向只与一个阈值比较并归为两值，丢弃了同号区间内的变化，限制了表示能力。

Method: 对每个特征方向使用K个有序阈值进行评估，将得到的二值响应作为独立输入分别传给后续可学习算子，使每个阈值响应学习独立变换；可区分最多K+1个有序激活状态，并可在"独立构造的特征方向数"与"每方向的响应数"间可控权衡。另外设置均匀坍缩对照实验。

Result: 在BinaryViT上，CIFAR-10平均末轮Top-1从74.60±0.30%升至75.83±0.15%，CIFAR-100从42.80±0.46%升至44.07±0.32%，可训练参数量几乎不变；均匀坍缩对照为75.33±0.15%，说明分别保留阈值响应更有价值。Binary VGG-16上观察到更好的参数—精度权衡，某配置比常规宽度缩放精度高0.64个百分点且参数少21.8%。

Conclusion: 保留分支的阈值扩展是改善二值表示及参数—精度权衡的有效方法。

Abstract: Binary neural networks (BNNs) reduce model storage and replace many arithmetic operations with efficient bitwise computations, but the single-sign activation limits their representational capacity. Each preactivation direction is compared with one threshold and reduced to one of two values, discarding variation within each sign region. We propose \textbf{BiTE} (Branch-Preserving Binary Threshold Expansion), which evaluates each feature direction against $K$ ordered thresholds and retains the resulting binary responses as separate inputs to the following learned operator. BiTE can distinguish up to $K+1$ ordered activation states while allowing the following operator to learn a separate transformation for each threshold response. It also enables a controllable trade-off between the number of independently constructed feature directions and the number of ordered responses extracted from each direction. On BinaryViT, BiTE improves mean final-epoch Top-1 accuracy from $74.60\pm0.30\%$ to $75.83\pm0.15\%$ on CIFAR-10 and from $42.80\pm0.46\%$ to $44.07\pm0.32\%$ on CIFAR-100, with nearly unchanged trainable parameter counts. A matched uniform-collapse control reaches $75.33\pm0.15\%$ on CIFAR-10, supporting the value of retaining the threshold responses separately relative to the evaluated scalar collapse. Binary VGG-16 experiments further show a favorable observed parameter--accuracy trade-off, including a configuration that achieves $0.64$ percentage points higher mean accuracy with $21.8\%$ fewer parameters than conventional width scaling. Overall, these results support branch-preserving threshold expansion as an effective way to improve binary representations and the observed parameter--accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [140] [Fair and Efficient Distributed EV Charging Coordination Using Threshold Cryptography and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17577)
*Md. Mainul Islam,Muhammad Ismail,Hasan Kurban,Abdulrahman Takiddin,Erchin Serpedin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示分布式EV充电ADMM协调在拜占庭参与者攻击下仍能收敛因而无法被察觉，并据此提出"门限签名广播+上传重算"的检测框架与基于认证ML价格预测的局部恢复机制，以线性通信成本实现零误报的全偏差检测，并证明恢复过程仍收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADMM分布式充电协调默认所有参与者诚实，但现实中偏置运营商、中间人攻击者可向不同EV发送不一致信号，被攻陷EV也可偏离更新规则，这一假设不成立且缺乏对应防护。

Method: 先证明恶意（拜占庭）参与者攻击不会阻止收敛、因而不被检测，并给出闭式的最具破坏性攻击；再提出门限签名广播与上传重算相结合的检测框架；受影响EV利用经认证、感知车队的机器学习价格预测进行局部恢复。

Result: 仿真（真实负荷、价格与充电数据）显示：偏置运营商使劣势EV年成本升132.7%、优势EV降49.3%而不改变总负荷，防御后成本变化限制在2.6%内；外部MITM使受害者成本升57.6%、峰值负荷升5.8%、电网成本升15.8%，防御后分别控制在1.1%、0.2%、0.3%内；70%的EV被攻击时恢复在至少97.8%的运行中收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作证明ADMM协调存在被拜占庭攻击隐蔽操纵的漏洞，所提检测与恢复框架在近零误报与线性通信开销下有效限制经济与电网影响并保持收敛，提升了分布式充电协调的安全性。

Abstract: Distributed coordination of electric vehicle (EV) charging with the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) keeps EV constraints private but assumes that every participant follows the algorithm. This assumption fails in practice, where a biased operator or a man-in-the-middle (MITM) attacker can send inconsistent signals to different EVs, and compromised EVs can deviate from their prescribed updates. We first prove that attacks by such arbitrarily malicious, i.e., Byzantine, participants do not prevent convergence and therefore remain undetected, and derive the most damaging attack in closed form. We then propose a framework that combines a threshold-signed broadcast with upload recomputation, which detects every deviation without false alarms at linear instead of quadratic communication cost. To keep the optimization running, each affected EV recovers locally with a certified, fleet-aware machine learning price forecast, and we prove that the optimization remains convergent with a recovery cost bounded by the shape of the forecast error. Simulation results with real-world load, price, and charging data show that a biased operator raises the annual cost of disadvantaged EVs by 132.7% and lowers that of advantaged EVs by 49.3% without changing the aggregate load, whereas the defense limits all cost changes to 2.6%. An external MITM attacker raises the cost of its victims by 57.6%, the peak load by 5.8%, and the grid cost by 15.8%, whereas the defense keeps them within 1.1%, 0.2%, and 0.3% of benign operation. With up to 70% of the EVs attacked, the recovery converges in at least 97.8% of the runs.

</details>


### [141] [LightTAR: Light and Fast Target-Aware Representations for Multimodal Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17600)
*Eilam Shapira,Alan Arazi,Shoham Grunblat,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LightTAR：用监督式PLS从编码器末层与中间层联合提取少量目标相关特征，替代/补充PCA降维，简单廉价地提升多模态表格学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态表格中文本与图像常被编码器表示为嵌入向量，再用PCA降维，但PCA只保留输入方差，可能丢弃对预测目标有用的信息。

Method: LightTAR在标准PCA表示之外，用正则化偏最小二乘（PLS）从编码器的最后一层和一个中间层联合提取少量目标感知特征，使降维与预测任务对齐。

Result: 在21种表格模型（树集成、神经网络、表格基础模型）上测试，LightTAR提升了所有模型的平均预测性能，跨模型平均后在几乎每个MulTaBench数据集上都有改进，且相比PCA总运行时间仅小幅增加。

Conclusion: LightTAR可直接嵌入现有编码流程，无需修改编码器或学习器，即可提升表格学习器的预测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal tabular learning involves classification and regression on tables combining structured numerical and categorical features with unstructured text and images. These unstructured fields are commonly represented by pretrained encoder embeddings reduced with principal component analysis (PCA). This reduction prioritizes input variance but may overlook information relevant to predicting the target. We introduce LightTAR, a simple, inexpensive method that adapts dimensionality reduction to the prediction task.
