LightTAR augments the standard PCA representation with a few target-aware features extracted jointly from the final and an intermediate encoder layer using regularized partial least squares (PLS). We evaluate LightTAR with 21 tabular models, including tree ensembles, neural networks, and tabular foundation models. LightTAR improves average predictive performance for all models and, averaged across models, improves prediction on almost every MulTaBench dataset. These gains come with a small increase in total runtime relative to PCA representations. LightTAR integrates directly into existing encoding pipelines, improving tabular learners' predictive performance without modifying the encoder or the learner.

</details>


### [142] [Offline KL-Regularized Reinforcement Learning with Stochastic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17603)
*Andrew Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究KL正则化强化学习（RL微调语言模型的目标）在有限折扣MDP、未知随机转移下的离线样本复杂度，给出下界，并提出方差感知惩罚的悲观soft value iteration达到该率，同时分析参考策略自采样数据的覆盖性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL正则化RL是语言模型RL微调的目标，其离线数据常由参考策略自身采集。除bandit和确定性转移外，人们对其需要多少离线数据知之甚少，因此需要建立随机转移MDP下的样本复杂度理论。

Method: 研究有限折扣MDP（K个状态-动作对，有效时域H，未知随机转移）。对以集中度C≥4覆盖正则最优的数据，证明Ω(KCH³/(ε max{ε,β}))的估计下界；在固定设计下采用带方差感知惩罚的悲观soft value iteration进行估计；并分析参考策略采样数据的覆盖律（幂律指数随H/β增长），与plug-in估计器对比。

Result: 下界：ε小时任一估计器在某些实例上需要Ω(KCH³/(ε max{ε,β}))样本。上界：β≥1.4H时悲观soft value iteration达到该率（对数因子内，高概率）。参考数据覆盖幂律指数随H/β增长；同一温度区间内该幂律使估计器仅需多项式1/ε样本、无需覆盖假设；β≥H时该幂可额外将样本量放大1/ε的多项式因子。plug-in估计器在小温度、多稀有动作时可失败，而悲观估计器不会。

Conclusion: KL正则化离线RL的样本复杂度由参数β、H、K、C刻画；悲观估计器在参考数据上具鲁棒性，plug-in估计器则可能失败。

Abstract: KL-regularized reinforcement learning is the objective behind RL fine-tuning of language models, and its data are often collected by the reference policy itself. However, beyond bandits and deterministic transitions, little is known about how much offline data it needs. In this work, we study finite discounted Markov decision processes with unknown stochastic transitions, $K$ state-action pairs and effective horizon $H$. For data that cover the regularized optimum with concentrability $C\geq 4$, every estimator needs $Ω(KCH^3/(ε\max\{ε,β\}))$ samples on some instances when $ε$ is small, where $β$ is the temperature. Under a fixed design, pessimistic soft value iteration with a variance-aware penalty attains this rate with high probability, up to logarithmic factors, when $β\geq 1.4H$. When the data come from the reference policy itself, we show that it covers the optimum up to a power law whose exponent grows as $H/β$; in the same temperature range, this power law alone gives the estimator a sample complexity polynomial in $1/ε$, with no coverage assumption. For $β\geq H$ the power can also increase the sample size needed by a factor polynomial in $1/ε$. Finally, the plug-in estimator, which is minimax optimal with a generative model, can fail on reference data at small temperatures when many actions are rarely seen, while the pessimistic estimator does not.

</details>


### [143] [Neural Simulation-Based Inference for Computational Cognitive Models: A Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17608)
*Ti-Fen Pan,Yi Cheng,Anne GE Collins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该教程介绍将神经模拟推断（neural SBI）应用于认知建模的完整工作流程，帮助研究者绕过难以计算的似然函数，从而扩大可研究的理论范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知建模通常依赖似然方法（MLE、分层贝叶斯），但许多合理认知模型的似然难以计算，导致这些方法失效，极大限制了可探索的理论空间。neural SBI 虽已在多领域成功应用，但在认知建模中应用不足，主要缺乏可及且针对领域的指导。

Method: 提出一套清晰的 neural SBI 应用流程：通过模型模拟生成数据集，训练深度神经网络学习观测数据与认知模型之间的关系，从而在跳过似然计算的前提下完成参数估计、模型比较和基于模型的神经分析。

Result: 提供实践用例以及降低计算与模拟负担的具体建议，且不受经验数据规模限制，使标准建模的各项应用得以保留。

Conclusion: 该教程降低 neural SBI 的技术门槛，赋能认知科学家探索更丰富的理论空间，推动科学发现。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling is an increasingly essential methodology to elucidate the mechanisms underlying human and animal cognition. By fitting models to empirical data, researchers can perform parameter estimation, model comparison, and model-based neural analyses to assess competing theories, quantify individual differences, and investigate neural representations. Typically, fitting these models relies on likelihood-dependent methods (e.g., Maximum likelihood estimation or hierarchical Bayesian methods). However, for many reasonable cognitive models, computing the likelihood is computationally intractable, in which case these methods become inadequate. This substantially limits the range of theories that researchers can investigate. Simulation-based inference with neural networks (neural SBI) has emerged as a powerful toolkit to overcome this hurdle. By training deep neural networks on datasets generated through model simulations, neural SBI learns the relation between observable data and cognitive models. This approach enables all the applications of standard modeling while bypassing the likelihood computation, without constraints on empirical data size. Despite its successful adoption across diverse scientific fields, neural SBI remains underutilized in cognitive modeling, partly due to a lack of accessible, domain-tailored guidance. This tutorial addresses this gap by providing a clear workflow for applying neural SBI to cognitive models. We also provide practical use cases and recommendations for reducing the computational and simulation burden, facilitating efficient implementation in real-world research settings. By lowering the technical barriers to neural SBI, this tutorial aims to empower cognitive scientists to investigate richer theory spaces and ultimately propel scientific discovery.

</details>


### [144] [Joint World Action Models through Differentiable Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17626)
*Mintae Kim,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WATO（世界动作轨迹优化）通过在训练阶段将动作与未来状态联合纳入动力学约束的轨迹优化问题，并把任务损失经优化轨迹回传以训练生成器，使生成的联合世界-动作轨迹既任务有效又动力学一致；部署时只生成候选轨迹并用世界模型排序，无需迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合世界动作模型（WAM）同时生成未来动作与状态，但单纯的轨迹模仿不能保证轨迹对任务有效、也不能保证其符合环境动力学。

Method: 将生成的动作与未来状态同时作为参考，构建动力学约束的轨迹优化问题；仅训练时使用轨迹优化，并对优化后的轨迹求导，把任务损失传回生成器；部署时直接生成候选轨迹并用世界模型排序，不做迭代轨迹优化。

Result: 在7个视觉控制环境中，平均成功率由模仿式联合WAM的79.9%提升至91.7%，优于强模型基方法、目标条件强化学习及生成式控制基线；相比CEM平均快11.5倍，部署时世界模型评估次数减少600.5倍。

Conclusion: WATO通过训练时的可微轨迹优化有效提升了联合世界动作模型的轨迹质量与效率，实现了任务感知、动力学一致且部署高效的生成式控制方法。

Abstract: Joint world action models (WAMs) generate future actions and states together, modeling world-action trajectories rather than actions alone. However, trajectory imitation does not ensure that these trajectories are task-effective or consistent with dynamics. We present \textbf{world action trajectory optimization (WATO)}, which optimizes generated actions and future states jointly for control. WATO uses both as references in a dynamics-constrained trajectory optimization problem and differentiates the task loss through the optimized trajectory to train the generator. This makes generated trajectories task-aware and dynamics-consistent while using trajectory optimization only during training. At deployment, WATO directly generates candidate trajectories and ranks them with a world model, without iterative trajectory optimization. Across $7$ visual control environments, WATO improves average success from $79.9\%$ for the imitation joint WAM to $91.7\%$, outperforming strong model-based, goal-conditioned RL, and generative control baselines. Compared with cross-entropy method (CEM), WATO is on average $11.5\times$ faster and uses $600.5\times$ fewer world model evaluations at deployment.

</details>


### [145] [A mesh-free deep energy method for phase-field modeling of thermally driven brittle fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17630)
*Han Zhang,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari,Babak Shahbodagh,Mohammad Vahab,Cosmin Anitescu,Timon Rabczuk,Elena Atroshchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于多分辨率神经表征的无网格深度能量法，模拟瞬态热载荷下裂纹的自发萌生、间距选择、选择性扩展与止裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场热驱动断裂需解析路径未知的局部裂纹带和厚度随时间演化的热边界层，而现有基于神经能量的热断裂计算都需预先布置缺陷。

Method: 用两个各带多分辨率编码的网络分别表征位移与相场、瞬态温度；在每次迭代重抽样的积分点上估计力学能与后向欧拉热方程增量泛函；将不可逆性限制于裂纹集，跨载荷步携带优化器状态并在每步内衰减学习率以抑制伪损伤。

Result: 两个缺口板起裂峰值载荷与独立有限元参考吻合；淬火玻璃带中裂纹路径由直线变为振荡再分叉，振荡阈值接近线性稳定性预测；陶瓷板形成与实验同型裂纹阵列且间距接近已发表基准；板与淬火圆环上裂纹阵列比同材料场有限元结果更密。

Conclusion: 无需预设裂纹路径、萌生位置或裂纹自适应网格即可自发预测热驱动断裂的萌生、间距与路径演化，验证了该无网格深度能量法的有效性。

Abstract: Phase-field simulation of thermally driven fracture requires resolving localized crack bands whose paths are unknown in advance and thermal boundary layers whose thickness evolves during the transient. Neural energy-based computations of thermal fracture have so far started from defects placed in advance. This paper proposes a mesh-free deep energy method based on multiresolution neural representations that predicts spontaneous crack nucleation, crack spacing, and selective crack propagation and arrest under transient thermal loading. Two networks, each with its own multiresolution encoding, represent the displacement and phase fields and the transient temperature, and the mechanical energy and the incremental functional of the backward Euler heat equation are estimated on integration points redrawn at every iteration. Restricting irreversibility to the crack set, carrying the optimizer state across load steps and decaying the learning rates within each step help prevent spurious damage accumulation. On two notched plates the initiation peak loads agree closely with an independent finite element reference. On a quenched glass strip under an analytical temperature field the crack path changes from straight to oscillatory and then to branched, with an oscillation threshold close to the linear stability prediction. Under a prescribed boundary temperature a quenched ceramic slab forms a crack array of the type observed in experiment, with a spacing close to that of a published benchmark. On the slab and a quenched annulus the arrays are denser than those of a finite element computation on the same material field. No crack paths, nucleation sites or crack-adapted meshes are prescribed.

</details>


### [146] [Classifier-Guided Latent Diffusion for Small-Molecule Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17650)
*Ali Forooghi,Luis Rueda,Alioune Ngom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于冻结ChemBERTa潜空间的潜扩散管线，用于性质引导的小分子生成：用Transformer解码器从潜表示重建SELFIES，并在潜空间训练无条件扩散模型，辅以可微性质预测器引导采样，实现向血脑屏障通透性与BACE1抑制两个终点定向生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能学习复杂化学分布并支持条件/引导生成，是有前景的分子设计工具；但需验证在预训练分子嵌入的潜空间上进行扩散并结合可微性质引导，是否能生成有效、新颖且可定向调控性质的分子。

Method: 1) 用冻结的ChemBERTa编码器编码规范化SMILES，得到分子潜嵌入；2) 训练Transformer解码器从潜嵌入重建SELFIES；3) 在ChemBERTa潜空间训练无条件去噪扩散模型；4) 用可微潜性质预测器引导扩散采样以定向生成；5) 生成SELFIES转SMILES，经RDKit校验，并评估有效性、唯一性、新颖性与预测性质分数。研究两个二分类药物相关终点：血脑屏障通透性与人β-分泌酶1(BACE1)抑制。

Result: 结果表明，在预训练分子嵌入上做潜扩散可生成化学有效且新颖的分子，同时能够向目标分子性质进行可控引导（显著提升目标性质预测分数）。

Conclusion: 潜扩散结合预训练分子表示与可微性质引导是小分子性质定向生成的有效范式；但摘要未给出量化指标与基线对比，其优越性仍需完整实验验证。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as a promising class of generative models for molecular design because of their ability to learn complex chemical distributions and support conditional or guided generation. In this work, we investigate a latent diffusion pipeline for property-guided small-molecule generation. The proposed framework represents canonicalized SMILES strings using a frozen ChemBERTa encoder, trains a Transformer-based decoder to reconstruct SELFIES strings from molecular latent embeddings, and learns an unconditional denoising diffusion model in the ChemBERTa latent space. To enable property-directed generation, a differentiable latent property predictor is used to guide the diffusion sampling process. Generated SELFIES strings are converted to SMILES, validated using RDKit, and evaluated using molecular validity, uniqueness, novelty, and predicted property scores. We study guidance toward two drug-relevant binary endpoints: blood--brain barrier permeability and inhibition of human $β$-secretase 1. The results suggest that latent diffusion over pretrained molecular embeddings can generate chemically valid and novel molecules while enabling controllable steering toward desired molecular properties.

</details>


### [147] [Pattern-Based Matrix Analysis Under Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17674)
*Andrei Ermakov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出「模式距离」度量：在最优置换行列后，矩阵与给定几何模式（带状、块对角、箭头形等）的接近程度，统一刻画潜在排序、社区发现、带宽最小化等结构问题；并给出交替线性分配算法及「模式+低秩」分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩近似只刻画「线性低维简单性」，而许多矩阵的简单性体现在置换后质量集中于某种几何结构。作者希望用统一框架补充这一互补的「结构简单性」概念。

Method: 定义模式距离（最优行列置换下到指定模式的偏离量）；问题一般NP难，故设计基于线性分配（Linear Assignment）子问题的交替优化算法；进一步提出模式加低秩分解：置换步骤即在残差上做模式拟合。

Result: 合成恢复任务、真实相关矩阵与图邻接矩阵、Transformer注意力头上验证：单一通用算法可恢复性质不同的多种矩阵结构；在各模式族中收益在匹配参数预算下依然存在，且在密度匹配的随机掩码对照下大幅减弱。

Conclusion: 置换结构简单性是低秩之外的重要概念，可用统一可计算的模式距离框架处理，并能扩展到低秩残差组合；该框架对多种矩阵族具有通用性。

Abstract: Low-rank approximation captures one important notion of matrix simplicity: a matrix is simple if it is well described by a low-dimensional linear model. This paper studies a complementary notion. We consider matrices that become structurally simple only after permuting rows and columns, so that their mass concentrates in a prescribed geometric pattern such as a band, block-diagonal, arrowhead, or related form. We introduce a pattern distance that measures how close a matrix is to a given pattern after optimal reordering, giving a common framework for structural problems such as latent ordering, community detection, and bandwidth minimization. The problem is NP-hard in general; we derive an alternating algorithm based on Linear Assignment subproblems. We further introduce a pattern-plus-low-rank decomposition that combines permutation-structured fitting with a low-rank residual, whose permutation step is pattern fitting on the residual; its gains across pattern families persist at a matched parameter budget and are substantially reduced under a density-matched random-mask control. We evaluate the framework on synthetic recovery tasks, real correlation and graph adjacency matrices, and transformer attention heads, showing that a single universal algorithm recovers qualitatively different forms of matrix structure.

</details>


### [148] [Representational Geometry as a Diagnostic for Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17684)
*Brandon Ismalej,Alex Cloninger,Gal Mishne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究对比三种对抗训练下的MLP与普通网络，发现对抗训练改变网络内部表征几何，且鲁棒准确率与表征稳定性对同一防御的排序可能相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒性通常仅用"攻击下的准确率"衡量，忽略了不同对抗训练目标如何影响网络潜表征的组织方式，因此需要从表征层面审视鲁棒性。

Method: 在MNIST上训练MLP，采用三种对抗训练方案与一个vanilla基线；用M-PHATE将跨训练epoch的样本嵌入以观察数据几何演化；在倒数第二层度量干净与扰动点间位移、MMD分布距离及最近邻恢复率。

Result: 对抗训练导致网络内部数据表征组织的结构性变化；鲁棒准确率与表征稳定性对相同防御方法给出相反的排序。

Conclusion: 建议将潜表征及其训练轨迹视为诊断性产物，跨网络集合重新评估模型鲁棒性的审计方式。

Abstract: Adversarial training is a common technique for making neural networks more robust to small, intentionally crafted input perturbations, and robustness is commonly measured through accuracy under attack. By exploring inter-network representations across different adversarial training objectives, we can analyze whether multiple networks organize their latent representations similarly. We study Multilayer Perceptrons trained on MNIST under three adversarial training regimes and compare the resulting population training dynamics and geometry to those of a vanilla network. We use M-PHATE to embed data samples across training epochs and examine how the geometry of the data changes during training under adversarial objectives. On penultimate-layer representations we measure displacement between clean and perturbed points, distributional distance through maximum mean discrepancy, and nearest-neighbor recovery. Our visualizations and quantitative analyses show that adversarial training leads to structural changes in how networks organize internal data representations, and robust accuracy and representational stability can rank the same defenses in opposite ways. These findings support a discussion of re-evaluating how practitioners approach the auditing of model robustness by treating latent representations and their training trajectories as diagnostic artifacts across sets of networks.

</details>


### [149] [Logically Consistent Accurate Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17735)
*Kate Yuan,Rohan Alur,Manish Raghavan,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用推理过程协调LLM预测系统的不一致性，微调监督语言模型联合修正不一致并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体预测系统已可与人类专家媲美，但存在关键缺陷：无法产生逻辑一致的预测。相关事件的预测常违反概率公理，导致这些预测无法同时正确，损害决策价值。而朴素方法（如投影到一致集）会牺牲准确性。

Method: 核心洞察是克服一致性与准确性权衡的关键在于支撑每个预测的推理。作者微调一个监督语言模型，利用该推理来联合协调不一致性，同时提升相关预测的准确度。

Result: 在ForecastBench上，该方法使检索增强的GPT-5.1预测器的Brier分数提升9.3%，同时将结构性不一致指标降低82-90%。通过系列基线与消融实验，未发现其他方法能同时兼顾一致性与准确性提升。

Conclusion: 利用推理进行联合协调可突破一致性与准确性的权衡，同时改善二者，验证了推理作为一致性监督信号的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic forecasting systems based on large language models (LLMs) are now competitive with expert human forecasters, but these systems still suffer from a critical limitation: the inability to produce forecasts that are logically consistent. For example, forecasts for related events often fail to satisfy the axioms of probability, which in turn implies that related forecasts cannot simultaneously be correct and undermines their utility for decision makers. Enforcing consistency is not trivial -- naive approaches (e.g., projection onto the set of consistent forecasts) can degrade accuracy. We argue that the key to overcoming the apparent tradeoff between consistency and accuracy lies in the reasoning which supports each forecast. We finetune a supervisory language model that leverages this reasoning to jointly reconcile inconsistencies while improving the accuracy of related forecasts. On ForecastBench, our method improves the Brier score of a retrieval-augmented GPT-5.1 forecaster by 9.3% while reducing measures of structural inconsistency by 82-90%. We interrogate our method via a comprehensive series of baselines and ablations, and find that no alternative method matches our gains in consistency and accuracy simultaneously.

</details>


### [150] [Complex Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17746)
*Dong-Sig Han,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出复值扩散桥框架——共形最优传输（COT）与复薛定谔桥（CSB），并给出可扩展的I²CSB及用于量子电动力学模拟的物理知情CSB算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散桥虽能实现任意数据分布间变换，但其优化空间巨大且涉及高阶结构，难以处理受缩放与旋转规律约束的复向量空间问题（如哈密顿结构、几何与耗散动力学）。

Method: 1）引入共形最优传输（COT），在几何提升下为具几何与耗散动力学的物理系统构建复值传输框架；2）据此提出复薛定谔桥（CSB）类动态扩散桥，其算法可利用共形几何先验；3）为可扩展性提出图像到图像CSB（I²CSB），采用解析螺旋后验的可解扩散桥技术；4）在量子电动力学仿真中实现物理知情CSB算法，模拟量子逻辑门行为。

Result: 大量实验结果验证了所提理论，并显示出复值扩散相较于现有方法的优势；I²CSB在图像任务上具备可扩展性与可解性，物理知情CSB能复现量子逻辑门逻辑行为。

Conclusion: 复值扩散桥是处理具几何/耗散结构物理系统的有效范式，共形几何先验与解析后验设计兼顾理论严谨性与可扩展性，为物理与量子计算场景的扩散建模开辟新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion bridges have emerged as versatile frameworks for facilitating transformations between arbitrary data distributions. However, these approaches are hindered by an immense optimization space and its underlying high-order structures. This paper explores a variant of diffusion bridges for solving real-world problems in complex vector spaces governed by scaling and rotation laws, such as Hamiltonian structures. To address this, we introduce conformal optimal transport (COT), a complex-valued transportation framework designed for physical systems with geometric and dissipative dynamics under a geometric lifting. Building on this, we propose a new class of dynamical diffusion bridges, called complex Schrödinger bridges (CSBs), whose derived algorithms can handle diffusion-based tasks using a conformal geometric prior. For scalability, we propose image-to-image CSB (I$^2$CSB), a tractable diffusion-bridge technique with an analytic spiral posterior. Finally, we implement a physics-informed CSB algorithm within simulations of quantum electrodynamics, mimicking the logical behavior of quantum logic gates. Our extensive results validate the underlying theory while highlighting advantages of complex-valued diffusion.

</details>


### [151] [Antidistillation is Cheap: Lightweight Distillation-Resistance via Stylistic Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17772)
*Anh Tu Ngo,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级防御：在恶意查询时向教师模型激活中动态注入风格引导向量，破坏序列级知识蒸馏，同时不增加明显开销并保持文本自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型以黑盒API形式提供时，竞争者可通过序列级蒸馏以极低成本克隆专有能力；现有防御依赖代理模型操纵下一词采样，计算开销大且增加实时推理延迟。

Method: 在推理阶段检测恶意查询后，将风格引导向量（style-steering vectors）动态注入教师模型激活中；主机可自定义目标防御风格并动态混合以最大化风格方差；干预仅作用于恶意查询，正常用户仍获得原始输出。

Result: 实验表明该框架能有效破坏学生学习，取得显著的抗蒸馏效果，同时开销可忽略并保持文本自然性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种低成本、可定制的黑盒API抗蒸馏防御方案，在保持语义效用与流畅度的同时误导 adversaries，平衡了商业保护与正常服务质量。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is a highly effective paradigm for transferring capabilities from large language models (LLMs) to smaller, more specialized student models. In commercial deployment where frontier models are restricted behind black-box APIs, sequence-level distillation remains a potent vector for competitors to clone proprietary capabilities at a fraction of the development cost. Existing defenses mostly rely on surrogate models to manipulate next-token sampling, which increases computational overhead and real-time inference latency. In this work, we propose a lightweight defense that dynamically injects style-steering vectors into the teacher model's activations upon malicious queries. This framework offers exceptional flexibility, allowing hosts to customize target defensive styles and dynamically blend them to maximize stylistic variance. Crucially, this inference-time intervention preserves semantic utility and fluency during malicious queries, misleading adversaries into believing that they are extracting pristine teacher outputs, while standard consumers continue to receive original, unmodified responses. Our experiments demonstrate that this framework successfully disrupts student learning, achieving substantial anti-distillation performance while incurring negligible overhead and preserving text naturalness. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/distill-resist-38C6/.

</details>


### [152] [Direction or Magnitude? Learning Scalars over Task-Calibrated Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17784)
*Ziqing Wen,Xiaohui Song,Jiahuan Wang,Ping Luo,Junlin Zeng,Dongsheng Li,Kele Xu,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALAR: 用固定输入基 + 随机输出字典，仅学习逐层标量系数，参数量约为 LoRA 的 4% 而效果相当，并降低显存、提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEFT 希望在保持适配质量的同时降低训练成本；现有方法（如 LoRA）仍需较多可训练参数和梯度/优化器状态开销。

Method: 从任务激活构建固定输入基，为每个输入方向分配随机输出字典；微调只学习层特定的标量系数，减少梯度与优化器状态存储；采用分组计算先聚合输出原子再作用于 token 激活；在兼容层间共享字典以降低固定基存储。

Result: 常识推理上以约 4% 的 LoRA 可训练参数达到相当精度；在常识推理、数学推理、代码生成与指令微调后知识评测上均有效适配；Qwen2.5-7B 上峰值训练显存低 20%、吞吐高 6%。

Conclusion: SCALAR 兼顾适配质量、紧凑可训练状态与训练效率，适合资源受限场景的微调。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) aims to preserve adaptation quality while reducing training costs. We propose SCALAR, which achieves performance comparable to LoRA with substantially fewer trainable parameters, lower peak training memory, and higher throughput. SCALAR constructs a fixed input basis from task activations and assigns a random output dictionary to each input direction. Fine-tuning learns only layer-specific scalar coefficients, reducing gradient and optimizer-state storage. Grouped computation aggregates output atoms before applying the adapter to token activations, while dictionary sharing across compatible layers reduces fixed-basis storage. Experiments on commonsense reasoning, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and knowledge evaluation after instruction tuning demonstrate effective adaptation at small trainable budgets. On commonsense reasoning, SCALAR achieves accuracy comparable to LoRA with about 4% of its trainable parameters. System measurements on Qwen2.5-7B show 20% lower peak training memory and 6% higher throughput than LoRA. The combination of adaptation quality, a compact trainable state, and training efficiency makes SCALAR a promising approach to resource-constrained fine-tuning.

</details>


### [153] [Federated Physics-Informed Thermal Field Prediction Across Unseen Geometries in Distributed Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17794)
*Amirul Islam Saimon,Zhenyu,Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-PIGANO：在多个制造站点本地训练物理信息几何感知神经算子，仅聚合几何编码器与预测网络而不共享零件几何，实现新设计的快速热场预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造中每次几何变化都需重新热仿真，多方案设计评估成本高；现有几何感知模型假设多样几何集中可用，但各站点只有不可共享的专有设计。

Method: 提出联邦框架Fed-PIGANO，各站点训练PI-GANO模型，联邦聚合几何编码器与预测网络，不共享几何数据。在两维稳态金属AM热基准上评估，几何来自5个多边形族、分布3站点、4种组成差异场景，7次重复。

Result: 12个场景-站点组合平均相对L2误差1.83%–2.04%，仅本地训练最高达20.46%；PDE残差更低；精度与集中式（汇聚所有站点几何）模型相当。

Conclusion: 联邦学习使制造站点在无需共享几何的情况下互相受益，支持形状优化、逆向设计和不确定性量化等任务的快速热预测。

Abstract: In additive manufacturing (AM), each change in part geometry requires a new thermal simulation, which makes design evaluation over many candidates costly. Geometry-aware models reduce this cost by predicting thermal fields on unseen parts with a single learned model, but they assume that diverse geometries are available centrally. This assumption is restrictive when each manufacturing site holds only a limited set of proprietary part designs that it cannot share. We propose Fed-PIGANO, a federated framework that trains physics-informed geometry-aware neural operator (PI-GANO) models at individual sites and aggregates their geometry encoders and prediction networks without sharing part geometries. We evaluate the framework on a 2D steady-state thermal benchmark for metal AM, with geometries from five polygonal families distributed across three sites under four scenarios that range from identical to strongly different family compositions. Over seven replications, Fed-PIGANO achieves mean relative L2 errors of 1.83% to 2.04% across all 12 scenario-site cases, compared with up to 20.46% for local-only training, and consistently yields lower PDE residuals. Its prediction accuracy is comparable to that of a centralized model trained on the pooled geometries of all sites. These results demonstrate that federated learning lets manufacturing sites benefit from each other's geometries without sharing them, enabling rapid thermal prediction on new part designs for tasks such as shape optimization, inverse design, and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [154] [Electrocardiographic Imaging via Posterior Sampling under Operator Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17851)
*Ricarda Buttmann,Andreas M. Lehrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpDis-PS：一个建模正演算子信号相关失配的扩散后验采样器，用于心电成像(ECGI)的心脏表面电位重建，在肺电导率变化与皮下脂肪层等失配条件下优于经典正则化与学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECGI 是病态逆问题。扩散后验采样器假定正演算子准确，但ECGI中的算子只是估计（受呼吸、体位、皮下脂肪等影响），由此产生的算子失配是结构化的、且依赖心脏信号，不能被当作信号无关噪声处理。

Method: 提出 OpDis-PS：学习失配如何随信号变化，将其并入修正后的正演算子中，并在所有算子条件下使用同一固定先验进行重建。研究两类现实失配来源：肺电导率变化与附加皮下脂肪层。

Result: 在两类失配条件下，OpDis-PS 的重建精度都高于经典正则化方法和学习型预测器，且在留出的心脏病理数据上提升最大。

Conclusion: 将算子失配显式建模为信号相关项并校正正演算子，可显著提升扩散后验采样在ECGI中的鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Electrocardiographic imaging (ECGI) reconstructs heart-surface potentials from body-surface potentials, an ill-posed inverse problem. Diffusion posterior samplers combine a learned prior with an explicit forward operator that they assume to be accurate. In ECGI this operator is only an estimate, because the body changes with respiration and posture, and body composition such as subcutaneous fat is rarely known for an individual patient. The resulting operator discrepancy is structured and depends on the heart signal. We introduce OpDis-PS, a diffusion posterior sampler that models this discrepancy instead of treating it as signal-independent noise. OpDis-PS learns how the discrepancy depends on the signal, folds this into a corrected forward operator, and reconstructs with a fixed prior across all operator conditions. We study two realistic sources of discrepancy, changing lung conductivity and an added subcutaneous-fat layer. Across both, OpDis-PS reconstructs more accurately than classical regularization and learned predictors, with its largest gains on held-out cardiac pathologies.

</details>


### [155] [Fuse-All MoE Inference Kernel with Load Balancing on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17857)
*Rupeng Liu,Bangsheng Tang,Zongwei Zhou,Chengji Yao,Yejin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fuse-All MoE 是一种 TPU 内核设计，联合优化专家计算、输出通信与负载均衡，在 PD 分离服务下分别针对 prefill（EP16）与 decode（EP8TP2）优化 MoE 推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 仅激活部分专家，能在不按比例增加单 token 计算量的情况下扩展 LLM 参数容量，但中间数据搬运与专家负载不均限制了 MoE 在 TPU 上的推理效率。论文旨在解决这些瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Fuse-All MoE 内核，将 token 收集、专家投影、门控激活与输出写回融合，使专家计算中间结果保留在片上；采用固定 token-slot 目标，使调度与专家放置可变而保持路由语义不变。在 PD 分离服务中，prefill 阶段通过调度与专家重分配平衡 EP16 负载；decode 阶段采用对称 EP8TP2，将活跃专家计算在两个 TensorCore 间共享。

Result: 对偏斜 EP16 prefill 工作负载，内核运行时间从均衡参考时间的 2.3x 降至 1.09x；在 32–512 decode token 范围内，对称 EP8TP2 相比 fused EP16 最高降低内核延迟 21.3%；配合服务栈优化，整体系统吞吐较 Megablox EP16 基线最高提升 34.5%。

Conclusion: 融合式内核设计结合调度与专家重分配，可有效缓解 TPU 上 MoE 推理的数据搬运与负载不均问题，并显著提升 PD 分离服务的吞吐与延迟表现。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures activate only a subset of experts for each token, allowing large language models (LLMs) to scale parameter capacity without a proportional increase in computation per token. However, intermediate data movement and uneven expert loads limit the efficiency of MoE inference on TPUs. To address these bottlenecks, we introduce Fuse-All MoE, a TPU kernel design that jointly optimizes expert computation, output communication, and load balancing. The kernel fuses token collection, expert projections, gated activation, and output writes, retaining expert computation intermediates on chip. Fixed token-slot destinations allow scheduling and expert placement to change while preserving routing semantics. In prefill-decode (PD) disaggregated serving, Fuse-All MoE balances EP16 prefill workloads through scheduling and expert redistribution, while symmetric EP8TP2 shares active expert computation between the two TensorCore devices within each chip during decode. Our experiments show that Fuse-All MoE reduces kernel runtime for a measured skewed EP16 prefill workload from 2.3x to 1.09x the same fixed reference time for a balanced workload. Across 32-512 decode tokens, symmetric EP8TP2 reduces kernel latency by up to 21.3% relative to fused EP16. With accompanying serving stack optimizations, the integrated system improves serving throughput by up to 34.5% over the Megablox EP16 baseline.

</details>


### [156] [Disentangling Forgetting from Utility: Lightweight LLM Unlearning via Orthogonal Null-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17864)
*Xiuyuan Wang,Weiming Liu,Zongxin Yang,Fan Wang,Liemiao Jin,Rubin Qin,Wei Chen,Bin Shi,Chaochao Chen,Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UUE框架，将大模型遗忘重构为可控模型编辑：在保留知识的左零空间中更新参数，并用凸无目标目标函数将遗忘集嵌入推向对跖点以求闭式解，兼顾高效遗忘与通用能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM保留隐私或危险信息带来安全风险，需要遗忘特定知识而不损害通用能力。但现有基于微调的遗忘方法因知识在参数中纠缠，难以兼顾：计算低效且易导致通用能力退化。

Method: 将遗忘重构为可控模型编辑。1）把参数更新投影到保留知识表示的正交左零空间，严格保证编辑对通用能力干扰最小；2）不用定向映射，而提出凸的无目标目标函数，把遗忘集嵌入推向其对跖点，并推导闭式解析解；3）通过轻量可插拔适配器实现（含低秩变体UUE-L），无需修改冻结主干。

Result: 在TOFU与WMDP基准上，UUE与UUE-L的遗忘效果优于现有方法，遗忘效力显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过零空间约束解耦遗忘与效用、闭式凸解与轻量适配器，UUE为LLM遗忘提供了理论有据、高效且实用的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) pose significant security risks by retaining private or hazardous information, creating an urgent need for unlearning techniques that excise specific knowledge without compromising general utility. However, prevailing fine-tuning-based unlearning methods struggle to achieve this balance, often suffering from computational inefficiency and unintended utility degradation due to the entangled nature of knowledge within model parameters. To disentangle forgetting from utility, we propose UUE, a theoretically grounded framework that reformulates unlearning as controllable model editing. First, UUE projects parameter updates into the orthogonal left null space of retained knowledge representations, strictly ensuring that the editing process causes minimal interference to the model's general capabilities. Second, distinct from targeted mapping, we introduce a convex untargeted objective that derives a closed-form analytical solution to effectively disrupt undesired knowledge by pushing forget-set embeddings toward their antipodal points. We implement UUE via lightweight, pluggable adapters (including a low-rank variant, UUE-L), enabling efficient deployment without modifying the frozen backbone. Extensive experiments on TOFU and WMDP benchmarks demonstrate that UUE and UUE-L achieve superior unlearning efficacy, significantly outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [157] [Process Mining-Enabled Federated Anomaly Detection for the Industrial IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17869)
*Francesco Vitale,Salvatore Bramante,Francesco Flammini,Hangli Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于过程挖掘的联邦异常检测方法，用局部节点数据与全局参考的相似度动态加权聚合本地模型，缓解工业IoT非IID数据导致的误报问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦异常检测可整合多节点数据降低误报，但工业IoT场景下各工厂运行条件与环境差异导致时间序列高度异构（非IID），标准联邦平均无法进行细粒度聚合，误报率上升。

Method: 采用过程挖掘评估各局部节点数据与全局节点所提供参考的相似度；全局节点据此相似度指标在聚合时动态加权各本地模型，生成个性化更新并分发到网络各节点。

Result: 在来自不同运行条件制造厂的工业IoT时间序列数据上评估：相比孤立本地训练基线F1提升46.52%，相比标准FedAvg提升11.99%，相比标准联邦聚合误报相对减少51.24%。

Conclusion: 过程挖掘驱动的相似度加权聚合能有效应对非IID异构数据，显著提升联邦异常检测的F1并大幅降低误报，适用于多条件工业IoT部署。

Abstract: While federated anomaly detection enables the integration of local data insights across diverse nodes to reduce false positives, it inherently struggles with non-IID data. In practice, industrial IoT plants often operate under varying conditions and experience diverse environmental phenomena, resulting in significant heterogeneity in the monitored time series. In such settings, standard federated learning approaches fail to perform nuanced aggregation that accounts for these cross-node differences, ultimately leading to increased false positive rates. In this paper, we propose a process mining approach to evaluate the similarity of local node data against a reference provided by a global node. The global node utilizes these similarity metrics to dynamically weight local models during aggregation, producing personalized updates distributed across the network. We evaluate our approach using industrial IoT time-series data collected from manufacturing plants operating under distinct conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that our process mining-enabled federated anomaly detection pipeline achieves superior performance, yielding a 46.52% and 11.99% relative improvement in F1-score compared to isolated local training baselines and standard FedAvg, respectively, alongside a 51.24% relative reduction in false positives over standard federated aggregation.

</details>


### [158] [Protection-Oriented Assessment of Machine Learning-Based Fault Detection in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17872)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Changhun Kim,Christian Bergler,Andreas Maier,Johann Jäger,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向保护的评估框架，在PROTECT-90数据集上比较三种ML故障检测器与序分量参考方法，揭示高AUC下仍存在可观的漏跳与误跳问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的电力系统保护故障检测研究多用聚合指标（如AUC），可能掩盖误跳与漏跳的实际后果，需要面向保护性能的细致评估。

Method: 构建PROTECT-90数据集（90kV双回线路9022个仿真故障事件）；采用五种窗口条件、因果窗口末端标注、按事件分组的交叉验证、固定安全预算下的工作点选择；报告安全性、可靠性、检测时延、决策裕度及事件级滋扰断言；对比梯度提升树、CNN、逻辑回归及序电流参考。

Result: 三种ML检测器AUC-ROC均超过0.999，但在10^-3安全预算下漏跳率为0.007%-1.99%；清除后误跳率约0.12%-0.16%/窗口，11.3%-19.1%事件存在滋扰断言。漏跳最一致地与较高故障电阻相关；解析电流代理与波形电流的秩相关仅0.62；无单一拓扑状态或故障相占主导。

Conclusion: 聚合指标严重高估实际保护性能，需采用面向保护的细粒度评估；漏跳与故障电阻相关；因数据集仅含故障事件，安全性仅基于故障前与清除后窗口评估，缺少独立非故障扰动验证。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML)-based fault detectors are increasingly proposed for power system protection, yet aggregate metrics can obscure the consequences of false and missed trips. We present a protection-oriented assessment of three ML detectors and a sequence-current reference on PROTECT-90, comprising 9,022 simulated fault episodes on a 90 kV double-circuit line. The assessment combines five window conditions, causal window-end labelling, episode-grouped cross-validation, and operating-point selection under a fixed security budget. It reports security, dependability, detection timing, decision margins, and episode-level nuisance assertions. Gradient-boosted trees, a convolutional neural network, and logistic regression achieve AUC-ROC values above 0.999, yet at a security budget of $10^{-3}$ their developed-fault missed-trip rates range from 0.007% to 1.99%. Post-clearing false-trip rates of approximately 0.12-0.16% per window coexist with nuisance assertions in 11.3-19.1% of episodes. Among the analysed physical descriptors, missed trips are most consistently associated with higher fault resistance within the sampled range. An analytical current proxy suggests a fault-current gradient only partly reproduced by waveform-derived current (rank correlation 0.62). No single topology state or faulted phase consistently dominates within the sampled domain. Because PROTECT-90 contains fault episodes only, security is assessed on pre-fault and post-clearing windows rather than independent non-fault disturbances.

</details>


### [159] [Delayed Effects of Temporary Weight Decay in Muon Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17876)
*Md Rifat Arefin,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Ernie Chang,Chinnadhurai Sankar,Rylan Conway,Aristide Baratin,Irina Rish,Adithya Sagar,Patrick Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现临时性的权重衰减（weight decay）在移除时可能恶化验证损失，但在最终训练预算处反而改善损失；效果取决于衰减窗口的位置、时长与总收缩量，且与权重范数亏缺导致的"相对步长"持续放大机制有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临时优化干预（如限时权重衰减）通常在移除时刻被评估，作者想探究其真实长期效应：移除时的损失是否代表最终结果？延迟效应是否长期存在？并希望解释其背后的机制。

Method: 在 Muon 训练的 decoder-only Transformer（162M、405M、1.4B、3.4B）上做临时权重衰减实验：将固定时长衰减窗口从早期移到晚期、改变窗口时长（匹配总收缩量）、跨规模迁移调度（162M→1.4B）；用权范数动态递推近似、固定范数回放实验、以及范数重置实验来定位机制；另用 Gram 干预验证相关动态。

Result: ≤1.4B 模型上，窗口从早移晚会反转最终效果；162M 下即使总收缩量相近，窗口时长仍改变最终损失。162M 选定并直接迁移到 1.4B 的调度：移除时比无衰减差 0.26 nats，73% 训练后才反超，最终好 0.028 nats，并优于最佳采样常数衰减基线。早期衰减留下持续的权重范数亏缺，使后续相对步长更大；范数递推与固定范数回放复现了性能排序与相近损失差；范数重置实验表明窗口末更小权范数解释了大部分最终损失改善。

Conclusion: 临时优化调度具有远超移除时刻的延迟效应，应在目标最终训练预算处评估，而非仅在干预结束时评估；权重范数及其对相对步长的影响是核心机制。

Abstract: A temporary optimization intervention can increase validation loss when it ends yet reduce it at the final training budget. We demonstrate this with temporary weight decay in Muon-trained decoder-only Transformers with 162M, 405M, 1.4B, and 3.4B parameters. For models up to 1.4B parameters, moving a fixed-duration decay window from early to late training reverses its final effect. At 162M, changing window duration alters final loss even at approximately matched total shrinkage. Most strikingly, a schedule selected at 162M and transferred without retuning to 1.4B is 0.26 nats worse than no decay when removed, overtakes it only after 73% of training, and ultimately finishes 0.028 nats better while also outperforming the best sampled constant-decay baseline. Early decay leaves a persistent weight-norm deficit, which keeps relative steps larger after the intervention ends. An approximate norm-dynamics recurrence captures the slow convergence of these relative steps, and fixed-norm replays of the measured trajectories reproduce the performance ordering and a similar loss difference. At 162M and 405M, norm-reset experiments show that smaller weight norms at the end of the decay window explain most of the final loss improvement. A separate Gram intervention exhibits related post-removal norm and relative-step dynamics. These results show that temporary optimization schedules can have delayed effects long after removal and should therefore be evaluated at the intended final training budget, not only at removal.

</details>


### [160] [From Local Prediction Error to Final Outputs: A Theory of Expert Sharing in Multi-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17879)
*Tiantong Wang,Zhiren Gong,Yixin Wang,Tiantong Wu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究MoE中跨层/跨步共享专家带来的"局部误差下降"能否在组合后保留到最终输出，给出精确的共享/合并准则与端到端误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE可跨层或跨计算步共享专家，从而汇聚不同上下文信息、降低局部预测误差；但多步系统最终以输出为评价标准，局部收益能否在组合中存活尚不明确。

Method: 在特定线性回归模型中，权衡方差节省与跨角色共享引入的偏差，推导精确的共享与合并准则；在固定线性传播映射下刻画期望平方最终输出风险（保留误差方向与相关性）；对一般路由计算推导考虑分布偏移、周边组件误差与专家选择变化的条件端到端误差界；并通过受控实验与Transformer符号任务实验、干预测试进行验证。

Result: 构造出共享降低平均局部误差却增大最终输出误差的案例；识别出正交全池化加各向同性噪声可在最终输出保留每个局部改进的条件；实验实现严格的局部—最终风险反转；低数据符号任务中训练Transformer表现出统计显著的teacher-forced单步增益，但自由运行最终输出增益不显著。

Conclusion: 区分了共享何时改善局部估计、局部增益何时能存活到最终输出，以及将可复用操作归因于单个专家所需证据；指出仅凭正确预测与路由标签不足以证明专家实现了所归因的操作。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models can share experts across layers or computational steps, pooling training information from different contexts. This can reduce local prediction error, but multi-step systems are ultimately evaluated by their final outputs. We study when these local gains survive composition. In specified linear regression models, we derive exact sharing and merging criteria by balancing variance savings against bias from sharing across roles with different target behavior. Under a common fixed linear propagation map, we characterize expected squared final-output risk, retaining error directions and correlations. We construct cases where sharing lowers average local error yet increases final-output error. Within this setting, we also identify when orthogonal full pooling with isotropic noise preserves every local improvement at the final output. For general routed computations, we derive conditional end-to-end error bounds that account for distribution shifts, errors in surrounding components, and changes in expert selection. Separately, we show that correct predictions and route labels alone do not establish that individual experts implement the attributed operations. Controlled experiments realize a strict local-to-final risk reversal. In low-data symbolic settings, trained Transformers exhibit statistically supported teacher-forced one-step gains without statistically resolved free-running final-output gains. Separate intervention tests distinguish successful multi-layer actions from single-expert attribution and generalization to unseen transitions. Together, these results distinguish when sharing improves local estimation, when those gains survive to the final output, and what evidence is needed to attribute reusable operations to individual experts.

</details>


### [161] [Quantum Graph Neural Networks: Architectures, Trainability, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17886)
*Zeinab Sharifi,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇量子图神经网络（QGNN）综述，提出统一形式化框架（编码器—图条件电路—可观测量—经典头），将文献划分为五类架构，系统评估其资源代价、归纳偏置与证据强度，并给出最低报告协议。核心结论：对称性是最有支撑的归纳偏置，但现有证据薄弱，尚无在经典图数据任务上确立量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2019年首个基于哈密顿量的QGNN以来，该领域涌现大量架构、应用与主张，但支撑这些主张的证据从未被系统评估。作者希望填补这一空白，厘清QGNN在可扩展性、表达力与长程信息流方面是否真正超越经典GNN。

Method: 1）提出统一形式化：任何QGNN均可描述为编码器、图条件量子电路、可观测量与可选经典头的组合，并给出置换等变性的电路级条件及用于成本比较的资源模型；2）将文献归为五个架构家族（卷积型、等变型、消息传递与循环型、哈密顿量型、混合型），按量子比特成本、对称性、可扩展性与证据进行对比；3）梳理置换、旋转与洛伦兹等对称与几何归纳偏置，综述表达力、可训练性、噪声与可扩展性结果；4）覆盖高能物理、化学、金融、医疗、地球观测与交通等应用；5）审计数据集与评测实践，提出最低报告协议。

Result: 设计空间丰富，对称性是最有充分支持的归纳偏置；但证据总体薄弱——结果多为模拟、图规模小、仅与经典模型持平或参数更少，且在经典图数据任务上尚未确立任何量子优势。数据集与评测实践存在明显缺陷。

Conclusion: 作者提出五项开放挑战与研究议程：可证明的量子优势、等变与几何设计、容错实现以及共享基准。综述覆盖至2026年，侧重近期工作。

Abstract: Quantum graph neural networks (QGNNs) apply parametrized quantum circuits to graph-structured data, with the aim of overcoming limits of classical graph neural networks in scalability, expressivity, and long-range information flow. Since the first Hamiltonian-based model in 2019, the field has produced many architectures, applications, and claims, but the evidence behind them has not been assessed systematically. This survey introduces a unified formalism that describes every QGNN as an encoder, a graph-conditioned circuit, observables, and an optional classical head, together with a circuit-level condition for permutation equivariance and a resource model for comparing costs. We organize the literature into five architecture families (convolutional, equivariant, message-passing and recurrent, Hamiltonian, and hybrid) and compare them by qubit cost, symmetry, scalability, and evidence. We then review symmetry and geometric inductive biases (permutation, rotation, and Lorentz), the results on expressivity, trainability, noise, and scalability, and applications in high-energy physics, chemistry, finance, healthcare, earth observation, and transportation. An audit of datasets and evaluation practices leads to a minimum reporting protocol. Our main finding is that the design space is rich and symmetry is the best-supported inductive bias, whereas the evidence remains thin: results are mostly simulated, on small graphs, at parity with classical models or with fewer parameters, and no advantage has yet been established on tasks over classical graph data. We identify five open challenges and a research agenda covering provable advantage, equivariant and geometric designs, fault-tolerant implementations, and shared benchmarks. The coverage extends through 2026 and emphasizes recent work.

</details>


### [162] [TIDE: Reclaiming Lost Performance In On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17924)
*Mathew Jacob,Morgan Borjigin-Wang,Kan Zhu,Po Hao Chen,Yi Pan,Andy Ruan,Nayani Modugula,Ethan Lavi,Luis Ceze,Malte Schwarzkopf,Akshay Narayan,Deepti Raghavan,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIDE 是一种强化学习后训练系统，通过动态复用 rollout GPU 用于训练、并在推理引擎间迁移长尾 rollout，在保持 on-policy 语义的同时提升同步共置 RL 训练的 GPU 利用率，最高实现 1.36x 端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 的同步共置 RL 后训练存在严重的资源浪费：长尾 rollout 使推理引擎空闲等待，或只能以低效的 batch size 进行解码，导致 GPU 利用率低下。

Method: TIDE 采用两种互补技术：(1) 在长尾 rollout 完成期间，动态将 rollout GPU 改用于训练；(2) 在推理引擎之间动态迁移 rollout 以提升解码吞吐。系统通过自适应迁移策略自动判断资源改用的时机，无需针对具体场景定制配置，并可泛化到多种应用与硬件。

Result: 在评估中，TIDE 相比共置系统实现最高 1.36x 端到端加速，优于所有评估的 on-policy 基线，并达到最优吞吐的 97%。

Conclusion: TIDE 表明，通过动态资源复用与 rollout 迁移，可在不牺牲 on-policy 训练语义的前提下显著提升同步共置 RL 后训练的资源利用率，且具备良好的通用性与接近最优的性能。

Abstract: Synchronous, colocated RL post-training of large language models (LLMs) today suffers from severe under-utilization due to long-tail rollouts. Inference engines fall idle waiting for long-tail rollouts or decode at low, inefficient batch sizes. To address this, we present TIDE, an RL training system that preserves on-policy training semantics while recovering resource utilization. TIDE does so by two complementary techniques: (1) dynamically repurposing rollout GPUs for training while long-tail rollouts complete and (2) dynamically migrating rollouts between inference engines to boost decoding throughput. TIDE automatically determines the right time to repurpose rollout resources through an adaptive migration policy, obtaining performance improvements that generalize across multiple applications and hardware configurations without requiring bespoke configuration. In our evaluation, TIDE provides end-to-end speedups of up to 1.36x over colocated systems, outperforming all evaluated on-policy baselines and achieving up to 97% of optimal throughput.

</details>


### [163] [Differentially Private Federated Class-Incremental Learning for EEG Mental State Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17944)
*Baran Can Gül,Ali Gülümser,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究小规模联邦（K=5客户端）下差分隐私联邦类增量学习，提出FedEEG（突触智能巩固+滞后自适应FedProx）与head-scoped客户端级DP，在EEG数据上显著减少灾难性遗忘，并在匹配轮次预算下将可达ε降低200倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP联邦学习评估多基于大规模客户端，依赖子采样与跨客户端平均来放大隐私并平滑噪声；而可穿戴EEG等生物信号场景仅有少数受试者组成的小联邦，该情形与形式化客户端级DP的相互作用尚未被刻画。

Method: 构建K=5客户端联邦（公开EEG数据集+自制四通道MUSE 2录制集）；提出掩码式逐类训练协议，避免类增量流式下的退化梯度与负监督缺失；提出FedEEG，结合Synaptic Intelligence巩固与滞后自适应FedProx项；采用端上均衡批回放；并引入head-scoped客户端级DP以保护分类头。

Result: FedEEG相较FedAvg、FedProx及联邦LwF基线减少灾难性遗忘；端上均衡批回放使平均遗忘再降56.7%；类范围诊断（6个种子）达50.5%±3.6%准确率，为随机水平的2.02倍；head-scoped DP单独在匹配轮次预算下将可达ε降低200倍，结合更少通信轮次达ε≈10.4，且效用与非隐私训练无统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 小客户端联邦是DP-FL中被忽视但可行的机制：针对性的遗忘缓解与head-scoped DP可在极弱隐私预算下保持效用，为可穿戴EEG等个性化生物信号应用提供实用路径。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) under differential privacy (DP) is typically evaluated on large client cohorts, where subsampling and cross-client averaging amplify privacy and smooth injected noise. Personal biosignal applications such as wearable EEG instead involve small federations of a handful of subjects, a regime whose interaction with formal client-level DP is largely uncharacterized. We study differentially private federated class-incremental learning in this small-cohort regime, combining a public EEG dataset with a custom four-channel MUSE 2 recording set into a K=5-client federation, and give a masked per-class training protocol that avoids degenerate gradients and missing negative supervision under class-incremental streaming. We propose FedEEG, combining Synaptic-Intelligence consolidation with a lagged adaptive FedProx term, and show it reduces catastrophic forgetting relative to FedAvg, FedProx, and a federated Learning-without-Forgetting baseline; on-device balanced-batch replay reduces mean forgetting by a further 56.7%. A class-scoping diagnosis yields $50.5\%\pm3.6\%$ accuracy with 6 seeds and 2.02 times chance. We also introduce head-scoped client-level DP, which protects the classification head and alone reduces the achievable $\varepsilon$ by 200 times at a matched round budget; combined with fewer communication rounds it reaches $\varepsilon\!\approx\!10.4$ with utility not statistically distinguishable from non-private training.

</details>


### [164] [Reasoning Models Confabulate to Defend Injected Beliefs About Their Own Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17958)
*Yang Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过注入无关概念的形成痕迹激活编辑，可切断模型对自己所接受请求的信念锚定：模型会推理、报告并回答被注入的主题，即便被提示真实主题也难以纠正，构成对CoT可监控性的具体限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控与自我报告被视为AI监督的杠杆，其前提是模型对自身行为的叙述锚定于真实请求。作者要检验这一锚定是否可被破坏，从而评估CoT可监控性的边界。

Method: 对推理模型施加提示文本中不可见的激活编辑，注入一个离题概念的"形成痕迹"。在六个覆盖不同规模、架构与训练配方的模型上测试：强制引述、最终回答、加入含真实主题的警告、直接告知真相，并辅以注意力图与电路归因分析。

Result: 强制引述在48个模型-概念单元中有47个返回注入主题；四个可正常生成最终作品的模型中99%代际跟随注入答案；警告真实主题后真实主题回答仍不足5%；被直接告知真相时模型编造用户"自我纠正"的故事并仍按注入作答。编辑后模型反而更关注真实请求，但该注意力因果无效，注入内容沿完整任务电路传递，注意力图与电路归因都看似"在任务上"；同一编辑还改写请求的语言与格式。

Conclusion: 该注入触及的是模型对自身输入的指称，激发的是其为该输入所做的辩护，行为与CoT被同步破坏，给出CoT可监控性的一项具体限制；打开模型内部（注意力、电路归因）也无法提供补救。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring and self-report are proposed as levers for AI oversight, on the assumption that a model's account of what it is doing is anchored to what it was asked. We show this anchor can be cut. An activation edit invisible in the prompt text, the injected forming-trace of an off-topic concept, overwrites a reasoning model's belief about its own request: across six models spanning scale, architecture, and recipe, the injected topic becomes the task it reasons about, reports back, and answers: the forced quote-back returns the injected topic in 47 of 48 model-concept cells, and on the four models with clean final haiku the injected answer follows in 99% of generations. Warnings barely move it: even one naming the true topic yields under 5% true-topic answers. Handed the truth in clean tokens, the model confabulates a story about the user ("maybe they got confused and corrected themselves") and answers with the injection anyway, its behavior and chain-of-thought corrupted together: a concrete limit on CoT monitorability. Opening the model up offers no reprieve: it attends to the true request even harder after edits, yet that attention is causally inert, and the injected content rides intact task circuits, so attention maps and circuit attributions read as on-task too; the same edit redirects the request's language and format, not only its topic. What the injection reaches is the model's reference to its own input; what it elicits is its defense of it.

</details>


### [165] [DeltaVisionX: A Low-Cost Vision-Guided Automation System Using YOLOv11 and PLC-Based Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17959)
*Lara Sobh,Marc Nahhas,Francisco Katach El Halabi,Michel Owayjan,Fadi Elias Sleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出低成本工业自动化系统 DeltaVisionX，将 YOLOv11 实时视觉检测与 Delta DVP-SE PLC 气动分拣结合，通过编码器同步实现 0.97 mAP、100ms/帧推理与 80 件/分钟吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业对自动化分拣需求增长，但商用视觉-控制集成方案成本高、依赖进口；作者希望证明本地开发的低成本方案可减少人工依赖并提升效率。

Method: 自建并标注、增强数据集训练 YOLOv11；检测结果传给 Delta DVP-SE PLC 驱动气动活塞分拣；用编码器跟踪实现检测与执行机构的同步。创新点在编码器同步的视觉-PLC 集成，而非检测模型本身。

Result: mAP 达 0.97，单帧推理 100ms，编码器跟踪保证检测-执行同步，吞吐量 80 件/分钟。

Conclusion: 低成本本地化自动化方案可行，能降低人工依赖、提升制造效率；后续将接入高速 Delta 机器人与 EtherCAT 伺服控制实现高级抓放。

Abstract: This paper presents DeltaVisionX, a low-cost industrial automation system that integrates real-time computer vision with PLC-based actuation for object classification and sorting. The system uses the YOLOv11 (You Only Look Once) deep learning model to detect objects on an operating conveyor belt and communicates detection results to a Delta DVP-SE PLC, which actuates pneumatic pistons for sorting. A custom dataset was created, labeled, and augmented to train the YOLOv11 model, achieving a mean average precision of 0.97 with real-time inference of 100 ms per frame. Encoder-based tracking ensures reliable synchronization between detection and actuation, supporting a throughput of 80 objects per minute. Results show that affordable, locally developed automation can reduce labor dependency and improve manufacturing efficiency. The novelty of the proposed system lies in its encodersynchronized vision-PLC integration rather than in the detection model itself. Future work includes integrating a highspeed Delta robot with EtherCAT-based servo control for advanced pick-and-place operations.

</details>


### [166] [Analyzing Learning-Dynamics Across Loss and Prediction Choices for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17967)
*Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解耦了扩散/去噪生成模型中"预测空间"（预测x/ε/v）与"损失空间"的影响，证明改变损失空间等价于时间步重加权，并给出线性瓶颈下的误差下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪生成模型可预测x、ε或v，损失也可在这三个空间度量，共9种组合虽有代数等价关系，但去噪精度和样本质量差异显著，缺乏定量理解。

Method: 将预测与损失空间解耦分析：证明对固定预测目标，改变损失空间等价于时间步重加权并做启发式分析；推导秩-m线性瓶颈压缩D维噪声输入时，噪声/速度预测存在D-m的不可约平方误差下界，而数据预测可利用低维结构，结合误差转换得出瓶颈容量必要条件；在U-Net与JiT上以CIFAR-10、Imagenette、ImageNet-256验证。

Result: 实验支持理论预测：压缩下预测选择显著影响去噪精度与样本质量，噪声预测误差接近理论下界，不同预测目标对瓶颈容量有不同要求。

Conclusion: 提供了架构瓶颈与预测/损失选择相互作用的定量理解，指导去噪生成模型中预测目标与容量设计的匹配。

Abstract: Denoising generative models predict clean data ($x$), noise ($ε$), or velocity ($v$), while training losses can be measured in any of these spaces. Although the resulting nine combinations are algebraically related, they can yield substantially different denoising accuracy and sample quality. We disentangle the effects of prediction and loss spaces. For a fixed prediction target, we show that changing the loss space is exactly equivalent to timestep reweighting and provide a heuristic analysis of how this reweighting affects generation quality. We then show that a rank-$m$ linear bottleneck compressing a $D$-dimensional noisy input imposes an irreducible squared-error floor of $D-m$ on noise and velocity prediction, whereas clean-data prediction can exploit low-dimensional data structure. Combining this bound with prediction-dependent error conversion yields distinct necessary conditions on bottleneck capacity. Experiments with U-Net and JiT across CIFAR-10, Imagenette, and ImageNet-256 support these theoretical predictions. Under compression, prediction choice strongly affects both denoising accuracy and sample quality, with noise-prediction errors closely matching the theoretical lower bound. Our results provide a quantitative understanding of how architectural bottlenecks interact with prediction and loss choices in denoising generative models.

</details>


### [167] [When Does Oversampling Help? A Decision Framework for Imbalanced Data Streams Under Label Delay and Concept Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17981)
*Ahmed Awad,Anirudh Sengar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在存在标签延迟的类别不平衡数据流中，持续"再评估"已能恢复延迟造成的损失，流式过采样的边际价值极为有限（仅在一个真实数据配置中可检测到），因此方法选择应依据数据流特征动态决定，而非照搬合成基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式过采样器通常在"即时标签、先测后训"假设下评估，其在联合类别不平衡、概念漂移与标签延迟条件下的行为缺乏系统研究；本文要回答：在延迟标签下，加装流式过采样是否值得，还是持续再评估就足以弥补延迟代价。

Method: 开展流式过采样与持续再评估的广泛实验对比，提出一个决策框架，围绕三个轴（漂移密度、少数类可学习性、延迟相对精化窗口的比例）组织，并操作化为"恢复通道"（再评估）与"少数类覆盖通道"（过采样）之间的竞争；在6个合成流与6个真实流上评估15种分类器。

Result: 再评估能在漂移活跃流上恢复延迟导致的损失，但在平稳流上几乎无作用；过采样相对再评估的边际贡献仅在1个真实数据配置中具统计显著性；合成流的成本—性能Pareto建议无法迁移到真实数据，真实数据中前沿按漂移机制分层。

Conclusion: 方法选择应以数据流自身特征为条件，并随流演化持续重新估计，而不应继承合成基准得出的结论。

Abstract: For imbalanced data streams with delayed labels, is it worth adding a streaming oversampler, or does continuous re-evaluation of earlier predictions already recover the cost of the delay? We address this through an extensive experimental comparison of streaming oversampling and continuous re-evaluation under joint class imbalance, concept drift, and label delay. Streaming oversamplers are typically evaluated under the test-then-train assumption of immediate labels; their behaviour under these conditions, assessed through re-evaluation, has not been systematically studied. We close this gap with a decision framework that predicts when oversampling adds value over re-evaluation, organised around three axes: drift density, minority-class learnability, and delay relative to the refinement window, and operationalised as a competition between a recovery channel (re-evaluation) and a minority-coverage channel (oversampling). Evaluating $15$ classifiers on $6$ synthetic and $6$ real streams, we find: re-evaluation recovers delay-induced loss on drift-active streams but is silent on stationary ones; oversampling's marginal contribution over re-evaluation is statistically detectable in only one real-data configuration; and cost--performance Pareto recommendations from synthetic streams do not transfer to real data, where fronts partition by drift regime. Method selection should therefore be conditioned on the stream's profile and re-estimated as it evolves, not inherited from synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [168] [RiverScan: Learning to Accumulate on River Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18005)
*He Zhu,Zhiyi Gao,Youhuan Yang,Han Quan,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RiverScan，一种基于面积加权选择性状态空间递归的河流网络空间算子，通过拓扑序累加上游贡献来做大流域径流预测，在多瑙河 608 个站点上优于时序与图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大流域径流预测需同时刻画异构的局地水文响应及其向下游的累积；河网连通性虽提供天然结构，但如何有效组合不同大小子流域的贡献仍未被解决。

Method: 将任务形式化为"学习累加"。RiverScan 按拓扑顺序递归地把局地时间表示与上游累积状态结合：以汇水面积比例刻画子流域规模，用可学习保留门（retention）控制上游信息向下游的持续程度，整体为选择性状态空间递归结构。在 608 个站点上做实验，并配合结构对照与消融分析。

Result: 整体预测性能优于所评估的时序与图基线；结构对照与消融表明，按水文累积方式组合异构子流域表示能显著提升径流预测，且正确拓扑带来的收益随河网层级升高而增大。

Conclusion: 物理结构化的累加是水文建模中有效的归纳偏置，并为更细粒度、完全分布式的 AI 水文模型提供依据。

Abstract: Accurate runoff prediction across large river basins requires models to capture heterogeneous local hydrological responses and their accumulation downstream. River-network connectivity provides a natural structure for this task, but using it effectively requires learning how to combine contributions from subcatchments of different sizes. We frame this challenge as learning to accumulate and introduce RiverScan, a spatial operator based on area-weighted selective state-space recurrence. RiverScan recursively combines local temporal representations with accumulated upstream states in topological order. Contributing-area fractions account for subcatchment size, while learned retention controls how upstream information persists downstream. Experiments on 608 stations in the Danube basin show that RiverScan outperforms the evaluated temporal and graph baselines in overall predictive performance. Structural controls and ablations demonstrate that composing heterogeneous subcatchment representations in a manner aligned with hydrological accumulation substantially improves runoff prediction, with the benefits of correct topology growing along the river-network hierarchy. These findings establish physically structured accumulation as an effective inductive bias for hydrological modeling and motivate finer-grained, fully distributed AI-based hydrological models.

</details>


### [169] [Sample-Efficient Control of Magnetoconvective Flows via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18008)
*Jannis Becktepe,Dmitry Krasnov,Nils Thuerey,Jörg Schumacher,Sebastian Peitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 φ-PICT，一个面向主动流动控制（AFC）的可微分、GPU 加速的磁流体（MHD）求解器，经基准验证后集成于 FluidGym，实现高效梯度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MHD 流动（如聚变包层冷却）对工程至关重要，流动控制可优化传热等关键性能；机器学习虽推动了 AFC 发展，但其在 MHD 流动上的应用几乎空白，缺乏高效可微求解工具。

Method: 开发可微分、GPU 加速的 MHD 求解器 φ-PICT，并在 MHD 基准算例上验证正确性与计算效率；将其集成到 FluidGym 框架中以支持基于梯度的优化算法。

Result: φ-PICT 在基准测试上验证通过且计算高效；集成后可实现高样本效率的梯度算法，使传热提升超过 25%；在逆向设计任务中优化器精度比黑盒方法高 10^3 倍。

Conclusion: 可微分 GPU MHD 求解器显著提升 MHD 主动流动控制的优化效率与精度，代码已开源（github.com/upb-sciml/phiPICT）。

Abstract: Magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) fluid flows are central to demanding engineering applications, most prominently fusion blanket cooling, where flow control is a promising tool to optimize key properties such as heat transfer. While active flow control (AFC) has recently advanced through machine learning, its application to MHD flows remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present $φ$-PICT, a novel differentiable GPU-accelerated MHD solver targeted at AFC. We validate $φ$-PICT against MHD benchmarks and demonstrate its high computational efficiency. Integrated into FluidGym, $φ$-PICT enables sample-efficient gradient-based algorithms to improve heat transfer by $>25$% and allows optimizers to achieve $10^3\times$ higher accuracy in inverse design than black-box methods. $φ$-PICT is publicly available at https://github.com/upb-sciml/phiPICT.

</details>


### [170] [Conflict, Feasibility, Faithfulness, Displacement: Four Quantities That Come Apart in Federated Dual-Cone Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18014)
*Grzegorz Gwardys,Karol Wojciechowski,Mateusz Gwardys,Bartosz {Ś}widerski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验了FedFV、FedGH、FedMGDA+等多目标梯度聚合器"通过消除客户端更新冲突来提升联邦学习"的理据，用仅改变FedAvg更新方向的UPGrad作对照，发现冲突、加权均值可行性、更新对客户端梯度的忠实性、更新位移这四个被混为一谈的量实际相互分离，相关增益脆弱且不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FedFV、FedGH、FedMGDA+等方法以多目标梯度聚合消除客户端更新间冲突为出发点。作者质疑这一理据：冲突、可行性、忠实性、位移被当作同一件事，需要拆解验证增益是否真实、可复现、可归因。

Method: 以UPGrad作对照——它在一阶意义上不增加任何客户端的损失，且仅改变FedAvg更新的方向。通过Dirichlet划分、改变Dirichlet浓度、均匀客户端权重、匹配的随机旋转、全参与与部分参与、以及多重比较校正等控制实验，将这四个量分离考察。

Result: CIFAR-10在Dirichlet划分下UPGrad提升+0.78 pp（14/15种子，p<0.001），但仅在全参与时成立；Oxford-IIIT Pet上+0.52 pp的增益在纳入协议未用数据后消失（+0.07 pp）。增益随异质性增大而增大，但冲突最大的划分却无增益。均匀权重（无视冲突）可复现CIFAR-10增益并超过Pets增益，而匹配随机旋转不能；无公平性效应通过多重比较校正；CIFAR-10上该保证未传递到客户端梯度。

Conclusion: 多目标聚合的"冲突消除"理据不成立：其收益脆弱、依赖于实验设定，甚至可由与冲突无关的均匀权重获得。作者据此提出评估此类方法应报告的内容规范。

Abstract: FedFV, FedGH and FedMGDA+ aggregate client updates with multi-objective gradient aggregators to remove conflict between them. We examine this rationale with UPGrad, which increases no client's loss to first order, in a comparison that changes only the direction of FedAvg's update. Four quantities the rationale treats as one -- conflict between client updates, feasibility of their weighted mean, faithfulness of the transmitted updates to the clients' gradients, and displacement of the update -- come apart. Under Dirichlet partitioning UPGrad improves CIFAR-10 accuracy by +0.78 pp (14/15 seeds, p < 0.001), only with full participation; on Oxford-IIIT Pet its +0.52 pp gain vanishes (+0.07 pp) once the data our protocol left unused are kept. Varying only the Dirichlet concentration, the gain grows with heterogeneity, yet the split with the most conflict shows none. Uniform client weights, blind to conflict, match the CIFAR-10 gain and exceed the Pets gain; a matched random rotation does neither. No fairness effect survives multiplicity correction, and on CIFAR-10 the guarantee does not reach the clients' gradients. We state what evaluations should report.

</details>


### [171] [Deep Latents, Long Memory: Latent persistence explains temporal correlations in transformer activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18089)
*Francesco Cagnetta,Santiago Acevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用层激活的自相关函数来量化LLM特征的抽象性，发现自相关慢衰减尾随深度增长，且稀疏自编码器特征的持久性与自相关强相关，消融持久特征会降低自相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "抽象随深度增加"的说法缺乏可操作的定量度量，本文希望给出可检验的抽象量化方法。

Method: 定义层激活在间隔lag两位置间的平均点积为自相关函数；在受控层级潜变量合成数据上验证，在真实文本中用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征作为潜变量代理，分析特征持久性与自相关的关系，并做消融实验。

Result: 合成与自然数据中自相关慢衰减尾随深度增长；合成数据里该增长源于抽象潜变量编码；真实文本中SAE特征持久性与自相关强度强相关，且持久时间随深度增加；消融持久SAE特征后自相关下降。

Conclusion: 深层潜特征在更长上下文跨度上保持，支持"深度递增抽象"的观点，并提供了基于自相关的可定量检验框架。

Abstract: The hidden layers of Large Language Models (LLMs) are believed to encode representations of the data whose abstraction increases with depth. However, in the absence of an operational notion of abstraction, this statement is hard to test quantitatively. Here, we propose an alternative view, building on the principle that the correlations of a sequence reflect the structure of the process that generated it. We define the autocorrelation function of a layer as the average dot product between its activations at two context positions separated by a lag. Latent variables encoded persistently along the context create slowly decaying tails in the autocorrelation, and we find these tails to grow across layers in both synthetic and natural data. In synthetic data with controlled hierarchical latent structure, this growth stems from the encoding of abstract latent variables, which govern long stretches of the input. Indeed, the persistence of latent codes predicts the qualitative structure of autocorrelations and accounts for most of the empirical measures. In real text, we take sparse-autoencoder features as proxies for latent variables, and find that their persistence correlates strongly with the magnitude of autocorrelations across datasets and LLMs. In particular, the persistence time increases with depth, as do autocorrelations, implying that deeper latent features persist for longer context spans. We complement this analysis by explicitly ablating the persistent sparse-autoencoder features and observing a consequent decrease in the autocorrelations.

</details>


### [172] [RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$: On-Device Reinforcement Learning Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18035)
*Zexin Li,Xinwei Gao,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RL^RT 是一个在嵌入式 GPU 上运行的端侧强化学习 runtime，通过"刷新上界"和带 slack 感知的 GPU permit 机制，把 actor（策略推理）与 learner（训练）的耦合变成可调参数，从而在保证实时性的同时利用空闲时间训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧 RL 需在同一块嵌入式 GPU 上周期性运行 actor 和 learner，且与其他负载共享资源。由于 learner 负责产生 actor 的权重，现有设计要么让 actor 停顿等待新权重，要么放任策略漂移（使用过期权重），难以兼顾实时性与策略新鲜度。

Method: 提出 RL^RT runtime：(1) 引入刷新上界（refresh bound），限制两次刷新之间 actor 的发布次数；(2) 设计带 slack 感知准入的 GPU permit，优先保证 actor 执行，并在基础调度器之上用剩余空闲时间填充训练任务。

Result: 在两块 Jetson 板卡上，基于真实的权重刷新开销评测，RL^RT 将 16.8M 参数策略的截止期错失率控制在 1% 以下，而无该机制时错失率高达 85%–100%。

Conclusion: 将 actor–learner 耦合显式建模为可调参数，并配合优先级准入与空闲时间填充，可有效解决端侧 RL 实时性与训练共存的矛盾，显著降低截止期错失。

Abstract: On-device reinforcement learning runs a periodic actor and a learner on one embedded GPU shared with other workloads. Because the learner produces the actor's weights, existing designs stall the actor or let its policy drift. We present RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$, an on-device runtime that makes this coupling a tunable parameter: a refresh bound limits actor releases between refreshes, and a GPU permit with slack-aware admission prioritises the actor and fills spare time with training atop any base scheduler. Under the real refresh cost on two Jetson boards, RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$ keeps a 16.8 M-parameter policy's deadline misses below 1%, against 85 to 100% without it.

</details>


### [173] [Clean Tickets, Wrong Orders: Catching Silent Errors with a Language Model in Your Browser Tab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18284)
*Umar Ahmed,Maxim Dunaev,Mohammad Nour Al Awad,Sergey Ivanov,Ivan Khodnenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一个微调的0.8B小模型通过WebAssembly完全在浏览器本地运行，把用户输入的点单文本解析成结构化订单，并让访客体验和寻找"无声错误"（格式合规但无人下单）这一危险失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型做文本到结构解析存在两种失败：明显的格式错误（易被系统拒绝）与"无声错误"——输出格式干净合规但并非用户真实所下订单，后者更危险。作者希望通过可交互网页demo让公众直观认识该风险，同时验证浏览器端本地推理的可行性。

Method: 将微调的0.8B模型通过llama.cpp编译为WebAssembly，在访客浏览器标签页内完全本地运行，订单文本不出设备。页面边输入边解析订单，用移植为TypeScript的PIZZA基准原生匹配器对每张票据打分，对菜单外内容返回"?"使模型可面对开放网络；并提供交互功能让访客寻找或尝试制造无声错误。

Result: 在全部1,357条PIZZA测试订单上，浏览器部署模型有99.0%的输出与服务器端评估一致，取得相同精确匹配率0.882；另报告了其在3台消费级笔记本上的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 浏览器端WASM本地推理可高度复现服务器端评估结果，为隐私保护型、可交互的模型风险演示（尤其是无声错误）提供了可行且可信的实践方式。

Abstract: A small language model that turns a typed order into structure can fail in two ways. It can print something broken, which any system rejects. Or it can print a clean, well formed order that nobody placed. Our companion work calls the second kind a silent error, and it is the dangerous one. We present a web demo in which a fine-tuned 0.8B parameter model runs entirely inside the visitor's browser tab, through llama.cpp compiled to WebAssembly. No order text leaves the device. The page parses orders as the visitor types, scores every ticket with the PIZZA benchmark's own matcher ported to TypeScript, refuses anything off the menu with "?" so the model can face the open web, and lets visitors hunt for silent errors or try to cause one. Run in the browser on all 1,357 PIZZA test orders, the deployed model writes the same output as the server evaluation for 99.0% of them and reaches the same exact match (0.882); we also report its latency on 3 consumer laptops. Demo: https://wrong.cuyor.com

</details>


### [174] [Enrichment-Oriented Virtual Screening of Perovskite Solar Cells through Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18043)
*Yezeyuan Zheng,Ziyang Hu,Maojun Sun. Ruijian Han,Jian Huang,Guanhua Chen,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出替代模型引导的强化学习框架，将钙钛矿配方生成视为虚拟筛选，用TabPFN引导生成并提升候选清单中的高效邻近命中率，但结果仅支持富集导向的优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿太阳能电池实验优化受吸收层组成、器件层与工艺组合多样性制约，需在制备前用虚拟筛选优先排序配方。

Method: 构建代理模型引导的强化学习框架；冻结的TabPFN引导配方生成；以890个实测器件作参考池进行邻域评估；在1846个器件上五折评估；比较3种学习策略与6种基线（每轮3000次代理评估）。

Result: top-10选择将平均PCE从19.68%提升至23.74%；K=100时PPO/RPPO邻域支持命中率为23%/24%（随机2.9%的7.9/8.3倍），最佳基线（L-SHADE与Ax BO并列）为10%（3.4倍）；9000配方中65.4%在相似度0.80匹配组成族，0.90时降至11.7%。

Conclusion: 支持富集导向的候选优先级排序，但不支持实验性能、种子鲁棒性或等预算优势的结论。

Abstract: Experimental optimization of perovskite solar cells is constrained by combinatorial diversity in absorber compositions, device layers, and processing, which makes virtual screening attractive for prioritizing recipes before fabrication. We develop a surrogate-guided reinforcement-learning framework that casts recipe generation as virtual screening, enriching candidate shortlists near high-performing experimental neighbors. A frozen Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network (TabPFN) guides recipe generation, while a reference pool of 890 measured devices enables neighborhood-based assessment. Five-fold evaluation on 1,846 devices shows top-ten selection raises mean power conversion efficiency (PCE) from 19.68\% to 23.74\%. Three learned policies are compared with six baselines under 3,000 surrogate evaluations per run. In top-100 shortlists, Proximal policy optimization (PPO) and recurrent PPO (RPPO) achieve neighborhood-supported hit rates of 23\% and 24\% (7.9- and 8.3-fold Pure Random's 2.9\%), versus 10\% (3.4-fold) for the baselines with the highest supported hit rate (L-SHADE and Ax BO, tied) at $K = 100$. Gains coexist with weak-support fractions. Of 9,000 recipes, 65.4\% match a composition family at similarity 0.80, falling to 11.7\% at 0.90. Results support enrichment-oriented prioritization but not experimental performance, seed robustness, or matched-budget advantage.

</details>


### [175] [f-Divergence Self-Play for Tabular Anomaly Detection via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18370)
*Hoang Tran Vuong,Linh Ngo Van,Dang Nguyen,Thin Nguyen,Phuoc Nguyen,Mehrtash Harandi,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiSPaT，一个自博弈微调框架，用f-散度最小化理论强化LLM对表数据"正常性"的建模，在表格异常检测中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据混合数值、类别、文本等异构特征，难以学习正常性表示。已有LLM方法将行序列化为文本，但仅用一次性监督微调，对"收紧正常描述"的信号有限。

Method: 基于f-散度最小化，推导训练目标与缩小真实正常数据分布差距的紧致近似。采用交替优化：当前策略生成合成样本作为伪异常，判别器（critic）学习区分其与真实正常样本，该信号驱动策略更新，逐步对齐模型分布与真实正常分布。

Result: 在多样基准上，DiSPaT一致优于先前的LLM方法、深度学习方法及经典无监督表格异常检测器。

Conclusion: 自博弈与散度最小化结合的微调能有效增强LLM对正常性的刻画，为异构表格异常检测提供更强的框架。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data poses significant challenges due to heterogeneous feature types-mixing numerical, categorical, and textual attributes, which complicate learning meaningful representations of normality. Recent work has applied large language models (LLMs) to this problem by serializing table rows as text sequences, yet these approaches rely on one-shot supervised fine-tuning that offers limited signal to tighten the model's description of normality. We propose DiSPaT, a self-play fine-tuning framework that strengthens the model's understanding of normal data. Building on the theoretical foundation of f-divergence minimization, we derive a tight approximation connecting our training objective to reducing the distributional gap between real normal data and model-generated samples. DiSPaT operates through an alternating optimization: at each iteration, the current policy generates synthetic samples that serve as pseudo-anomalies, while a critic discriminator learns to distinguish these from real normal samples; this signal drives policy updates that progressively align the model distribution with the true normal-data distribution. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DiSPaT consistently outperforms prior LLM-based methods, deep learning approaches, and classical unsupervised detectors for tabular anomaly detection.

</details>


### [176] [When Copying Is Hard to Beat: A Negative Result in Clinical Assessment Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18142)
*Marius Fracarolli,Stefan Riezler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告一个负结果：在ICU次日临床评估预测任务上，所评估的模型相对基于持续性的强基线没有稳定优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生可查看前一日问卷答案，形成文档持续性（persistence）这一强大基线；作者想检验复杂模型是否真能在次日临床评估预测上超越该基线，并厘清评估设置如何影响结论。

Method: 在29,715个ICU日对上预测45个条目，采用患者不相交划分；比较生理信息引导的门控模型与单行临床规则、四个模型检查点（三族），并系统变动生理窗口截止时间、是否排除来自父答案的68.5%单元格、以及解码规则（分类读出、仅状态读出、仅预测解码、转移矩阵）。

Result: 门控模型item-macro-F1仅比临床规则高+0.0019 [+0.0006,+0.0031]但准确率下降；窗口截止于前次表格参考时间时优势为+0.0001 [-0.0006,+0.0008]（不成立）；排除父答案派生单元格后同样无优势，两者结合则略处劣势；四个检查点均不及持续性，微调模型仅在两格上不同；分类读出将变化单元格准确率提升八倍，但仅状态读出总体得分高于仅预测解码并与转移矩阵相当。

Conclusion: 负结果说明，文档流程、输入截止时间、观测掩码与解码规则共同决定了关于“预测记录化评估”的结论，未来研究需在这些设置上谨慎对照持续性基线。

Abstract: We report a negative result in next-day clinical assessment forecasting: the evaluated models show no consistent advantage over strong persistence-based baselines. Clinicians can view the previous day's questionnaire answers, a plausible source of documentation persistence. Predicting 45 items on 29,715 ICU day-pairs with patient-disjoint splits, a physiology-informed gated model improves item-macro-F1 over a one-line clinical rule by +0.0019 [+0.0006,+0.0031], while losing accuracy. No macro-F1 advantage is established when the physiology window ends at the previous form's reference time (+0.0001 [-0.0006,+0.0008]), or when scoring excludes the 68.5% of cells derived from parent answers; combining both changes yields a small disadvantage. Four checkpoints across three families underperform persistence, and a fine-tuned one differs from it on only two cells. A categorical read-out raises changed-cell accuracy eightfold, but a state-only read-out scores higher overall than forecast-only decoding and matches a transition matrix. This negative result highlights how the documentation process, input cutoff, observation mask and decoding rule shape conclusions about forecasting recorded assessments.

</details>


### [177] [STAMP: Manufacturing-Informed Generative Design and Shape Optimization of Sheet Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18144)
*Jihoon Kim,Jun Hyeok Song,Wonho Ha,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAMP 把成形板材的几何制造约束直接嵌入形状表示（材料布局+参考曲面高度场+固定名义厚度），配合边界条件扩散生成与预测梯度优化，在汽车发动机罩内板上实现可制造面板的生成与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式设计板材面板时，需要在探索布局、提升性能的同时保证几何可制造性（如避免参考面底切、厚度固定）。以往做法常靠惩罚项约束，难以保证所有生成结果都满足制造条件，且依赖大规模训练数据。

Method: 提出 STAMP 表示：每个面板由材料布局和参考曲面描述（沿拉伸方向垂直平面每个占据位置一个高度），名义厚度沿法向给定；支持曲面、加强筋和开口，排除底切，无需制造惩罚项。用边界条件扩散模型生成替代布局，并以预测器梯度引导曲面优化，同时保留轮廓、开口和固定区域。在 CarHoods10k 汽车发动机罩内板数据上验证。

Result: 相同存储量下深度图重建曲面精度优于点云与体素基线；仅 5 个训练设计时三次训练有一次产出新颖多样布局；500 个设计（训练集 6.5%）即达到全量数据的布局多样性；11 个留出设计族中基于预测器的挑选平均距最优 FEA 变形 0.18 mm（随机为 1.59 mm），全部 11 族均优于随机；在 10% 深度变化约束下，未见网络预测 6 个面板平均变形降低 24.8%，质量平均增加 3.2%。

Conclusion: 将制造条件内嵌于形状表示，使所有生成与优化结果天然可制造，且不依赖训练集规模；工程师可在该制造知情设计空间中高效探索与优化面板。

Abstract: Generative design of formed sheet panels must preserve geometric manufacturing conditions while exploring layouts and improving performance. STAMP embeds these conditions directly in the shape representation. Each panel is described by its material layout and a reference surface with one height at each occupied position in a plane perpendicular to the drawing direction. Nominal thickness is prescribed along the normal. The representation allows curved surfaces, ribs, and openings. It excludes reference-surface undercuts and keeps nominal thickness fixed without manufacturing penalty terms. Because these conditions belong to the representation, every generated and optimized panel satisfies them regardless of training-set size. Boundary-conditioned diffusion generates alternative layouts, and predictor gradients guide a surface optimization that retains outlines, openings, and designated fixed regions. We demonstrate STAMP on automotive hood inner panels using CarHoods10k. With a comparable number of stored values, depth maps reconstruct surfaces more accurately than point-cloud and voxel baselines. From only five training designs, one of three training runs yields novel and varied layouts. A generator trained on 500 designs, 6.5% of the training set, already reaches the layout variety of the full-data model. Across 11 held-out design families, predictor-based selection among existing dataset designs yields an average gap of 0.18 mm to the best available FEA deformation, versus an expected 1.59 mm for random selection. It does better than random selection in all 11 families. At a 10% depth-change bound, a network not used in the search predicts an average deformation reduction of 24.8% for six generated and existing panels, with a 3.2% average mass increase computed from geometry. Engineers can thus explore and optimize panels within this manufacturing-informed design space.

</details>


### [178] [Which Model, For Which Variable? Per-Variable Forecast Combination in Sparse ICU Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18149)
*Marius Fracarolli,Stefan Riezler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在五个ICU数据库上对94个生理变量做24小时预测，证明不存在"单一最优架构"，按变量加权组合14个模型可在全部数据库上超过最优单模型6.1–11.0% MSE，且增益集中于稀疏变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测论文通常只报告单一最佳架构，但这种"谁最好"的结论隐含有变量特异的差异：没有模型在每个变量上都最优，胜出者随变量而变。作者希望系统地检验这一点，并利用这种异质性提升预测。

Method: 在MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV、eICU、HiRID和UMM五个ICU数据库上，对94个通道做24小时预测，使用由14个模型构成的模型池，并按变量拟合组合权重；与最优单模型、无拟合权重的排序调和加权（每变量取前五模型）、简单的等权平均进行对比。随后将模型池扩展为按通道的梯度提升树、ARIMA和ETS，重新评估。

Result: 测试集上，组合模型在五个数据库均优于最优单模型，MSE降低6.1–11.0%（平均8.7%）；最稀疏三分之一的通道提升11.9%、检验类数值提升7.5%，而最稠密三分之一仅提升3.1%。基于排序的调和加权（无拟合权重）可恢复约五分之四的增益。扩展模型池后，梯度提升树成为五个数据库上经验证选择的最优单模型，但按变量组合仍比它再提升2.9–5.9%（五个中四个显著），等权平均则无此效果。

Conclusion: 与单变量基准一致，学习式组合优于简单平均，但前提是权重受到约束；作者给出了"何时选哪个模型"的建议，并显示偏好排序会随数据库规模变化。

Abstract: Clinical forecasting papers typically report a single best architecture. We show that on multivariate ICU physiology this question conceals variable-specific differences: no architecture is best on every variable, and which model wins changes from variable to variable.
