Across five ICU databases (MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, eICU, HiRID, and UMM (University Medicine Mannheim), a German single-center ICU) we forecast 94 channels over a 24-hour horizon with a pool of 14 models, and combine them with weights fitted per variable. On test, the combination beats the best single model on all five databases by 6.1-11.0% mean squared error (mean 8.7%). The gain is concentrated where clinical data is thinnest: the sparsest third of channels improves by 11.9% and laboratory values by 7.5%, against 3.1% for the densest third. Rank-based harmonic weighting of the best five models per variable, with no fitted weights at all, recovers about four-fifths of the gain. Extending the pool afterwards with a per-channel gradient-boosted tree, ARIMA and ETS makes the tree the validation-selected best single model on all five databases; per-variable combination still improves on it by 2.9-5.9% (significant on four of five), while equal weighting of the same members does not. As on univariate benchmarks, learned combination beats simple averaging - but only once the weights are constrained. We also report which model to prefer when, and show that the ordering changes with database size.

</details>


### [179] [On the Limits of Sufficient Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18163)
*Klaus Weinbauer,Peter F. Stadler,Thomas Gärtner,Sagar Malhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化刻画了结构化数据（图、字符串等）上分类器充分解释的存在条件，并给出评估充分性上限的统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 充分解释已被广泛用于图、字符串、图像等多种模态，但缺乏统一的形式化分析，无法判断某任务上充分解释是否可能存在。

Method: 定义由实例空间、分类器和select关系（规定可被选为解释的子实例/特征子集）组成的通用解释形式化框架；分析分类器与select关系的交互如何决定充分解释器的存在性；将分析扩展到概率充分性和删除充分性；据此设计统计量以界定任务可达到的最大充分性。

Result: 在语言、蛋白质和分子分类数据集上评估这些统计量，发现多数任务上完全充分性无法达到；该上界对任意解释器均成立。

Conclusion: 充分解释的存在性由分类器与select关系共同决定；所提边界提供了任务级参照，可在绝对意义上评判任意解释器的充分性。

Abstract: We formally characterize the conditions under which sufficient explanations exist for classifiers on structured data such as graphs and strings. We focus on post-hoc explainers that select parts of the input as sufficient explanations. An explanation is sufficient if the selected part alone ensures the predicted label. Sufficient explanations have been widely adopted across data modalities, including graphs, strings, and images. However, a unified formal analysis of sufficient explanations remains missing. We bridge this gap with four main contributions: (i) We define a general explanation formalism consisting of an instance space, a classifier, and a select relation. The select relation specifies which potential sub-instances or feature subsets can be selected as explanations from each instance; (ii) We show that the existence of sufficient explainers is fully characterized by the interplay between the classifier and the select relation; (iii) We extend this analysis to practically relevant variants of sufficiency, including probabilistic sufficiency and deletion sufficiency; (iv) Based on the formal analysis, we devise statistics that bound the maximum achievable sufficiency for a given task. We evaluate these statistics on language, protein, and molecular classification datasets and find that full sufficiency is unattainable on most of them. Since these bounds hold for every explainer, they provide a task-level reference against which any explainer's sufficiency can be judged in absolute terms.

</details>


### [180] [MarathonTS: a benchmark of long synthetic time series from window assembly and long-series generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18190)
*Ilyas Varshavskiy,Sergey Shulgin,Shuhrat Khalilbekov,Bonu Boboeva,Azizjon Azimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MarathonTS 是首个在同一协议下比较"窗口拼接式"与"长序列直接生成式"两类合成长时序方法的开放基准，覆盖 9 个公开小时级数据集、83 项保真度指标、14 项 TSTR 任务、记忆性与计算成本评估，并附带独立的统计分析阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多合成时序应用需要比生成模型训练窗口长得多的序列。现有做法分两类：从短窗口池中拼接（需决定选哪些窗口、顺序、接缝处理），或直接用长序列生成器。此前缺少在统一协议下对这两类方法的对比评测。

Method: 所有方法在 9 个公开小时级数据集上、10 个随机种子下生成等长序列。评估维度包括：(1) 保真度——12 组共 83 项指标，对照 train、validation 和 out-of-time 三个真实参考；(2) 效用——仅用合成数据训练、真实数据测试（TSTR vs TRTR），涵盖 14 个分类/回归任务；(3) 对训练数据的记忆程度；(4) 计算成本。统计分析独立成阶段，读取已发布的表格，输出各切片排名、带检验与不确定度的研究问题回答，以及预设的确证性族结果。

Result: 主实验中，方法在确证性设计的两个单元格上均有差异；窗口选择比接缝处理更重要，接缝处理并未带来提升；没有任何方法能让下游模型达到真实序列的训练效果。排行榜的顶层梯队可在其他硬件上复现，但梯队内的具体名次不可复现。

Conclusion: 两类合成长时序方法可在统一协议下公平比较，其中窗口选择是关键因素，接缝处理收益有限，合成数据与真实数据在下游效用上仍有差距。代码、数据哈希与全部结果均已公开，以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Many applications of synthetic time series require a series that is much longer than the windows on which a generative model is trained. Such a series can be assembled from a pool of short generated windows, which requires deciding which windows are taken, in what order, and how their seams are processed, or it can be produced directly by a long-series generator. This study presents MarathonTS, to our knowledge the first open benchmark that compares both families under one protocol. Every method produces a series of the same length on 9 open hourly datasets with 10 seeds. The series is evaluated by its fidelity to the real data (83 metrics in 12 groups, against three real references: train, validation and an out-of-time split), by its utility (models are trained on the synthetic series only and tested on real data, TSTR against TRTR, in 14 classification and regression tasks), by its memorisation of the training data and by its computational cost. The statistical analysis is carried out as a separate stage that reads the published tables and gives the rankings of every method in every slice, the answers to the research questions with tests and uncertainty, and the results of a pre-specified confirmatory family. In the main run, the methods differ in both confirmatory cells; the choice of windows matters more than the processing of their seams, which does not improve the series, and no method trains the downstream models as well as the real series. The top tier of the leaderboard is reproduced on other hardware, whereas the exact places within it are not. For the reproducibility of the study, the code, the data hashes and all results are publicly available (links below).

</details>


### [181] [T2ST: Data-Efficient Spatio-Temporal Foundation Models via Temporal Data and Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18199)
*Yue Xu,Kaiyun Wei,Wenying Duan,Zichen Xu,Shuai Ma,Xiaoxi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T2ST 提出一种数据高效的时空基础模型训练框架，利用有限的时空观测配合选定的辅助时间序列，并从冻结的时间序列基础模型中蒸馏知识，仅用10%数据即可达到与全量数据相当甚至更优的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空基础模型（STFMs）需要大规模训练语料，但同步观测与空间元数据难以获取和整理，导致合适数据稀缺；而时间序列数据丰富且易采集，每个位置的观测本身即构成时间序列，因此希望利用丰富的时间序列数据来弥补时空观测的不足。

Method: 核心洞见是：时间模式学习可借助丰富的外部时间序列，而空间依赖学习仍需基于真实时空观测。据此，T2ST 通过检索匹配序列来强化稀疏观测的时间模式，并选择可靠的补充序列以扩大时间覆盖；同时采用时间关系蒸馏对齐历史片段间关系，以及多尺度预测蒸馏在粗细时间分辨率上迁移预测行为，实现跨异构架构的知识迁移。

Result: 仅使用原始时空数据的10%，T2ST 使 STFMs 在6个长期和12个短期预测数据集上的整体预测性能与全量数据模型相当或更优。

Conclusion: T2ST 为时空基础模型的数据高效训练建立了新范式，证明稀有时空数据下借助丰富时间序列与知识蒸馏可有效训练 STFM。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal foundation models (STFMs) require large-scale training corpora, yet synchronized observations and spatial metadata are costly to acquire and curate, limiting the availability of suitable data. By contrast, time-series data are abundant and easier to collect. Since observations at each location form a time series, this motivates a natural question: can abundant time-series data enhance STFMs when spatio-temporal observations are scarce? To bridge this gap, we propose T2ST, a data-efficient training framework that augments limited spatio-temporal observations with selected auxiliary time series and transfers knowledge from a frozen time-series foundation model. Our key insight is that temporal pattern learning can benefit from abundant external time series, while spatial dependency learning remains grounded in real spatio-temporal observations. Guided by this insight, T2ST retrieves matched sequences to reinforce sparsely observed temporal patterns and selects reliable complementary sequences to broaden temporal coverage. Temporal relation distillation aligns relationships among historical patches, while multi-scale prediction distillation transfers forecasting behavior at fine and coarse temporal resolutions, enabling knowledge transfer across heterogeneous architectures. Using only 10\% of the original spatio-temporal data, T2ST enables STFMs to achieve overall forecasting performance competitive with or superior to their full-data counterparts across six long-term and twelve short-term forecasting datasets. These results establish a new paradigm for data-efficient training of spatio-temporal foundation models. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/T2ST-65F3/.

</details>


### [182] [NeoResearch: Literature- and Experience-Guided Autonomous Time Series Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18235)
*Ze Guo,Mingyue Cheng,Xiaoyu Tao,Shuo Yu,Shangtong Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeoResearch：以LLM为研究者的文献与经验引导自主研究框架，发现轻量级领域特定时间序列预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建有效预测模型需大量专家知识、网络科学文献证据与迭代实验，而将知识与研究经验转化为模型设计需保留来源语境并在任务特定假设下验证适用性，现有方法难以支撑。

Method: 建立专家式双循环研究流程，将假设驱动的架构探索与迭代训练/超参优化通过实验反馈耦合；智能体自主挖掘网络文献，抽取建模机制、实证证据与适用条件以形成可检验的架构假设；采用分层经验记忆，将文献发现与实验结果关联并整合条件性经验，指导架构发现与训练优化并支持跨任务复用。

Result: 在十个数据集上实验，表现出有竞争力的性能，并验证了双循环、文献抽取与记忆复用各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM智能体可结合文献知识与实验经验自主发现轻量级领域特定预测模型，双循环流程与分层记忆有助于知识的复用与落地。

Abstract: Developing effective forecasting models for diverse time-series domains requires substantial expert knowledge, evidence from scientific literature on the web, and iterative experimentation. However, translating this knowledge and research experience into model designs requires preserving source context and testing applicability under task-specific assumptions. In this paper, we propose NeoResearch, a literature- and experience-guided autonomous research framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) as researchers to discover lightweight, domain-specific forecasting models. Specifically, NeoResearch establishes an expert-inspired dual-loop research workflow, coupling hypothesis-driven architecture exploration with iterative training and hyperparameter optimization through experimental feedback. To enable knowledge-grounded discovery, the agent autonomously mines scientific literature from the Web, extracting modeling mechanisms, empirical evidence, and applicability conditions to formulate task-grounded, testable architecture hypotheses. Furthermore, a hierarchical experience memory links literature findings to experimental outcomes and consolidates conditional lessons, guiding architecture discovery and training optimization while supporting reuse across forecasting tasks. Experiments on ten datasets show competitive performance and support dual loops, literature extraction, and memory reuse. Our code is available.

</details>


### [183] [Small Is Not Dead: Distance-Aware Cluster Revival for e-means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18238)
*Eyal Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 e-means（FAISS 中的 mini-batch k-means）的"死簇复活"机制加了一条基于簇心距离的附加条件，避免误删覆盖稀有孤立点群的簇心，在保持原基准结果不变的同时显著降低含孤立小群数据的测试误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: e-means 的死簇复活机制仅依据簇大小决定是否删除并重用簇心，无法区分"冗余而小"与"覆盖稀有孤立点群而小"的簇，在含孤立点群的数据上反复删掉代价高昂的簇心，导致误差升高。

Method: 在原有计数规则上增加距离条件：仅当小簇簇心与另一簇心距离较近（相对于运行估计的均方误差）时才删除该簇。理论依据是：当簇心等于其点均值时，该平方距离是删心导致每点平均平方误差增量的上界。实现成本为每五个 mini-batch 对小小簇做一次最近簇心搜索，无可测运行时间增加。仅为 CPU 概念验证。

Result: 在 e-means 原嵌入基准上从不阻止删除，结果与 FAISS 完全一致；在开发阶段未使用的含孤立小群数据集上，两轮后测试误差相比纯计数复活降低约 10%–80%，并保留 k-means++ 初始化的优势；其他评估数据集上与"开/关复活"中较优者相差约 1% 以内。另外报告了分析预测的两种失效模式。

Conclusion: 基于簇心距离的第二条件能以近零成本修复死簇复活的盲目性，在不损害原有性能的前提下提升含稀有孤立群数据上的稳健性；GPU 实现留待未来工作。

Abstract: e-means (Bessa et al., 2026) is a recent mini-batch k-means algorithm, now part of the FAISS library, that reaches the error of Lloyd's algorithm about an order of magnitude faster on large embedding datasets. Every five mini-batches, it removes every cluster whose running point count falls below a tenth of the average and reuses its centre to split a large cluster. In the authors' ablation, removing this mechanism, called dead-cluster revival, raises the error more than removing any other component added to the moving-average update. The rule, however, considers only cluster size: it cannot distinguish a cluster that is small because it is redundant from one that covers a rare, isolated group of points, and on data with such groups it repeatedly removes centres whose removal is costly. We add a second condition, based on the distance between centres: a small cluster is removed only if its centre is close to another centre, relative to a running estimate of the mean squared error. When a centre equals the mean of its points, this squared distance is an upper bound on the average increase in squared error, per point of the cluster, caused by removing the centre. The condition costs one nearest-centre search for the small clusters every five mini-batches and adds no measurable running time. On the e-means paper's embedding benchmarks, it never prevents a removal, so the results are identical to those of FAISS. On datasets with small isolated groups not used during development, it reduces the test error after two epochs by roughly 10% to 80% relative to count-based revival, and it preserves the advantage of k-means++ initialization; on the other evaluation datasets, it stays within about 1% of the better of revival enabled and disabled. We also report two failure modes that the analysis predicts. This is a CPU proof of concept; GPU implementations are left to future work.

</details>


### [184] [Neural Classifiers as Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18239)
*Sergey Salishev,Anton Makarov,Oleg Granichin,Renata Avros,Zeev Volkovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架，将监督深度分类视为香农意义下纠错码的解码问题，并据此解释损失函数、决策边界几何与现代表示几何，并导出量化、蒸馏与正则化新准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度分类为何依赖交叉熵、softmax 决策边界为何稳定、Neural Collapse 与 LeJEPA 等表示几何为何出现，目前缺乏统一的解释框架；作者希望用编码理论给出一个连贯的理论视角。

Method: 建立监督分类与纠错码最大似然解码的对应：交叉熵优化使 softmax 分类器与 ML 解码对齐，logits 映射为到类专属码字的 Hamming 距离；由此推导 KL、Jeffreys 等散度目标的出现，并分析 Neural Collapse 与 LeJEPA 几何在同性噪声下作为最优星座的性质；同时指出深度网络在特征空间隐式学习码本。

Result: 该框架解释了标准散度目标的来源与决策边界的几何稳定性，把 Neural Collapse 与 LeJEPA 识别为各向同性噪声下的最优解码星座，并区别于经典 ECOC 说明码本由网络隐式习得；基于码冗余导出无损量化、改进知识蒸馏和架构正则化的新准则。

Conclusion: 分类可被理解为纠错码解码，这一视角统一了损失设计、表示几何与现代模型中的潜空间结构，并扩展到自监督编码器，为理解深度表示几何提供凝聚解释和可操作的设计准则。

Abstract: A unified framework is proposed that characterizes supervised deep classification as the decoding of error-correcting codes in the Shannon sense. Cross-entropy optimization is shown to align softmax classifiers with maximum-likelihood decoding, where logits map to Hamming distances to class-specific codewords. This correspondence explains the emergence of standard divergence objectives (e.g., Kullback-Leibler, Jeffreys) and provides a geometric basis for the stability of decision boundaries. Furthermore, the geometries of Neural Collapse and LeJEPA are identified as optimal constellations for decoding under isotropic noise. Unlike classical Error-Correcting Output Codes, deep networks are shown to learn effective codebooks in the feature space implicitly. This insight is leveraged to derive novel criteria for lossless quantization, improved knowledge distillation, and architectural regularization based on code redundancy. Finally, this perspective is extended to self-supervised encoders, offering a cohesive explanation for the geometry of latent representations in modern neural models.

</details>


### [185] [ParetoM$^3$: Learning on the Pareto Set under Preference Guidance via Min-Max-Min Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18252)
*Pei Tang,Songtao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出惩罚式min-max-min（M³）问题与单循环一阶算法ParetoM³，在偏好函数引导下于弱Pareto集上求解多目标优化，并给出非渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标间天然冲突，需从Pareto集中选出符合偏好的折中解；如何在非凸多目标下统一"偏好优化"与"弱Pareto最优性"的求解仍具挑战。

Method: 利用在弱Pareto集上取零的merit函数，构造统一的惩罚式min-max-min（M³）目标，将偏好优化与弱Pareto最优性约束合为一体；并设计单循环一阶算法ParetoM³求其驻点解。

Result: 在局部Kurdyka–Łojasiewicz假设下，证明惩罚参数足够大时ParetoM³可找到M³问题的ε-驻点，且对原问题近似弱Pareto最优，并给出显式非渐近收敛速率；同时表明该驻点概念在同类假设下可覆盖近似偏好驻点与近似KKT条件等已有准则。

Conclusion: ParetoM³在合成基准、图像分类、回归与数学推理实验中均能可靠获得符合偏好的弱Pareto最优解，理论与实用性兼具。

Abstract: Learning across multiple inherently conflicting objectives requires selecting desirable trade-offs from the Pareto set. In this work, we study preference-guided multi-objective optimization, where a predefined preference function is minimized over the weak Pareto set of multiple nonconvex objectives. Leveraging a merit function that vanishes on the weak Pareto set, we propose a penalty-based min-max-min formulation, termed the M$^3$ problem, which unifies preference optimization and the enforcement of weak Pareto optimality in a single objective. We further develop ParetoM$^3$, a single-loop first-order algorithm for computing stationary solutions of the resulting M$^3$ problem. Under a local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz assumption, we prove that ParetoM$^3$ finds an $ε$-stationary point of the M$^3$ problem that is also approximately weakly Pareto optimal for the original multi-objective problem when the penalty parameter is sufficiently large, with explicit non-asymptotic convergence rates. We also show that this stationarity notion recovers existing optimality criteria, including approximate preference stationarity and approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions, under the same assumptions used in prior works. Experiments on a synthetic benchmark, image classification, regression, and math reasoning demonstrate that ParetoM$^3$ reliably attains preferred weak Pareto optimal solutions while achieving strong practical performance. Code is available at https://github.com/tpgoodboy/ParetoM3.

</details>


### [186] [Right Token, Right Time: From Per-Token Gating to Trajectory-Aware Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18274)
*Nima Eshraghi,Lovedeep Gondara,Sagarika Suresh,Xiaotong Xu,Jithin Pradeep,Fanny Chevalier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出激活引导（SAE steering）中"何时施加干预"是决定性因素，将引导重构为固定预算下的逐token分配问题，提出CFP与CLABS两种门控方法，在保持流畅性的同时取得最大行为转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SAE激活引导在每个生成token上都施加干预，使表示偏离模型训练分布，损害生成质量。作者认为干预的时机（when）是被忽视的关键控制轴。

Method: 将引导建模为固定可引导token预算下的逐token分配问题，使各方法仅在选择哪些token上不同。提出两种门控：CFP（无记忆，通过稀疏可解释特征投影选择与目标行为最不对齐的token）；CLABS（轨迹感知，把分配视为闭环反馈控制，跟踪已累积的对齐度并在落后处花费预算）。在线性引导响应模型上推导保证闭环稳定性的增益区间，并证明积分项减小稳态跟踪偏差。

Result: 在相同预算下，CFP与CLABS在各类预算门控中取得最大行为转变；CLABS在预算紧张（分配最关键）时表现最强，且最好地保持流畅性。

Conclusion: 干预时机是激活引导的有效控制轴，基于预算分配与闭环反馈的门控方法能在不牺牲生成质量的前提下实现更强行为控制。

Abstract: Activation steering via sparse autoencoders (SAEs) enables behavioral control of large language models without task-specific fine-tuning. Yet, standard methods apply the steering signal at every generated token, displacing representations from the distribution the model was trained on and eroding generation quality. We show that \emph{when} along a generation the intervention is applied is a decisive axis of control. We cast steering as a per-token allocation problem under a fixed budget of steerable tokens, so that methods differ only in which tokens they select, making allocation the sole variable. Within this framework we introduce two gates. The first, Contrastive Feature Projection (CFP), is memoryless and selects the tokens least aligned with the target behavior through a sparse, interpretable feature projection. The second, Closed-Loop Adaptive Bernoulli Steering (CLABS), is trajectory aware. It treats allocation as closed-loop feedback control, tracking the alignment a generation has accumulated so far and spending budget where the trajectory falls behind. On a linear steering-response model we derive an explicit gain region that guarantees closed-loop stability of CLABS and show that its integral term reduces the steady-state tracking offset. Empirically, CFP and CLABS achieve the largest behavioral shift among budgeted gates at matched budgets, with CLABS strongest under the tight budgets where allocation matters most, while best preserving fluency.

</details>


### [187] [Emulating physics-based XCO2 retrievals across OCO-2 viewing modes with compact neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18333)
*Xianlei San,Chengxing Zhai,Hui Su,Kuai Dai,Shuangchen Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用SVD压缩OCO-2光谱并结合小型MLP来模拟全物理CO2柱浓度反演，在多观测模式与多年份上取得约1 ppm量级误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星光谱的全物理XCO2反演计算代价高昂，需要一种高效且跨观测模式、跨年份稳定的替代（仿真）方法。

Method: 按波段对OCO-2光谱做SVD压缩，将光谱得分、先验信息与观测几何输入紧凑的多层感知机，预测全物理反演结果与其业务先验之间的差值，再加到先验上得到XCO2估计；用2015–2019年观测训练，2020年验证，2021–2025年测试。

Result: 2021–2025测试中，相对ACOS产品的年平均RMSE分别为海洋耀斑0.89 ppm、陆地耀斑1.35 ppm、天底观测1.43 ppm；在按已发表Transformer光谱模型筛选准则处理的东亚天底观测上，2020–2022年平均误差低于该模型报道值。

Conclusion: 紧凑神经网络结合压缩光谱可高效仿真全物理反演，并在多种观测模式与多年份上保持稳定精度。

Abstract: Operational full-physics retrieval of the column-averaged dry-air mole fraction of carbon dioxide from satellite spectra is computationally demanding. We use singular value decomposition to compress spectra from the Orbiting Carbon Observatory-2 separately by band. Compact multilayer perceptrons use the resulting spectral scores, prior information, and viewing geometry to predict the difference between the full-physics retrieval and its operational prior. Adding the predicted difference to the prior yields an estimate of the retrieved mole fraction. The models were trained on 2015-2019 observations and validated on 2020 observations. In 2021-2025 tests, the mean annual root-mean-square errors relative to the Atmospheric Carbon Dioxide Observations from Space retrieval product were 0.89, 1.35, and 1.43 parts per million for ocean glint, land glint, and nadir observations, respectively. On East Asian nadir observations screened using the published criteria for the spectral Transformer, our model yielded a lower mean annual error than reported for that model in 2020-2022. These results show that compact neural networks using compressed spectra can efficiently emulate the full-physics retrieval across multiple viewing modes and years.

</details>


### [188] [DECODE: Decomposable Environments for COntrolled Sequential-Decision Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18367)
*Diego Silva Prado,Bruno Caldas de Souza,Tiago Amaral Penteado de Jesus,Ricardo Guimarães Herrmann,Cristiano Breuel,Cristiano Bevitori Maffia De Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECODE 是一个在消费信贷催收场景下构建的基准，通过受控实验检验求解器改进方案，揭示规划视野、信息可得性与响应模型对决策得分的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当序列决策系统未达最优时，其得分无法说明哪些修改能改善决策，缺少可对照、可控的实验手段。

Method: 构建消费者信贷催收生成器，提供五个部分可观测环境（共享已知动态矩阵，变化记忆参数与过程噪声）；实验仅改动选定输入或规划规则，其余求解器固定，每次比较明确状态输入、响应输入与规划视野。

Result: 在确定性基础环境上，规划从1个月延至6个月使两个梯度提升求解器得分提高15–17个百分点（相对隐藏状态下的精确最优）；最优求解器仍比最优低15.6点；按序干预中，三项状态输入修改使得分降6.3点，替换为真实响应后再升15.6点，净恢复约9点。

Conclusion: 中间效应依赖响应模型与改动顺序，无法唯一归因；但在这些限制内，DECODE 使关于求解器性能的假设可在已知动态与明确信息条件下通过指定干预加以检验。

Abstract: When a sequential decision system falls short of an optimum, its score does not reveal which modifications would improve its decisions. DECODE is a benchmark for testing such modifications through controlled experiments on planning and available information. Its consumer-credit collections generator provides five partially observed environments that share known dynamics matrices and vary a memory parameter and process noise. The experiments change selected inputs or planning rules while keeping the rest of the solver fixed. Each comparison specifies the solver's state inputs, response inputs and planning horizon. On the deterministic base environment, which was selected for a large gap between six-month and one-month planning with the hidden state, extending planning from one to six months increases the scores of two gradient-boosted solvers by 15 to 17 percentage points of the exact optimum, computed with the hidden state and borrower traits. Even at this horizon, the best graded solver, whose repayment response is fitted on logs, remains 15.6 points below that optimum. In a specified intervention sequence, three changes to its state inputs and their evaluation during search lower its score by 6.3 points, and replacing its fitted repayment model with the true response, evaluated with hidden borrower traits, then raises it by 15.6, a net recovery of nine of those points. Because the intermediate effects are conditional on the repayment model and the order of the changes, they do not uniquely assign the gap to separate causes. Within these limits, DECODE makes hypotheses about solver performance testable through specified interventions under known dynamics and explicit information conditions.

</details>


### [189] [Where Tree Ensembles Decide: Structural Local Explanation with Decision Predicate Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18382)
*Sylvio Barbon Junior,Luca Longo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出局部决策谓词图（local DPG），将样本实际执行的树规则汇集为可验证记录，并从中选出"关键谓词"，给出预测可被改变的具体阈值及共享该阈值的树，无需外部解释器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树集成模型精度高，但单次预测的计算分散在数百条决策规则中。特征归因与反事实解释只涉及特征影响或输入变化，未指明预测到底响应哪些阈值；结构化解释压缩规则却丢失了背后的树，因此存在解释空白。

Method: 1) 构建local DPG：收集样本执行的规则，形成可验证的计算记录，类支持可复现每个被解释预测；2) 定义关键谓词——估计其跨越最能将模型推向目标类的已执行谓词，并提供具体阈值与共享该阈值的树，无需外部解释器学习；3) 在16个数据集、4类集成模型家族上，与随机谓词、最近阈值谓词、TreeSHAP首位特征规则对比；4) 通过植入故障检验阈值定位能力。

Result: 区分不同预测邻居的能力优于随机与最近阈值谓词；单次跨越在46–69%的错误预测中恢复真实类，而将同一规则用于TreeSHAP排名第一特征仅29–50%；在植入故障下，46–71%的误差中定位到故障阈值，随机仅6–21%。

Conclusion: 该关键谓词免训练编辑未能降低未见数据误差，但它通过指出预测可在何处被改变，补充了归因与反事实解释，揭示了阈值层级的可操作信息。

Abstract: Tree ensembles are among the most accurate models for tabular data, yet the computation behind a single prediction is spread over hundreds of decision rules. Feature attributions and counterfactuals explain such predictions through the influence of features or a change of the input, leaving open which of the thresholds applied by the trees a prediction responds to, while structural explanations compress these rules and lose the trees behind them. To address this gap, we propose a local Decision Predicate Graph (local DPG), which gathers the rules executed for a sample into a verifiable record of the computation, with a class support that reproduces every explained prediction. From these rules we select the critical predicate, the executed predicate whose crossing is estimated to move the model most strongly toward a chosen class, which offers the user a concrete threshold and the trees that share it, without an external explainer. Across 16 datasets and four ensemble families, it separates differently predicted neighbours better than random and nearest-threshold predicates. A single crossing recovers the true class in 46-69% of wrong predictions, against 29-50% when the same rule is applied to the feature ranked first by TreeSHAP, a measure close to the selection criterion, and on planted faults it finds the faulty threshold in 46-71% of the resulting errors, against 6-21% at random. Although training-free edits of these predicates did not reduce the error on unseen data, the critical predicate complements attributions and counterfactuals by naming where a prediction can be changed.

</details>


### [190] [WeatherDreamer: Learning a World Model from Multimodal Earth Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18393)
*Kate Duffy,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Thomas J. Vandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WeatherDreamer世界模型，仅从18个全球观测系统端到端学习"观测-潜状态-预报"循环，无需再分析资料，单GPU约1.5分钟生成逐小时全球分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型依赖独立且昂贵的资料同化(DA)系统（如每6小时更新一次的分析场），观测到观测的模型虽摆脱该依赖，但大多舍弃了DA中递归滤波循环（背景状态在稀疏、不规则观测间延续）的能力。

Method: 构建世界模型：从观测推断潜状态，用学习动力学逐小时向前传播，每小时用新观测校正，并可预测任一观测系统在任意位置的测量值；训练不依赖再分析，也不输入观测误差，通过多观测系统联合学习更新算子。

Result: 在留出站点，集成预报的温度、露点、风在12小时内超过NOAA GEFS，且校准优于GEFS与ECMWF集合；降水、云顶高度、太阳辐照度（推理时不作输入）短时效技巧持平或超过业务产品；相对GSI首猜，地表站点偏差在10%内、湿度敏感微波通道在25%内，但温度探测通道偏大至5倍；学到的观测误差接近微波探测仪与散射计风速的已知误差；可分离观测几何与大气状态，恢复83-96%临边变暗并生成无缝星下点影像。

Conclusion: 同化与预报可直接从观测中联合学习，实现低成本、逐小时全球分析，摆脱对再分析与独立DA系统的依赖。

Abstract: Machine learning weather models now rival operational global forecasts but still start from analyses produced by separate, expensive data assimilation (DA) systems, which for global models are typically updated every six hours. Observation-to-observation models avoid this dependence, but most omit the recursive filtering cycle that lets DA systems carry a background state through sparse, irregular observations. We introduce WeatherDreamer, a world model that learns this cycle end-to-end from 18 global observing systems, without reanalysis. It infers a latent state from observations, carries it forward hour by hour with learned dynamics, corrects it each hour with new observations, and can predict what any of its observing systems would measure at any location. At held-out stations, its ensembles beat NOAA's Global Ensemble Forecast System (GEFS) for temperature, dew point and wind out to 12 h and are better calibrated than GEFS and the ECMWF ensemble. Precipitation, cloud-top height and solar irradiance, never inputs at inference, are inferred with skill matching or exceeding operational products at short leads. Against the first guess of NOAA's Gridpoint Statistical Interpolation (GSI) DA system, WeatherDreamer's short-range departures are within 10% of GSI's at surface stations and within 25% for moisture-sensitive microwave channels, but up to five times larger for temperature-sounding channels. Never given observation errors, its learned update assumes errors close to known instrument and retrieval errors of microwave sounders and scatterometer winds. WeatherDreamer also separates viewing geometry from atmospheric state, recovering 83-96% of observed limb darkening and producing seamless nadir-view imagery. Producing an hourly global analysis in about 1.5 minutes on one GPU, WeatherDreamer shows that assimilation and forecasting can be learned together directly from observations.

</details>


### [191] [Deep Neural Networks Training Efficiency: Does Grokking Modify its Dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18396)
*Eduardo Cueto-Mendoza,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究grokking（过拟合后延迟出现的分布外泛化）是否改变深度网络的训练效率（单位能量下的准确率）。在模算术与MNIST两个基准上，使用16/32/64位精度及Orthograd，短程（≤500轮）与长程（≤20,000轮）实验均未发现grokking对效率曲线造成显著改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking现象已被广泛观察，但其对训练效率（准确率/能量）动态的影响尚不清楚。作者希望检验grokking是否会在效率通常单调下降的趋势中产生可察觉的偏离。

Method: 在模算术与MNIST两个典型场景中，训练两类神经网络，采用16、32、64位精度，并用Orthograd在不同强度下施加于训练早期以诱导grokking；设置短程（≤500 epoch）与长程（≤20,000 epoch）两组实验，以测试准确率在持续train-test差距后急剧提升来识别grokking，同时监测准确率/能量效率曲线。

Result: 短程实验：逐级增强Orthograd未能诱发grokking，效率平滑下降。长程实验：Orthograd成功诱发grokking，但效率曲线仍近似单调，在grokking转变处无明显局部上升。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，grokking并未实质性改变整体训练效率动态；单位能量准确率的下降趋势在grokking前后保持一致。

Abstract: We study whether grokking, the delayed emergence of out-of-distribution generalization after apparent overfitting, alters training efficiency in deep neural networks, where efficiency is measured as accuracy per unit energy. Using two canonical settings, modular arithmetic and MNIST, we analyze two neural network models under 16-, 32-, and 64-bit precision and use Orthograd to test whether grokking produces noticeable deviations from the typically decreasing dynamics of training efficiency. We consider both short training runs of up to 500 epochs and long training runs of up to 20,000 epochs, and in both cases identify grokking as a sharp improvement in test accuracy after a sustained train-test gap. In the short-horizon experiments, applying Orthograd at progressively stronger levels during early training does not induce grokking, and efficiency remains smoothly decreasing. In the long-horizon experiments, Orthograd induces grokking, but the efficiency curves remain approximately monotonic and show no appreciable local increase at the grokking transition. These results suggest that, in our setting, grokking does not materially alter overall training-efficiency dynamics.

</details>


### [192] [Fuse-PINN: Combining Finite Element Methods and Physics-Informed Neural Networks to enable massively parallel simulations of non-linear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18409)
*Stanislas Pamela,Nicolas Pailliez,Emmanuel Franck,Victor Michel-Dansac,Laurent Navoret,Vignesh Gopakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FEM-NN的数学严谨表述及Fuse-PINN框架，实现PINN在GPU高性能计算上的可扩展并行化，并在磁约束聚变相关非线性物理模型上取得近乎完美的多GPU扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN难以在高性能计算架构上扩展，无法处理大空间域内具有湍流动力学的非线性物理模型；多数研究局限于简单方程、少量变量的玩具模型，学界甚至普遍认为"PINN无法扩展"。

Method: 提出有限元法神经网络（FEM-NN）的数学严谨表述，并实现为模块化综合框架Fuse-PINN；该表述为PINN在基于GPU的HPC系统上的可扩展并行化奠定基础，同时引入能量自然梯度下降（Energy Natural Gradient Descent）优化器以实现高精度收敛。

Result: 在磁约束聚变相关的物理模型（包括具有细尺度动力学的非线性对流模型）上进行了验证；随着问题规模增大，多GPU运行实现了近乎完美的扩展性（near-perfect scaling）。

Conclusion: 该工作反驳了"PINN无法扩展"的观点，证明PINN可通过FEM-NN表述与并行框架在GPU-HPC上高效扩展，胜任大规模非线性湍流物理问题。

Abstract: Research in the domain of Physics-Informed Neural Networks applied to PDE modelling has advanced significantly in recent years, providing a wide ecosystem of novel methods. However, one frequent limitation is the inability of PINNs to scale out on High-Performance Computing architectures to address more ambitious challenges, particularly non-linear physics models with turbulent dynamics in large spatial domains. Typical studies restrain demonstrations to toy models with simple physics equations, few variables, and rarely any complex non-linear dynamics evolved over long timescales. Part of the AI community has even adopted the idea that "PINNs don't scale". This work proves otherwise. We present a mathematically robust formulation of Finite-Element-Method for Neural Networks (FEM-NN) implemented into a comprehensive, modular framework: Fuse-PINN. This formulation provides the basis for a sclable parallelisation of Physics-Informed Neural Networks on GPU-based HPC systems. Furthermore, an Energy Natural Gradient Descent optimiser is implemented to allow high-precision convergence. Fuse-PINN is demonstrated on physics models relevant to Magnetic Confinement Fusion, including non-linear convective models with fine-scale dynamics. Near-perfect scaling is achieved on multi-GPU runs with increasing problem size.

</details>


### [193] [PACT: A Decoder-Confidence-Guided Two-Round Receiver for Joint Channel and Phase-Noise Estimation in Sub-THz OFDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18450)
*Ruilin Wang,Xiaodai Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解码器置信度引导的两轮接收机，用PACT联合估计信道与相位噪声，在子THz OFDM下实现显著BER增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子THz OFDM编码接收同时受频率选择性衰落与振荡器相位噪声(PN)影响，二者联合补偿困难，现有学习型方法性能有限。

Method: 两轮解码器置信度引导接收机：导频感知复数Transformer(PACT)通过交互的复数分支联合估计频域信道与等效时域PN；结合带状LMMSE均衡与神经归一化最小和译码；利用译码伪导频与连续星座感知置信度，经PACT输入与注意力引导第二轮精修，两轮参数共享。

Result: Eb/N0=30 dB时，两个基准设置下码块错误率较最佳学习型基线分别降低约70%和57%；冻结权重测试显示可跨信道/PN模型、载频及FFT/带宽配置适用。

Conclusion: 解码器置信反馈与联合信道-PN估计能有效提升子THz OFDM接收性能，并具备良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Frequency-selective fading and oscillator phase noise (PN) jointly impair coded sub-THz OFDM reception. We propose a decoder-confidence-guided two-round receiver combining a {pilot-aware complex transformer} (PACT) estimator, banded linear minimum mean-square error equalization, and neural normalized min-sum decoding. PACT jointly estimates the frequency-domain channel and effective time-domain PN through interacting complex-valued branches. Decoder-derived pseudo-pilots and continuous constellation-aware confidence guide second-round refinement through PACT inputs and attention, with parameters shared across rounds. At $E_b/N_0=30$~dB, the PACT receiver achieves approximately $70\%$ and $57\%$ lower code-block error rates than the best-performing learning-based baseline in the two benchmark settings, respectively. Frozen-weight tests further demonstrate applicability across channel/PN models, carrier frequencies, and FFT/bandwidth configurations.

</details>


### [194] [Black-Box Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Models via Ensemble-of-Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18453)
*Wang Ma,Debarun Bhattacharjya,Junkyu Lee,Nhan H Pham,Harsha Kokel,Qiang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两级集成黑盒不确定性估计方法：内层为固定提示的随机解码，外层为语义保持的提示扰动；在嵌入空间用总协方差定律精确分解得到偶然不确定性(AU)与认知不确定性(EU)，仅需黑盒采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 可靠使用需要区分提示固有的歧义与知识缺口，但贝叶斯/深度集成方法在 LLM 规模下计算不可行且需白盒访问；现有黑盒方法只输出单一标量，无法区分 AU 与 EU。

Method: 两级集成：内层为固定提示的随机解码，外层为语义保持的提示扰动；在连续嵌入空间度量变异性，用总协方差定律实现 within/between 扰动的精确分解估计 AU/EU，仅需黑盒采样。另给出三项理论：(i) 嵌套蒙特卡洛的有限样本偏差恒等式及闭式偏差校正 EU 估计;(ii) 连接扰动总体 AU/EU 与贝叶斯目标的桥接界;(iii) 分离结果，证明仅一致性约束的黑盒方法必然漏检某类失败模式而扰动 EU 可检测。

Result: 在 5 个短问答基准、4 个指令微调 LLM(2 个家族、2 种规模)上，与强白盒基线相当；同等生成预算下错误排序达到领先黑盒方法水平且能分解 AU/EU；配对干预显示 AU/EU 分别选择性响应提示歧义与知识缺口；NQ 长答案上可扩展到声明级事实性而无需重训。

Conclusion: 两级集成实现了黑盒、可分解 AU/EU 的 LLM 不确定性估计，并有理论保证与跨模型/任务实证支持。

Abstract: Reliable use of large language models (LLMs) requires uncertainty estimates that separate ambiguity intrinsic to a prompt from genuine knowledge gaps. Bayesian and deep-ensemble methods deliver this aleatoric/epistemic decomposition in principle, but are computationally prohibitive at LLM scale and require white-box access. Existing black-box methods, in contrast, summarize to a single scalar that cannot make the distinction. We introduce a two-level ensemble that bridges this gap. Stochastic decoding of a fixed prompt forms the inner ensemble; meaning-preserving semantic perturbations of the prompt form the outer ensemble. Variability is measured in a continuous embedding space, and the law of total covariance gives an exact within-/between-perturbation decomposition to estimate aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty (AU/EU); it requires only black-box sampling access. On top of this estimator, we contribute three theoretical results that ground the framework: (i) a finite-sample bias identity for nested Monte Carlo with a closed-form bias-corrected epistemic estimator; (ii) a Bayesian bridge bounding the gap between perturbation-population AU/EU and Bayesian targets; and (iii) a separation result identifying a class of failure modes that any consistency-only black-box method provably misses while perturbation-based EU detects. Across five short QA benchmarks and four instruction-tuned LLMs spanning two families and two scales, our estimator is competitive with strong white-box baselines, and at a matched generation budget it matches leading black-box methods on error ranking while decomposing AU and EU; controlled paired interventions show that AU and EU respond selectively to distinct, actionable sources of failure: prompt ambiguity and model knowledge gaps; and a long-form pilot on the NQ long-answer split shows the framework extends to claim-level factuality without retraining.

</details>


### [195] [Sample-efficient prediction of high-entropy alloy strength through outcome-aware robust sequential learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18462)
*Ahmed Shoyeb Raihan,Md Habibor Rahman,Ashish Nimbarte,Imtiaz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ROSE-HEA，一种样本高效的高熵合金抗拉强度预测框架，其采集策略预判可能结果如何更新GP描述并重估参考域上的最坏情况预测风险，在501条记录的回顾性评估中以152.84 MPa的RMSE和0.7443的R²优于EI/UCB及八种传统回归器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金拉伸测试数据获取成本高、实验难度大，而准确的强度预测对成分与工艺决策至关重要，因此需要在有限训练预算下实现样本高效的UTS预测。

Method: 提出ROSE-HEA顺序实验框架：采集准则预测候选UTS结果将如何更新预测及互补高斯过程描述的支持度，再在参考域上重新评估剩余的最坏情况期望预测风险以选择信息量大的候选。基于501条HEA记录的回顾性评估，在30次匹配运行（共享初始观测与测试条件）中与EI、UCB贝叶斯优化、随机GP采样及八种传统回归器对比。

Result: 从25个初始观测出发再采集100个响应后，ROSE-HEA取得最低平均RMSE（152.84 MPa）和最高平均R²（0.7443），RMSE相对EI和UCB分别降低6.16%和6.37%；所选观测可提升六类回归器的预测并覆盖更广设计空间；仅用125个观测即超过最强传统回归器用185个观测的平均性能，相当于训练观测减少32.4%。

Conclusion: 结果证明了结果感知的鲁棒顺序学习在受限训练预算下改善HEA抗拉强度预测的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of high-entropy alloy (HEA) strength can guide composition and processing decisions; however, obtaining tensile-test data for HEAs can be costly and experimentally demanding. This study proposes Robust Outcome-aware Sequential Experimentation for High-Entropy Alloys (ROSE-HEA), a sample-efficient framework for predicting ultimate tensile strength (UTS) from alloy composition and processing conditions. Its acquisition policy anticipates how possible UTS outcomes update predictions and the support for complementary Gaussian process (GP) descriptions. It then reassesses the worst-case expected predictive risk remaining across a reference domain to select informative candidates. A retrospective evaluation on 501 HEA records compares ROSE-HEA with expected-improvement (EI) and upper-confidence-bound (UCB) Bayesian optimization (BO), random GP sampling, and eight conventional regressors across 30 matched runs with shared initial observations and test conditions. Starting from 25 initial observations and acquiring 100 additional responses, ROSE-HEA achieves the lowest mean root-mean-square error (RMSE), at 152.84 MPa, and the highest mean R^2, at 0.7443. Mean RMSE is reduced by 6.16% and 6.37% relative to EI and UCB, respectively. The selected observations improve prediction across six regressor families and provide broader design-space coverage. ROSE-HEA also surpasses the strongest conventional regressor's mean predictive performance using 125 rather than 185 observations, corresponding to 32.4% fewer training observations. These findings demonstrate the potential of outcome-aware robust sequential learning to improve UTS prediction in HEAs under constrained training budgets.

</details>


### [196] [Asymptotic Margins and Feature Confinement in Conic Classification Heads under Softmax Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18507)
*Omer Nezih Gerek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究锥形分类头在softmax交叉熵训练下是否能使特征有界。作者引入"渐近margin"（用二阶锥规划计算），证明margin为正时损失下确界为零、仅在特征发散时趋零，margin为负时损失强制、极小点落在显式球内；实验发现所有训练锥头各格均正margin。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥形函数构建的分类头为每类给出特征空间的有界区域，并被用于拒绝未知类样本。作者想检验：当用softmax交叉熵训练这种头时，这些区域是否也能让特征保持有界，即softmax损失能否约束特征范数。

Method: 对分数为线性项加欧氏范数的头，先刻画每类区域由旋转二次曲面（离心率为某学习比值的倒数）界定；再为每类定义渐近margin，比较该类分数与其他分数在特征发散时的增长速率，并用二阶锥规划求解；分析margin正负与损失下确界、强制性、极小点位置的关系；推广到其他范数包括多面锥函数；在CIFAR-10/100上对67个锥头（1210个格）计算margin，并与原型头对比。

Result: 理论上，margin为正⇔该类损失下确界为零且仅在特征发散时趋零；margin为负⇒损失强制且极小点位于显式球内。实验上CIFAR-10/100中67个训练锥头的全部1210个格均为正margin，而原型头按理论预期保持10格中9格有界。

Conclusion: 由于softmax只依赖分数差，有界区域类仍可有正margin，故有界区域并不能约束特征；有界区域与特征有界是两个不同性质，需用渐近margin来判定。

Abstract: Classification heads built on conic functions give each class a bounded region of the feature space, and such regions are used to reject samples of unseen classes. We ask whether these regions also keep the features bounded when the head is trained with softmax cross-entropy. For a head whose class scores combine a linear term with a Euclidean norm, we first show that the region of each class is bounded by a quadric of revolution, whose eccentricity is the reciprocal of one learned ratio. We then attach to each class an asymptotic margin, which compares the growth of its score with the growth of all the other scores and can be computed by a second-order cone program. The loss of a class has infimum zero exactly when its margin is positive, and it then approaches zero only along diverging features. When the margin is negative, the loss is coercive and its minimizers lie in an explicit ball. Since softmax depends only on the differences between scores, a class with a bounded region can still have a positive margin, so bounded regions do not confine the features. The result extends to other norms, including the polyhedral conic functions. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, all 1210 cells of 67 trained conic heads have positive margins, whereas a prototype head keeps nine of its ten cells bounded, as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [197] [On numerical issues of divergences between nearly equal distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18511)
*Brandon Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出分类分布间常见散度运算（正/反向KL、广义JSD、蒙特卡洛KL估计、熵）存在与softmax类似的数值问题，并给出稳定的重写形式与实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barron等(2026)已发现softmax与soft交叉熵的数值问题，作者推测同类舍入/相消问题也存在于分类分布的散度计算中，而这类散度广泛用于知识蒸馏、强化学习、近端方法等优化目标。

Method: 分析当两分布近似相等时前向与梯度计算中的舍入与相消误差；针对正/反向KL、广义JSD、蒙特卡洛KL估计与熵逐一提出数值稳定的重写公式，并给出各场景的示例实现，用基于性质的测试（property-based tests）验证。

Result: 证实上述散度运算确实存在数值不稳定问题，稳定的重写形式在性质测试中通过验证，能改善前向数值与梯度计算。

Conclusion: 散度运算的数值稳定性问题普遍存在且可被系统性修正；作者提供了可直接采用的稳定公式与实现，建议在相关优化目标中替换原始计算方式。

Abstract: Barron et al. (2026) recently identified numerical issues in the softmax and soft cross-entropy loss. We show that similar issues are present in standard divergence operations between categorical distributions. This includes the forward and reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and generalized Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD), as well as Monte Carlo KL estimates and entropy. When the distributions are nearly equal, rounding and cancellation issues affect the forward and gradient computations. These issues can affect settings using divergences in optimization objectives, such as knowledge distillation, reinforcement learning, and proximal methods. We propose numerically stable reformulations and provide example implementations for each setting, validated by property-based tests.

</details>


### [198] [WeightBench: Evaluating Language Models as Weight-Space Programmers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18553)
*Aayush Marasini,Barsat Khadka,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 WEIGHTBENCH，一个系统评估 LLM 在权重空间编程（WSP）能力的基准，含 5 条赛道共 42 个任务，覆盖从 2K 参数控制策略到 7B 语言模型的权重读取、修复、编写、不可行编辑识别与无数据压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示 LLM 可直接操作其他网络参数（读取功能、修复故障网络、从零生成可用网络，无需梯度下降），但缺乏系统、可复现的评测基准，难以比较不同模型在这一新范式下的能力边界。

Method: 构建 WEIGHTBENCH：42 个任务分为五条赛道——读权重、修权重、写权重、识别不可能（而非仅困难）的编辑、无数据压缩；被测网络规模跨度从 2K 参数控制策略到 7B 参数语言模型；配套任务注册表、OS 级沙箱运行框架与在线排行榜，并以宏观（macro）分数汇总评测。

Result: 前沿智能体已能读取小网络、并在不实际运行的情况下写出可工作的控制策略；但尚不能修补语言模型、无数据压缩，也无法区分"不可能编辑"与"困难编辑"。全 42 任务 macro 分数：Claude Opus 5.5 0.535、Sonnet 5.5 0.466、Haiku 5.5 0.402、DeepSeek v4-flash 0.215；在 Opus 运行过的 38 个任务上，GPT-6.1-sol 为 0.568，Opus 为 0.546。

Conclusion: WEIGHTBENCH 为权重空间编程提供了统一评测标尺，表明该能力在小型网络上已初步成立、在大型模型与无数据场景下仍显著欠缺；代码、任务注册表、沙箱与排行榜公开，可推动后续研究。

Abstract: Recent work shows that large language models (LLMs) can operate directly on the parameters of other neural networks. They can read what a network was trained to do, fix a network that fails, and write working networks from scratch, all without gradient descent. This capability has been termed Weight-Space Programming (WSP). Exploring this new paradigm systematically requires a robust benchmark. We introduce WEIGHTBENCH, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs of increasing capability on 42 WSP tasks across five tracks: reading, fixing and writing weights, recognizing edits that are impossible, and data-free compression. The networks whose weights the LLMs read and edit range from 2K-parameter control policies to 7B-parameter language models. Experimental benchmark results show that frontier agents can already read small networks and write working control policies without ever running them, but cannot yet patch language models, compress them without data, or tell an impossible edit from a hard one. On all 42 tasks, Claude Opus 5.5 reaches a macro score of 0.535 (Sonnet 5.5 0.466, Haiku 5.5 0.402, DeepSeek v4-flash 0.215); on the 38 tasks it was run on, GPT-6.1-sol reaches 0.568 against Opus's 0.546. Code, the task registry, our OS-level sandbox harness, and the live leaderboard are publicly available at https://github.com/Aayush-Marasini/WeightBench.

</details>


### [199] [Pairing AIS with RF: Analysis of a Markovian Approach for Maritime Data Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18563)
*Daniel Grimm,Kyra Jongman,Renato Cordeiro Ferreira,Giuseppe Cascavilla,Alfredo Cuzzocrea,Willem-Jan Van Den Heuvel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将Pair HMM用于把星载RF检测与AIS航迹对齐，在合成RF数据上评估其在多候选匹配和暗船检测上的表现，并对误差与偏差做诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上活动监测对安全至关重要，AIS存在被欺骗或关闭（暗船）的漏洞，而星载RF观测独立但无法识别船名，需要把RF检测与AIS航迹对齐才能识别船只。

Method: 按CRISP-DM流程，采用Pair Hidden Markov Model结合时空信息并对噪声与时间不同步建模，将单个RF检测与AIS航迹序列对齐；在NOAA AIS数据上生成合成RF检测，与最近邻基线比较，并用偏差测试分析轨迹长度、采样密度、静止性和AIS–RF距离等误差来源；另提出与无匹配转移的零模型比较的对数几率得分及其长度校正变体。

Result: 长度校正的Forward得分在多候选检测验证集上top-1精度0.7776，暗船检测AUC 0.637；单次RF检测时得分主要由AIS航迹本身决定，误差偏向更长、更静止的轨迹。对数几率得分将精度提升至0.8580、AUC提升至0.852，其长度校正变体不再显著偏向静止轨迹和短消息间距，但仍偏好距RF检测较远的候选。未拟合的时间加权最近邻基线更优（精度0.9013、AUC 0.904）。

Conclusion: 所设计的单检测手工PHMM方案接近但未超过简单的时间加权最近邻基线，说明该任务上复杂模型收益有限；误差偏差分析指出对静止性、轨迹长度和AIS–RF距离的残余偏差，是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Monitoring maritime activities is important for safety and security, particularly for detecting illegal activities, but remains challenging due to the size and complexity of the maritime domain. The Automatic Identification System (AIS) is commonly used for this purpose, despite its known vulnerabilities. In contrast, space-based radio frequency (RF) observations provide an independent source of vessel activity but lack ship identification and therefore cannot identify vessels on their own. Following the CRISP-DM process, this study applies a Pair Hidden Markov Model (PHMM) to align individual RF detections with AIS trajectories, using temporal and spatial information while modeling noise and timing discrepancies. We evaluate it on NOAA AIS data with synthetic RF detections against nearest-neighbor baselines and analyze its errors with bias tests on trajectory length, sampling density, stationarity and AIS--RF distance. The length-corrected Forward score reaches a top-1 precision of $0.7776$ on the validation split of multi-candidate detections, where errors are possible, and an AUC of $0.637$ for detecting dark vessels among the detections that pass the prefilter. With a single RF detection, the score mostly reflects the AIS trajectory itself, and its errors favor longer and more stationary trajectories. A log-odds score, which compares the full model with a null model without match transitions, raises precision to $0.8580$ and AUC to $0.852$. Its length-corrected variant no longer shows a significant bias towards stationary trajectories and short inter-message distances, but still favors candidates farther from the RF detection. A time-weighted nearest-neighbor baseline without fitting remains ahead (precision $0.9013$, AUC $0.904$), so this single-detection, hand-designed PHMM formulation approaches but does not beat it.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Towards High-Performance Multimodal Discovery: Evaluating Retrieval Efficacy and Operational Scalability in Digital Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17726)
*Krishna Priya,Sai Navya Jyesta,Sachith Withana,Neelesh Karthikeyan,Beth Plale*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文评估面向百万级文献库的多模态向量检索方案：将页面图像（含文本与插图）编码为数值向量而非依赖OCR文本，兼顾语义与版权，实验证明图文融合显著提升检索效果并可亚线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数字馆藏（百万级册）中查找特定材料困难；文本具有多模态性（密集叙述+图表、标注图、复杂版式）；传统倒排索引（Lucene/Solr）对视觉语义"盲"，无法捕捉图表语义。

Method: 将页面图像整体转换为数值向量（对页面上的文本与图像统一做VLM嵌入），替代OCR文本表示；基于向量数据库构建检索系统，评估多模态检索的语义精度与架构可扩展性。

Result: 融合视觉与文本特征显著提升相关材料的发现效果，尤其在图表内容对概念理解关键的领域；系统检索达到亚线性扩展，向量数据库优化配置是将VLM嵌入转化为生产级库级方案的关键。

Conclusion: 向量化多模态表示在缓解版权与逆向工程风险的同时保留了文本与视觉语义，经优化的向量数据库可支撑库级发现服务，为传统倒排索引提供有效补充。

Abstract: Large-scale digital repositories with million plus digitized volumes serve as vital engines for scientific and scholarly discovery. However, as collections grow, finding specific research or educational material remains a significant challenge. This difficulty is compounded by the multimodal nature of texts, that is, texts which combine dense narrative content with essential illustrative charts, annotated diagrams, and complex layouts. Historically, discovery has been supported by full-text search indexes implemented as inverted indexes (e.g., Lucene or Solr). While these systems scale well for textual strings, they are fundamentally "blind" to the semantic nuance of visual information. We explore the benefit of discovery through texts that have been converted to numerical vectors. By representing page images as numerical vectors rather than OCR-extracted text, and doing so for both text and images on a page, we capture both textual and visual semantics while mitigating reverse engineering and copyright infringement concerns. The primary contribution of this research is an evaluation of the multimodal retrieval landscape, encompassing both semantic precision and architectural scalability. Our findings demonstrate that integrating visual and textual features significantly enhances the discovery of relevant material, particularly in domains where diagrammatic content is essential for conceptual understanding. We assess retrieval efficacy, demonstrating that the system achieves sub-linear scalability. Our findings suggest that an optimized vector database configuration is the critical factor in transforming VLM-based embeddings into a production-ready, library-scale discovery solution.

</details>


### [201] [Who Counts as Exposed? Measurement Choices in an Open, Re-estimable Index of Occupational AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18168)
*Luca Mouchel,Pierre Bouquet,Yossi Sheffi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建一个开源、可重估的任务级职业AI暴露度指数，通过改变"上下文"与"校准"两大设计选择，揭示指数结果高达多大程度取决于这些人为决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业AI暴露度指数被广泛用于经济分析、劳动力规划与政策制定，但指数中内嵌的设计选择决定了哪些工人被判定为"暴露"，而此前研究很少量化这些选择的实际影响。

Method: 对18,796个职业—任务对，用7个开源权重推理模型在同一评分标准下打分；每个模型分别在有无上下文（检索的新闻与研究段落、职业描述、瓶颈推理协议）下评分，集成结果再分有无单纯形约束校准（对齐Claude使用数据）两种版本。

Result: 与2023主流基准最大差异不在于对AI能力的判断，而在于是否给予依赖尚未建成的软件集成的暴露以"信用"（数据无法裁决）。在校准版本并采用基准权重时，暴露工人数少于基准；仅计直接暴露时则更多。全分辨率下集成排序优于基准（对照观测使用数据和独立体力任务分类）。上下文主要改变暴露水平而非排序。上下文与校准均将暴露向高薪职业偏移，校准影响更大。

Conclusion: 暴露指数作为政策输入时应明确声明设计选择、报告备选方案下的数值，并随系统演进而重估；作者同时发布未校准版本以提高透明度。

Abstract: Occupational AI exposure indices are used as inputs to economic analysis, workforce planning, and policy, and the choices built into them decide which workers are described as exposed. We build an open, re-estimable task-level index and vary its two main design decisions, context and calibration, to show how much the answer depends on them. For each of the 18,796 occupation--task pairs in \onet, seven open-weight reasoning models rate exposure under a common rubric. Each model rates with or without a context of retrieved news and research passages, workplace descriptors, and a bottleneck-based reasoning protocol, and the ensemble is combined with or without simplex-constrained calibration to observed Claude usage. The largest difference from the dominant 2023 benchmark is not a disagreement about what AI can do but about how much credit to give exposure that depends on software integrations not yet built, a choice that no data can settle. At the benchmark's own weight, the calibrated index counts fewer exposed workers than the benchmark, and when only direct exposure counts it counts more. At full score resolution, the ensemble ranks tasks better than the benchmark against observed usage and an independent physical-task classification. Adding context mainly shifts the level of exposure rather than improving the ranking. Context and calibration both relocate exposure toward higher-wage occupations, and calibration, which anchors the index to one platform's users, does more of it, so we also release the uncalibrated arm. Exposure indices used as policy inputs should state such choices, report their numbers under the alternatives, and be re-estimated as systems change.

</details>


### [202] [Explanations that Transfer: Interpreting Medical Retrieval with Sparse AutoEncoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18179)
*Diane Bouchacourt,Gaëtan Brison*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将稀疏自编码器（SAE）引入医学信息检索，把稠密文本嵌入转为可解释的稀疏表示，在保持检索性能的同时提升跨域可迁移性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学AI问答依赖从大型文档库检索医学知识，微调可提升检索相关性，但稠密嵌入高度不透明、难以理解其工作机制；同时针对特定医学数据集适配易丧失对新临床语料的可迁移性。

Method: 对检索嵌入应用SAE，将稠密表示分解为语义可解释的稀疏编码；训练目标结合重建损失与InfoNCE对比损失，直接保持查询-文档相关性；在3个嵌入模型、6个医学检索数据集（域内与域外）上评估。

Result: 在医学语料上训练的稀疏编码在域内检索上优于原始嵌入，且域外性能损失小于稠密嵌入，目标语料与训练语料差异越大优势越明显；所得特征语义可由激活最强的查询与文档推断，可解释特定文档被检索的原因，并能泛化到训练分布之外。

Conclusion: SAE结合对比损失是提升医学检索可解释性与跨域鲁棒性的有效途径，稀疏表示在性能与可解释性间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Medical AI systems that answer users' queries rely on retrieving medical knowledge from large document collections. In this context, finetuning general models on medical datasets can improve retrieval relevance. Yet the dense embeddings underlying modern retrieval systems remain largely opaque, making it difficult to understand the mechanics behind their results. Moreover, adapting to a specific medical dataset risks losing transferability to new clinical corpora. In this work, we tackle these issues by applying Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to medical information retrieval, transforming dense text embeddings into semantically interpretable representations. To do so, our model combines a reconstruction objective with an InfoNCE contrastive loss that directly preserves query-document relevance. We evaluate three embedding models across six medical retrieval datasets, both in-domain and out-of-domain. Trained on a medical corpus, our sparse codes improve in-domain retrieval over the original embeddings, and lose less out-of-domain performance than their dense counterparts, especially when the target corpus is distant from the training one. Importantly, our experiments show that the method yields interpretable features whose semantic content can be inferred from the queries and documents that activate them most strongly. The interpretation of these features sheds light on why a particular document was retrieved, and transfers beyond the training distribution.

</details>


### [203] [RACE: Ranking-Uncertainty Aware Capacity-Adaptive Customization for Efficient Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18244)
*Tianqi Liu,Kaipu Sun,Kairui Fu,Wenkai Wang,Sihao Liu,Hui Zang,Ran Chen,Tao Wang,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RACE提出一种不确定性感知的自适应压缩框架，按实例复杂度动态调整模型结构与输入序列压缩率，缓解容量-复杂度失配，在精度、效率与适应性上优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐压缩方法采用统一压缩率，忽视用户意图复杂度差异，导致"容量-复杂度失配"：简单实例浪费算力、复杂实例容量不足。

Method: 以Top-K预测的分布稳定性构建list-wise排序不确定性指标作为实例复杂度代理；用自监督训练轻量预测器从浅层特征预测该不确定性；据此进行两维实例级瘦身——超网络式通道敏感度提取器识别参数冗余，重要性感知渐进序列过滤降低数据冗余。

Result: 在四个常用数据集上，RACE在准确率、效率与适应性方面均优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: 不确定性可作为有效的动态资源分配信号，实现容量自适应的高效序列推荐。

Abstract: Efficient sequential recommendation has emerged as a promising paradigm for providing real-time personalized service. To deploy models under resource-constrained environment, model compression becomes a necessity, yet existing methods typically apply uniform compression ratios, overlooking the heterogeneity in user intent complexity. This "one-size-fits-all" approach leads to a fundamental Capacity-Complexity Mismatch, where computational resources are either wasted on simple instances or insufficient for complex ones. To address this, we propose RACE (Ranking-uncertainty Aware Capacity-Adaptive Customization for Efficient Sequential Recommendation), a framework that dynamically adapts both model structures and input sequences with uncertainty-aware compression ratios. To ensure capacity-adaptive inference, RACE captures the model's intrinsic uncertainty of its predictions and transforms it into a proactive resource allocator. Specifically, we derive a list-wise ranking-uncertainty metric from the distributional stability of top-$K$ predictions, which serves as a measurable proxy for instance complexity. A lightweight predictor is then trained via self-supervision to anticipate this uncertainty from early-layer features. Guided by this signal, RACE performs instance-specific slimming in two dimensions: a hypernetwork-style channel sensitivity extractor identifies parameter redundancy, while importance-aware progressive sequence filtering mitigates data redundancy. Extensive experiments on four popular datasets demonstrate that RACE outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on accuracy, efficiency, and adaptability.

</details>


### [204] [MeReader: An Offline, Privacy-Preserving, Progress-Aware AI Assistant for Narrative eBook Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18513)
*Mohammed Efaz,Udo Bub,Itilekha Podder*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MeReader是一个隐私保护、离线、进度感知的AI阅读助手，在消费级硬件上为叙事阅读提供情境化陪伴，避免剧透并评测出质量-延迟前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的系统在线处理整本书，存在叙述剧透与隐私风险；且缺乏将进度感知、隐私保护与电子书叙事阅读结合的集成系统。

Method: 将检索与生成都限制在读者当前位置；采用向量相似度、BM25与摘要嵌入的混合检索架构；引入进度感知边界机制；在经典英文书籍上以定量理解指标与LLM独立评判做评测。

Result: 发现质量-延迟前沿：2b-4b中型本地模型理解能力与5b+大模型相当且响应质量可接受；进度感知边界机制在不损害检索质量与正确性的前提下防止未来内容泄露。

Conclusion: 本地化、以隐私为中心的方法可增强而非替代数字阅读体验，是云依赖服务的有力替代方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces MeReader, a privacy-preserving, offline and progress-aware AI assistant designed to function as a contextual companion for narrative reading on consumer hardware. Unlike conventional Large Language Model (LLM)-based systems that process documents/books online in an all-texts-at-once type of way, thereby risking narrative spoilers, MeReader confines both retrieval and generation to the reader's distinct position in the text. The system integrates a hybrid retrieval architecture, combining vector similarity, BM25, and summary embeddings, to ensure high fidelity to the reader's current context. We evaluate the system across classical English books using both quantitative comprehension metrics and qualitative LLM-based independent judging. The results identify a quality-latency frontier, showing that mid-sized local models (2b-4b) can achieve comprehension parity with larger counterparts (5b+) while maintaining acceptable response quality. Importantly, the proposed progress-aware boundary mechanism is shown to prevent future content leakage without compromising retrieval quality and correctness. These findings suggest that a localized, privacy-centric approach can augment, rather than replace, the digital reading experience, offering a good alternative to cloud-dependent services. Therein, there remains a lack of amalgamate systems which combine progress-aware, privacy-preserving, and narrative eBook reading with AI support. It is in this very gap that the development of MeReader came to be; synthesizing said strands into an offline, book-constrained assistant focused on enhancing comprehension without compromising narrative continuity or user privacy.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Go under Chinese Rules with a Succinct Game History is Complete for Exponential Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18201)
*Markus Bläser,Felix Czerner*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 论文证明，在"简洁历史"（用布尔电路表示对局历史）输入模型下，带禁重复规则的围棋（Go）判定黑棋是否有必胜策略是 EXPSPACE 完全的，从而回答了自 Robson(1984) 以来的公开问题，且其归约的起始局面可经由合法走子到达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围棋在对局可能指数级长时需用禁重复（super-ko）规则防止无限循环。Robson(1984) 已证明带禁重复规则的国际象棋与西洋跳棋是 EXPSPACE 完全，但围棋是否同样 EXPSPACE 完全长期悬而未决；此外以往归约的起始局面常不合法可达。

Method: 引入"简洁对局历史"概念：用一个布尔电路作为判定器，输入棋盘位置输出 1/0，指示该局面是否已出现在历史中，以此判定某步是否因造成重复而非法。基于此输入模型构造从 EXPSPACE 困难问题到围棋必胜策略判定的归约。

Result: 证明在历史以简洁方式给出时，判定黑棋在带禁重复规则围棋中是否有必胜策略是 EXPSPACE 完全的；并且困难性证明中的起始局面可由合法对局到达。

Conclusion: 围棋（带禁重复规则、简洁历史）的复杂度被确认为 EXPSPACE 完全，填补了自 Robson 以来的空白；同时提升了对局合法性假设，使结论更具说服力。

Abstract: The complexity of games is a central topic in combinatorics, algorithms, and computational complexity theory. It asks how difficult it is to determine the winner of a combinatorial game under optimal play. Many games, such as Hex or Othello, are PSPACE-complete: the game tree has polynomial depth, so the game can be decided in PSPACE. However, some games allow exponentially long plays, for instance Chess (without a generalized 50-move rule) or Go under Japanese rules; these are complete for exponential time. To prevent infinite games, some games employ a no-repetition rule (super-ko rule), for instance Go under Chinese rules. Robson (1984) proved that Chess and Checkers with a no-repetition rule are even harder: deciding whether the first player has a winning strategy is EXPSPACE-complete. Since then, it has been an open problem whether Go with a no-repetition rule is EXPSPACE-complete, too.
  The complexity of a game under a no-repetition rule is typically interpreted as follows: given a board position, decide whether the first player has a winning strategy such that no repetition occurs from that position on. We introduce the concept of a succinct game history: a Boolean circuit that, given a board position, outputs 1 if the position is in the game history and 0 otherwise. It serves as a "judge" that tells the players whether a move is illegal because it creates a repetition. We prove that deciding whether Black has a winning strategy in Go with a no-repetition rule is EXPSPACE-complete when the game history is given succinctly. Moreover, the starting position in our hardness proof is reachable by legal gameplay, which has not always been the case in prior proofs.

</details>


### [206] [Disenfranchisement in Spatial Voting Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18468)
*Vincent Lageschulte,Joel Nishimura*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 论文引入"franchise elections"（选民资格选举），研究意识形态/议题空间内群体成员投票改变选民构成的递归自稳定问题，证明二维空间中任何社会选择函数都会产生复杂行为，并以Yolk函数展示新意识形态轴引入者面临被剥夺资格风险、以及任意周期的循环现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投票权、群体成员资格与可接受言论边界都存在递归自稳定问题：划定纳入/排除规则的人往往正是这些规则的最终裁决者。作者希望理解意识形态或议题多样性如何影响这种纳入与排除的稳定性。

Method: 将主体置于意识形态/议题空间中，由聚合主体观点的社会选择函数诱导主体对群体构成的偏好（主体偏好能改善自身社会选择结果输出的群体）；在此模型下定义franchise elections，即现有成员投票决定选民范围变更；并从理论上分析二维空间下的动力学性质，选取投票理论中的Yolk社会选择函数进行示例分析。

Result: 在二维意识形态空间中，franchise elections可产生复杂行为，且这一复杂性对任意社会选择函数均成立。基于Yolk函数的示例表明：试图引入一条垂直于既有左右谱系的新意识形态轴的主体，除非与谱系中任一方结盟，否则面临被剥夺资格（disenfranchisement）的风险；且franchise elections可出现任意周期的循环。

Conclusion: 群体自我界定边界的选举机制内在地不稳定，其复杂性不依赖于特定社会选择规则，而源于二维空间结构与偏好递归性；这为理解意识形态多样性下成员资格与言论边界的波动提供了形式化解释。

Abstract: The questions of voting rights, group membership, and the boundaries of acceptable discourse all involve a recursive, self-stability issue, wherein the voters, group members, and acceptable commentators are often the ultimate arbiters of their own rules for inclusion and exclusion. In order to understand how ideological or issue diversity may impact the stability of such inclusion and exclusion, we introduce franchise elections, where voting members of a group vote to change the electorate. In particular, when agents are positioned inside an ideological or issue space, a social choice function that aggregates agent viewpoints induces a preference for agents over group composition, where agents prefer groups that improve the social choice function outcome for them. We demonstrate that when ideological space is two-dimensional, these franchise elections can generate complex behavior and that this complexity exists for any social choice function. Using the Yolk social choice function from voting theory, we illustrate that franchise elections can predict that agents attempting to introduce a new ideological axis perpendicular to an existing left-right spectrum risk disenfranchisement unless they ally with either side of the spectrum, and that franchise elections can cycle with any period.

</details>


### [207] [Stochastic Games with Distributional Entropy Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18470)
*Shankaranarayanan Krishna,Durgam Latha,Dion Reji,Ashutosh Trivedi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文引入"分布熵目标随机博弈"，研究两玩家零和博弈中 MIN 收敛分布、MAX 分散分布的三种语义（平均分布、采样 profile、采样转移），并给出可解性、Bellman 刻画与复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon 熵是非线性的，因此"先平均再求熵"与"先采样再求熵"会得到不同博弈；这一语义差异对博弈论结构与可解性有根本影响，此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 形式化三种语义：averaged-distribution、sampled-profile、sampled-transition；使用收缩 Bellman 算子与有限时域逼近刻画上下值；对固定有理折扣与有理承诺间隙做复杂度分析（PSPACE、NP 上界与 NP 难近似下界）。

Result: 平均分布语义即便在四状态确定性竞技场也可能不确定，而两种采样语义确定且存在最优平稳随机策略；平均分布下值支配任一采样值，采样 profile 与采样转移值一般不可比；间隙问题属于 PSPACE，单一熵最小化子情形属于 NP；三种语义下熵优化在单玩家确定性博弈中即为 NP 难近似（固定加性常数）。

Conclusion: 语义选择决定博弈的确定性与值之间的关系；熵目标的优化问题本质困难，即便在最简单设定下也难近似。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{stochastic games with distributional entropy objectives}, two-player zero-sum games whose configurations are probability distributions over the states of a finite arena and whose stage reward is Shannon entropy. Over an infinite discounted horizon, MIN seeks to concentrate the distribution, while MAX seeks to disperse it. Because entropy is nonlinear, it matters whether strategic and transition randomness are averaged before entropy is evaluated or sampled first. We formalise three resulting semantics: averaged-distribution, sampled-profile, and sampled-transition.
  This semantic choice fundamentally changes the game-theoretic structure. Averaged-distribution games need not be determined, even on a four-state deterministic arena, whereas both sampled semantics are determined and admit optimal stationary randomised strategies. We give contractive Bellman characterisations and convergent finite-horizon approximations for the upper and lower averaged values and the two sampled values. Moreover, even the lower value under averaged-distribution semantics dominates the value under either sampled semantics, while the sampled-profile and sampled-transition values are incomparable in general.
  For fixed rational discount and positive rational promise gap, the averaged-distribution (upper and lower value) gap problems and both sampled gap problems lie in PSPACE; for a single entropy minimiser, the averaged and sampled-profile problems lie in NP. Finally, under all three semantics, entropy optimisation is NP-hard to approximate within a suitable fixed additive constant, already for deterministic one-player games, where pure memoryless strategies are optimal.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [When Should LLM Agents Forget? Memory Filtering Under Query-Critical Resolver Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17338)
*Samarth Vinayaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM记忆系统中"硬删除陈旧事实"在解析器出错时会删除查询关键事实、损害下游准确率，主张将删除视为成本敏感决策，并经验证后再用，否则默认保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统常用硬过滤删除被判定为过时的事实，但忽略了解决器(resolver)误判导致的假删除风险，作者要检验这一删除步骤对下游准确率的真实影响。

Method: 跨三个模型做受控的敏感性干预，在从完美解析到最低可靠性的范围内调节解析器可靠性；并做配对的目标性干预以区分假删除与假保留的影响；在冻结的102/102校准/测试切分上比较部署匹配门控、始终硬删除与测试知情oracle选择。

Result: 硬删除收益从完美解析时的+0.186~+0.230转为最低可靠性下的-0.176~-0.181；假删除造成的退化显著大于假保留，而不删除基线也可能因错误标签误导生成而失败。部署匹配门控达0.655准确率，对比始终硬删除0.641、oracle选择0.662；全局准确率交叉点需金标签，是模型与任务特定诊断而非部署阈值。

Conclusion: 删除应作成本敏感处理：在匹配数据上验证查询关键的假删除风险，若无法验证则默认采用无标签保留策略；不应把全局准确率交叉点当作部署阈值。

Abstract: LLM memory systems often delete facts classified as stale. We show that this hard-filtering step can reduce downstream accuracy when resolver errors delete query-critical facts. Across three models, a controlled sensitivity intervention moves hard-drop from gains of +0.186 to +0.230 at perfect resolution to losses of -0.176 to -0.181 at the lowest tested reliability. Paired targeted interventions show that false-drop causes significantly greater degradation than false-keep, while a non-deleting baseline can still fail when incorrect labels steer generation. On a frozen 102/102 calibration/test split, a simple deployment-matched gate reaches 0.655 accuracy versus 0.641 for always hard-drop and 0.662 for test-informed oracle selection. Global-accuracy crossovers require gold labels and are model- and task-specific diagnostics, not deployment thresholds. Deletion should instead be treated as cost-sensitive: validate query-critical false-drop risk on matched data, or default to label-free retention when such validation is unavailable.

</details>


### [209] [The Transformer Paradigm: Dynamic Processing and a New Principle of Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17953)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将Transformer理解为基于"动态构造变换"新推理范式的系统，而非深度学习的简单量变扩张，并提出SIDPP框架来解释提示敏感性与相关研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为现有观点仅把Transformer看作深度学习的规模扩张（量变），未能抓住其推理原理上的本质差异，因此需要一个能刻画其内在机制的概念框架。

Method: 概念性/理论分析：把Transformer视为由参数化"变换概念"作用于token表示的系统；推理时部分变换由当前输入动态构建——先自适应已有变换再施加；在机制-结构层面通过"输出权重互联"（某些网络的输出决定另一些网络的权重）实现这种动态处理。

Result: 提出该处理模式命名为"序列级交互式动态并行处理"（SIDPP），并据此给出对强提示敏感性（prompt sensitivity）的解释框架。

Conclusion: Transformer代表一种新范式；SIDPP为可解释性、可控性、替代AI架构、神经形态系统及动态处理的生物学实现等方向提供研究纲领。

Abstract: We argue that the Transformer revolution is not adequately understood as a merely quantitative expansion of deep learning, but as the emergence of a new paradigm based on a different principle of inference. We interpret the Transformer as a system in which token representations are transformed by parameterized transformations, or transforming concepts. During inference, some of these transformations are dynamically constructed from the current input: previously acquired transformations are first adapted and then applied. At the mechanical-structural level, this dynamic processing is realized through output-weight interconnections, whereby outputs of some networks determine the weights of others. The resulting mode of processing, which we call Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing (SIDPP), provides a framework for explaining strong prompt sensitivity and opens research directions concerning interpretability, control, alternative AI architectures, neuromorphic systems, and possible biological realizations of dynamic processing.

</details>


### [210] [Does the Ranking Hold? A Practitioner-Facing Audit of Hallucination Detectors on Small, Quantized, On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17339)
*Shubhra Mittal,Gaurav Naresh Mittal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对四种幻觉检测器在4-bit量化的小模型（Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen2.5-3B）上进行单卡可复现审计，发现跨模型检测器排名会反转（Spearman ρ=-0.80），且廉价词法基线不劣于采样一致性方法；同时指出TruthfulQA-Generation的token重叠标注器近乎随机（AUROC 0.46-0.59），属标注器失效而非检测器失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测研究多基于7B-70B模型、充足采样预算和精确匹配/ROUGE正确性标签，而实际端侧部署的是2B-3B级4-bit量化小模型，这类模型处于证据空白区，缺乏可靠性依据。

Method: 在单张Colab T4 GPU上以4-bit NF4量化加载Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct与Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct，在闭卷TriviaQA上评估三类范式的四种检测器：长度归一化token-margin校准、词法n-gram基线、两种采样一致性方法（语义熵与SelfCheckGPT-NLI），并用bootstrap置信区间与秩相关分析排名迁移。

Result: 跨模型排名迁移差：Spearman ρ=-0.80、Kendall τ-b=-0.667，检测器排名在两模型间反转；廉价词法基线与采样一致性检测器在bootstrap 95%置信区间下统计不可区分。TriviaQA上同流程AUROC为0.69-0.89，而TruthfulQA-Generation仅0.46-0.59，接近随机。

Conclusion: 检测器选择不能从大模型研究直接迁移到小量化模型，需按具体部署模型重新审计；应将检测器选择明确表述为资源受限端侧场景下误差率公平分配问题，并公开完整测试框架、提示与逐条分数以便复现。

Abstract: Hallucination detection for large language models is dominated by methods evaluated on 7B-70B research models with abundant sampling budgets and exact-match or ROUGE correctness labels. The small models practitioners actually ship on-device, Phi-3-mini, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, and Gemma-2-2B-Instruct, typically at 4-bit precision, sit outside this evidence base. We present a reproducible one-GPU audit of four detectors spanning three paradigms: length-normalized token-margin calibration, a lexical n-gram baseline, and two sampling-based consistency methods (semantic entropy and SelfCheckGPT-NLI) on two small open instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct) loaded at 4-bit NF4 quantization on a single Colab T4 GPU, evaluated on closed-book TriviaQA. Cross-model ranking transfer is poor: Spearman rho is -0.80 and Kendall tau-b is -0.667, meaning the detector ranking actively inverts between the two models, and the cheap lexical baseline is statistically indistinguishable from the sampling-based consistency detectors on both models under bootstrap 95 percent confidence intervals. As a second, methodological contribution, we show that the standard token-overlap-to-multiple-choice correctness labeler for TruthfulQA-Generation produces near-chance AUROC (0.46-0.59) for all four detectors on both models, a labeler failure, not a detector failure, since the same detectors reach 0.69-0.89 AUROC on TriviaQA under the identical pipeline. We release the harness, prompts, and per-item scores and frame the detector choice explicitly as a fairness-of-error-rates allocation problem for practitioners deploying on resource-constrained, on-device hardware.

</details>


### [211] [LoRAWeaver: Directional Adapter Routing in LoRA Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17340)
*Inish Melroy D Souza,Tom Jackson,Vitor Castro,Rebecca Higginson,Andrea Soltoggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的适配器路由方法，将LoRA适配器选择视为适配器输出空间中的表示对齐问题，通过余弦相似度匹配适配器条件下的查询表示与任务原型来选适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调（如LoRA）降低了适配成本，但反复适配导致任务特定适配器池不断增长，使推理时的适配器选择变复杂。现有路由与基于编码器的方法在新增适配器时往往需要重新训练，难以适用于动态场景。

Method: 将适配器路由建模为适配器输出空间中的表示对齐问题；免训练方法：计算适配器条件下的查询表示，与任务原型做余弦相似度匹配，据此选择适配器；不学习路由网络。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B与LLaMA-2-13B上，于48任务FLAN-v2基准及分布外任务评测：跨任务设定下优于免训练基线，并接近训练过的检索编码器；消融实验证实适配器输出空间中方向对齐带来的收益。

Conclusion: 适配器路由可有效转化为输出空间中的表示对齐问题，免训练的路由方案在动态适配器池下具备实用价值，且方向对齐是关键因素。

Abstract: Large language models are expensive to update through full retraining. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces the cost of adaptation, but repeated adaptation creates a growing pool of task-specific LoRA adapters. This pool complicates adapter selection during inference. Existing routing and encoder-based methods often require retraining when new adapters are added, which limits their use in dynamic settings. This paper treats adapter routing as a representation alignment problem in the adapter-output space. The proposed training-free method selects adapters by matching adapter-conditioned query representations against task prototypes using cosine similarity. Experiments with LLaMA-2-7B and LLaMA-2-13B on a 48-task FLAN-v2 benchmark and on out-of-distribution tasks show that the method outperforms training-free baselines in the cross-task setting and approaches a trained retrieval encoder without learning a router. An ablation confirms the benefit of directional alignment in the adapter-output space.

</details>


### [212] [Tabular Population Priors as Structured Retrieval for EHR Medication Set Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17341)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用药预测是固定词表的结构化预测任务，但现有临床RAG系统误将其当作开放式病例推理，检索相似病人病历让LLM推断用药集合。作者提出用"基于稀疏疾病-药物共现表的表格人群先验"作为更任务对齐的检索对象，仅替换PACERAG的病人检索器，F1从0.303提升至0.338（相对+11.5%），Jaccard从0.218提升至0.237，且无额外LLM调用、无非推理期病人索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床RAG把固定词表的结构化用药集合预测当成开放式的病例推理：检索相似病人记录作上下文，但检索对象（病人记录）与目标（离散药物类别标签）之间是间接的、不对齐的。作者希望找到一种与任务目标更直接对齐的检索对象。

Method: 从训练入院记录估计稀疏的疾病-药物共现表，据此为每次预测生成按概率排序的用药类别候选，作为"表格人群先验"检索对象。以PACERAG为固定下游流程，只替换其病人检索器，其余组件与LLM调用保持不变，在MIMIC-IV数据上对比。

Result: 在1,004例留出MIMIC-IV入院记录上，F1从0.303升至0.338（相对+11.5%），Jaccard从0.218升至0.237；无额外LLM调用，无非推理期病人索引开销。非LLM的top-k基线F1=0.248，说明该先验在接入LLM前即已具备预测能力。

Conclusion: 对于固定词表的EHR集合预测任务，聚合层面的人群统计量比病人级最近邻检索更任务对齐、更高效且更可审计，可作为更合适的RAG检索来源。

Abstract: Medication prediction from electronic health records is a fixed vocabulary structured prediction problem, yet recent clinical RAG systems often treat it like open ended case based reasoning: retrieve similar patient admissions and ask an LLM to infer a medication set. This retrieval object is indirect the model receives retrieved patient records while the target is a discrete set of medication class labels. We propose a more task aligned retrieval object: a tabular population prior ranked medication class candidates from a sparse condition medication co occurrence table estimated from training admissions. Using PACERAG as a fixed downstream pipeline and replacing only its patient retriever, this drop in swap improves F1 from 0.303 to 0.338 (+11.5% relative) and Jaccard from 0.218 to 0.237 on 1,004 heldout MIMIC IV admissions, with no additional LLM calls and no inference time patient index. A non LLM top k baseline reaches F1=0.248, confirming the prior is predictive before LLM integration. Aggregate population statistics can provide a more task aligned, efficient, and auditable retrieval source than patient level nearest neighbors for fixed vocabulary EHR set prediction.

</details>


### [213] [An Operationalization of Opaque Serial Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17495)
*Nathan Sheffield,Alek Westover,Lukas Finnveden,Alexa Pan,Julian Stastny,Ryan Greenblatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"NLS深度"这一指标，用以刻画模型在不经过可解释瓶颈的情况下能进行的未言明串行推理长度，从而作为CoT可监控性的架构层面代理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
