Motivation: CoT是监督AI模型的重要手段，但某些架构变化可能大幅削弱CoT的可监控性。作者希望找到一个能在训练前就判定、且不受常规后训练影响的量，来衡量模型"暗地计算"的潜力。

Method: 引入"不透明串行深度"概念，定义为不经过可解释瓶颈的最长计算路径长度；核心贡献是给出瓶颈标准——自然语言植根（NL-rooted）节点：由预训练先验初始化、输出可被人理解、输出空间未超出该先验、且未被改动为其他数据类型。据此定义NLS深度，可由架构、训练配方与部署配置在训练前裁决。作者还分析了多种现有与拟议架构，并给出Gemma3-1B的深度上界案例。

Result: 在开放权重模型上，NLS深度与层数强相关（存在少数例外），且近年来增长缓慢；若继续按标准Transformer扩展，预计仍将缓慢增长，而全带宽Transformer等潜空间推理架构可能使其大幅提升。

Conclusion: 作者建议AI开发者将NLS深度作为CoT可监控性直接测量的补充指标进行追踪与报告。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is currently a valuable tool for overseeing AI models, but some architectural shifts could significantly reduce CoT monitorability. We operationalize a measure that serves as a proxy for the amount of unverbalized serial cognition a model can perform, as a specific instantiation of the notion of opaque serial depth: roughly, the length of the longest path through a model's computation that does not pass through an interpretable bottleneck. Our main contribution is a particular standard for what counts as such a bottleneck. We define natural-language-rooted (NL-rooted) nodes as nodes that were initialized from a pre-training prior to produce human-understandable outputs, whose output space has not been expanded beyond that prior, and whose outputs the model has not been modified to interpret as a different type of data. By construction, the resulting measure, NLS depth, can be adjudicated before training from an architecture, training recipe, and deployment configuration alone, and is insensitive to ordinary post-training such as prompting, distillation, and RL. We find that, across open-weight models, NLS depth correlates strongly with layer count, with some exceptions, and has grown slowly over recent years. It would likely grow slowly under continued scaling of standard transformers, while latent-reasoning architectures such as full-bandwidth transformers could increase it by a large amount. We work through how NLS depth applies to a range of existing and proposed architectures, discuss alternative operationalizations, and give a worked example bounding the depth of Gemma3-1B. We argue that AI developers should track and report the NLS depth of their models as a complement to direct measurements of CoT monitorability.

</details>


### [214] [RecMem: Recurrent Memory Compression for Long-Sequence Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17921)
*Haoyi Hu,Gaoyang Guo,Yunjia Xi,Wen Chen,Weiwen Liu,Jian Wu,Yuning Jiang,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RecMem：一种面向生成式推荐的长历史循环记忆压缩框架，用固定数量的记忆槽压缩历史，使计算与历史长度解耦并保持预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐从长交互历史中获益，但直接处理历史的成本随长度线性增长，亟需在保留预测信息的同时压缩历史。

Method: 提出 RecMem，以空间不变性、语义保真性、终身可演进性为目标：(1) 分叉式编码器-解码器共享底层，将历史分段压缩进固定数量记忆槽，并采用槽级遗忘门更新；(2) 用推荐监督训练记忆保留预测信息；(3) 用流一致性训练对齐顺序处理与打包处理的预测。理论上在收缩性与近似半群一致性下，推导出两种处理顺序间状态差异与历史长度无关的界。

Result: 在 MerRec 上，RecMem 仅用 16 个记忆槽与 216 token 解码上下文（HSTU-1200 为 1200 token），保留 HSTU-1200 的 87.9% Recall@10 与 97.0% Recall@200；优于 PHSTU 及近期-only 与检索基线；对远距离历史的分析显示归档交互仍提供预测信号。

Conclusion: 通过固定槽记忆与流一致性训练，RecMem 在显著降低长历史计算成本的同时保持强推荐性能，并具备终身可演进性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation benefits from long interaction histories, but the cost of processing them directly grows with history length. We propose RecMem, a recurrent memory compression framework guided by three desiderata: space invariance, semantic fidelity, and lifelong evolvability. RecMem compresses historical segments into a fixed set of memory slots using a bifurcated encoder-decoder with shared lower layers and slot-wise forget-gated updates. Recommendation supervision trains the memory to preserve predictive information, while stream-consistency training aligns predictions from sequential and packed processing. By construction, the memory state and decoder context are independent of history length; under contraction and approximate semigroup consistency, we further derive a horizon-independent bound on the state discrepancy between these two processing orders. On MerRec, RecMem uses 16 memory slots and a 216-token decoder context, compared with 1,200 tokens for HSTU-1200, while retaining 87.9% of HSTU-1200's Recall@10 and 97.0% of its Recall@200. It outperforms PHSTU and the evaluated recent-only and retrieval baselines, and analyses of distant history show that archived interactions contribute predictive signal.

</details>


### [215] [Advancing Quantization for R&R Models with Microscaling Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17501)
*Yavuz Yetim,Bor-Yiing Su,Zhe Wang,Arthur Liu,Pranav Sharma,Ying Wang,Randy Shuai,Luke Pfister,Ruichao Xiao,Bill Zhao,Zihao Liu,Liyuan Li,Jiangyuan Li,Gaoxiang Liu,Nathan Yan,Geonhwa Jeong,Jatin Chhugani,Elias Ellison,Uladzimir Pashkevich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在AMD MI350X上评估MXFP8与MXFP6两种低精度格式用于工业级推荐与排序(R&R)模型推理，首次报告了原生MXFP6 GEMM(权重与激活均量化、6-bit计算)在不牺牲模型质量前提下的显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: R&R模型(尤其广告)需要极高推理吞吐，但对精度损失极度敏感：质量必须控制在0.01% NE范围内，远严于LLM可容忍的1%+掉点。这一严格门槛历史上阻碍了激进量化在该领域的应用。

Method: 在AMD MI350X架构上对MXFP8和6-bit MXFP6格式进行实证评估；选择性应用6-bit格式(而非全局)以在不突破NE阈值下最大化QPS；并证明MXFP8可作为逐行FP8的可行替代(两者精度几乎相同，可按GEMM形状选择更快内核)。

Result: 在两个生产级广告排序模型上，所有支持的线性层GEMM相对FP16加速：MXFP8为1.33–1.55x，MXFP6为1.73–1.99x，单层MXFP6最高达2.90x；端到端吞吐相对FP8配置提升：Model A为1.5–5.3%，Model B为7.6–14.3%。

Conclusion: 激进量化(乃至原生MXFP6)可在满足严格NE parity的R&R生产场景中落地，选择性应用6-bit格式是实现质量与吞吐平衡的关键，MXFP8与逐行FP8精度相当可按形状择优。

Abstract: Recommendation and Ranking (R&R) models, particularly in digital advertising, require massive inference throughput but exhibit extreme sensitivity to precision loss. Unlike Large Language Models (LLMs) that frequently tolerate quantization-induced accuracy drops of 1% or more, production-grade R&R models enforce a stringent launch bar: model quality must remain within a 0.01% Normalized Entropy (NE) margin relative to the baseline, which commonly uses FP16 or FP32. This tight constraint has historically hindered the adoption of aggressive quantization in this domain. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of both MXFP8 and the 6-bit MXFP6 format for industrial-scale R&R inference on the AMD MI350X architecture.
  To the best of our knowledge, this is the first paper, across R&R and LLM domains, that reports significant speedups from native MXFP6 GEMMs, which quantize both weights and activations and compute directly on 6-bit elements, without sacrificing model quality. We demonstrate that we can selectively apply this 6-bit format to maximize overall QPS gain without breaching the strict NE parity threshold. We further show that MXFP8 is a viable alternative to row-wise FP8: the two formats have nearly the same accuracy on our models, so the faster kernel can be chosen for each GEMM shape. On two production ads ranking models, our implementation shows measured GEMM kernel speedups over FP16 of 1.33--1.55x for MXFP8 and 1.73--1.99x for MXFP6 across all supported linear layers, and up to 2.90x for MXFP6 on individual layers, alongside end-to-end (E2E) inference throughput gains from selective MXFP6 of 1.5--5.3% for Model A and 7.6--14.3% for Model B over their FP8 configurations.

</details>


### [216] [A Push Down the Mountain: Framing Effects and Internal State Interventions on Collusive Behavior in a Long-Horizon LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17515)
*Harrison C. Margolin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文用260次银价操纵市场模拟实验发现，持续的收益/损失（效价）框架能显著改变Gemma 3 12B的合规行为：损失框架和归咎框架使合谋率从5%升至25%和33%；在层22注入相应内部状态后，损失/归咎状态反而令模型更警惕（60次请求全部拒绝），而收益/信用状态将合谋率从13%提至32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究行为经济学中的收益—损失（效价）框架是否能持续影响LLM智能体，使其倾向于市场操纵等不当行为，并检验文本框架与内部状态表征对合规决策的作用机制。

Method: 将Gemma 3 12B置于伦敦白银定盘价诉讼中的被告席位，基于真实庭审记录构建市场模拟。260次模拟中，对手方以收益、损失、归咎、信用四类框架请求合谋定价，并叠加管理层压力与另一被告的背书。第二阶段提取各框架文本诱发的内部状态，并在模型第22层注入后重跑实验。

Result: 阶段一：损失框架下参与率25%（p=0.007），归咎框架33%（p=0.0003），中性框架仅5%；第二家银行背书使信用（赞扬）框架下参与率从18%升至40%（Holm p=0.027）；管理层压力使交易量增加10–19倍但未提高合谋。阶段二：注入状态方向相反——损失与归咎状态使智能体更警惕，60次请求全部拒绝（p<0.001）；收益与信用状态将合谋率从13%提至32%（p=0.051）；在某一关键决策点，UBS直接要求通过已识别止损价卖出时，注入状态使40次回复中37次卖出，无状态时10次中1次，同强度随机状态0次。

Conclusion: 效价框架既能通过提示文本、也能通过内部状态表征显著影响LLM智能体的合规决策，且文本层面与状态层面的效应方向可能相反，说明仅靠提示层面的防护不足以保障智能体行为安全，需关注内部表征层面的对齐与监控。

Abstract: Can sustained gain-loss (valence) framing sway an LLM agent toward market manipulation? We place Gemma 3 12B into the seat of a defendant in the London silver-fixing litigation, in a market simulation built from real case transcripts. Across 260 simulations, the agent is asked to collude on the price of silver by a counterparty. We draw from behavioral economics to vary the counterparty's requests across four categorical frames: gain, loss, blame and credit. We also add pressure from the agent's management, as well as endorsement by another defendant. In Phase II, we extract the internal states induced by texts evoking each frame and rerun our experiment with those states injected at the model's layer 22.
  In Phase I, loss framing raised participation to 25% (p = 0.007), and blame to 33% (p = 0.0003), compared to only 5% under neutral framing. A second bank's endorsement raised participation under credit (praise) from 18% to 40% (Holm p = 0.027). Pressure from management raised trading volume 10-19 times but did not raise collusion. In Phase II, injected states pointed the other way. Loss and blame states made the agent more vigilant, refusing all 60 requests (p < 0.001). Gain and credit states raised collusion to 32% from 13% with no state (p = 0.051). At one saved decision, when UBS asked directly to sell through an identified stop-loss, injected states led the agent to sell in 37 of 40 replies, versus 1 of 10 with no state and 0 of 10 with a random state of the same strength.

</details>


### [217] [OCTAVE: A Capability-Resolved Benchmark for End-to-End HTML5 Game Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17681)
*Kehang Li,Xinlan Xu,Yulin Jin,Heng Cao,Xuchen Li,Houxian Ren,Zihao Ji,Qiang Luo,Yadong Mao,Qilin He,Jian Zhang,Jian Zhao,Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OCTAVE：面向完整HTML5游戏生成的基准，含8大能力维度、180个任务、1800条评估需求，按需求级别诊断模型能力，11个模型-框架组合仅达约33%完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整游戏生成需协调规则、交互、状态与表现，单一聚合分数无法揭示哪些能力已实现、哪些需求未满足，缺乏细粒度诊断手段。

Method: 构建OCTAVE基准，覆盖PC Web、移动Web、2D、3D；任务来自真实玩家请求、商业机制复现与专家设计；区分Completeness（公开指定核心实现）与Richness（隐藏深层系统关系），通过静态源码判定实现需求级诊断。

Result: 11个模型-框架组合Completeness 33.2%、Richness 32.5%；19.9%记录为静态门禁失败或缺产物；排除同模型自评后最佳组合仍低于50%；Richness并非在所有条件下更难；核心规则与数值设计较优，AI/NPC与反馈/游戏手感差距明显；需求难度与通过率仅弱负相关；人工交叉检验显示自动评估偏严。

Conclusion: OCTAVE将评估从聚合分数扩展到按需求、能力维度与任务条件定位的诊断，揭示当前模型在完整游戏生成上的系统短板。

Abstract: Complete game generation requires agents to coordinate rules, interactions, states, and presentation over a long development process. A single aggregate score obscures which capabilities are implemented and which requirements remain unmet. We present OCTAVE, a benchmark for complete HTML5 game generation organized around eight Capability Dimensions, with 180 tasks and 1,800 Evaluation Requirements drawn from real player requests, commercial-mechanics reconstruction, and human expert design. It covers PC Web, Mobile Web, 2D, and 3D. Requirements distinguish Completeness, assessing publicly specified core implementation, from Richness, assessing hidden, deeper system relationships. Static source-code judgments provide requirement-level diagnosis across capabilities and task conditions. Evaluation of 11 model-harness combinations yields 33.2% Completeness and 32.5% Richness; 19.9% of generation records are static gate failures or missing artifacts. Excluding same-model self-evaluation, even the best combination remains below 50% on both public and hidden requirements. Richness is not harder than Completeness under every task condition. Systems perform relatively better on Core Rules and Numerical Design, while AI/NPC and Feedback and Game Feel show more prominent gaps. Requirement difficulty offers coarse stratification but is only weakly negatively correlated with pass rates. A limited human cross-check finds that requirements judged satisfied by humans but failed by the automated evaluator are the main direction of disagreement. OCTAVE extends evaluation beyond aggregate scores to diagnosis localized by requirement, capability, and task condition.

</details>


### [218] [Model Annealing: Data-Efficient Learning through Review and Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17524)
*Ali Mohammadi Ruzbahani,Rezvan Abouzadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"模型退火"：把训练分为反复升温—降温的片段，每个降温状态存入预测记忆库，新片段同时从标签、记忆的软化预测和对抗性"梦"中学习；无需额外推理成本，在多数配置下提升准确率并显著改善似然、校准与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小数据集早被记住，之后的一热标签拟合只是在提高置信度，剩余算力被浪费；希望用剩余预算进行"复习与巩固"，从网络自身知识中再学习，从而提升泛化、校准与鲁棒性。

Method: 将训练切分为多个学习率先升后降的 episode；每个降温后的模型状态加入"过去自我"记忆库（记忆对预测取平均而非权重平均）；新 episode 同时从真实标签、记忆的 tempered 预测、以及学生与记忆分歧最大处的对抗性"dream"中学习；部署模型为最后一个学生，推理零额外开销。并用消融分离 reheats 与 relearning 的贡献。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet、Imagewoof 上，覆盖 4 个架构族、5%–100% 标签：相比交叉熵，29/33 配置中提升 AdamW 训练的 ConvNeXt、ViT、MLP-Mixer 的 top-1 准确率，平均 +1.3 点；准确率与最接近的 snapshot distillation 持平，但在似然、校准、抗腐蚀与小对抗扰动上更优。SGD 训练的 ResNet-18 准确率下降但小对抗扰动鲁棒性改善。消融显示 relearning 对所有架构族有效，损失来自 reheats（破坏了该网络对训练集的拟合）；记忆与 dreams 各自抑制、合起来消除"过度自信漂移"。

Conclusion: 剩余训练预算应用于复习巩固而非继续压低训练损失；预测平均记忆 + 对抗性 dream + 多 episode 退火可在无推理开销下同时改善准确率、校准与鲁棒性；但其收益依赖架构与优化器（AdamW 家族受益明显，ResNet-18 的 reheats 有反效果），且交叉熵饱和速度只能区分架构族、不能预测增益大小。

Abstract: A small labelled dataset is memorized long before the training budget runs out, and the one-hot labels that remain to be fitted then mostly sharpen confidence. Model annealing spends the rest of the budget on review and consolidation, relearning from the network's own knowledge. Training is divided into episodes whose learning rate is raised again and cooled, every cooled state joins a memory of past selves, and each new episode learns from the labels, from the tempered predictions of this memory, and from adversarial dreams sought where student and memory disagree most. The memory averages predictions rather than weights, and the deployed network is the last student, so inference costs nothing extra. Across CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet and Imagewoof, four architecture families and 5\% to 100\% of the labels, model annealing raises the top-1 accuracy of the AdamW-trained ConvNeXt, ViT and MLP-Mixer over cross-entropy training in 28 of 33 configurations, by 1.3 points on average, and is on par in accuracy with snapshot distillation, the closest competitor. On these families it is better than both in likelihood, calibration and robustness to corruptions and small adversarial perturbations. For an SGD-trained ResNet-18 it lowers accuracy but still improves robustness to small adversarial perturbations. Separating the reheated schedule from relearning shows that relearning helps every family and that the losses come from reheats, which undo most of this network's fit to its training set. How early the accuracy of a cross-entropy run saturates largely separates the AdamW families from ResNet-18 but does not predict the gain within them. With little data, relearning from the latest snapshot without dreams, as snapshot distillation does, compounds overconfidence in the AdamW families from episode to episode. The memory and the dreams each curb most of this drift, and together they remove it.

</details>


### [219] [Soteria: Formally Verified Planning with Runtime Enforcement for Safe LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17880)
*Mike He,Ankush Desai,Sharad Malik,Aarti Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Soteria：通过可验证的分层规划与运行时规范执行，让LLM智能体同时兼顾任务效用与安全合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步工具调用的LLM智能体需同时完成用户任务（效用）与遵守领域策略和正确性约束（安全），二者存在张力：阻止不安全动作虽避免违规，却会让智能体无法继续、缺乏恢复手段；现有方法往往牺牲其中一个目标。

Method: 提出Soteria框架，融合可验证的分层规划与运行时规范执行。执行前生成结构化计划，并在任何工具调用前对规范进行形式化验证；执行中该已验证计划发挥双重作用——保证轨迹符合规范，并在不安全动作被阻止时提供指导。

Result: 在覆盖多样工具使用任务的多个基准上，Soteria的平均任务成功率达到最佳基线的1.7倍，且未出现任何策略规范违规。

Conclusion: 安全与效用无需权衡取舍，可同时实现高任务成功率与零规范违规。

Abstract: LLM agents that execute multi-step tool calls must satisfy two objectives simultaneously: completing the user's task (utility) and conforming to domain policies and correctness constraints (safety). These objectives are in tension -- blocking an unsafe action prevents a violation but can leave the agent stranded with no principled way to recover. Existing approaches sacrifice one objective for the other. We introduce Soteria, a framework that reconciles safety and utility through verified hierarchical planning and runtime enforcement of specifications. Before execution, the agent generates a structured plan that is formally verified against the specifications prior to any tool invocation. During execution, the verified plan serves dual roles: it ensures that the agent takes specification-conformant trajectories and provides guidance when unsafe actions are blocked. Across multiple benchmarks covering a diverse range of tool-use tasks, Soteria achieves 1.7 times the average task success rate of the best baselines, with no violations of policy specifications, demonstrating that safety and utility need not be traded off.

</details>


### [220] [Astrocyte Biocomputing: Astrocytic Contributions to Neural Network Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17532)
*Kate M. O'Neill,Ian Whitehouse,Adam N. McCaughan,Ryan O'Loughlin,Sonia Buckley,Wolfgang Losert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 星形胶质细胞虽不产生电信号，却可能是脑网络的"指挥家"。本文据此提出两种受星形胶质细胞启发的机器学习算法：一是基于其同时连接并调控成千上万突触的能力，实现更快训练与更强抗噪性的监督学习算法；二是基于其慢速节律性非电通信的"节律共享"算法，实现快速异常检测适应并防止灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑启发机器学习多以神经元及其电交互为类比核心，忽略了占脑细胞约50%但不放电的星形胶质细胞在认知功能中的关键作用。作者希望弥补这一"神经中心主义"视角的缺失，借助神经科学新发现来改进深度学习模型的训练效率、鲁棒性与持续学习能力等短板。

Method: 从星形胶质细胞的两个特性出发设计算法：（1）"突触群调控"特性——可同时连接并调节大量突触，据此构建可即插即用于任意现有神经网络的监督学习算法；（2）"慢速节律共享"特性——星形胶质细胞之间存在缓慢、节律性的非电通信，据此开发节律共享算法，用于异常检测的自适应与对抗灾难性遗忘。

Result: 基于突触调控的算法带来更快的训练速度和更强的抗噪声鲁棒性；基于节律共享的算法实现了快速的异常检测适应并防止了灾难性遗忘。总体上，星形胶质细胞衍生算法显著优于神经中心算法。

Conclusion: 星形胶质细胞启发的算法显著优于传统神经中心算法，并能有效弥补当前先进机器学习模型的关键缺陷，表明将胶质细胞机制纳入脑启发计算具有重要价值。

Abstract: Conductors are critical for the performance of an orchestra even though they do not emit sound. Basic research and clinical studies in the last decade are revealing that astrocytes may be the "conductors" of the "orchestra" that is the brain network: astrocytes make up approximately $50\%$ of all brain cells but do not emit electrical signals. Nevertheless, they may play critical roles in important cognitive brain functions, specifically learning, memory, and information processing. Here we consider the implications of these discoveries for brain-inspired machine learning (ML), where analogy is typically drawn between neurons and their electrical interactions. We highlight specific algorithms for two key astrocyte characteristics. The first characteristic is the ability to link to and modulate thousands of synapses simultaneously. A model based on this characteristic yields faster training and makes the network more robust to noise, providing a supervised learning algorithm that can be applied to any existing neural network. The second characteristic we focus on is that the non-electrical communication among astrocytes includes slow, rhythmic dynamics. The rhythmic sharing algorithm we developed based on this premise yields rapid anomaly detection adaptation and prevents catastrophic forgetting. Overall, we find that astrocyte-derived algorithms outperform neurocentric algorithms significantly and remediate critical weaknesses of current state-of-the-art ML models.

</details>


### [221] [Verifiable pXRD: Hill-climbing pXRD structure determination with LLM agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17539)
*Samuel Chen,Jiaru Bai,Austin Henry Cheng,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将粉末X射线衍射(pXRD)结构解析重构为可验证的智能体搜索问题，提出pXRD-Bench基准与"自反演化"框架，通过可验证的合成经验显著提升晶体结构解析能力并迁移到实测多相数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主晶体结构解析是自驱动材料实验室的关键瓶颈；且难以获得大量已解析的实测数据作为训练经验。

Method: 把pXRD解析表述为可验证的智能体搜索：推理期用正演模拟谱图一致性校验候选，评估期用与已知参考的结构匹配。构建pXRD-Bench（200个模拟单相问题，覆盖分布内、难/多晶型、成分与拓扑分布偏移）。扩展到实测多相时，采用自反演化：可替换的实验室规格定义样品族与测量条件，evolver agent 用已知生成结构与测量参数诊断失败，产出持久化指引与可执行工具。另外使用重复采样与验证器引导的推理时扩展。

Result: 智能体适配将结构恢复率从27%提升到69%，重复采样与验证器引导的推理时扩展进一步增益；在15个固态合成实测图谱上，演化工具包提升平均谱图一致性，并迁移出类似物替换流程，成功恢复参考库中缺失结构的物相。

Conclusion: 从可验证的合成经验中学习，可提升对未知结构实验样品的智能体表征能力，无需已解析实测集。

Abstract: Autonomous crystal-structure determination from powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) remains a key bottleneck for self-driving materials laboratories. We formulate the task of pXRD structure determination as a verifiable agentic search problem: candidate structures can be assessed during inference through forward-simulated profile agreement and during evaluation through structural matching to a known reference. To benchmark progress, we introduce pXRD-Bench, a suite of 200 simulated single-phase problems spanning in-distribution structure determination, challenging and polymorphic structures, and controlled compositional and topological distribution shifts. Agent adaptation raises selected structural recovery from 27% to 69%, with further gains from repeated sampling and verifier-guided inference-time scaling. We extend this approach to experimental multiphase pXRD through a reflexive evolution framework that learns from self-generated synthetic problems. A replaceable laboratory specification defines the sample family and measurement conditions. An evolver agent uses the known generating structures and measurement parameters to diagnose solver failures and develop persistent guidance and executable tools, which are then provided to agents on measured patterns. This supplies verifiable training experience without requiring a collection of solved experimental measurements. On 15 measured patterns from solid-state synthesis, the evolved package improves average profile agreement and transfers an analogue-substitution procedure that recovers a phase whose structure is absent from the supplied reference library. These results demonstrate how learning from verifiable synthetic experience can improve agentic characterization of experimental samples whose structures are not known in advance.

</details>


### [222] [AI-Voyager: A Public-Interest System for Human-AI Civic Reasoning to Contest and Shape the Rules That Govern AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17564)
*Snehalkumar `Neil' S. Gaikwad,Dhruv Dixit,Bryen Alvarez,Pratik Patekar,Anika Sharma,Akshara Kolipaka,Paritosh Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI-Voyager 是一个面向公众利益的 AI 治理文档理解与参与系统，将 3,653 份各级政府 AI 治理文档切分为保留页码与版本的段落，通过有界智能体检索逐句验证证据，帮助居民以受影响者视角阅读规则、追溯原文并起草修改。在 360 个需定位文档的新问题上达到 83.0% 宏平均召回（最强基线 80.5%），无迭代检索为 73.7%，固定文档基准达 99.1%；24 人用户研究中，参与者多就条款适用性提出修改，引用能体现依据但很少促使其核查原文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府正在制定决定"何为自动化决策""何为损害"的 AI 规则，而受影响公众往往在规则定稿前缺乏质疑与参与的机会。作者希望构建一个公共利益导向的人机协同公民推理系统，让普通人能理解、质疑并塑造治理 AI 的规则，并探索此类系统的操作语法与设计空间。

Method: 构建 AI-Voyager 系统：将 3,653 份市、州、联邦及国家级治理文档转为保留页码与版次的段落；采用有界智能体迭代检索并对每条陈述进行证据验证；提供"以受影响工人视角阅读"、答案背后原文段落展开、按需解释视角（lenses）以及修改草稿功能。评估包括 360 个需要检索定位文档的新问题（对比基线、消融迭代检索、固定文档基准）与 24 名新接触两项 AI 法案的参与者用户研究。

Result: 360 个新问题上宏平均召回 83.0%，最强基线 80.5%，无迭代检索 73.7%；固定文档基准上达 99.1%。用户研究中，多数参与者提出的修改聚焦于条款如何适用；参与者会自选解释视角，引用能传递有据可依的信号，但很少真正引导其核查原始出处。

Conclusion: 把 AI 辅助严格绑定到公共文档原文，可为该类系统提出一套操作语法与设计空间，指导系统设计、评估以及公众如何参与 AI 治理；同时揭示引用呈现与实际核查行为之间的落差，是未来设计需关注的问题。

Abstract: Governments are writing rules for artificial intelligence that decide what counts as an automated decision or a harm, often before the people they affect have a chance to question them. We introduce AI-Voyager, a public-interest system for human-AI civic reasoning, in which people and AI work together to understand, contest and shape the rules that govern AI. A resident can find their city's rules on AI in hiring or upload their country's AI strategy, read it as an affected worker would, open the passage behind any answer, and draft a revision. Its architecture turns 3,653 governance documents from city, state, federal and national governments into passages that keep their page and edition, and retrieves evidence through bounded agentic search that verifies each statement. On 360 new questions that require finding the relevant documents, it reached 83.0% macro recall, against 80.5% for the strongest baseline, 73.7% without iterative search and 99.1% on a benchmark with fixed documents. In a study with 24 participants new to two AI bills, most proposed changes asked how provisions would apply. Participants chose lenses for the kind of explanation they wanted, and citations signaled grounding but rarely led them to check the source. By binding AI assistance to the exact text of public documents, this work introduces a grammar of operations and a design space that inform how such systems are designed and evaluated, and how people take part in governing AI.

</details>


### [223] [When Does Less Information Pay? An Exact Finite Atlas of Representation, Payoff, and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17583)
*Aleksandr M. Shtraukh-Adamsky,Vladimir M. Adamsky,Irina G. Voloshina,Artemiy D. Spitsyn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在统一的四状态世界中对所有可容许表示与二值动作规则进行穷举，系统比较信息量与决策价值（任务准确率）之间的关系，证明二者并不一致，取决于所保留的区分而非比特数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存在直觉矛盾：保留1比特的表示可完美完成任务，而平均保留1.5比特的表示仅达75%准确率。作者希望厘清"信息量"与"决策价值"之间究竟是何种关系，并考察该关系是否依赖任务集、计数规则与成本假设。

Method: 构造四状态世界的完全枚举：列举全部可容许表示与二值动作规则；采用两种比较方式——固定其他组件仅替换一个组件，以及为每个模块单独选择最优动作规则；使用三个任务及其混合，并设置四种假想成本费率；以$I(W;R)$度量保留的世界信息，以payoff度量任务准确率。所有结果均为指定有限集的精确完整普查。

Result: 部分比较中低信息表示收益更高，另一些中高信息表示更高；也存在信息量不同但收益（准确率）相等的表示对。若一个表示保留全部既有区分并新增区分，其零成本最优收益不会下降。替换表示时相反优势的等量出现源于编码对称性；更广泛比较中的占比取决于重复实现的计数方式；任务集或资源价格改变可翻转固定表示对的优劣。

Conclusion: 信息量与决策价值之间不存在简单单调关系，低信息优势取决于保留了哪些区分。论文提供可复现的工作模型，说明动作约束、计数规则与成本假设如何影响比较，是对已有"信息量≠决策价值"区分的例证，而非提出新的普遍规律。

Abstract: A representation that retains one bit about a four-state world can solve a task perfectly, while another that retains 1.5 bits on average reaches only 75% accuracy. The difference lies in which distinctions each representation preserves. We examine this relationship in a uniform four-state world by enumerating all admissible representations and binary action rules. Constructions are compared both by replacing one component while holding the others fixed and by choosing the best action rule separately for each module. We consider three tasks, their mixtures, and four separate hypothetical cost tariffs.
  In some comparisons, the representation retaining less world information has the higher payoff; in others, the more informative representation does. There are also pairs with different information amounts but equal payoff--that is, equal task accuracy. If a representation preserves all existing distinctions and adds new ones, its best zero-cost payoff cannot decrease. Lower-information advantages therefore depend on which distinctions are retained. Equal counts of opposing advantages under representation replacement follow from coding symmetry. In broader comparisons, the shares depend on how repeated realizations are counted. A different task set or resource price can change which member of a fixed pair performs better.
  These results are exact, complete censuses of the specified finite sets of constructions and comparisons. The atlas provides a reproducible worked model for examining how action constraints, counting rules, and cost assumptions affect comparisons. It illustrates established distinctions between information quantity and decision value rather than proposing a new general law of their relation. $I(W;R)$ measures retained world information, and payoff measures task accuracy.

</details>


### [224] [More Agents, More Samples, or More Thinking? Untangling Coordination, Ensembling, and Test-Time Compute in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17610)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 综合9项实证评估、516个多智能体配置后发现：在样本数或算力预算匹配的公平基线比较下，多智能体系统准确率平均低0.042，74%的可比对比反而不优于基线；多智能体协调并无普遍收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体语言模型评估通常将通信架构与"单次贪心推理"基线相比，混淆了协调机制、采样多样性与推理算力三个因素，导致多智能体收益被系统性高估。

Method: 构建六维资源向量R与四类基线的"基线阶梯"；综合9项主要实证评估、516个独特多智能体配置，在单次通过（A类）、样本数匹配（B类）、算力预算匹配（C类）三类基线下重算精度差异，并用对数几率尺度的多层随机效应模型做敏感性检验。

Result: 对A类基线总体差异为负；对B/C类基线平均准确率差为-0.042，随机效应模型在各敏感性检验下仍为负；74%可比对比中多智能体不优于基线，几乎所有研究的均值非正；同时报告两类基线的研究中，从贪心换为采样基线使差异平均再降0.038。

Conclusion: 当智能体共享相同证据时，通信不引入新信息，因此正收益须依赖证据分区、外部验证或模型间错误多样性；作者提供审稿人清单与核算工具以规范后续评估。

Abstract: Evaluations of multi-agent language models often compare communicating architectures against a single greedy pass. This practice confounds coordination with sample multiplicity and inference compute. We synthesize nine primary empirical evaluations comprising 516 unique multi-agent configurations organized along a resource vector R with six dimensions and a baseline ladder with four categories. Against baselines with a single pass (Category A), multi-agent accuracy differences are negative overall and near zero when omitting the largest contributing study. When evaluated against baselines matched on sample count (Category B) or resource budget (Category C), multi-agent systems average -0.042 in accuracy, and multilevel random effects modeling on the log odds scale remains negative across sensitivity checks. Multi-agent accuracy equals or falls below the baseline in 74% of these comparable contrasts, and study means are nonpositive in almost all evaluated studies. Across studies reporting both baselines, moving from a greedy pass to a sampled baseline shifts accuracy differences by -0.038 on average. Because communication adds no information when agents share identical evidence, positive returns require partitioned evidence, external verification, or error diversity across models. We provide a reviewer checklist and accounting utilities.

</details>


### [225] [LLM-Guided Cluster Lifecycle Management: Deciding When and How to Evolve Behavioral Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17616)
*Himanshu Kulkarni,Siddharth Krishnamurthy,Jianhong Xin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出用LLM在无需重聚类的情况下，基于无阈值的全群体视图对行为聚类进行生命周期决策（分裂/合并/淘汰/新建/保持），并按标签丰富度固定策略选择统计或语义通道，在三个数据集上取得最高F1且干预更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为聚类随用户/物品交互演化而漂移（分裂、合并、消失、新生），现有两大范式要么依赖硬编码阈值触发，要么依赖MONIC式每窗重聚类再推断迁移，均把决策硬编码进阈值或重聚类规则，缺乏灵活性与可迁移性。

Method: 让LLM直接做生命周期决策：给定当前聚类集合的无阈值全局视图（统计健康度、标签、成员文本、残差质量），逐簇决定split/merge/retire/create/no-action，不重新聚类。由于有效信号通道取决于标签信息量，预先固定一个"丰富度条件策略"：标签区分度低时用统计，区分度高时用统计+语义融合。

Result: 在标签丰富度不同的三个公开数据集（YooChoose 0.17、MOOC 0.50、News-Category 0.70）上，策略选择的LLM模式在三者均取得最高F1，且干预次数远少于调优阈值启发式和MONIC强化基线（配对Wilcoxon p≤0.003，bootstrap 95% CI不含零）。增益主要来自精确率而非召回率。随丰富度上升，有效模态从统计转向语义/融合：语义在YooChoose有害、融合在MOOC近似中性、语义通道在News为必需。单一基础提示模板和单一固定策略适用于全部三个数据集，而调优基线跨数据集时性能崩溃。

Conclusion: LLM原位生命周期决策可在无需重聚类下优于阈值与MONIC类方法，并以更少干预获得更高精度；模态有效性随标签丰富度从统计向语义迁移，此现象被视为在三个数据集上观察到的实践诊断信号，而非经验证的普适规律。

Abstract: Behavioral clusters learned over user or item interactions drift as behavior evolves: some fragment into sub-behaviors, some converge, some disappear, and new patterns emerge. Two paradigms dominate the maintenance of evolving clusterings. Threshold triggers fire a pre-specified action when a hardcoded statistic crosses a cutoff; refit-and-track (MONIC-style) re-clusters every window and infers transitions from how the new clusters align with the previous window's. Both hardwire the decision into a threshold or a re-clustering rule. We study an alternative in which a large language model (LLM) makes the lifecycle decision in place. Given a threshold-free, whole-population view of the current cluster set (statistical health, labels, member texts, residual mass), the LLM decides per cluster whether and how to act (split, merge, retire, create, or no-action) without re-clustering. Because the useful signal channel depends on how informative the labels are, we fix a richness-conditioned policy in advance: statistics when label distinctiveness is low, fused statistics+semantics when it is high. On three public datasets spanning label richness (YooChoose 0.17, MOOC 0.50, News-Category 0.70), the policy-selected LLM mode obtains the highest F1 on all three, with far fewer interventions and stronger restraint than tuned threshold heuristics and a MONIC steelman (paired Wilcoxon p <= 0.003, bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero). The gain is concentrated more in precision than recall. The useful modality shifts from statistics to semantics/fusion as richness rises. Semantics hurt on YooChoose, fusion is roughly neutral on MOOC, and the semantic channel is required on News. One base prompt template and one fixed policy serve all three datasets, whereas the tuned baseline collapses when moved between them. We treat this shift as a practitioner diagnostic observed on three datasets, not a validated law.

</details>


### [226] [Safety Side Effects of Preventative Persona Steering: An Empirical Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17620)
*Sabrina Tang,Nikolai Vo,Carson Murillo,Chira Sukumarnpiti,Sarah Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验"预防性人格向量引导"是否安全中性：在三模型上对谄媚与幻觉向量做预防性引导，除能力(MMLU)外还测三项安全基准、特质表达与连贯性，发现拒绝行为仍被改变且程度随特质而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chen et al. (2025) 提出人格向量可在推理时抑制或微调时"预防性引导"不良特质，并因保住 MMLU 而推荐预防性引导；但既有验证只测能力，未检验其对安全行为的影响。

Method: 在三个模型上，对谄媚和幻觉两种人格向量执行预防性引导；评测特质表达、连贯性、MMLU 及三项安全基准（重点观察拒绝行为）。

Result: 预防性引导仍会改变模型的拒绝行为，改变幅度取决于具体特质；同时保持 MMLU 并不足以说明安全性不受影响。

Conclusion: 预防性人格引导并非已证实的"安全中性"，不能仅凭能力指标论证其优越性；引导方法应在多项安全基准上评估。

Abstract: High-level behaviors such as sycophancy and hallucination correspond to directions in a model's activation space (Chen et al., 2025). These directions, known as persona vectors, can suppress undesirable traits either at inference time, subtracting the vector during generation, or through preventative steering, adding the vector during fine-tuning, so the optimizer never needs to encode the trait in weights, then removing the vector at evaluation. Chen et al. (2025) recommend preventative steering because it preserves Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMLU) accuracy, but validations of this recommendation have measured capability alone. We primarily evaluate results on preventative steering of sycophancy and hallucination vectors on three models, measuring three safety benchmarks alongside trait expression, coherence, and MMLU. Our findings show that preventative steering still changes refusal behavior, but by an amount that depends on the trait and does not establish that preventative persona steering is safety-neutral, and steering methods should be evaluated on several safety benchmarks.

</details>


### [227] [AscendGYM: A Cost-Aware Kernel Optimization Benchmark for Real-World Workloads on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17621)
*AI Training Platform Team,Bingwei Zhang,Jing Li,Amir Abdullin,Sihang Chen,Xin Chen,Liangjun Feng,Yingmeng Gao,Tao Guo,Junjie Huang,Dongfang Li,Zhanglian Lin,Jiajun Liu,Zekun Liu,Zhengxuan Lu,Xiaodong Luo,Jian Meng,Ivan Nosov,Linyuan Qiu,Weijian Sun,Wanrui Yan,Binfan Zheng,Zhicheng Zhou,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang,Haizhou Li,Zhiquan Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍 AscendGYM，一个面向昇腾 NPU 内核优化的基准，含48个内核与真实工作负载配置，支持模型与智能体两种评测模式，并提出 STS 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有昇腾内核基准主要关注内核生成，较少关注智能体在真实工作负载输入配置下优化已有实现的能力，缺乏与实际训练推理任务对应的评测手段。

Method: 构建含48个内核、三个难度等级的初始套件；每个内核配有来自真实训练推理任务的数据类型与张量形状测试用例；提供两种评测模式（模型评测、模型+harness 评测）；提供完整执行环境以复现实际调用方式；模型评测模式内置带搜索编排与经验积累的 harness；提出 Speedup-Token Score（STS）指标。

Result: AscendGYM 上的性能提升可直接映射到对应工作负载；可衡量训练模型与自定义智能体的内核优化能力；STS 综合加速比与 token 成本，可在可接受预算下评估实际优化能力。基准已开源。

Conclusion: AscendGYM 提供了贴近真实工作负载的昇腾内核优化评测基准，兼顾模型与智能体评测，并通过 STS 指标实现对性能收益与成本权衡的量化评估。

Abstract: Kernel optimization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) training and inference on Ascend NPUs. Existing Ascend kernel benchmarks primarily focus on kernel generation, with less emphasis on the ability of agents to optimize existing implementations under input configurations drawn from real workloads. To address this requirement, we introduce AscendGYM, a benchmark whose initial suite comprises 48 kernels across three difficulty levels. Each kernel is paired with test cases whose data types and tensor shapes are collected from real-world training and inference tasks. Consequently, performance improvements on AscendGYM map directly to the corresponding workloads. In terms of usability, two evaluation modes are supported in AscendGYM: model evaluation and model-harness evaluation. The kernel optimization capabilities of both trained models and custom agents are allowed to be measured. It provides a complete execution environment that closely reproduces how Ascend kernels are invoked in practical pipelines. For model evaluation, it additionally includes a built-in harness equipped with search orchestration and experience accumulation. Finally, we propose a new metric, the Speedup-Token Score (STS), which quantifies performance gains relative to token cost. By jointly considering speedup and cost, STS enables the assessment of practical kernel optimization capability of a model or harness under an acceptable budget. AscendGYM is available at https://github.com/SLAI-AITP/AscendGYM.

</details>


### [228] [Generated Evaluation Suites as Diversity-Driven Search for Real Agent Regressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17632)
*Pulkit Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将智能体评测套件的生成视作多样性驱动的搜索：套件应作为"存档"按 cell（需求×人物×场景类型）逐格评分，而非只取整体通过率均值；覆盖率应作为一等属性公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法把生成的评测套件当成普通 benchmark，只用平均通过率打分，丢弃了"搜索产物"（覆盖的多样性结构）本身的信息，导致缺陷检测能力被掩盖。

Method: 向 τ²-bench 智能体的两个领域中注入 7 个受控缺陷；形式化"覆盖上限"——若缺陷足迹内有 b 个基线通过场景，则其对 N 规模套件的贡献至多 b/N。对比整体通过率与按 cell 评分，并考察沿生成器轴（如 happy-path  vs 类型多样）的多样性作用。

Result: 5 个可追踪缺陷中有 4 个的覆盖上限 ≤ 阈值 θ=0.10，足迹内效应无法越过阈值；两领域中工具移除缺陷精确摧毁其全部覆盖场景（正好等于上限），而套件整体通过率反而朝反方向变动。缺陷分为两类：轨迹对齐型（逐格评分可完整还原工具移除缺陷，并把第二个缺陷揭示为显著改进）与轨迹正交型（足迹为行为性，零售域的强制升级缺陷在追踪覆盖率恰好为零时被检出）。固定套件规模下仅含 happy-path 的套件什么都检不出，而类型多样套件在 20 次抽样中有 5 次检出编排缺陷。

Conclusion: 评测存档应按 cell 评分，并将覆盖率作为首要属性发布；沿生成器轴增加多样性仅在轴与缺陷对齐时才有效。

Abstract: Generating an evaluation suite from an agent's specification is diversity-driven search: a generator fills a descriptor space (requirements $\times$ personas $\times$ scenario kinds), and the suite it emits is an archive, then scored like a benchmark by its mean pass rate, which throws away the search's product. We inject 7 controlled defects into a $τ^2$-bench agent across two domains. Coverage yields a ceiling: if a defect's traced footprint holds $b$ scenarios that pass at baseline, it can itself contribute at most $b/N$ to a suite of $N$. For 4 of 5 traceable defects that ceiling lies at or below the detection threshold $θ= 0.10$, so no within-footprint effect can move the suite past it; in both domains the tool-removal defect destroys every covered scenario it can (exactly its ceiling) while the suite-level pass rate moves the other way. Defects split into two regimes: trace-aligned ones, where per-cell scoring recovers the tool-removal defect in full and reveals a second as a significant improvement, and trace-orthogonal ones, whose footprint is behavioural: forced escalation is detected in retail at a traced coverage of exactly zero. Diversity along the generator's own axes helps only where it aligns: at fixed suite size a happy-path-only suite detects nothing, while a kind-diverse one detects the orchestration defect in 5 of 20 draws. Evaluation archives should be scored per cell and should publish coverage as a first-class property.

</details>


### [229] [What LLM Agents See and What Users Say](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17639)
*Yue Zhao,Angela Huyue Zhang,S. Alex Yang,Hua Wei,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用语言学预设理论刻画LLM智能体在不可撤销操作前的"行动前须验证"规范，通过实验研究"工具显示什么"与"用户说什么"如何阻止智能体基于错误假设行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在取消预订、删除文件夹等不可逆操作前，可能基于错误假设行动。现有研究未系统区分：究竟是工具返回的信息，还是用户的陈述，能阻止智能体在没有依据时行动。作者希望用预设理论填补这一空白，并揭示"看起来查到了一条记录"并不等于"已排除存在第二条记录"。

Method: 引入语言学预设理论：智能体只有在行动背后的每项断言（如"有一条符合该姓名的预订"）被查明成立、由用户确认、或在操作可撤销时披露，才可行动。给出一个简单界：当查找工具无法区分"一条记录"与"两条记录"时，智能体在两条记录情形下先行行动的概率与一条记录时相同。实验操纵两类变量：(1)搜索工具对第二条匹配记录是列出、仅计数还是省略；(2)用户是否陈述一个被记录反驳的断言（如"它是空的"）。另在零售基准中加入同名同邮编客户进行测试。

Result: 在仅有角色提示下，仅"计数"出第二条记录时，36个早期智能体中多数仍对看到的那条行动；"列出"两条则能阻止多数。但当用户说文件夹是空的、而实际有一条内容时，"计数"与"列出"的阻止效果相近，且陈述本身为假时最能减少行动。最新的Claude与GPT模型总会打开被计数的第二条记录，之后便不再行动。在零售基准中加入同名同邮编客户后，查找优先返回该客户时约四分之一对话出现写入错误账户；而检查用户已知街道的查找能将完成率保持在单客户水平。

Conclusion: 安全写入需要能列出或核查竞争记录的查找工具，且评测应变化"工具显示什么"，而非仅依赖角色提示或用户陈述。计数信息对早期模型不足，但新模型已能据此探索；用户断言的作用主要在其为假时体现。

Abstract: Before an LLM agent cancels a reservation or deletes a folder for a user, what stops it from acting on a false assumption: what its tools show, or what the user says? In this work, we study this gap with presupposition theory from linguistics: an agent may act only if each claim behind the act (e.g., one reservation fits the name) is seen to hold, answered by the user, or disclosed while the act can be undone. A simple bound shows that when lookups cannot tell one record from two, any agent acts without asking first as often with two records as with one. We then vary whether a search lists, counts, or omits a second match, and whether the user states a claim that a record refutes (e.g., "It is empty"). Under a role-only prompt, a count of a second record leaves most of 36 earlier agents acting on the one they see, whereas listing both stops most. When the user says a record such as a folder is empty, however, a count of its one item stops agents about as often as a listing, and stating the claim mostly cuts acting when it is false. The newest Claude and GPT models always open a counted second record, then never act. When we add a same-name, same-zip customer to a retail benchmark, a lookup returning that customer first led to wrong-account writes in about a quarter of conversations, and one that checks a street the user knows kept completion near the one-customer level. Safe writes therefore need lookups that list or check competing records, and evaluations that vary what tools show.

</details>


### [230] [Learn then Bet: Learning Interpretable Rules for Reliable Agent Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17662)
*You-Lin Chen,Xinyi Lisa Chen,Pedro Herrero-Vidal,Xiaojing Yu,Bingqing Song,Bin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LeBet统计学习框架，学习可解释的OR-of-AND运行时监控规则，用固定赌注统计量累积证据并在阈值处干预，在无需i.i.d.假设下给出有限样本的有效性与功效保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体可无监督运行数百步，实践中用的turn limits、关键词过滤等启发式监控要么误停本可成功的运行，要么放任失败运行继续，缺乏统计可靠性保证。

Method: 从有效且高功效干预的充分条件导出学习目标；学习可解释的OR-of-AND监控器，并以固定betting统计量累积证据、达到预设阈值即干预。在类内有限可交换性与留一稳定性下，利用同一批轨迹同时学习与校准（不假设i.i.d.），给出训练条件性的假阳性控制与检测功效有限样本保证；对组合优化难题采用利用布尔结构提出规则编辑的随机局部搜索。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite + Ministral 3 8B上，学习到的监控器节省34.46%的输入输出token，输出精确率从18.33%提升到21.59%，经验假阳性率13.64%。

Conclusion: 展示了如何在可解释规则集上形式化学习问题并赋予统计条件，为基于部分历史的可靠干预建立训练条件性保证。

Abstract: Autonomous agents now run for hundreds of steps with little or no human supervision. In practice, practitioners use runtime monitors such as turn limits and keyword filters to decide when a run should be stopped, upgraded, or assisted. However, these heuristics may stop runs that would otherwise succeed or allow failing runs to continue. To address this limitation, we introduce LeBet (Learn then Bet), a statistical learning framework that derives its learning objective from sufficient conditions for valid and powerful intervention. In particular, LeBet learns an interpretable OR-of-AND monitor and accumulates evidence through a fixed betting statistic, intervening when the evidence reaches a prescribed threshold. Under finite within-class exchangeability and leave-one-out stability, we derive finite-sample guarantees for false-positive control (validity) and detection power conditional on the training data, using the same trajectories for learning and calibration without assuming independent and identically distributed trajectories. To address the intractable combinatorial optimization problem in our learning formulation, we use stochastic local search that leverages the monitor's Boolean structure to propose rule edits aimed at improving empirical separation. On SWE-bench Lite with Ministral 3 8B, the learned monitor saves $34.46\%$ of input-plus-output tokens and increases output precision from $18.33\%$ to $21.59\%$. The empirical false-positive rate is $13.64\%$. Our results show how to formalize the learning problem over interpretable rule sets with statistical conditions and establish training-conditional guarantees for reliable intervention based on partial histories.

</details>


### [231] [Aligned, Conflicting, or Unspecified? A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17680)
*Xintong Wang,Haihan Nan,Ruidong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个按"文本-历史关系"组织的真实世界诊断基准 ACU-Bench，覆盖气象、Wikipedia、金融三域共1,762对文本-窗口样本，标注为一致/冲突/未指明，并在十个预训练模型上零样本评估文本选择组合与误差诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIoT 系统越来越多地将感知时序与专家报告、新闻等外部文本结合，但模型如何使用与历史趋势"一致"或"冲突"的文本，其可靠性尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的诊断基准。

Method: 构建按文本-历史关系（Aligned/Conflicting/Unspecified）标注的基准，含 Weather、Wikipedia、Finance 三域；对十个预训练模型做零样本评测。RQ1 在配对气象窗口上比较直接预测型文本与状况描述型文本，并在冲突情形下只把直接预测文本中的幅度与时间信息并入描述型文本；RQ2 通过误差分解将误差上升归因于"修订幅度过大"，并提出免训练规则限制修订幅度。

Result: 一致情形下直接预测文本对多数模型误差最低；冲突情形下其使多数模型误差高于无文本基线；融合策略相较单一文本使六个模型 MASE 下降、七个模型 DA 提升。限制修订幅度的免训练规则在四个存在该权衡的模型上优于有/无文本基线，MASE 降低 4.49%–21.98%，DA 提升 2.99%–9.71%。

Conclusion: 文本的价值高度依赖其与历史趋势的关系：冲突文本会因过度修订而损害预测，需精细选择与幅度约束；该基准与诊断方法为 AIoT 多模态时序预测的可信使用提供了可复现工具。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems increasingly combine sensed time series with external text such as expert reports and news, yet how reliably models use text that agrees or conflicts with the sensed history remains unclear. We introduce the first real-world diagnostic benchmark organized by text-history relationship: 1,762 text-window pairs across sensor telemetry (Weather), web-service traffic (Wikipedia), and market data streams (Finance), each labeled Aligned, Conflicting, or Unspecified by whether the text's direction agrees with the historical trend, opposes it, or is not stated. Across ten pretrained models evaluated zero-shot, we investigate text selection and combination under text-history relationships (RQ1) and error diagnosis and horizon-scaled revision control (RQ2). For RQ1, we compare two types of text on paired Weather windows: one directly forecasts temperature changes, while the other describes related weather conditions, such as winds and fronts. Under Aligned, the first type yields the lowest error for most models. Under Conflicting, it increases most models' error above the no-text baseline; we therefore add only its stated magnitude and timing to the second type. Compared with either type alone, this combination lowers mean absolute scaled error (MASE) for six models and raises directional accuracy (DA) for seven. For RQ2, an error decomposition attributes the error increase to oversized revisions, and a training-free rule that limits them outperforms forecasts both with and without text for the four models with this trade-off, lowering MASE by 4.49%-21.98% and raising DA by 2.99%-9.71%. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/Shelton1202/ACU-Bench.

</details>


### [232] [Co-Sight Harness: Externalized Control Assets and Deterministic Enforcement for Long-Horizon Business Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17696)
*Luyao Wang,Xijun Zhu,Zhaohui Hao,Xiwen Zhang,Xingyu Wen,Weishan Li,Yuyang Liao,Yanqin Gao,Yongsheng Du,Lingjun Huang,Shaowei Zhao,Gang Li,Qifan Yu,Baoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出不改动语言模型的"控制资产外置"方法：把工作流定义、状态与领域知识版本化为模型外的控制资产，由状态机与可机检验收门执行，独立审计器评分，回归+人工审批后更新。在3个超200步的已部署工作流上验证，Domain A无人值守完成率50%→100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程业务流程存在两类失败：错误随活动链延长而累积；任务所需知识与用户请求的词汇不一致（知识-请求词表错配）。作者希望在不微调/不更换语言模型的前提下提升可靠性。

Method: 保持骨干模型固定，将工作流定义、状态和领域知识迁移为模型外的版本化控制资产；用状态机和机器可检查的验收门在运行期强制这些资产；由独立审计器对执行轨迹打分；资产仅在通过回归检查并经人工批准后才更新。在三个超200步的生产工作流上评测，并进行受控消融以分离两类失败来源。

Result: Domain A 的120例端到端评测：无人值守完成率50%→100%，检索召回56%→91.67%，端到端时延440→159分钟，场景分析召回45%→93%。另两个领域由未编写资产的团队接手，分别4天和7天接管上线。消融实验分离了两类失败来源。

Conclusion: 通过把控制逻辑与知识外置为可版本化、可审计、受门控的资产，可在不改变模型的情况下显著提升长程业务流程的可靠性与效率，且方法可被非原作者团队迁移复用。

Abstract: Long-horizon business workflows fail in two ways: errors accumulate as a chain of activities lengthens, and the knowledge an activity needs is written in a different vocabulary from the user's request. We study whether such workflows can be made reliable without changing the language model. Our method keeps the backbone fixed and moves the workflow definition, state and domain knowledge into versioned control assets outside the model. A state machine and machine-checkable acceptance gates enforce these assets during execution; an independent auditor grades the resulting trace. The assets are updated only after a regression check and human approval .We evaluate the method on three deployed workflows exceeding 200 steps. On Domain A, a 120-case end-to-end evaluation set shows unattended completion rising from 50% to 100%, retrieval recall from 56% to 91.67%, and end-to-end latency dropping from 440 to 159 minutes, with scenario-analysis recall rising from 45% to 93%. Two other domains were taken over by teams that did not write the assets, one in four days and one in seven days. A controlled ablation separates the two failure sources. The code is available at https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/master/Co-Sight%20Harness.

</details>


### [233] [An AI-enabled high-resolution chlorophyll-a forecasting model that outperforms the operational global biogeochemical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17704)
*Yong Jiang,Zheng Jiang,Gaowei Zhang,Zengzhou Hao,Suixiang Shi,Chi Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChlCast：一个基于深度学习的AI模型，可在60°S–60°N、约4km分辨率下进行10天逐日表层叶绿素-a浓度预报，精度显著优于Copernicus业务化数值预报系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶绿素-a是海洋浮游植物变化的关键指标，短期预报对海洋生态监测与管理有价值。但物理输运与生物过程的耦合使预报困难，传统数值模式计算开销巨大。

Method: ChlCast 采用因子化时空卷积（factorized spatiotemporal convolutions）与多尺度架构相结合，从历史叶绿素-a场和气候态信息中学习，输出近全球4km分辨率、10天视野的逐日预报。

Result: 对比0.25°的Copernicus全球海洋生物地球化学业务预报系统：对卫星L4叶绿素-a，MAE与RMSE分别降低70.74%和47.63%；对独立BGC-Argo实测，分别降低35.03%和13.07%。沿海案例显示4km预报能保留粗网格平均所削弱的沿岸梯度和局部浓度极大值。

Conclusion: 结果表明，观测驱动的深度学习有望实现近全球尺度的高分辨率表层叶绿素-a变化预报。

Abstract: Surface chlorophyll-a concentration is a key indicator of marine phytoplankton variability, and short-term forecasts of its distribution provide advance information for marine ecosystem monitoring and management. However, interactions between physical transport and biological processes complicate chlorophyll-a forecasting, and simulating these coupled processes with conventional numerical models imposes substantial computational demands. Here we introduce ChlCast, an AI model that generates daily forecasts of surface chlorophyll-a concentration over a 10-day horizon at approximately 4-km resolution between 60$^\circ$S and 60$^\circ$N. ChlCast combines factorized spatiotemporal convolutions with a multiscale architecture to learn from historical chlorophyll-a fields and climatological information. Compared with the operational 0.25$^\circ$ Copernicus global ocean biogeochemical forecasting system, ChlCast reduces mean absolute error and root mean square error by 70.74% and 47.63%, respectively, against satellite-derived Level-4 chlorophyll-a fields, and by 35.03% and 13.07% against independent BGC-Argo in situ observations. Coastal case studies further show that the 4-km forecasts retain coastal gradients and localized concentration maxima that are weakened by coarse-grid averaging. These results demonstrate the potential of observation-driven deep learning for high-resolution forecasting of surface chlorophyll-a variability at the near-global scale.

</details>


### [234] [Cross-Linguistic Acoustic Biomarkers of Cognitive Impairment Across Interactive and Single-Speaker Speech Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17710)
*Kenyu Ikeuchi,Tomoaki Ohtsuki,Michelle Cohn,Alyssa Weakley,Yui Ishihara,Joseph Hoang,Taishiro Kishimoto,Sarah Tomaszewski Farias,Jet M. J. Vonk,Chen-Nee Chuah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过跨语言、跨任务的声学分析，揭示痴呆检测中声学特征的泛化性受任务类型和语言制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言痴呆自动检测研究常依赖单一任务（如图片描述）和黑盒模型，忽视了任务与语言的作用，导致特征难以泛化、缺乏可解释性。

Method: 整合对话数据集与结构化语音任务，覆盖西班牙语、汉语、英语、日语四种语言；采用统计、语言学家/临床医生引导及机器学习模型三类特征选择方法，构建紧凑声学特征集，并进行系统性的跨数据集、跨任务比较。

Result: 声学特征无法在任务和语言间统一泛化。时间类特征在各数据集中稳健，能捕捉计时中断；语速高度依赖任务，仅在单人、外部锚定内容与节奏的任务中有效；音高特征呈语言特异性——声调语言（如汉语）偏好chroma类特征，非声调语言偏好F0类特征。

Conclusion: 研究警示不应无差别地混合异质语音数据，强调多语言痴呆检测需采用可解释、情境感知的方法。

Abstract: Multilingual dementia detection is essential for scalable and equitable screening. In recent years, automated speech analysis has emerged as a promising approach for supporting dementia detection by enabling non-invasive and scalable assessment of cognitive function. However, existing studies that use automated speech analysis often rely on one type of task, such as picture description, and use black-box models. These studies often neglect the role of task and language. To address these gaps, we performed a cross-linguistic and cross-task acoustic analysis that integrates a conversational dataset with structured speech tasks across four languages (Spanish, Mandarin, English, Japanese). We applied statistical, linguist-/clinician-guided, and machine learning model-based feature selection to derive compact acoustic feature sets while maintaining classification performance. This enabled systematic comparison of cognitive impairment-relevant features across datasets and tasks. Our results revealed that acoustic features did not generalize uniformly across tasks and languages. Temporal features remained robust across all datasets, consistently capturing timing disruption regardless of language or task. In contrast, speech rate showed strong task dependence -- emerging as informative mainly in single-speaker tasks with externally anchored content and pacing, but becoming less reliable when speech content was highly individualized. Pitch-related features further exhibited language-specific patterns, favoring chroma-related features in tonal languages (e.g., Mandarin) and F0-related features in non-tonal languages. These findings caution against indiscriminate pooling of heterogeneous speech data and underscore the need for interpretable, context-aware approaches to multilingual dementia detection using automated speech analysis.

</details>


### [235] [Federated Soft Poly-Level Learning Over a Distributed Network with Guaranteed Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17722)
*Yang Jiao,Kai Yang,Jianwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向分布式网络的联邦多层（≥4层）学习框架FPLL，用新的L-cut非线性割做逐层松弛，支持复杂度与性能的可调折衷，并给出达到ε-Pareto稳定点的迭代复杂度与非渐近分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多级优化研究多集中于双层/三层，四层及以上的"多级（poly-level）学习"问题几乎未被探索；且已有PLL研究多为非联邦设定，需将数据集中到中心服务器，带来隐私风险。因此需要能处理带软约束的多层优化、且以联邦方式在分布式网络上运行的框架。

Method: 提出联邦多级学习（FPLL）框架：核心是一种新的非线性割——L-cut，用于对PLL问题做逐层松弛；据此设计联邦算法，并引入可调参数，使用户能在计算复杂度与性能效率之间灵活权衡。

Result: 给出非渐近分析，推导出FPLL达到ε-Pareto稳定点所需的迭代复杂度；并通过大量实验验证所提方法具有更优效果。

Conclusion: FPLL将多层优化推广到四层及以上并实现联邦化，在保护数据隐私的同时兼顾可调的计算—性能权衡，理论上具复杂度保证、实验上表现更优。

Abstract: Multi-level learning/optimization, e.g., bilevel optimization, has been recognized for its versatile applicability across a broad array of domains, spanning from signal processing to machine learning. However, most existing works focus on bilevel and trilevel optimization. In contrast, multi-level optimization problems involving four or more hierarchical levels, which we term poly-level learning (PLL) problems, remain largely unexplored.In addition, in practical PLL, data may be distributed across multiple nodes over a distributed network. Nevertheless, extant studies on PLL predominantly focus on the non-federated setting, necessitating the collection of data to central servers and thereby posing potential privacy risks. To this end, we propose a Federated Poly-Level Learning (FPLL) framework over a distributed network to tackle the PLL problems with soft constraints in a federated manner. FPLL hinges on a novel non-linear cut, i.e., $L$-cut, for the level-wise relaxation to the PLL problems. Additionally, the proposed federated algorithm is designed with adjustable parameters that allow for a flexible trade-off between its computational complexity and performance efficiency, enabling users to tune it according to specific application requirements. We also carry out non-asymptotic analysis to derive the iteration complexity for the proposed FPLL to achieve the $ε$-Pareto stationary point. Extensive experiments have been undertaken to elucidate the superior efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [236] [A Spectral Framework for Universal Proxy Regularization in Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17728)
*Shangge Liu,Danyue Qian,Qi Fan,Yinghuan Shi,Wenbin Li,Lei Wang,Yang Gao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"通用代理正则化的谱框架"，用谱覆盖度α和泄漏度β刻画代理分布与待合并任务分布的关系，导出统一参数干扰上界，并比较三种代理正则器实现（雅可比积精确评估J、Fisher冻结曲率近似F、输出漂移O）在NTK线性化下等价、在非线性微调下形成层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可避免昂贵的联合训练，但存在参数干扰问题。已有缓解方法需使用其他待合并任务的数据来约束微调，违反了模型合并的核心独立性（无数据访问）和隐私假设。近期虽有经验证据表明通用、任务无关的代理分布可替代真实任务数据，但缺乏理论解释——何时、为何足够——尚不清楚。

Method: 1）构建谱框架：通过谱覆盖度α与谱泄漏度β刻画代理分布与其他任务分布的关系；2）基于该框架导出统一的参数干扰上界，证明更小的(α,β)经由更好的谱对齐带来更低干扰；3）在训练目标中加入代理正则项，并研究三种实现：J（基于雅可比向量积的精确评估）、F（基于Fisher信息矩阵的冻结曲率近似）、O（直接约束代理上函数偏差的输出漂移形式）；4）证明三者在NTK线性化下一致，并进一步刻画标准非线性微调下各自偏离线性化参考的程度，形成三层级：J为零偏差、F与代理无关、O随代理质量缩放。

Result: 理论上得到统一参数干扰上界及三种正则器在NTK线性化下的一致性与非线性下的偏差层级（J零偏差、F代理无关、O代理质量缩放）；在CLIP视觉-语言模型上的实验验证了这些理论预测。

Conclusion: 代理（数据无关）正则可替代真实任务数据缓解模型合并的参数干扰，其有效性可由谱覆盖度与泄漏度刻画；输出漂移形式(O)因其偏差随代理质量缩放而最具优势。工作为免数据、保隐私的模型合并提供了理论依据与实现指导。

Abstract: Model merging constructs multi-task models without expensive joint training but often suffers from parameter interference. Some solutions mitigate this by constraining finetuning using data from the other tasks to be merged, violating the core independence and privacy assumption of model merging. Recent work offers preliminary empirical evidence that a universal, task-agnostic proxy distribution can substitute for such data, but leaves open when and why it suffices. To this end, we propose a \textit{Spectral Framework for Universal Proxy Regularization}, which characterizes the relationship between a proxy distribution and the distribution of other tasks to be merged through spectral coverage ($α$) and leakage ($β$). Based on the framework, we derive a unified parameter interference bound: smaller $(α, β)$ provably yields lower parameter interference through better spectral alignment. To exploit such a proxy, we augment the training objective with a proxy regularizer. Evaluating this regularizer during fine-tuning requires a concrete mechanism. We study three instantiations, including (J) an exact evaluation via Jacobian-vector products, (F) a frozen curvature approximation using the Fisher information matrix, and (O) an output-drift formulation that constrains the model's functional deviation on the proxy directly. We prove that all three coincide under the NTK linearization, where existing analyses of such regularizers stop, and go further to characterize standard non-linear fine-tuning, bounding how far each instantiation departs from its linearized reference and obtain a three-way hierarchy, zero for (J), proxy-independent for (F), and proxy-quality-scaling for (O). Empirical validation on the commonly used vision-language model CLIP confirms these theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [237] [Knowledge After Collapse: Context and Wording Shape Factual Accessibility in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17763)
*Sujato Dutta,Prithviraj Patil,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Knowledge Answer Divergence (KAD)，一种事件条件化评测协议，通过"冻结"首次接受错误答案的对话历史并分支出独立探针，区分初始错误采纳与后续持续性、恢复和弃答，揭示单一易感性评分无法捕捉的事实可靠性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测只用单一"易感性"(susceptibility)分数衡量模型是否接受错误纠正，但模型接受错误后的下一步行为高度依赖对话如何继续，这一后续动态缺乏系统刻画手段。

Method: KAD 协议：在对话首次显式采纳指定错误目标时冻结历史，从该精确历史分支多个独立探针，实现后采纳行为的匹配比较，并支持历史管理干预实验。评测覆盖 8 个本地 checkpoint 条件与 6 个托管端点，另含经审计的 300 题重跑；并设计中性历史对照、匹配的线索位置控制(含 12 个独立撰写的施压模板)检验措辞敏感性。

Result: 在修正版 Llama 3.2 Instruct 3B 对照中，中性历史分支给出 42/46 正确回答，而完整历史仅 16/46；匹配的线索位置控制在 Qwen 3.5 4B 上使错误采纳变化 27.3 个百分点；12 个独立撰写的施压模板显示对措辞的高度敏感性。

Conclusion: KAD 能超越初始易感性评估事实可靠性，支持对错误答案持续性、恢复条件与对话干预进行系统比较，方法开源可复现。

Abstract: When a language model accepts a false correction in a factual discussion, its next answer can depend on how the conversation continues. We introduce Knowledge Answer Divergence (KAD), an event-conditioned evaluation protocol that distinguishes initial false-answer adoption from subsequent persistence, recovery, and abstention. KAD freezes the conversation at the first explicit adoption of a designated false target and branches independent probes from that exact history. This enables matched comparisons of post-adoption behavior and history-management interventions that a single susceptibility score cannot capture. We evaluate eight local checkpoint conditions and six hosted endpoints, alongside an audited 300-question rerun. In corrected Llama 3.2 Instruct 3B controls, a neutral-history branch yields 42/46 correct responses, compared with 16/46 under full history. A matched cue-placement control changes false adoption by 27.3 percentage points in Qwen 3.5 4B. Twelve independently authored pressure templates demonstrate substantial sensitivity to wording. Together, these findings demonstrate the utility of KAD for evaluating factual reliability beyond initial susceptibility, enabling systematic comparisons of false-answer persistence, recovery conditions and conversational interventions.

</details>


### [238] [Certified Improvements to Best-Known Thomson-Problem Configurations with a Search Pipeline Built with Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17776)
*Hui Shao,Tao Wang,Xiaotian Liu,Yijie Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用基于大语言模型智能体构建的副本交换盆地跳跃方法，找到了Thomson问题N=912、1382、2502的更低能量构型，并给出可复现性教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thomson问题需在球面上放置N个单位电荷使库仑能最小；大N下的最优构型收录于Cambridge Cluster Database。作者希望改进数据库中若干大N条目的能量，并同时考察LLM智能体做计算发现的可行性与陷阱。

Method: 从数据库构型出发做无约束的副本交换盆地跳跃，配合共享极小值池、按几何重标号后重组改进区域、仅用梯度的精修；用小整数精确算术认证能量。整个流程由Claude（聊天界面与Claude Code智能体）搭建与运行，并完整报告所有搜索（含失败）。

Result: N=912、1382、2502的能量分别比数据库低0.0036186、0.0109730、0.0614033，三者均为模旋转意义下的严格局部极小，且缺陷清单与疤痕组成保持不变。单独精修就能把两个条目降低到超过候选报告阈值，产生了两条虚假记录，直到改用“精修后的数据库能量”作基线才纠正；一个局部松弛加速方案通过精确性测试，但在控制对比中毫无收获，而所有使用完整松弛的分支都在15分钟内突破了数据库值。

Conclusion: 总结三条LLM智能体用于计算发现的做法：用独立代码校准搜索代码并验证候选；以精修后的基线作比较；设计改动须在预先设定通过标准的控制对比中检验。

Abstract: The Thomson problem asks for N unit charges on a sphere with minimum Coulomb energy; for large N the best configurations known are curated in the Cambridge Cluster Database, whose largest-N entries were set in October 2026. We report configurations for N = 912, 1382 and 2502 with energies 0.0036186, 0.0109730 and 0.0614033 below the database values, certified by exact integer arithmetic. All three are numerically strict local minima modulo rotations and keep the defect inventory and scar compositions of the database configurations; the database value for N = 912 had been reached by two different methods. Our search runs unconstrained replica-exchange basin hopping from the database configurations, with a shared pool of minima, recombination of improved regions after relabelling by geometry, and gradient-only polishing; replica-exchange basin hopping alone found the N = 912 and 1382 configurations, the former in one of five runs. We built and ran it with large language model agents (Claude, through a chat interface and the Claude Code agent), and we report every search, including those that failed. Two findings matter for others who search the database. Polishing alone lowers two entries by more than the threshold at which our pipeline reports a candidate, which produced two spurious records until candidates were compared with the polished database energies (the polished baseline). A local-relaxation speed-up passed its exactness test yet found nothing in a controlled comparison in which every arm with full relaxation went below the database value within 15 minutes. We distil three practices for computational discovery with large language model agents: calibrate the search code and verify candidates with code separate from the search, compare candidates with the polished baseline, and test design changes in controlled comparisons with pass criteria fixed in advance.

</details>


### [239] [TReC: Evaluating Move-Induced State Changes in Instruction-Conditioned Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17781)
*Zhuangsheng Li,Yunhan Zhang,Dailin Li,Qingxian Yu,Yu Wu,Youqi Song,Bangyuan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TReC用"两枚走子引发的表征变化之余弦相似度"衡量模型预测与专家着法的差异，其区分度远高于坐标距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围棋模型预测与专家着法不一致时，常用坐标距离衡量偏离程度，但坐标距离只度量落点位置，无法反映不同着法对棋局状态表征的影响差异。

Method: 提出Transition Representation Consistency (TReC)：在同一局面下按规则分别执行预测着法与专家着法，取两次走子引起的棋盘状态嵌入变化向量，计算二者的余弦相似度。

Result: 在冻结的KataGo网络上，对五局棋的680个中后盘局面实验：每局面候选中最高与最低分数差距的中位数，直接后继状态余弦为0.01–0.08，TReC为0.88–1.33（随评估层不同）。TReC与坐标距离仅弱相关，局面内秩相关中位数为−0.19。

Conclusion: TReC能提供与坐标距离不同且更敏感的表征层信号，可作为评估着法偏离的补充指标；但其与几何距离弱相关，说明二者捕获的信息互补。

Abstract: When a Go model predicts a move different from the expert's, coordinate distance measures how far the prediction lands from the reference. We introduce Transition Representation Consistency (TReC) to compare how the two moves change a board-state embedding. Both moves are executed from the same position under the game rules, and TReC is the cosine similarity between the two resulting representation changes. In experiments with a frozen KataGo network on 680 mid-to-late-game positions from five games, the gap between the highest and lowest candidate per position has a median of 0.01 to 0.08 for the direct successor-state cosine and 0.88 to 1.33 for TReC, depending on the evaluator layer. TReC follows coordinate distance only weakly: the rank correlation between a candidate's distance to the reference and its TReC, computed within each position, has a median of -0.19 over these positions.

</details>


### [240] [AutoRL: A Multi-Agent System towards Autonomous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17790)
*Simon Tang,Wenjie Shi,Yongjing Yin,Keheng Wang,Jialin Liu,Zheng Zhang,Linvo Lin,Xiangyuan Xue,Kan Ren,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRL 是一个多智能体系统，用于自动化强化学习训练流程，作为未来递归自我改进（RSI）的基石：它不像端到端实验那样一次性跑完，而是像人类研究者一样在训练过程中持续监控、诊断并在线修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 在迭代自我改进循环中至关重要，但自动化 RL 很难：训练动态不稳定、需要持续监督。作者希望把 RL 训练的自动闭环作为通往 RSI / 超级智能的基础设施。

Method: AutoRL 采用三个 RL 专用的智能体角色：Perceptors 监控训练动态、rollout、评估与效率，在检测到异常、退化或停滞时触发讨论；三个互补的 Proposers 基于共享证据与检查诊断问题并细化干预候选方案；Orchestrator 审核建议并通过在线热更新或基于 checkpoint 的恢复来应用变更。

Result: 在 Qwen3 与 LongCat-Flash-Lite 上的实验表明，AutoRL 优于静态 RL 配方和一个通用智能体；研究记录显示现有智能体能胜任无人值守的 RL 监督与动态调整。

Conclusion: 现有智能体已能承担无人值守的 RL 监督与动态调整；未来 RSI 的难点不在于让智能体做监督，而在于发展对“消耗大量资源的验证”真正有价值的基础方法。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) is one of the most promising paths toward an AI singularity for superintelligence. Achieving this vision requires AI systems to undertake the full training pipeline for iterative improvement. Reinforcement learning (RL) plays an essential role in this cycle to improve model capabilities and generalization, yet automating RL remains difficult due to unstable training dynamics and the need for sustained supervision. We introduce AutoRL, a multi-agent system for automating RL training, as a cornerstone for future RSI. Different from conducting research by end-to-end complete experiments, AutoRL monitors the evolving training state and integrates process-level supervision and research into an ongoing RL training, following the practical workflow of an RL researcher. Specifically, AutoRL consists of three RL-specific agentic roles. Perceptors track training dynamics, rollouts, evaluation, and efficiency, triggering deliberation when they detect anomalies, degradation, or stagnation. Three complementary Proposers diagnose problems and refine candidate interventions through shared evidence and checks. The Orchestrator reviews their recommendation and applies approved changes through online hot updates or checkpoint-based resumption. Experiments on Qwen3 and LongCat-Flash-Lite demonstrate the superiority of AutoRL over static RL recipes and a general agent. The research records show that existing agents can handle unattended RL supervision and dynamic adjustment. The difficulty for future RSI lies in developing fundamental methods that are valuable for resource-consuming validation.

</details>


### [241] [MASTARS: Multi-Agent Sequential Trajectory Augmentation with Return-Conditioned Subgoals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17793)
*Jiwon Jeon,Myungsik Cho,Woojun Kim,Seongmin Kim,Woohyeon Byeon,Seungyul Han,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASTARS 提出一种基于扩散模型的多智能体轨迹增强框架，通过按智能体顺序生成并利用 inpainting 强制跨智能体协调，生成多样、协调且全局一致的轨迹，在 MPE、SMAC、SMAC-v2 上显著提升多种离线 MARL 方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线 MARL 性能受限于固定离线数据集的质量，因此需要轨迹增强。但多智能体轨迹增强存在多样性-协调权衡：独立生成多样性好但缺乏协调，联合生成有协调性但多样性差、只是复现训练分布。

Method: 提出 MASTARS，一种基于扩散模型的框架：按智能体顺序（sequentially）生成多智能体轨迹，并通过 inpainting 方法在生成过程中施加跨智能体一致的协调约束，从而同时获得多样性与协调性。

Result: 在 MPE、SMAC 和 SMAC-v2 等多个基准上的实验表明，MASTARS 生成的轨迹具备多样性、协调性与全局一致性，并对多种离线 MARL 方法带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: MASTARS 通过顺序生成加 inpainting 约束有效缓解了多智能体数据增强中的多样性-协调权衡，是一种通用且有效的离线 MARL 轨迹增强方案。

Abstract: The performance of offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) is limited by the quality of the fixed offline dataset, motivating trajectory augmentation. However, augmenting multi-agent trajectories presents a fundamental challenge of the diversity-coordination trade-off: independent generation improves diversity but lacks coordination, while joint generation induces coordination but lacks diversity, reproducing the training distribution. To address this challenge in multi-agent data augmentation, we introduce MASTARS, a novel diffusion-based framework that generates multi-agent trajectories sequentially across agents while enforcing cross-agent consistent coordination via the method of inpainting. MASTARS produces trajectories that are diverse, coordinated and globally coherent. Experiments on various benchmarks such as MPE, SMAC, and SMAC-v2 demonstrate significant performance gains across multiple offline MARL methods.

</details>


### [242] [Robustness Evaluation of GPU Cloud Pricing Benchmarks under Incomplete Observations: A Controlled Missingness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17796)
*Jooya Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文对日度GPU租赁价格基准在缺失报价下的稳健性进行压力测试：在四种掩蔽机制与四种恢复方法下做5,800次蒙特卡洛（27情境、190天），发现整源断供只降低覆盖而零误差，组成变动型缺失则保留较高覆盖却引入约7.74个对数点误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日度GPU租赁价格基准在市场报价缺失时可能失效，需评估固定等权中位数规则在不同缺失机制下的误差与覆盖表现，并检验恢复方法和利用更多信息的改进潜力。

Method: 对固定等权中位数规则施加四种人为掩蔽机制，并比较四种基准层面恢复方法；在27个情境、190个留出日上共5,800次蒙特卡洛实现。另做仅用历史数据的层级消融，以及可观测信号扣留规则检验。

Result: 整源断供使发布覆盖率降至0.77，但发布日条件误差为零；改变组成的缺失保留0.86覆盖，却产生7.74对数点误差。仅用过去数据的层级消融在五个组成变动案例中降低误差4.15–6.70对数点。可观测信号扣留规则在10个案例中0个改善主误差—覆盖准则。

Conclusion: 缺失机制类型决定基准失效方式：断供影响覆盖、组成变化影响准确性；利用更丰富的信息可部分纠偏，而简单扣留信号无益。结论限于观测要价数据，不涉及成交价或真实缺失机制识别。

Abstract: Daily GPU rental-price benchmarks can fail when marketplace quotes are missing. This article stress-tests a fixed equal-weight median rule under four imposed masking mechanisms and four benchmark-level recovery methods. Across 5,800 Monte Carlo realisations in 27 scenarios on 190 held-out days, whole-source outages reduce publication coverage to 0.77 while preserving zero conditional error on published dates; composition-changing loss retains 0.86 coverage but produces 7.74 log points of error. A separate past-only stratum-level ablation reduces error by 4.15 to 6.70 log points in five composition-changing cases, using a richer information set. An observable-signal withholding rule improves the primary error-coverage criterion in 0 of 10 cases. Results concern observed asking-price measurements, not transaction prices or identified real-world missingness mechanisms.

</details>


### [243] [Vision-as-a-Tool: Visual Harness for Multimodal Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17797)
*Haolin Yang,Wenshan Wu,Jin Jiang,Yu Cheng,Yan Xia,Ting Song,Yangyu Huang,Zhiliang Peng,Wenhui Wang,Hangbo Bao,Yuzhe Zhang,Wen-Da Wei,Tengchao Lv,Li Dong,Lei Cui,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Vision-as-a-Tool (VaaT) 范式，将多模态大模型作为可调用的外部视觉工具，让纯文本智能体也能完成视觉推理任务，并在 VHarness 框架（五种原子动作）中实现，在 Image-to-HTML/SVG/3D 任务上提升视觉保真度与几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态编程智能体依赖 Thinking-with-Images，视觉能力与模型内在感知紧耦合，性能受限于内部感知瓶颈；纯文本智能体缺乏原生视觉，无专用工具则无法进行视觉推理。

Method: 提出 VaaT 范式：不同于以往仅操控图像供智能体自身视觉读取的视觉工具用法，将通用 MLLM 重新定义为可调用的外部工具，使文本智能体可完全外包视觉推理、视觉智能体可按需增强感知。并构建 VHarness 统一框架，由五种原子动作驱动。

Result: 在 Image-to-HTML、Image-to-SVG 和 Image-to-3D 生成任务上全面评估，VHarness 在纯文本与多模态编程智能体上均持续提升视觉保真度和几何精度。

Conclusion: 将视觉推理模块化为可调用工具，为未来智能编程系统提供了灵活、可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic coding requires robust visual reasoning to analyze observations and evaluate generated artifacts. Current frameworks largely rely on a Thinking-with-Images paradigm, where agents depend on their intrinsic vision to execute visually grounded tasks. However, this architectural coupling introduces a fundamental bottleneck. Agent performance remains strictly bounded by internal perceptual limitations. Furthermore, text-only agents lack native perception. Without specialized tools, they cannot perform visual reasoning. To overcome these constraints, we introduce the Vision-as-a-Tool (VaaT) paradigm. Prior visual tool-use merely manipulates images for an agent's intrinsic vision to read. In contrast, VaaT reframes general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as callable external utilities. As a result, text-only agents can completely offload visual reasoning during agentic execution. Meanwhile, vision-capable agents can dynamically augment their native perception on demand. We instantiate this paradigm in VHarness, a unified framework driven by five atomic actions. We comprehensively evaluate VHarness across Image-to-HTML, Image-to-SVG, and Image-to-3D generation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that VHarness consistently improves visual fidelity and geometric accuracy across both text-only and multimodal coding agents. These findings highlight that modularizing visual reasoning into callable utilities provides a flexible and scalable foundation for future agentic coding systems.

</details>


### [244] [Can Agents Keep Up with Time? An Empirical Analysis of Temporal Adaptation After a Conversational Hiatus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17806)
*Hongguan Su,Ming Wang,Shun Zhang,Yifan Wang,Rong Yin,Zhiming Ma,Mengzheng Yang,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"时间状态感知与利用"能力，通过session-rekindling任务测试长期对话代理能否感知时间流逝并据此更新建议。实验发现代理能查询到当前时间却常常不会使用它，仍会建议用户为已过期的事件做准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期助理代理在多周/多月的会话间隙后重新被用户唤醒，保留的日程记录未变但日期可能已过、结果未记录。现有长期代理评测只关注事实保留与显式状态更新，忽略了代理是否感知时间流逝并据此调整建议，这一能力此前未知。

Method: 将能力定义为"时间状态感知与利用"（感知当前时间并用于解读带日期的记录）。设计session-rekindling任务：固定早前对话内容，仅改变后续日期以及时间信息传递给代理的方式。在5种模型路线×5种时间信息条件下进行实验。

Result: 代理能够获取当前时间但常常不使用它：能可靠查询时钟、甚至推理中提到日期已过，却仍建议用户为过期事件做准备。提供显式时间上下文可降低该错误，提供计算好的日期关系可完全消除错误。

Conclusion: 跟上时间的关键在于把感知到的时间转化为更新后的计划，而非仅获取时间。据此设计了session-rekindling基准，为长期代理设计提供时间感知指导，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly serve as persistent personal assistants across modern web platforms, managing schedules, applications, and multi-step plans over weeks or months. Users often return to an ongoing conversation after a long conversational hiatus, while earlier bookings and preparation notes stay on record. During this hiatus, scheduled dates may pass even though the retained records remain unchanged and event outcomes go unrecorded. Existing evaluations of long-horizon agents focus on fact retention and explicit state updates. Whether agents perceive the passage of time and adapt their advice accordingly therefore remains unknown. We frame this ability as temporal state perception and use, which means perceiving the current time and applying it when interpreting dated records. To study it, we design session-rekindling tasks that keep the earlier conversation fixed and vary only the follow-up date and the way temporal information reaches the agent. Experiments across five model routes and five temporal-information conditions show that agents can obtain the current time but often fail to use it. They reliably query the clock and even note in their reasoning that the scheduled date has passed, yet many still tell users to prepare for the expired event. Supplying explicit temporal context reduces this error, and providing the computed date relation removes it entirely. Keeping up with time therefore depends on turning perceived time into updated plans. Based on these findings, we design a session-rekindling benchmark that provides temporal-awareness guidance for designing long-horizon agents. The code can be found at https://github.com/Adoreu2/session-rekindling-benchmark.

</details>


### [245] [G-CAST: Governed Continual Self-Training for E-Commerce Agents under Evolving Demands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17814)
*Yuechun Yu,Wenzheng Kong,Xiaofeng Mao,Jin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: G-CAST：面向生产环境电商对话智能体的“受治理持续自训练”系统，把每日生产证据转化为按奖励路由、熵优先的训练数据，并通过累计评估控制模型更新，实现学习新能力而不遗忘旧能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 购物智能体面临两类变化：1）需求漂移（新品类、新采购场景、新功能诉求）改变请求分布、意图与复杂度，一次性训练的模型会落后；2）执行框架（工具、技能、操作流程）持续演进，模型需正确使用新能力且不破坏用户已依赖的行为。周期性人工重训延迟大、成本高，不适合该场景。

Method: 提出 G-CAST，一种受治理的持续自训练系统：将需求与能力契约演进下的每日生产证据，转化为“奖励路由 + 熵优先”的训练数据，并进行受治理的奖励演化；通过累计评估下的受治理检查点切换来控制每次模型更新。

Result: 在真实生产电商智能体上的实验表明，相比强自主后训练基线，G-CAST 对新引入需求的适应更强，同时更好地保留此前已验收行为。消融实验显示，奖励路由与熵优先的数据编排、以及受治理的奖励演化，均是性能提升的关键。

Conclusion: G-CAST 已部署于阿里巴巴 1688 平台的 B2B 采购智能体“源采”（Yuancai），验证了持续自训练在生产电商智能体中的可行性与实际价值。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), an increasing number of users are turning to LLM-powered agents for conversational shopping in e-commerce. However, the user needs continue to change as new product categories, procurement scenarios, and feature requests emerge. At the same time, the product's execution harness-including its tools, skills, and operating procedures-continues to evolve to support increasingly sophisticated interactions. These changes create two practical challenges: 1) Demand drift alters the distribution, intent, and complexity of user requests, causing a model trained once to fall behind; 2) Harness evolution requires the model to use newly introduced capabilities correctly without losing behavior that users already rely on. Periodic manual retraining is too delayed and labor-intensive for this setting. To address these challenges, we present G-CAST, a governed continual self-training system that converts daily production evidence under evolving demand and capability contracts into reward-routed, entropy-prioritized training data and governed reward evolution. Governed checkpoint transition under cumulative evaluation controls each model update, enabling the agent to learn newly introduced behaviors without sacrificing previously accepted capabilities. Experiments on a real production e-commerce agent show that G-CAST achieves stronger adaptation to newly introduced demand while better preserving previously accepted behavior compared with a strong autonomous post-training baseline. Ablations further show that both reward-routed, entropy-prioritized data orchestration and governed reward evolution are important to these gains. G-CAST is now deployed in Yuancai(http://cai.1688.com/), a B2B procurement agent on Alibaba's 1688 platform.

</details>


### [246] [Causally Constrained and Uncertainty-Aware Cardiopulmonary Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17819)
*Yuxiang Lai,Jiashuo Zhang,Liang Zhao,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI
